Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become the standard for improving reasoning in large language models, yet evidence increasingly suggests that RL does not teach new strategies; it redistributes probability mass over solutions the base model already contains. In this work, we ask: if RL merely steers the model toward paths it already knows, is the RL optimization loop itself necessary? Through token-level analysis across multiple model families and RL algorithms, we find that RL's beneficial footprint is a sparse, predictable correction concentrated at high-entropy decision points where the model is uncertain which branch to take. Only 1--3\% of token positions are affected, the promoted token always lies within the base model's top-5 alternatives, and targeted corrections at those few positions causally recover a large fraction of RL's accuracy gain, while random corrections fail. The base model's own entropy identifies these positions without any RL-trained model, and the entire correction is low-dimensional, representable in a tiny fraction of model parameters. These findings reframe reasoning improvement as sparse policy selection, not capability acquisition. We translate this insight into ReasonMaxxer, a minimal RL-free method that applies contrastive loss only at entropy-gated decision points, using a few hundred base-model rollouts and no online generation. Across three model families, six scales, and six math reasoning benchmarks, ReasonMaxxer matches or exceeds full RL performance while requiring only tens of problems and minutes of single-GPU training, a reduction in training cost of roughly three orders of magnitude.

</details>

Conclusion: 基准数据集和所提方法可推广至其他低资源口语语言。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation is a core component of discourse analysis, yet existing models are primarily developed and evaluated on high-resource written text, limiting their effectiveness on low-resource spoken varieties. In particular, dialectal Arabic exhibits informal syntax, code-switching, and weakly marked discourse structure that challenge standard segmentation approaches. In this paper, we introduce a new multi-genre benchmark (more than 1000 samples) for semantic segmentation in conversational Arabic, focusing on dialectal discourse. The benchmark covers transcribed casual telephone conversations, code-switched podcasts, broadcast news, and expressive dialogue from novels, and was annotated and validated by native Arabic annotators. Using this benchmark, we show that segmentation models performing well on MSA news genres degrade on dialectal transcribed speech. We further propose a segmentation model that targets local semantic coherence and robustness to discourse discontinuities, consistently outperforming strong baselines on dialectal non-news genres. The benchmark and approach generalize to other low-resource spoken languages.

</details>

*Jessica Huynh,Alfredo Gomez,Athiya Deviyani,Renee Shelby,Jeffrey P. Bigham,Fernando Diaz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了评分标准(rubric)的设计如何影响人类评分者和LLM自动评分器之间的一致性，发现提供代表性示例、减少位置偏差可以提高一致性，而增加复杂性和保守聚合方法则会降低一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM作为评判者(autoraters)被广泛用于评估和自动内容审核，但关于评分标准的设计如何影响人类与自动评分器之间的一致性缺乏统计分析。这项研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 研究对比了整体性(holistic)评分标准与分析性(analytic)评分标准的效果。整体性评分直接评估内容的"质量"，而分析性评分将标准分解为多个维度。研究者修改评分标准后观察人类评分者和自动评分器之间一致性的变化。

Result: 增加代表性示例和额外上下文、减少位置偏差的评分标准修改可提高人机一致性；而更高的评分标准复杂性和保守的聚合方法则倾向于降低一致性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在自动作文评分和指令遵循评估领域，实践者应仔细分析特定领域和评分标准的性能，以实现更高的人类-自动评分器一致性。

Abstract: Autoraters, also referred to as LLM-as-judges, are increasingly used for evaluation and automated content moderation. However, there is limited statistical analysis of how modifications in a rubric presented to both humans and autoraters affect their score agreement. Rubrics that ask for an overall or \emph{holistic} judgment - for example, rating the ``quality'' of an essay - may be inconsistently interpreted due to the complexity or subjectivity of the criteria. Conversely, rubrics can ask for \emph{analytic} judgments, which decompose assessment criteria - for example, ``quality'' into ``fluency'' and ``organization''. While these rubrics can be edited to improve the individual accuracy of both human and automated scoring, this approach may result in disagreement between the two scores, or with the associated holistic judgment. Designing and deploying reliable autoraters requires understanding not just the relationship between human and autorater annotations but how that relationship changes as holistic or analytic judgments are elicited. The results indicate that rubric edits providing representative examples and additional context, and reducing positional bias in the rubric increased human-autorater agreement, while higher rubric complexity and conservative aggregation methods tended to decrease it. The findings from the automatic essay scoring and instruction-following evaluation domains suggest that practitioners should carefully analyze domain- and rubric-specific performance to move towards higher human-autorater agreement.

</details>


### [39] [LatentRAG: Latent Reasoning and Retrieval for Efficient Agentic RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06285)
*Yijia Zheng,Marcel Worring*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LatentRAG是一种新型RAG框架，通过将推理和检索过程从离散语言空间转移到连续潜空间，在单次前向传播中直接生成潜token来处理复杂问答任务。该方法在保持性能的同时将推理延迟降低约90%，为高效agentic RAG提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单步RAG在简单问答任务中表现高效，但在处理复杂问题时能力不足。Agentic RAG通过多步迭代检索增强了复杂问题的处理能力，但代价是推理延迟显著增加，因为需要在每一步自回归生成冗长的thought和subquery序列。这种延迟差距限制了agentic RAG在实际应用中的可行性。

Method: LatentRAG将推理和检索从离散的语言空间转移到连续潜空间。在单次前向传播中直接从LLM的隐藏状态生成thought和subquery的潜token，而非逐token生成自然语言。框架包含三个核心组件：1）潜推理模块，直接从隐藏状态输出潜token进行thought和subquery生成；2）潜检索对齐，将LLM与密集检索模型在潜空间中对齐，支持基于潜subquery token的检索；3）并行潜解码机制，将潜token反向翻译为自然语言以提升可解释性。整个系统支持端到端联合优化。

Result: 在7个基准数据集上的实验表明，LatentRAG在性能上与显式agentic RAG方法相当，同时将推理延迟降低了约90%，显著缩小了与单步RAG之间的延迟差距。

Conclusion: LatentRAG提供了一种高效且可行的解决方案，在保持与显式agentic RAG相当性能的同时，大幅降低了推理延迟，显著缩小了与单步RAG之间的延迟差距。这项工作为RAG系统的高效化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Single-step retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) provides an efficient way to incorporate external information for simple question answering tasks but struggles with complex questions. Agentic RAG extends this paradigm by replacing single-step retrieval with a multi-step process, in which the large language model (LLM) acts as a search agent that generates intermediate thoughts and subqueries to iteratively interact with the retrieval system. This iterative process incurs substantial latency due to the autoregressive generation of lengthy thoughts and subqueries. To address this limitation, we propose LatentRAG, a novel framework that shifts both reasoning and retrieval from discrete language space to continuous latent space. Unlike existing explicit methods that generate natural language thoughts or subqueries token-by-token, LatentRAG produces latent tokens for thoughts and subqueries directly from the hidden states in a single forward pass. We align LLMs with dense retrieval models in the latent space, enabling retrieval over latent subquery tokens and supporting end-to-end joint optimization. To improve transparency and encourage semantically meaningful latent representations, we incorporate a parallel latent decoding mechanism that translates latent tokens back into natural language. Extensive experiments on seven benchmark datasets show that LatentRAG achieves performance comparable to explicit agentic RAG methods while reducing inference latency by approximately 90%, substantially narrowing the latency gap with traditional single-step RAG.

</details>


### [40] [Log-Likelihood, Simpson's Paradox, and the Detection of Machine-Generated Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06294)
*Tom Kempton,Viktor Drobnyi,Maeve Madigan,Stuart Burrell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了现有AI文本检测器性能欠佳的根本原因——隐藏空间中不同区域的信号被不当聚合导致的Simpson悖论，并提出基于贝叶斯决策理论的局部校准方法作为通用解决方案，显著提升了所有基线检测器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流检测器基于"机器生成文本在检测模型看来更可能"的似然假设，但存在关键缺陷：token级别人机区分信号在检测模型隐藏空间的不同区域中分布不均匀。现有检测器不加区分地跨区域平均token分数，犯了Simpson悖论式的错误——强局部信号被不当聚合所破坏。

Method: 提出基于贝叶斯决策理论的局部校准步骤。具体而言：(1) 学习轻量级预测器，对探测器模型隐藏空间中不同位置的token分数分布进行建模；(2) 不直接聚合原始token分数，而是聚合校准后的对数似然比；(3) 在此基础上引入局部校准的DMAP检测器。

Result: 单次干预（局部校准）即可显著且一致地提升所有基线检测器在各数据集上的表现。例如，Fast-DetectGPT在校准后在GPT-5.4文本上的AUROC从0.63提升至0.85。

Conclusion: 所提出的局部校准方法显著且稳定地提升了所有基线检测器的性能（如Fast-DetectGPT的AUROC从0.63提升至0.85），且基于该方法的新DMAP检测器达到了最优性能。本文的核心贡献在于提供了一个精确诊断框架和可模块化集成到任何token平均管道中的原则性解决方案。

Abstract: The ability to reliably distinguish human-written text from that generated by large language models is of profound societal importance. The dominant approach to this problem exploits the likelihood hypothesis: that machine-generated text should appear more probable to a detector language model than human-written text. However, we demonstrate that the token-level signal distinguishing human and machine text is non-uniform across the hidden space of the detector model, and naively averaging likelihood-based token scores across regions with fundamentally different statistical structure, as most detectors do, causes a form of Simpson's paradox: a strong local signal is destroyed by inappropriate aggregation. To correct for this, we introduce a learned local calibration step grounded in Bayesian decision theory. Rather than aggregating raw token scores, we first learn lightweight predictors of the score distributions conditioned on position in hidden space, and aggregate calibrated log-likelihood ratios instead. This single intervention dramatically and consistently improves detection performance across all baseline detectors and all datasets we consider. For example, our calibrated variant of Fast-DetectGPT improves AUROC from $0.63$ to $0.85$ on GPT-5.4 text, and a locally-calibrated DMAP detector we introduce achieves state-of-the-art performance across the board. That said, our central contribution is not a new detector, but a precise diagnosis of a significant cause of under-performance of existing detectors and a principled, modular remedy compatible with any token-averaging pipeline. This will serve as a foundation for the community to build upon, with natural avenues including richer distributional models, improved calibration strategies, and principled ensembling with hidden-space geometry signals via the full Bayes-optimal decision rule.

</details>

Abstract: Laughter is a social non-vocalization that is universal across cultures and languages, and is crucial for human communication, including social bonding and communication signaling. However, detecting laughter in audio is a challenging task, and segmenting is even more difficult. Currently, Machine Learning methods generally rely on costly manual annotation, and their datasets are mostly based on English contexts. Thus, we propose an unsupervised multilingual method that sets up the laughter segmentation task as an anomaly detection of energy-based segmented audio sequences. Our method applies an Isolation Forest on audio representations learned from BYOL-A encoder. We compare our method with several state-of-the-art laughter detection algorithms on four datasets, including stand-up comedy, sitcoms, and general short audio from AudioSet. Our results show that state-of-the-art methods are not optimized for multilingual contexts, while our method outperforms them in non-English settings.

### [42] [Teaching Thinking Models to Reason with Tools: A Full-Pipeline Recipe for Tool-Integrated Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06326)
*Qianjia Cheng,Yuchen Zhang,Zhilin Wang,Yuxin Zuo,Shunkai Zhang,Yuchen Fan,Yu Qiao,Bowen Zhou,Ning Ding,Yu Cheng,Yun Luo,Ganqu Cui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将工具使用行为注入强思维模型而不牺牲其无工具推理能力的TIR综合方法。核心发现包括：SFT效果取决于教师轨迹的可学性、工具轨迹比例控制可缓解灾难性遗忘、优化pass@k和响应长度优于训练损失、以及稳定的RLVR阶段配合适当SFT初始化可取得优异效果。在Qwen3-4B和30B模型上实现了开源模型中的SOTA性能，AIME 2025分别达到96.7%和99.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到一个反直觉现象：即使强思维模型几乎不实际调用工具，启用工具的评估也可能导致推理性能下降。这引发了研究如何将自然工具使用行为注入强思维模型，同时不牺牲其无工具推理能力的问题。

Method: (1) 构建可学性高的教师轨迹，优先选择本身适合工具增强解决方案的问题；(2) 控制工具使用轨迹比例以缓解灾难性遗忘；(3) 优化pass@k和响应长度而非训练损失；(4) 基于合适SFT初始化的稳定RLVR阶段，配合防止模式崩溃的显式保护措施。

Result: 在Qwen3思考模型（4B和30B规模）上应用该方法，在开源模型中实现了SOTA性能：AIME 2025上分别达到96.7%和99.2%。

Conclusion: TIR SFT的有效性关键在于教师轨迹的可学性；工具轨迹比例控制是保持文本推理能力的关键；pass@k和响应长度优化可最大化SFT收益同时保留RL探索空间；稳定RLVR阶段提供简单而有效的后续优化。

Abstract: Tool-integrated reasoning (TIR) offers a direct way to extend thinking models beyond the limits of text-only reasoning. Paradoxically, we observe that tool-enabled evaluation can degrade reasoning performance even when the strong thinking models make almost no actual tool calls. In this paper, we investigate how to inject natural tool-use behavior into a strong thinking model without sacrificing its no-tool reasoning ability, and present a comprehensive TIR recipe. We highlight that (i) the effectiveness of TIR supervised fine-tuning (SFT) hinges on the learnability of teacher trajectories, which should prioritize problems inherently suited for tool-augmented solutions; (ii) controlling the proportion of tool-use trajectories could mitigate the catastrophic forgetting of text-only reasoning capacity; (iii) optimizing for pass@k and response length instead of training loss could maximize TIR SFT gains while preserving headroom for reinforcement learning (RL) exploration; (iv) a stable RL with verifiable rewards (RLVR) stage, built upon suitable SFT initialization and explicit safeguards against mode collapse, provides a simple yet remarkably effective solution. When applied to Qwen3 thinking models at 4B and 30B scales, our recipe yields models that achieve state-of-the-art performance in a wide range of benchmarks among open-source models, such as 96.7% and 99.2% on AIME 2025 for 4B and 30B, respectively.

</details>


### [43] [Measuring Evaluation-Context Divergence in Open-Weight LLMs: A Paired-Prompt Protocol with Pilot Evidence of Alignment-Pipeline-Specific Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06327)
*Florian A. D. Burnat,Brittany I. Davidson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了"评估情境分歧"(evaluation-context divergence)现象，即语言模型在同一任务的不同框架(评估、部署、中立请求)下表现出的行为差异。通过配对提示协议测试5个开源模型的20个配对项目，发现模型间存在显著异质性：OLMo-3-Instruct呈"评估谨慎"模式，而Mistral-Small-3.2、Phi-3.5-mini、Llama-3.1-8B呈"部署谨慎"模式。基座模型实验表明对齐(alignment)是导致模式反转的关键阶段，且该效应具有法官依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全基准测试通常被用来预测语言模型部署后的行为，但这种推断存在脆弱性——模型行为可能因提示是否看起来像评估而改变。作者将这种现象定义为"评估情境分歧"，认为当前依赖安全基准评估模型部署后行为的方法可能存在根本性缺陷。

Method: 采用配对提示协议(paired-prompt protocol)，控制变量包括措辞变体、基准熟悉度和评判框架敏感性。对来自4个开源家族的5个指令微调检查点进行测试，并包含OLMo-3基座/指令模型的匹配消融实验。每个检查点产生20个配对项目、840次生成。

Result: OLMo-3-Instruct表现出评估谨慎模式：评估框架相比中立框架拒绝率提高11.8个百分点(p=0.007)，有害遵从率相比部署场景降低3.6个百分点(p=0.024，20项中0项反转)。Mistral-Small-3.2、Phi-3.5-mini和Llama-3.1-8B呈部署谨慎模式，拒绝率差异为-9至-20个百分点。基座模型实验表明对齐是模式反转的阶段；Llama-3.1-70B保持方向但幅度减弱。跨家族异质性具有评判者依赖性。

Conclusion: 开源LLM在评估情境分歧上表现出显著的家族间异质性。对齐过程导致部分模型行为模式反转。评判者的选择会显著影响结论——不同安全分类器可能捕捉不同的潜在构念。研究表明基于安全基准评估部署行为的可靠性存疑。

Abstract: Safety benchmarks are routinely treated as evidence about how a language model will behave once deployed, but this inference is fragile if behavior depends on whether a prompt looks like an evaluation. We define evaluation-context divergence as an observable within-item change in behavior induced by framing a fixed task as an evaluation, a live deployment interaction, or a neutral request, and present a paired-prompt protocol that measures it in open-weight LLMs while controlling for paraphrase variation, benchmark familiarity, and judge framing-sensitivity.
  Across five instruction-tuned checkpoints from four open-weight families plus a matched OLMo-3 base/instruct ablation ($20$ paired items, $840$ generations per checkpoint), we find striking heterogeneity. OLMo-3-Instruct alone is eval-cautious -- evaluation framing raises refusal vs. neutral by $11.8$pp ($p=0.007$) and reduces harmful compliance vs. deployment by $3.6$pp ($p=0.024$, $0/20$ items inverted) -- while Mistral-Small-3.2, Phi-3.5-mini, and Llama-3.1-8B are deployment-cautious}, with marginal eval-vs-deployment refusal effects of $-9$ to $-20$pp. The matched OLMo-3 base also exhibits the deployment-cautious pattern, identifying alignment as the inversion stage; within Llama-3.1, the $70$B model preserves direction with attenuated magnitude, ruling out a simple ``small-model effect that reverses at scale.'' One caveat: the cross-family heterogeneity is judge-dependent. Re-judging with a different-family safety classifier (Llama-Guard-3-8B) preserves the within-OLMo eval-cautious direction but flattens the cross-family contrast, indicating that the two judges operationalize distinct constructs.

</details>


### [44] [Don't Lose Focus: Activation Steering via Key-Orthogonal Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06342)
*Haoyan Luo,Mateo Espinosa Zarlenga,Mateja Jamnik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SKOP是一种新型LLM行为 steering方法，通过关键正交投影技术，在保持对关键token注意力的同时约束注意力重路由，解决了传统steering方法导致推理和检索性能下降的问题，实现了steeing效果与模型实用性之间的最优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Activation steering方法在控制LLM行为时，会导致注意力重路由（attention rerouting）： steering向量改变了query-key匹配，使注意力从上下文重要的token转移到信息量较少的token，造成推理和检索性能下降，形成steering效果与模型效用之间的权衡困境。

Method: SKOP通过在关键正交投影空间中进行干预，约束有害的注意力重路由。具体做法：保留模型对用于推理和检索的focus tokens的注意力模式，允许在不太重要的tail tokens之间重新分配注意力权重。

Result: 在多个steering基准测试中，SKOP实现了最佳的steering-utility权衡：utility degradation降低5-7倍，同时保留超过95%的vanilla steering效能。在长上下文检索场景下，SKOP能够保持鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: SKOP通过在focus tokens上保持原始注意力模式，同时允许tail tokens重新分配，显著降低了实用性能 degradation（减少5-7倍），同时保留超过95%的vanilla steering效果。在长上下文检索场景中表现尤为鲁棒。

Abstract: Activation steering controls LLM behaviour towards target behaviour by intervening in internal representations, yet it often degrades reasoning and retrieval performance. We argue that a primary cause of this trade-off is attention rerouting: steering vectors alter query-key matching, shifting attention away from contextually important tokens toward less informative ones. To address this, we propose Steering via Key-Orthogonal Projections (SKOP), a steering method that constrains harmful attention rerouting without eliminating steering efficacy. SKOP achieves this by preserving attention patterns on a small set of focus tokens the model relies on for reasoning and retrieval, while allowing redistribution among less critical tail tokens. Across multiple steering benchmarks, we show that SKOP achieves the best joint steering-utility trade-off, reducing utility degradation by 5-7x while retaining over 95% of vanilla steering efficacy. Our results further suggest that, in long-context retrieval settings where vanilla steering approaches are ineffective, SKOP can maintain robust performance by avoiding attention rerouting.

</details>


### [45] [SEQUOR: A Multi-Turn Benchmark for Realistic Constraint Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06353)
*Beatriz Canaverde,Duarte M. Alves,José Pombal,Giuseppe Attanasio,André F. T. Martins*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SEQUOR是一个用于评估长多轮对话中约束遵守能力的自动基准，通过模拟真实场景发现当前模型在长对话中指令遵循能力显著下降，多约束场景下性能下降超过40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的指令遵循基准主要关注单轮或短多轮场景，缺乏对模型在长时域指令遵循任务中表现的系统评估。

Method: SEQUOR采用模拟人物驱动的交互方式，从真实对话中提取约束条件，构建自动化评估框架。

Result: 模型指令遵循准确率随对话增长持续下降，单约束场景下降超11%，多约束场景下降超40%，约束动态变化场景下降超9%。

Conclusion: SEQUOR提供了一个有效评估助手指令遵循能力的基准，揭示了当前模型在长对话中指令遵循能力的显著缺陷，并指出多约束场景下的性能下降尤为严重（超过40%）。

Abstract: In a conversation, a helpful assistant must reliably follow user directives, even as they refine, modify, or contradict earlier requests. Yet most instruction-following benchmarks focus on single-turn or short multi-turn scenarios, leaving open how well models handle long-horizon instruction-following tasks. To bridge this gap, we present SEQUOR, an automatic benchmark for evaluating constraint adherence in long multi-turn conversations. SEQUOR consists of simulated persona-driven interactions built with constraints extracted from real-world conversations. Our results show that even when following a single constraint, instruction-following accuracy consistently decreases as the conversation grows longer, with drops exceeding 11%. This decline becomes larger when models have to follow multiple constraints simultaneously, reducing their accuracy by over 40%. In scenarios where constraints are added or replaced at arbitrary points of the conversation, model accuracy decreases by more than 9%. Taken together, our results reveal that current models still struggle to follow user instructions in multi-turn conversations, and provide a way for better measuring instruction-following capabilities in assistants.

</details>


### [46] [MiA-Signature: Approximating Global Activation for Long-Context Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06416)
*Yuqing Li,Jiangnan Li,Mo Yu,Zheng Lin,Weiping Wang,Jie Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: We propose MiA-Signature, a compressed representation of global activation patterns induced by queries in LLM systems. Using submodular-based concept selection and optional working memory refinement, MiA-Signature approximates full activation effects while remaining computationally tractable. This approach improves performance in RAG and agentic systems on long-context tasks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The paper addresses the tension between global ignition (reportable conscious access associated with distributed memory activation) and limited accessibility of all activated contents. It proposes that cognition may rely on a compact representation approximating the global influence of activation on downstream processing.

Method: MiA-Signature is instantiated via submodular-based selection of high-level concepts that cover the activated context space, with optional lightweight iterative refinement using working memory.

Result: Consistent performance gains across multiple long-context understanding tasks when integrating MiA-Signatures into both RAG and agentic systems.

Conclusion: MiA-Signature serves as an effective conditioning signal that approximates the effect of the full activation state while remaining computationally tractable. Integrating this signature into RAG and agentic systems yields consistent performance gains.

Abstract: A growing body of work in cognitive science suggests that reportable conscious access is associated with \emph{global ignition} over distributed memory systems, while such activation is only partially accessible as individuals cannot directly access or enumerate all activated contents. This tension suggests a plausible mechanism that cognition may rely on a compact representation that approximates the global influence of activation on downstream processing. Inspired by this idea, we introduce the concept of \textbf{Mindscape Activation Signature (MiA-Signature)}, a compressed representation of the global activation pattern induced by a query. In LLM systems, this is instantiated via submodular-based selection of high-level concepts that cover the activated context space, optionally refined through lightweight iterative updates using working memory. The resulting MiA-Signature serves as a conditioning signal that approximates the effect of the full activation state while remaining computationally tractable. Integrating MiA-Signatures into both RAG and agentic systems yields consistent performance gains across multiple long-context understanding tasks.

</details>


### [47] [From 124 Million Tokens to 1,021 Neologisms: A Large-Scale Pipeline for Automatic Neologism Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06426)
*Diego Rossini,Lonneke van der Plas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个融合规则过滤与LLM分类的模块化新词检测流水线，基于语法/额外语法形态学理论对527百万条Reddit帖子进行新词挖掘，成功将候选规模缩减99.99%至1,021个，最终人工确认599个真实新词，同时揭示LLM在新词分类上的跨模型分歧问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模社交媒体文本中持续涌现新词，亟需自动化的新词检测系统以追踪语言演变。然而现有方法难以在保持可扩展性的同时确保检测质量，且对于"何为新词"的界定缺乏统一的理论框架支撑。

Method: 该研究采用规则过滤与LLM分类相结合的可扩展模块化流水线，基于语法形态学与额外语法形态学两种词汇构成框架构建四分类体系（新词、实体、外来词、无），在527万条英语Reddit帖子（2005-2024）语料上进行实例化，通过多LLM独立分类与多数投票机制进行候选筛选，最终结合人工专家验证。

Result: 从124.6百万个独特词汇中筛选出1,021个新词候选，经验证其中599个（58.7%）为真正的词汇创新。实验揭示了不同LLM之间存在显著的分类分歧，凸显了大规模新词检测任务的复杂性。

Conclusion: 研究证明了结合规则过滤与大语言模型分类的流水线能够有效缩减候选集规模（99.99%），但跨模型分歧表明LLM在词汇创新识别上仍存在挑战，589个（57.7%）候选被判定为非新词，且手动标注仍是确保质量的关键环节。流水线具有良好的模块化与可迁移性。

Abstract: We present a scalable, modular pipeline for automatic neologism detection that combines rule-based filtering with LLM classification. The pipeline is grounded in two complementary word-formation frameworks, grammatical and extra-grammatical morphology, which jointly define the scope of what counts as a neologism and inform a four-class classification scheme (neologism, entity, foreign, none). While designed to be modular and transferable at the architectural level, the pipeline is instantiated on 527 million English-language Reddit posts spanning 2005-2024. From this corpus, we extract 124.6 million unique tokens and reduce them by over 99.99% to yield 1,021 neologism candidates, a set small enough for manual expert verification. Multiple LLMs independently classify each candidate via majority vote, with a final verification step, revealing substantial cross-model disagreement and highlighting the challenge of operationalizing neologism detection at scale. Manual annotation of all 1,021 candidates confirms that 599 (58.7%) are genuine lexical innovations. The pipeline code, vocabulary compilation scripts, and the annotated candidate list are available at https://github.com/DiegoRossini/neologism-pipeline.

</details>


### [48] [COVID-19 Infodemic. Understanding content features in detecting fake news using a machine learning approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06435)
*Vimala Balakrishnan,Lee Zing Hii,Eric Laporte*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探索了文本特征（词二元组）和语言特征（词性分布）在COVID-19假新闻检测中的应用，发现Random Forest算法表现最优，单独使用任一特征类型均有效，但特征组合未能进一步提升性能，表明传统机器学习方法可有效用于假新闻检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本和语言特征在假新闻检测中的应用研究不足，尽管已有实证表明这些特征有助于区分真假新闻，因此有必要系统评估其在机器学习框架下的检测能力。

Method: 采用传统机器学习算法（决策树、K近邻、逻辑回归、支持向量机、随机森林）对COVID-19期间收集的数据集进行实验，评估文本特征（词二元组）和语言特征（词性分布）单独及组合使用的检测效果。

Result: Random Forest取得最佳检测性能，SVM次之；文本和语言特征单独使用时均能改善检测效果，但两者结合后未产生显著提升；词二元组和词性标签的使用效果存在差异。

Conclusion: 研究发现，基于文本和语言特征的传统机器学习方法在假新闻检测中具有实用价值；Random Forest表现最佳，而特征融合策略未能带来显著提升，暗示不同类型特征可能存在信息冗余或冲突；该研究为COVID-19期间的假新闻检测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The use of content features, particularly textual and linguistic for fake news detection is under-researched, despite empirical evidence showing the features could contribute to differentiating real and fake news. To this end, this study investigates a selection of content features such as word bigrams, part of speech distribution etc. to improve fake news detection. We performed a series of experiments on a new dataset gathered during the COVID-19 pandemic and using Decision Tree, K-Nearest Neighbor, Logistic Regression, Support Vector Machine and Random Forest. Random Forest yielded the best results, followed closely by Support Vector Machine, across all setups. In general, both the textual and linguistic features were found to improve fake news detection when used separately, however, combining them into a single model did not improve the detection significantly. Differences were also noted between the use of bigrams and part of speech tags. The study shows that textual and linguistic features can be used successfully in detecting fake news using the traditional machine learning approach as opposed to deep learning.

</details>


### [49] [Towards Emotion Consistency Analysis of Large Language Models in Emotional Conversational Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06476)
*Sneha Oram,Ojaswita Bhushan,Pushpak Bhattacharyya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了三种LLM（Claude-3.5-haiku、GPT-4o-mini、Mistral-7B）在情感驱动对话场景中对其自身生成内容的一致性回应能力。实验通过具有递增强度虚假前提的查询进行测试，发现模型普遍存在一致性缺陷，尤其在中等情感强度内容上表现最差，为LLM在情感敏感场景中的应用提出了重要警示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在情感驱动对话场景中的一致性表现尚未得到充分研究。特别是在高风险敏感应用中，模型对其自身先前输出的回应能力至关重要。然而，现有研究缺乏对LLM在面对嵌入虚假前提的查询时的一致性评估框架。

Method: 研究采用自身查询框架方法，将LLM生成的文本作为同一模型的查询进行评估。实验设计基于两个维度（极端与适中情感）和三种查询类型（强度递增的虚假前提）展开，测试对象包括Claude-3.5-haiku、GPT-4o-mini和Mistral-7B三个模型。进一步采用基于注意力分数的分析方法追踪模型的认知处理模式。

Result: LLM整体表现低于平均水平，对查询中嵌入的虚假信念具有脆弱性。值得注意的是，中等情感强度内容导致更大的脆弱性，表明情感强度与欺骗效果之间存在非单调关系。注意力分析揭示模型认知处理模式发生转变，从评估模式转向生成模式。

Conclusion: LLMs显示出一致性表现不佳，容易受到查询中嵌入的虚假信念影响，在中等情感强度内容上尤为明显。注意力分析揭示了模型优先级从评估向生成的转移，为LLM在高风险情感敏感场景中的部署提出了重要警示。

Abstract: In this work, we conduct an analysis to examine the consistency of Large Language Models (LLMs) with respect to their own generated responses in an emotionally-driven conversational context. Specifically, the text generated by LLM is framed as a query to the same model, and its responses are subsequently assessed. This is performed with three queries across two dimensions of extreme and moderate emotions. The three queries are, in particular, false claim queries that contain inherently wrong assumptions (false presuppositions) in increasing order of intensity. Two commercial models, Claude-3.5-haiku, GPT4o-mini, and a medium-sized model, Mistral-7B, are considered in the study. Our findings indicate that LLMs exhibit below-average performance and remain vulnerable to false beliefs embedded within queries. This susceptibility is especially pronounced for moderate emotional content. Furthermore, an extended attention-score-based analysis highlights a shift in models' priority from evaluative to generative. The results raise important considerations for LLMs' deployment in high-stakes, emotionally sensitive contexts.

</details>


### [50] [Litespark Inference on Consumer CPUs: Custom SIMD Kernels for Ternary Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06485)
*Nii Osae Osae Dade,Tony Morri,Moinul Hossain Rahat,Sayandip Pal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have transformed artificial intelligence, but their computational requirements remain prohibitive for most users. Standard inference demands expensive datacenter GPUs or cloud API access, leaving over one billion personal computers underutilized for AI workloads. Ternary models offer a path forward: their weights are constrained to {-1, 0, +1}, theoretically eliminating the need for floating-point multiplication. However, existing frameworks fail to exploit this structure, treating ternary models as dense floating-point networks. We address this gap with custom SIMD kernels that replace matrix multiplication with simple addition and subtraction operations, targeting the integer dot product instructions available on modern CPUs. Our implementation, Litespark-Inference, is pip-installable and integrates directly with Hugging-Face, achieving 9.2x faster time-to-first-token, 52x higher throughput, and 14x memory reduction compared to standard PyTorch inference on Apple Silicon, with similar speedups on Intel and AMD processors.

</details>


### [51] [STALE: Can LLM Agents Know When Their Memories Are No Longer Valid?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06527)
*Hanxiang Chao,Yihan Bai,Rui Sheng,Tianle Li,Yushi Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文识别了LLM智能体在记忆更新中的一个关键失败模式——隐式冲突（即后续证据隐式使早期记忆失效）。通过STALE基准测试发现当前模型在此方面表现不佳（最佳仅55.2%），并提出CUPMem原型通过结构化状态整合和传播感知搜索来改进写时修订机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别了一种关键且未被充分探索的失败模式——隐式冲突（Implicit Conflict）：当后续观察在未明确否定的情况下使早期记忆失效，需要通过上下文推理和常识推理来检测。现有基准测试主要测量静态事实检索，忽视了当新证据出现时修订存储信念的能力。

Method: 提出STALE基准测试（400个专家验证的冲突场景，1200个评估查询，三个探测维度），涵盖100多个日常话题，上下文达150K tokens。评估了前沿LLM和专用记忆框架在隐式冲突场景下的表现。同时提出CUPMem原型系统，通过结构化状态整合和传播感知搜索强化写时修订机制。

Result: 系统性评估揭示了前沿LLM在检索更新证据与根据其行动之间存在普遍差距。即使是评估中表现最好的模型也仅达到55.2%的总体准确率。模型常接受用户查询中嵌入的过时假设，且难以识别一个方面的状态变化何时应使相关记忆失效。

Conclusion: CUPMem通过结构化状态整合和传播感知搜索，为状态感知记忆提供了一个有前景的方向。当前最佳模型仅达到55.2%的总体准确率，表明在隐式冲突检测和信念更新方面存在重大改进空间。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly expected to maintain coherent, long-term personalized memory, yet current benchmarks primarily measure static fact retrieval, overlooking the ability to revise stored beliefs when new evidence emerges. We identify a critical and underexplored failure mode, Implicit Conflict: a later observation invalidates an earlier memory without explicit negation, requiring contextual inference and commonsense reasoning to detect. To rigorously evaluate this capability, we introduce STALE, a benchmark of 400 expert-validated conflict scenarios (1,200 evaluation queries across three probing dimensions) spanning over 100 everyday topics with contexts up to 150K tokens. We propose a three-dimensional probing framework that tests State Resolution (detecting that a prior belief is outdated), Premise Resistance (rejecting queries that falsely presuppose a stale state), and Implicit Policy Adaptation (proactively applying updated states in downstream behavior). A systematic evaluation of frontier LLMs and specialized memory frameworks reveals a pervasive gap between retrieving updated evidence and acting on it, with even the best evaluated model achieving only 55.2% overall accuracy. Models often accept outdated assumptions embedded in a user's query, and they struggle to recognize when a change in one aspect of the user's state should invalidate related memories. To establish an initial baseline for state-aware memory, we further present CUPMem, a prototype that strengthens write-time revision through structured state consolidation and propagation-aware search, suggesting that explicit state adjudication is a promising direction for robust agentic memory.

</details>


### [52] [Efficient Pre-Training with Token Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06546)
*Bowen Peng,Théo Gigant,Jeffrey Quesnelle*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Token-Superposition Training (TST)，通过将多个连续token打包为"袋子"并使用multi-hot cross-entropy目标函数进行高效叠加训练，随后恢复标准训练，在无需任何侵入性修改的情况下实现最高2.5倍的预训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的预训练在规模上代价极其高昂且效率低下，现有方法需要复杂且侵入性的修改才能实现高数据吞吐量。

Method: 采用两阶段训练策略：(1)叠加阶段——将多个连续token打包成"袋子"，使用multi-hot cross-entropy (MCE)目标函数进行高效训练；(2)恢复阶段——恢复标准训练方式。方法完全兼容现有训练框架，无需对并行策略、优化器、分词器、数据或模型架构做任何修改。

Result: 在270M、600M、3B和10B A1B MoE模型规模上验证，效果稳健。在等loss条件下，10B规模下预训练时间最多可缩短2.5倍，同时在下游任务评估中也保持领先。

Conclusion: TST是一种无需修改模型架构、并行策略或优化器等核心组件的"即插即用"方法，在保持模型性能的同时，最多可减少50%的预训练时间，具有很高的实用价值。

Abstract: Pre-training of Large Language Models is often prohibitively expensive and inefficient at scale, requiring complex and invasive modifications in order to achieve high data throughput. In this work, we present Token-Superposition Training (TST), a simple drop-in method that significantly improves the data throughput per FLOPs during pre-training without modifying the parallelism, optimizer, tokenizer, data, or model architecture. TST is done in two phases: (i) A highly efficient superposition phase where we combine many contiguous tokens into one bag and train using a multi-hot cross-entropy (MCE) objective, and (ii) a recovery phase where we revert back to standard training. We extensively evaluate TST on the scale of 270M and 600M parameters and validate on 3B and a 10B A1B mixture of experts model, demonstrating that it is highly robust in different settings. Ultimately, TST consistently outperforms baseline loss and downstream evaluations, and under equal-loss settings, TST yields up to a 2.5x reduction in total pre-training time at the 10B A1B scale.

</details>


### [53] [Continuous Latent Diffusion Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06548)
*Hongcan Guo,Qinyu Zhao,Yian Zhao,Shen Nie,Rui Zhu,Qiushan Guo,Feng Wang,Tao Yang,Hengshuang Zhao,Guoqiang Wei,Yan Zeng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Cola DLM，一种层次潜空间扩散语言模型，通过信息分层分解实现文本生成。该模型使用Text VAE学习文本到潜空间的稳定映射，用块因果DiT建模连续潜空间中的全局语义先验，最后通过条件解码生成文本。与传统自回归模型不同，该方法将全局语义组织与局部文本实现分离，支持语义压缩和先验拟合，为离散文本和连续模态的统一建模提供了可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归语言模型虽然成功，但仍受限于固定从左到右的生成顺序；其他替代方案在生成效率、可扩展表征学习和全局语义建模方面难以兼顾。核心问题在于如何突破token级建模的局限，实现更灵活的语义组织和更高的生成效率。

Method: Cola DLM采用三阶段架构：(1) Text VAE学习稳定的文本到潜空间映射；(2) 块因果DiT在连续潜空间中建模全局语义先验；(3) 通过条件解码生成最终文本。从马尔可夫路径角度看，其扩散过程执行的是潜空间先验传输而非token级观测恢复，实现语义组织与文本实现的解耦。

Result: 实验涵盖4个研究问题、8个基准测试，在约2B参数的AR和LLaDA基线上进行了严格匹配的对比，Scaling曲线达约2000 EFLOPs规模。实验验证了Cola DLM的有效配置和强扩展性。

Conclusion: 层次连续潜空间先验建模可作为token级语言建模的原则性替代方案，其生成质量和扩展行为可能比似然更准确反映模型能力，同时为离散文本与连续模态的统一建模提供了具体路径。

Abstract: Large language models have achieved remarkable success under the autoregressive paradigm, yet high-quality text generation need not be tied to a fixed left-to-right order. Existing alternatives still struggle to jointly achieve generation efficiency, scalable representation learning, and effective global semantic modeling. We propose Cola DLM, a hierarchical latent diffusion language model that frames text generation through hierarchical information decomposition. Cola DLM first learns a stable text-to-latent mapping with a Text VAE, then models a global semantic prior in continuous latent space with a block-causal DiT, and finally generates text through conditional decoding. From a unified Markov-path perspective, its diffusion process performs latent prior transport rather than token-level observation recovery, thereby separating global semantic organization from local textual realization. This design yields a more flexible non-autoregressive inductive bias, supports semantic compression and prior fitting in continuous space, and naturally extends to other continuous modalities. Through experiments spanning 4 research questions, 8 benchmarks, strictly matched ~2B-parameter autoregressive and LLaDA baselines, and scaling curves up to about 2000 EFLOPs, we identify an effective overall configuration of Cola DLM and verify its strong scaling behavior for text generation. Taken together, the results establish hierarchical continuous latent prior modeling as a principled alternative to strictly token-level language modeling, where generation quality and scaling behavior may better reflect model capability than likelihood, while also suggesting a concrete path toward unified modeling across discrete text and continuous modalities.

</details>


### [54] [Long Context Pre-Training with Lighthouse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06554)
*Bowen Peng,Subho Ghosh,Jeffrey Quesnelle*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Lighthouse Attention通过一种训练阶段特有的对称选择式层次化注意力算法，解决长序列Transformer训练中注意力机制的二次方复杂度问题。该方法在预训练大部分阶段使用，训练末期可恢复为全注意力模型，实现更快的训练速度并达到更低的最终损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果Transformer在处理极长序列时，缩放点积注意力（SDPA）的时间复杂度和显存占用均为二次方，成为训练的主要瓶颈。

Method: 提出Lighthouse Attention，一种训练专用、对称的选择式层次化注意力算法：(1) 在SDPA外层封装子二次方的层次化预处理和后处理步骤，实现自适应压缩和解压；(2) 采用对称压缩策略，同时对Query、Key、Value进行池化操作，保持从左到右的因果性以提升并行度；(3) 两阶段训练方法——先用Lighthouse Attention进行大部分预训练，再用短时训练恢复全注意力模型。

Result: 小规模LLM预训练实验表明，与所有设置相同的全注意力训练相比，该方法在恢复阶段后实现了更快的总训练时间和更低的最终损失。

Conclusion: 该方法可作为长序列Transformer预训练的高效训练策略，通过恢复阶段确保模型可获得全注意力的表达能力。

Abstract: Training causal transformers at extreme sequence lengths is bottlenecked by the quadratic time and memory of scaled dot-product attention (SDPA). In this work, we propose Lighthouse Attention, a training-only symmetrical selection-based hierarchical attention algorithm that wraps around ordinary SDPA and can be easily removed towards the end of the training. Our hierarchical selection is also gradient-free, which exempts us from dealing with a complicated and potentially inefficient backward pass kernel. Our contribution is three-fold: (i) A subquadratic hierarchical pre- and post-processing step that does adaptive compression and decompression of the sequence. (ii) A symmetrical compression strategy that pools queries, keys and values at the same time, while preserving left-to-right causality, which greatly improves parallelism. (iii) A two stage training approach which we pre-train for the majority of the time with Lighthouse Attention and recover a full attention model at the end with a short training. We run preliminary small scale LLM pre-training experiments that show the effectiveness of our method compared to full attention training with all other settings matched, where we achieve a faster total training time and lower final loss after the recovery phase. Full code is available at: https://github.com/ighoshsubho/lighthouse-attention

</details>


### [55] [Automated Clinical Report Generation for Remote Cognitive Remediation: Comparing Knowledge-Engineered Templates and LLMs in Low-Resource Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06594)
*Yongxin Zhou,Fabien Ringeval,François Portet*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究针对远程认知康复治疗中的自动临床报告生成问题，对比了规则模板系统与GPT-4零样本生成两种方案，发现模板系统在临床可靠性上更优，而LLM在语言流畅性上占优，为低资源环境下的临床NLP系统设计提供了可复制的评估方法论和八条设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知康复治疗需求增长与言语治疗师资源有限之间的矛盾日益突出，远程康复系统产生了大量难以高效审阅的交互数据，现有的自动报告生成在低资源场景下缺乏参考标准，亟需可靠的自动化临床报告生成方案。

Method: 采用受控对比实验设计：规则模板系统编码言语治疗领域知识为显式决策规则和验证模板；GPT-4零样本系统使用相同专家验证的结构化变量输入。由8名言语治疗师和高年级学生使用九项标准问卷进行双系统输出评估。

Result: 规则模板系统在流畅性、连贯性和结果呈现方面得分更高；GPT-4生成内容更简洁。方向性差异在各评估维度一致，但经多重比较校正后均未达统计显著性，可能受限于专家评估的规模。

Conclusion: 两种方案存在临床可靠性与语言质量之间的权衡。建议包括：结合领域知识与灵活生成的混合架构、强化可解释性、建立领域特定评估标准等。本研究提供了低资源环境下临床NLP系统的可复制的评估方法论，为生成式AI在医疗领域的负责任应用提供了参考。

Abstract: The growing demand for cognitive remediation therapy, combined with limited speech therapist availability, has accelerated the adoption of remote rehabilitation tools. These systems generate large volumes of interaction data that are difficult for clinicians to review efficiently. This paper investigates automated clinical report generation for avatar-guided, home-based cognitive remediation sessions in a low-resource setting with no reference reports. We present and compare two approaches: (1) a rule-based template system encoding speech therapy domain knowledge as explicit decision rules and validated templates, ensuring clinical reliability and traceability; and (2) a zero-shot LLM-based approach (GPT-4) aimed at more fluent and concise output. Both systems use identical pre-extracted, expert-validated structured variables, enabling a controlled factual comparison. Outputs were evaluated by eight speech therapists and final-year students using a nine-criterion questionnaire. Results reveal a clear trade-off between clinical reliability and linguistic quality. The template-based system scored higher on fluidity, coherence, and results presentation, while GPT-4 produced more concise output. Directional differences are consistent across evaluation dimensions, though no comparison reached statistical significance after correction, reflecting the scale constraints of expert clinical evaluation. Based on evaluator feedback, we derive eight design recommendations for clinical reporting systems in remote rehabilitation settings. More broadly, this work contributes a replicable methodology combining expert elicitation, taxonomy-driven generation, and multi-dimensional human evaluation for clinical NLG in low-resource settings, and illustrates how controlled comparisons can inform the responsible adoption of generative AI in healthcare.

</details>


### [56] [UniSD: Towards a Unified Self-Distillation Framework for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06597)
*Yiqiao Jin,Yiyang Wang,Lucheng Fu,Yijia Xiao,Yinyi Luo,Haoxin Liu,B. Aditya Prakash,Josiah Hester,Jindong Wang,Srijan Kumar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniSD是一个统一的自蒸馏框架，用于解决自回归LLM中自蒸馏面临的挑战（自由形式轨迹、任务依赖的正确性、不稳定的监督），通过集成多教师一致性、EMA教师稳定化、token级对比学习、特征匹配和 divergence clipping等多种机制。在6个基准、6个模型、3个模型族上的实验表明，UniSDfull相比基模型提升+5.4分，比最强基线提升+2.8分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归LLM的自蒸馏面临三大挑战：自生成轨迹是自由形式的、正确性取决于任务、看似合理的推理仍可能提供不稳定或不可靠的监督。现有方法仅考察孤立的设计选择，缺乏对各组件有效性、作用和相互作用的系统理解。

Method: 提出UniSD统一框架，系统研究自蒸馏。核心机制包括：(1) 多教师一致性提升监督可靠性；(2) EMA教师稳定化；(3) Token级对比学习；(4) 特征匹配；(5) Divergence clipping。进一步整合为UniSDfull管道。

Result: 跨6个基准、6个模型（来自3个模型族）的实验表明：UniSD揭示了自蒸馏何时优于静态模仿、哪些组件驱动收益、以及组件如何跨任务交互。UniSDfull取得最强整体性能：比基模型提升+5.4分，比最强基线提升+2.8分。

Conclusion: 自蒸馏是一种无需更强外部教师的实用且可控制的LLM适应方法。UniSD框架提供了系统理解自蒸馏各组件作用的途径。

Abstract: Self-distillation (SD) offers a promising path for adapting large language models (LLMs) without relying on stronger external teachers. However, SD in autoregressive LLMs remains challenging because self-generated trajectories are free-form, correctness is task-dependent, and plausible rationales can still provide unstable or unreliable supervision. Existing methods mainly examine isolated design choices, leaving their effectiveness, roles, and interactions unclear. In this paper, we propose UniSD, a unified framework to systematically study self-distillation. UniSD integrates complementary mechanisms that address supervision reliability, representation alignment, and training stability, including multi-teacher agreement, EMA teacher stabilization, token-level contrastive learning, feature matching, and divergence clipping. Across six benchmarks and six models from three model families, UniSD reveals when self-distillation improves over static imitation, which components drive the gains, and how these components interact across tasks. Guided by these insights, we construct UniSDfull, an integrated pipeline that combines complementary components and achieves the strongest overall performance, improving over the base model by +5.4 points and the strongest baseline by +2.8 points. Extensive evaluation highlights self-distillation as a practical and steerable approach for efficient LLM adaptation without stronger external teachers.

</details>


### [57] [StraTA: Incentivizing Agentic Reinforcement Learning with Strategic Trajectory Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06642)
*Xiangyuan Xue,Yifan Zhou,Zidong Wang,Shengji Tang,Philip Torr,Wanli Ouyang,Lei Bai,Zhenfei Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: StraTA通过引入显式轨迹级策略和分层GRPO训练框架，有效解决了LLM智能体在长期决策任务中的探索和信用分配难题，在ALFWorld、WebShop、SciWorld三个基准上均达到最优性能，验证了层次化战略规划对提升智能体决策能力的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体优化方法主要采用纯粹反应式策略，导致在长时序决策任务中探索能力不足、信用分配困难，难以有效处理需要战略性规划的多步骤复杂任务。

Method: StraTA框架包含三个核心组件：1) 从初始任务状态采样紧凑策略；2) 分层GRPO风格的rollout设计，条件化后续动作于策略之上；3) 策略生成与动作执行的联合训练，辅以多样化策略rollout和关键自我判断机制增强判别能力。

Result: ALFWorld达到93.1%成功率，WebShop达到84.2%，SciWorld达到63.5%总分且超越前沿闭源模型。StraTA在样本效率和最终性能两个维度均持续超越强基线方法。

Conclusion: StraTA通过轨迹级策略抽象有效解决了LLM智能体在长期决策任务中的探索不足和信用分配困难问题，在多个基准测试上实现最优性能，超越前沿闭源模型，展示了将高层战略规划与低层动作执行相结合的潜力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as interactive agents, but optimizing them for long-horizon decision making remains difficult because current methods are largely purely reactive, which weakens both exploration and credit assignment over extended trajectories. In this work, we present Strategic Trajectory Abstraction (StraTA), a simple framework that introduces an explicit trajectory-level strategy into agentic reinforcement learning (RL). StraTA samples a compact strategy from the initial task state, conditions subsequent actions on that strategy, and trains strategy generation and action execution jointly with a hierarchical GRPO-style rollout design, further enhanced by diverse strategy rollout and critical self-judgment. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and SciWorld show that StraTA consistently improves both sample efficiency and final performance over strong baselines. StraTA reaches success rates of 93.1% on ALFWorld and 84.2% on WebShop. On SciWorld, StraTA attains a 63.5% overall score, outperforming frontier closed-source models.

</details>


### [58] [EMO: Pretraining Mixture of Experts for Emergent Modularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06663)
*Ryan Wang,Akshita Bhagia,Sewon Min*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EMO是一种模块化MoE架构，通过文档边界约束使同文档token共享专家池，无需人工定义即可实现模块化专家分组。实验表明其支持高效的选择性专家使用（25%专家仅损失1%性能），并展现出语义层面的专业化，为大模型的模块化、高效部署提供了新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统大语言模型采用单体架构，即使应用仅需特定能力（如代码、数学）也需加载完整模型。MoE虽能选择性激活专家，但实际限制专家子集用于特定领域会导致严重性能下降，限制了其在内存受限场景的实用性。

Method: EMO通过文档边界约束，让同一文档内的token共享专家池选择，而不同文档可使用不同池。这种自监督约束使相似领域的token在预训练中自然聚集到相同专家，实现无需人工先验的模块化。

Result: 在1T tokens上预训练的EMO（1B活跃/14B总量）作为完整模型达到标准MoE性能。在选择性专家使用测试中：保留25%专家仅下降1%，保留12.5%专家仅下降3%，而标准MoE在相同设置下崩溃。EMO的专家在语义层面（数学、代码等领域）专业化，而非标准MoE的低层句法专业化。

Conclusion: EMO展示了如何通过简单约束实现模块化的大模型部署，在保持性能的同时支持灵活的专业化使用，为内存受限环境下的稀疏模型应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large language models are typically deployed as monolithic systems, requiring the full model even when applications need only a narrow subset of capabilities, e.g., code, math, or domain-specific knowledge. Mixture-of-Experts (MoEs) seemingly offer a potential alternative by activating only a subset of experts per input, but in practice, restricting inference to a subset of experts for a given domain leads to severe performance degradation. This limits their practicality in memory-constrained settings, especially as models grow larger and sparser. We introduce EMO, an MoE designed for modularity-the independent use and composition of expert subsets-without requiring human-defined priors. Our key idea is to encourage tokens from similar domains to rely on similar experts. Since tokens within a document often share a domain, EMO restricts them to select experts from a shared pool, while allowing different documents to use different pools. This simple constraint enables coherent expert groupings to emerge during pretraining using document boundaries alone. We pretrain a 1B-active, 14B-total EMO on 1T tokens. As a full model, it matches standard MoE performance. Crucially, it enables selective expert use: retaining only 25% (12.5%) of experts incurs just a 1% (3%) absolute drop, whereas standard MoEs break under the same setting. We further find that expert subsets in EMO specialize at semantic levels (e.g., domains such as math or code), in contrast to the low-level syntactic specialization observed in standard MoEs. Altogether, our results demonstrate a path toward modular, memory-efficient deployment of large, sparse models and open new opportunities for composable architectures.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [59] [Auto Research with Specialist Agents Develops Effective and Non-Trivial Training Recipes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05724)
*Jingjie Ning,Xiaochuan Li,Ji Zeng,Hao Kang,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动化的"提交试验循环"系统，通过专门的AI智能体在无人干预的情况下自动提出假设、编写代码、执行实验、吸收反馈并迭代改进机器学习配方。系统在三个任务上验证了效果：Parameter Golf验证bpb降低0.81%、NanoChat-D12 CORE提升38.7%、CIFAR-10 Airbench96时间减少4.59%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习研究长期以来依赖人类专家手动设计实验、调整配方（recipes），这一过程效率低下且难以规模化。现有AutoML方法多聚焦于单模型超参数优化或单次生成，缺乏持续学习与代码改进的能力。

Method: 构建了一种闭环自动研究系统，包含：1）专家智能体分区配方表面并跨试验共享谱系测量；2）每次提交试验携带假设、可执行代码修改、评估器所有权的结果和反馈；3）系统将失败（崩溃、超预算、大小失败、准确率门未达标）转化为程序级配方编辑而非一次性建议。共运行1197个头条试验和600个Parameter Golf对照试验，全程无人类选择提案、编辑配方、覆盖分数或修复失败试验。

Result: 三个头条运行中：（1）Parameter Golf验证bpb降低0.81%；（2）NanoChat-D12 CORE提升38.7%；（3）CIFAR-10 Airbench96 wallclock减少4.59%。对157个头条提交和程序重写进行了严格的架构域审计，包括NanoChat注意力内核路径变更等程序重写。

Conclusion: 该自动研究循环能够在无需人类干预的情况下自主编写代码、提交实验、吸收反馈、组合已知技术并改进公开起点配方。谱系反馈机制是将评估器结果（包括失败）有效转化为后续程序级编辑的关键。

Abstract: We study auto research as a closed empirical loop driven by external measurement. Each submitted trial carries a hypothesis, an executable code edit, an evaluator-owned outcome, and feedback that shapes the next proposal. The output is not a generated paper or a single model checkpoint, but an auditable trajectory of proposals, code diffs, experiments, scores, and failure labels. We instantiate this loop with specialist agents that partition recipe surfaces and share measured lineage across trials. The central empirical finding is that lineage feedback lets agents turn evaluator outcomes, including crashes, budget overruns, size failures, and accuracy-gate misses, into later program-level recipe edits rather than one-shot suggestions. Across 1,197 headline-run trials plus 600 Parameter Golf control trials after one-time setup and launch, humans did not choose proposals, edit recipes, override scores, or repair failed trials during the search. In the three headline runs, the same submitted-trial loop reduces Parameter Golf validation bpb by $0.81\%$, raises NanoChat-D12 CORE by $38.7\%$, and reduces CIFAR-10 Airbench96 wallclock by $4.59\%$, with each task measured by its own external evaluator and legality checks. The trace includes a strict architecture-domain audit of 157 headline-run submissions and program rewrites such as a NanoChat attention-kernel path change. Within this scope the loop autonomously writes code, submits experiments, absorbs feedback, applies and combines known techniques inside each environment, and improves public starting recipes.

</details>


### [60] [Multiagent Stochastic Shortest Path Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06056)
*Martin Jonáš,Antonín Kučera,Vojtěch Kůr,Jan Mačák,Vojtěch Řehák*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: This paper introduces the multi-agent stochastic shortest path (MSSP) problem where k agents cooperatively try to reach a target state while minimizing expected arrival time. The authors analyze computational and strategy complexity under both autonomous and coordinated settings, develop efficient strategy-synthesis algorithms, and validate them experimentally against baseline methods.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The multi-agent stochastic shortest path problem extends the classic stochastic shortest path problem to a multi-agent setting where k agents cooperatively aim to reach a target state while minimizing the expected time for any agent to arrive.

Method: The authors analyze the computational and strategy complexity of the MSSP problem in both autonomous and coordinated settings, and design efficient strategy-synthesis algorithms based on this analysis.

Result: The designed algorithms were experimentally evaluated on instances of increasing size, showing favorable performance compared to natural baseline approaches.

Conclusion: The paper presents efficient algorithms for the MSSP problem and demonstrates their effectiveness through experimental evaluation against natural baselines.

Abstract: We introduce and study the multi-agent stochastic shortest path (MSSP) problem, in which $k$ agents strive to reach a target state, aiming to minimize the expected time to reach the target by any agent. We analyze the computational and strategy-complexity of the problem in both autonomous and coordinated settings, and we design efficient strategy-synthesis algorithms. The algorithms are experimentally evaluated on instances of increasing size against natural baselines.

</details>


### [61] [Power-Efficiency and Scalability Analysis of Magnetically-Actuated Satellite Swarms via Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06286)
*Yuta Takahashi,Seang Shim,Hiraku Sakamoto,Shin-ichiro Sakai*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于凸优化的框架，用于分析磁驱动卫星编队的功耗问题。研究表明，增加卫星数量可以提高编队维持的功率效率，磁驱动编队架构是构建大型空间系统的一种高效替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天基分布式孔径具有可扩展性，能够实现超出火箭尺寸限制的高增益、窄波束和大孔径能力，但其长期维持面临轨道动力学不稳定性带来的挑战。磁力矩器产生的磁相互作用提供了一种无推进剂的位置控制策略，但电磁力/力矩模型的非线性导致非凸功耗约束，使系统级配置分析困难。

Method: 开发了一种基于凸优化的框架来分析大型磁驱动卫星编队的功耗。通过将非凸约束转化为凸优化问题，实现对系统级配置的有效分析。

Result: 分析结果表明，增加卫星数量可以提高编队维持的功率效率。

Conclusion: 磁驱动编队架构为构建大型空间系统提供了一种功率高效的替代方案，相比传统的少卫星电磁编队飞行概念更具优势。

Abstract: This correspondence presents a convex-optimization-based evaluation framework of satellite-swarm-based apertures maintained by magnetic-field interactions. Spaceborne distributed apertures are composed of multiple satellites and are attractive for scientific and commercial missions because their scalability enables high-gain, narrow-beam, and large-aperture capabilities beyond the launch-size limitations. A key challenge is that the long-term maintenance of such virtual structures requires consistent formation control amid unstable orbital dynamics, and magnetic interactions generated by satellite-mounted magnetorquers offer a desirable propellant-free position-control strategy. However, the nonlinearities of the electromagnetic force and torque model lead to a nonconvex power-consumption constraint, making system-level configuration analysis difficult. To address this issue, we develop a convex optimization-based framework to analyze the power consumption of large magnetically actuated satellite swarms. The resulting analysis shows that increasing the number of satellites can improve formation-keeping power efficiency. This indicates that magnetically actuated swarm architectures provide a power-efficient alternative to the conventional few-satellite electromagnetic formation-flight concept for constructing large-scale space systems.

</details>


### [62] [Improving the Efficiency of Language Agent Teams with Adaptive Task Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06320)
*Elizabeth Mieczkowski,Alexander Ku,Tiwalayo Eisape,Dilip Arumugam,John Matters,Katherine M. Collins,Ilia Sucholutsky,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: LATTE is a distributed systems-inspired framework for coordinating LLM teams using a shared evolving coordination graph, achieving better efficiency (tokens, time, communication) and fewer coordination failures than existing approaches while maintaining task accuracy.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing LLM team coordination occupies two extremes: highly structured methods with fixed roles and pipelines (inflexible), or fully unstructured teams (adaptable but inefficient with error propagation, conflicts, and resource waste). A balanced approach is needed.

Method: LATTE uses a shared, evolving coordination graph that encodes sub-task dependencies, agent assignments, and progress states. Agents collaboratively construct and maintain this graph, enabling dynamic work allocation, adaptive coordination, and emergent task discovery while preserving consistency under partial observability.

Result: Across diverse collaborative tasks and base models, LATTE reduces token usage, wall-clock time, communication overhead, and coordination failures (file conflicts, redundant outputs) while matching or exceeding accuracy compared to MetaGPT, decentralized teams, Leader-Worker hierarchies, and static decompositions.

Conclusion: LATTE provides an effective middle-ground between rigid structured coordination and chaotic unstructured teams, offering a promising direction for LLM multi-agent systems that can dynamically adapt while maintaining coordination consistency.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in teams, yet existing coordination approaches often occupy two extremes. Highly structured methods rely on fixed roles, pipelines, or task decompositions assigned a priori. In contrast, fully unstructured teams enable adaptability and exploration but suffer from inefficiencies such as error propagation, inter-agent conflicts, and wasted resources (measured in time, tokens, or file operations). We introduce Language Agent Teams for Task Evolution (LATTE), a framework for coordinating LLM teams inspired by distributed systems, where processors must operate under partial observability and communication constraints. In LATTE, a team of agents collaboratively construct and maintain a shared, evolving coordination graph which encodes sub-task dependencies, individual agent assignment, and the current state of sub-task progress. This protocol maintains consistency while empowering agents to dynamically allocate work, adapt coordination, and discover new tasks. Across multiple collaborative tasks and a variety of base models, we demonstrate how LATTE reduces token usage, wall-clock time, communication, and coordination failures (e.g. file conflicts and redundant outputs) while matching or exceeding the accuracy of standard designs including MetaGPT, decentralized teams, top-down Leader-Worker hierarchies, and static decompositions.

</details>


### [63] [Coordination Matters: Evaluation of Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06557)
*Maria Ana Cardei,Matthew Landers,Afsaneh Doryab*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出协调感知评估视角，通过STAT测试平台补充回报指标来诊断过程级协调机制。研究发现相似回报可能掩盖不同的协调策略，为合作式多智能体强化学习评估提供新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合作式多智能体强化学习基准测试主要关注聚合结果指标（如回报率、成功率和完成时间），无法揭示智能体如何在智能体、任务和联合分配选择组合性扩展的设置中进行协调。

Method: 开发了STAT，一个受控的承诺约束型空间任务分配测试平台，系统性变化智能体数量、任务数量和环境规模，同时固定观察访问和任务规则；评估了六种具有代表性的基于值的多智能体强化学习方法，跨越不同集中化程度。

Result: 相似回报趋势可能反映不同的协调机制，包括冗余分配、分配多样性和任务完成效率的差异；在承诺约束型任务分配中，规模化性能不仅受名义动作空间大小影响，还受分配压力、稀疏决策机会和相互依赖智能体间的冗余选择影响。

Conclusion: 协调感知评估应作为基于回报的多智能体强化学习基准测试的必要补充，以揭示不同方法如何通过不同的协调机制实现相似性能。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) benchmarks commonly emphasize aggregate outcomes such as return, success rate, or completion time. While essential, these metrics often fail to reveal how agents coordinate, particularly in settings where agents, tasks, and joint assignment choices scale combinatorially. We propose a coordination-aware evaluation perspective that supplements return with process-level diagnostics. We instantiate this perspective using STAT, a controlled commitment-constrained spatial task-allocation testbed that systematically varies agents, tasks, and environment size while holding observation access and task rules fixed. We evaluate six representative value-based MARL methods across varying levels of centralization. Our results show that similar return trends can reflect distinct coordination mechanisms, including differences in redundant assignment, assignment diversity, and task-completion efficiency. We find that in commitment-constrained task allocation, performance under scale is shaped not only by nominal action-space size, but also by assignment pressure, sparse decision opportunities, and redundant choices among interdependent agents. Our findings motivate coordination-aware evaluation as a necessary complement to return-based benchmarking for cooperative MARL.

</details>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [64] [LLMorphism: When humans come to see themselves as language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05419)
*Valerio Capraro*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"LLMorphism"概念，指人类认知类似于大语言模型的偏倚信念。随着对话式LLM的普及，人们可能产生反向推断——若LLM能像人一样说话，人可能像LLM一样思考。这种偏差通过类比迁移和隐喻可及性两种机制传播。文章区分了LLMorphism与机械论、拟人论等概念，并探讨了其在工作、教育、医疗等领域的广泛影响，最终指出当前公共讨论遗漏了问题的另一半：不仅高估机器的心智，也可能低估人类的心智。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着对话式大语言模型的普及，人们可能产生"反向推论"——既然LLM能像人类一样说话，人类也可能像LLM一样思考。这种推断存在认知偏差，因为语言输出的相似性并不代表认知架构的相似性。

Method: 通过概念辨析、机制分析（类比迁移和隐喻可及性）和跨领域影响评估来展开论证，并将LLMorphism与相关概念（机械论、拟人论、计算主义等）进行区分。

Result: 该研究建立了LLMorphism的理论框架，阐明了其传播机制（类比迁移和隐喻可及性），并系统梳理了其在工作、教育、医疗、责任认定等领域的应用及其边界条件与抵抗形式。

Conclusion: 提出了双向思维归属问题：不仅要对机器赋予过多心智，还要警惕对人类赋予过少心智。呼吁学界和公众关注LLMorphism这一新兴偏见，可能对人类尊严、工作、教育等多个领域产生深远影响。

Abstract: LLMorphism is the biased belief that human cognition works like a large language model. I argue that the rise of conversational LLMs may make this bias increasingly psychologically available. When artificial systems produce human-like language, people may draw a reverse inference: if LLMs can speak like humans, perhaps humans think like LLMs. This inference is biased because similarity at the level of linguistic output does not imply similarity in cognitive architecture. Yet, LLMorphism may spread through two mechanisms: analogical transfer, whereby features of LLMs are projected onto humans, and metaphorical availability, whereby LLM vocabulary becomes a culturally salient vocabulary for describing thought. I distinguish LLMorphism from mechanomorphism, anthropomorphism, computationalism, dehumanization, objectification, and predictive-processing theories of mind. I outline its implications for work, education, responsibility, healthcare, communication, creativity, and human dignity, while also discussing boundary conditions and forms of resistance. I conclude that the public debate may be missing half of the problem: the issue is not only whether we are attributing too much mind to machines, but also whether we are beginning to attribute too little mind to humans.

</details>


### [65] [The Pedagogy of AI Mistakes: Fostering Higher-Order Thinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05472)
*Hadi Hosseini*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究将生成式AI的错误和幻觉重新定义为教学资源，通过在数据库设计课程中设计AI整合教学大纲，利用结构化的AI错误交互培养学生的批判性思维和高阶认知技能。研究表明，这种方法能有效提升学生的元认知投入和学科严谨性，同时增强其AI素养和学科能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI频繁产生错误和幻觉，传统观念视其为应用障碍，但研究者认为这些"缺陷"实际上可以被转化为教学契机，用于激发学生的分析、评估和反思等高阶思维过程。

Method: 采用混合方法研究设计，在数据库设计课程中实施AI整合教学大纲，通过结构化的AI交互活动让学生接触并分析AI生成的错误，对学生的元认知投入、学科严谨性以及自评AI素养和学科能力进行评估。

Result: 结构化的AI错误交互能够促进学生的元认知投入、增强学科严谨性，并与学生的AI素养和学科能力感知呈正相关。

Conclusion: 将AI的局限性重新定位为教学工具，而非需要规避的缺陷，证明了教育者可以通过有意设计AI与学生的互动来培养高阶思维能力。该研究为AI辅助教学的设计提供了实证依据，强调了错误分析在发展元认知和学科严谨性方面的价值。

Abstract: As generative AI becomes increasingly integrated into higher education, its frequent errors and hallucinations, often seen as limitations, offer a unique pedagogical opportunity. By framing AI as a ``learning companion'' whose imperfect outputs prompt analysis, evaluation, and reflection, we argue that instructors can engage students in the fundamental processes of higher-order thinking. This paper presents a design-oriented study in which an AI-integrated syllabus in a \textit{database design} course deliberately leverages AI's limitations to foster critical thinking and higher-order cognitive skills aligned with Bloom's taxonomy of learning. Using a mixed-methods approach, we examine how structured interaction with AI-generated errors supports metacognitive engagement, reinforces disciplinary rigor, and relates to students' perceived AI literacy and subject-matter competency.

</details>


### [66] [Rigorous Interpretation Is a Form of Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05508)
*Isabelle Lee,Emmy Liu,Cathy Jiao,Brihi Joshi,Dani Yogatama,Fazl Barez,Michael Saxon*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: This paper argues that interpretability should evolve from a diagnostic tool to a core evaluation methodology for machine learning models, enabling deeper understanding of why models behave certain ways rather than just measuring what they produce.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current model evaluation relies on surface-level behavioral metrics (accuracies, win rates), treating explanation and evaluation as separate concerns. The paper argues that interpretability can serve not just as diagnostic tool but as a more principled evaluation framework.

Method: Conceptual exploration of three functional roles of interpretability in model evaluation, with argument for scientific standards as requirements.

Result: Identified three evaluative functions of interpretability: (1) root cause identification for fixing problems, (2) detection of subtly faulty mechanisms that invalidate outputs, (3) predictive understanding of model weaknesses to anticipate issues.

Conclusion: Interpretability methods must meet scientific standards (falsifiable, reproducible, predictive) to fulfill their evaluative potential as a principled form of model evaluation.

Abstract: Current machine learning models are evaluated through behavioral snapshots, with benchmark accuracies, win rates and outcome-based metrics. Model explanations and evaluations, however, are fundamentally intertwined: understanding why a model produces a behavior can be as important as measuring what it produces. If we trusted interpretability, we argue that it can serve not merely as diagnostics but as a richer and more principled form of model evaluation beyond surface-level performance metrics. We explore three ways interpretability can function evaluatively: (1) fixing problems by identifying the root causes of unwanted behavior, (2) detecting subtly faulty mechanisms that invalidate model outputs, and (3) predicting potential issues before they arise by fully understanding the model's weaknesses. To fulfill its evaluative potential, we argue that interpretability methods must generate claims that are falsifiable, reproducible, and predictive -- that is, interpretability must meet scientific standards.

</details>


### [67] [The Missing Evaluation Axis: What 10,000 Student Submissions Reveal About AI Tutor Effectiveness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05648)
*Rose Niousha,Samantha Boatright Smith,Bita Akram,Peter Brusilovsky,Arto Hellas,Juho Leinonen,John DeNero,Narges Norouzi*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 研究提出AI导师系统的行为维度评估框架，通过大规模数据分析发现学生的实际反馈利用情况能够揭示单纯教学质量评估无法捕捉的性能差异，为AI导师系统评估提供了更完整、可操作的评估视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI导师系统评估仅关注教学（pedagogical）质量，忽略了学生实际行为反应——学生是否真正利用了反馈，这是一个关键的评估维度。

Method: 对10,235份代码提交及对应AI导师反馈进行分析，比较两个不同学期的AI导师，通过行为指标（学生是否根据反馈采取行动、行动是否正确）量化评估，并结合学生感知帮助度调查进行验证。

Result: 行为层面的指标揭示了仅凭教学质量评估无法捕捉的差异，且行为信号与学生感知帮助度的关联性更强。

Conclusion: 提出了一个包含行为维度的新评估框架，弥补了仅依赖教学质量的评估不足，为AI导师系统的全面性能评估提供了可操作的分析工具。

Abstract: Current Artificial Intelligence (AI)-based tutoring systems (AI tutors) are primarily evaluated based on the pedagogical quality of their feedback messages. While important, pedagogy alone is insufficient because it ignores a critical question: what do students actually do with the feedback they receive? We argue that AI tutor evaluation should be extended with a behavioral dimension grounded in student interaction data, which complements pedagogical assessment. We propose an evaluation framework and apply it to 10,235 code submissions with corresponding AI tutor feedback from an introductory undergraduate programming course to measure whether students act on tutor feedback and whether those actions are applied correctly. Using this framework to compare two deployed AI tutors across different semesters in a large-scale introductory computer science course reveals substantial differences in student engagement patterns that are not captured by pedagogy-only evaluation. Moreover, these engagement-based behavioral signals are more strongly associated with student perception of helpful feedback than pedagogical quality alone, providing a more complete and actionable picture of AI tutor performance.

</details>


### [68] [A Benchmark for Strategic Auditee Gaming Under Continuous Compliance Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06340)
*Florian A. D. Burnat,Brittany I. Davidson*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Continuous post-deployment compliance audits, mandated by emerging regulations such as the EU AI Act and Digital Services Act, create a class of strategic gaming distinct from the one-shot input/output gaming studied in prior work. Regulated systems can delay outcome reporting, drift their reports within plausible noise envelopes, exploit longitudinal sample attrition, and cherry-pick among ambiguous metric definitions. We formalize continuous auditing as a $T$-round Stackelberg game between an auditor that commits to a temporal policy and an adaptive auditee, and identify a structural feature of any noise-aware static-auditor design: a cover regime in which coverage gaps and granularity gaps cannot be closed simultaneously. We make this formal as Observation 1 and show that two minimal extension policies, each derived from the observation, close the regime along orthogonal axes: a sample-size-aware static rule (Periodic-with-floor) closes the granularity-failure case, while a history-conditioned suspicion-escalation policy closes the coverage-failure case for the naive Drift strategy -- and neither closes both, exactly as the observation predicts; an audit-aware OffAuditDrift strategy that exploits Stackelberg commitment defeats both. To support empirical study we contribute a non-additive harm decomposition (welfare loss $W$, coverage loss $C$) that exposes how attrition shifts harm from the regulator-accountable surface to a regulator-invisible one; an initial library of five auditee strategies (Delay, Drift, Cherry-pick, Attrition, OffAuditDrift) and five auditor policies, calibrated to summary statistics from published audits of the DSA Transparency Database; and a reproducible simulator with a small, extensible Python interface.

</details>


### [69] [From Review to Design: Ethical Multimodal Driver Monitoring Systems for Risk Mitigation, Incident Response, and Accountability in Automated Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06439)
*Bilal Khana,Waseem Shariff,Rory Coyne,Muhammad Ali Farooq,Peter Corcoran*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文针对自动驾驶车辆中的驾驶员监控系统（DMS），审查现有监管框架（GDPR、EU AI Act、IEEE标准）的适用性，识别其在车内多模态感知场景中的监管空白，并提出针对DMS的模块化伦理设计框架，涵盖用户可配置同意机制、公平感知模型开发、透明性与可解释性工具，以及驾驶员情感健康保障措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着车辆自动化程度提升，DMS成为确保人类监督与安全的关键，但其部署引入了复杂的伦理与法律挑战，包括隐私、知情同意、数据所有权和算法公平性等。现有的通用监管框架（GDPR、EU AI Act、IEEE标准）缺乏针对车内感知技术特殊风险的专门规范。

Method: 采用“审查-设计”视角：①批判性审查现有监管工具与伦理框架；②基于审查结果提出模块化伦理设计框架；③制定风险分析与失效缓解策略，强调主动事件响应与问责机制。

Result: 提出针对DMS的模块化伦理设计框架，包含：用户可配置同意机制、公平感知模型开发、透明性与可解释性工具、驾驶员情感健康保障；并形成面向DMS场景的风险分析与失效缓解策略。

Conclusion: 本文为开发透明、可信赖、以人为中心的下一代自动驾驶车辆驾驶员监控系统提供了设计指导与监管建议，填补了现有框架在车内AI感知领域的技术-伦理交叉空白。

Abstract: As vehicles transition toward higher levels of automation, Driver Monitoring Systems (DMS) have become essential for ensuring human oversight, safety, and regulatory compliance in a vehicle. These systems rely on multimodal sensing and AI-driven inference to assess driver attention, cognitive state, and readiness to take control. While technologically promising, their deployment introduces a complex set of ethical and legal challenges - ranging from privacy and consent to data ownership and algorithmic fairness. While overarching frameworks such as the GDPR, EU AI Act, and IEEE standards offer important guidance, they lack the specificity required for addressing the unique risks posed by in-cabin sensing technologies.
  This paper adopts a review-to-design perspective, critically examining existing regulatory instruments and ethical frameworks -- such as the GDPR, the EU AI Act, and IEEE guidelines -- and identifying gaps in their applicability to the distinctive risks posed by multimodal, AI-enabled in-cabin monitoring. Building on this review, we propose a modular ethical design framework tailored specifically to Driver Monitoring Systems. The framework translates high-level principles into actionable design and deployment guidance, including user-configurable consent mechanisms, fairness-aware model development, transparency and explainability tools, and safeguards for driver emotional well-being.
  Finally, the paper outlines a risk analysis and failure mitigation strategy, emphasizing proactive incident response and accountability mechanisms tailored to the DMS context. Together, these contributions aim to inform the development of transparent, trustworthy, and human-centered driver monitoring systems for next-generation autonomous vehicles.

</details>

### [70] [Nitsum: Serving Tiered LLM Requests with Adaptive Tensor Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05467)
*Vikranth Srivatsa,Zijian He,Pu Guo,Dongming Li,Yiying Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Nitsum是一个分布式LLM服务系统，其核心创新是将张量并行度（TP）从静态部署参数转变为运行时可动态调整的控制面，通过联合优化TP级别、Prefill/Decode GPU分割和请求调度来解决多租户场景下的分層SLO挑战。关键技术包括TP感知的权重重用和快速KV迁移以降低动态调整开销，实现最高5.3倍的SLO合规吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM服务呈现多租户特征，需在同一部署下同时处理延迟敏感的交互式请求和宽松的后台工作负载，导致分層SLO场景。然而现有系统主要关注请求级控制（如队列和批处理），而将执行配置（如张量并行度）保持静态，这种方式在多层级资源竞争下限制了系统适应性。

Method: Nitsum采用三层联合优化方法：(1) TP级别动态调整：将张量并行度从静态部署选择转变为运行时控制面；(2) Prefill/Decode GPU资源动态分割：根据负载实时分配计算资源；(3) 请求调度优化：结合上述配置进行整体调度。同时引入TP感知的权重重用机制和快速KV状态迁移，以降低动态调整TP的开销。

Result: 实验表明，Nitsum在真实轨迹和针对性微基准测试中，SLO合规吞吐量相比现有最优系统提升最高达5.3倍。

Conclusion: Nitsum通过将TP作为运行时控制面，结合TP感知的权重复用和快速KV迁移技术，实现了在真实轨迹和微基准测试中SLO合规吞吐量最高5.3倍的提升，证明了动态调整TP级别等执行配置的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: LLM serving is increasingly multi-tenant: the same deployment must handle latency-critical interactive requests and more relaxed background workloads under a fixed GPU budget. This creates a tiered-SLO setting where maximizing overall goodput (requests that satisfy both TTFT and TPOT targets) is challenging because workload mix, request lengths, and load intensity vary over time. Existing systems mainly optimize request-level controls (e.g., queuing and batching) while keeping execution configuration largely static, which limits adaptation under multi-tier contention.
  We present Nitsum, a distributed LLM serving system that treats tensor parallelism (TP) as a first-class runtime control surface rather than a static deployment choice. Nitsum jointly optimizes TP level, prefill/decode GPU split, and request scheduling. To make frequent TP adaptation practical, Nitsum introduces TP-aware weight reuse and fast KV migration. Experiments on real traces and targeted microbenchmarks show that Nitsum improves SLO-compliant goodput over SoTA by up to 5.3 times.

</details>


### [71] [A Scalable Digital Twin Framework for Energy Optimization in Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05581)
*Raphael Hendrigo de Souza Gonçalves,Wendel Marcos dos Santos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一个可扩展的数字孪生框架，用于数据中心能源优化。框架集成物联网数据采集、云计算和机器学习技术，实现实时监控、预测和智能能源管理。使用LSTM模型预测能源需求，在小型数据中心环境中验证了能效提升和PUE改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心能耗巨大，需要实时监控和智能优化管理以提高能源效率

Method: 构建集成IoT数据采集、云计算和机器学习技术的数字孪生框架；开发小型数据中心环境监测功耗、温度和工作负载；采用LSTM模型预测能源需求

Result: 实验表明能效得到改善，功耗降低，PUE（电源使用效率）提升

Conclusion: 该框架在小规模受控环境中验证，展现出作为可持续数据中心管理可扩展且经济有效解决方案的潜力

Abstract: This study proposes a scalable Digital Twin framework for energy optimization in data centers.The framework integrates IoT-based data acquisition, cloud computing, and machine learning techniques to enable real-time monitoring, forecasting, and intelligent energy management. A controlled small-scale data center environment was developed to monitor variables such as power consumption, temperature, and computational workload. Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) models were employed to predict energy demand and support operational decision-making. Experimental results demonstrated improvements in energy efficiency, including reductions in power consumption and enhancements in Power Usage Effectiveness (PUE). Despite being evaluated in a constrained environment, the proposed framework demonstrates strong potential as a scalable and cost-effective solution for sustainable data center management.

</details>


### [72] [LLM-Enhanced Deep Reinforcement Learning for Task Offloading in Collaborative Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05727)
*Hao Guo,Kaixiang Xv,Ziwu Ge,Lei Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出LeDRL框架，通过结合轻量级LLM与自注意力增强的DRL解决边缘计算任务卸载问题，克服DRL样本效率低和LLM实时性差的缺点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘计算中动态任务卸载面临低延迟高可靠性要求，现有方法存在局限：DRL样本效率低且易陷入局部最优，LLM无法实现实时决策。

Method: LeDRL构建结构化上下文感知提示词捕获节点状态、任务语义和链路动态；通过自注意力对齐模块处理策略先验；反射评估器从历史轨迹中提取语义反馈指导未来提示词优化。

Result: LeDRL在任务成功率、收敛速度和实时响应性上优于基线方法，成功率提升超过17%；在Jetson边缘设备上完成原型系统CoEdgeSys部署验证。

Conclusion: 混合LLM-DRL框架能有效结合语义理解与实时决策能力，为边缘计算任务卸载提供可行解决方案。

Abstract: Collaborative edge computing uses edge nodes in different locations to execute tasks, necessitating dynamic task offloading decisions to maintain low latency and high reliability, especially under unpredictable node failures. Although deep reinforcement learning (DRL) and large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for task offloading, DRL often suffers from high sample inefficiency and local optima, whereas LLMs struggle with real-time decision-making. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{LeDRL}, a hybrid decision framework that couples a \emph{lightweight LLM} with self-attention-enhanced DRL for real-time task offloading. LeDRL constructs structured, context-aware prompts capturing node status, task semantics, and link dynamics to derive high-level strategy priors.
