These are selectively processed by a self-attention-based alignment module for context-aware policy optimization. A reflective evaluator distills semantic feedback from past trajectories to guide future prompts, enabling more informative and temporally generalizable LLM queries. Extensive experiments show that LeDRL outperforms baselines in task success rate, convergence speed, and real-time responsiveness across diverse network scales, achieving over 17\% improvement in success rate. Furthermore, we deploy LeDRL on Jetson-based edge devices using our prototype system \textit{CoEdgeSys}, demonstrating its robustness and feasibility under resource constraints. Our code is available at:https://github.com/GalleyG5/LeDRL.git.

</details>


### [73] [Relay Buffer Independent Communication over Pooled HBM for Efficient MoE Inference on Ascend](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06055)
*Tianlun Hu,Tiancheng Hu,Shengsheng Litang,Sheng Wang,Xiaoming Bao,Yuxing Li,Wei Wang,Zhongzhe Hu,Lijun Li,Hongwei Sun,Jingbin Zhou\\*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对Ascend系统上的MoE推理，提出无中继缓冲区的通信设计，通过直接内存访问和轻量级控制状态消除中间缓冲，实现Prefill和Decode两种高效调度，显著降低延迟并提升实际部署性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE推理中dispatch和combine是主要瓶颈，存在路由驱动的布局转换、临时中继和输出恢复开销，现有方法使用buffer-centric路径导致大量中间缓冲和重排序操作

Method: 1) 提出relay-buffer-free通信设计，直接放置到目标expert窗口和直接读取远程expert窗口；2) 基于全局池化高带宽内存和对称内存分配；3) 设计两种调度：Prefill调度（高吞吐量）和Decode调度（低延迟）

Result: 实验表明两种调度均降低dispatch/combine延迟，Serving级别改进TTFT、保持竞争性TPOT，并在实际延迟约束下扩大可行调度空间

Conclusion: 在Ascend系统上，去除中间缓冲和输出恢复可有效加速MoE推理，改进TTFT、保持TPOT并扩大调度空间

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) inference requires large-scale token exchange across devices, making dispatch and combine major bottlenecks in both prefill and decode. Beyond network transfer, routing-driven layout transformation, temporary relay, and output restoration can add substantial overhead. Existing MoE communication paths are often buffer-centric, using explicit inter-process relay and reordering buffers around collective transfer. This report presents a relay-buffer-free communication design for MoE inference acceleration on Ascend systems. The design reorganizes dispatch and combine around direct placement into destination expert windows and direct reading from remote expert windows. Built on globally pooled high-bandwidth memory and symmetric-memory allocation, it removes most intermediate relay and reordering buffers while retaining only lightweight control state, including counts, offsets, and synchronization metadata. We instantiate the design as two schedules for the main phases of MoE inference: a prefill schedule with richer planning state for throughput-oriented execution, and a compact decode schedule for latency-sensitive execution. Experiments on Ascend-based MoE workloads show reduced dispatch and combine latency in both settings. At the serving level, the implementation improves time to first token (TTFT), preserves competitive time per output token (TPOT), and enlarges the feasible scheduling space under practical latency constraints. These results indicate that, on platforms with globally addressable device memory, reducing intermediate buffering and output restoration around expert execution is an effective direction for accelerating MoE inference.

</details>


### [74] [FalconGEMM: Surpassing Hardware Peaks with Lower-Complexity Matrix Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06057)
*Honglin Zhu,Jiaping Cao,Jiang Shao,Siyuan Feng,Qian Qiu,Peng Chen,Xu Zhang,Yixian Zhou,Man Lung Yiu,Guang Ji,Minwen Deng,Wenxi Zhu,Jintao Meng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FalconGEMM是一个跨平台框架，通过代码生成、并行优化和轻量级性能模型，自动部署和选择低复杂度矩阵乘法算法(LCMA)，在GPU和CPU多种架构上实现突破性性能提升，相比主流GEMM库提升7.59%-17.85%，相比AlphaTensor提升12.41%-55.61%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习（尤其是大语言模型LLM）的训练和推理对矩阵乘法性能有极高需求，但现有GEMM库和低复杂度矩阵乘法算法(LCMA)在实际部署中存在跨平台适配困难、性能优化不足、策略选择不智能等问题，制约了LCMA的理论性能优势在异构硬件上的实际发挥。

Method: FalconGEMM包含三大核心模块：①部署模块通过代码生成实现跨硬件可移植执行；②执行模块采用组并行优化策略最大化片上数据复用、并行资源和带宽利用率；③决策模块基于轻量级分析性能模型，根据矩阵形状和硬件配置自动选择最优策略。

Result: 在H20、A100等GPU和ARM、x86等CPU架构上，针对多种数据类型的LLM工作负载测试中，FalconGEMM相比cuBLAS、CUTLASS、Intel MKL等主流GEMM库性能提升7.59%-17.85%，相比AlphaTensor等LCMA竞品提升12.41%-55.61%。

Conclusion: FalconGEMM成功将低复杂度矩阵乘法算法的理论优势转化为异构硬件上的实际生产部署性能，为现代硬件环境提供了切实可行的性能突破解决方案。

Abstract: Peak breaking Matrix Multiplication is a promising technique to improve the performance of DL, especially in LLM training and inference. We present FalconGEMM, a cross-platform framework that automates the deployment, optimization, and selection of Lower-Complexity Matrix Multiplication Algorithms (LCMAs) across diverse hardware. There are three key innovations: (1) a Deployment Module that enables portable execution across various hardware and input configurations through code generation; (2) an Execution Module with Group-Parallel Optimizations that maximizes on-chip data reuse, utilizes parallel resources, and reduces bandwidth overhead; and (3) a Decision Module featuring a lightweight analytical performance model to select the optimal strategy based on matrix shapes and hardware profiles. Extensive evaluation is conducted on LLM workloads across GPU (H20, A100) and CPU (ARM, x86) architectures with multiple data types. FalconGEMM succeeds in delivering peak breaking performance and outperforms GEMM libraries (e.g., cuBLAS, CUTLASS, Intel MKL, etc) by 7.59%-17.85% and LCMA competitors like AlphaTensor by 12.41%-55.61%. Our framework makes the theoretical promise of LCMAs practical for production deployment across the heterogeneous landscape of modern hardware.

</details>


### [75] [Tackling the Data-Parallel Load Balancing Bottleneck in LLM Serving: Practical Online Routing at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06113)
*Tianci Bu,Yuan Lyu,Zixi Chen,Chendong Song,Hong Liang,Tsepten Gurung,Yuwei Fan,Yinyu Ye,Zijie Zhou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: BalanceRoute提出了一种针对大规模LLM服务中数据并行(DP)负载不均衡问题的在线路由算法家族，包含BR-0和BR-H两个版本，可在毫秒级调度预算内有效降低DP工作节点间的负载不平衡，提升端到端服务吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大规模LLM服务中，模型通过张量并行(TP)或专家并行(EP)分片并跨DP工作节点复制时，每个解码步骤末尾的同步屏障延迟由负载最重的工作节点决定。LLM解码的独特挑战包括：KV缓存迁移成本高导致分配具有粘性、每个请求的负载随时间增长、请求到达非平稳、路由决策需在100ms内完成跨数百待处理请求和数十工作节点的调度。

Method: 提出BalanceRoute算法家族，包含两个版本：1) BR-0无需预测基础设施，采用分段线性F-score捕获"填充安全边界"与"溢出边界"请求之间的非对称性，两阶段分解确保每步成本符合毫秒级调度；2) BR-H通过常数级前视窗口H和轻量级终止分类器接口泛化BR-0，将F-score扩展为视界折扣形式。

Result: 在144-NPU集群上部署BalanceRoute，基于生产轨迹和公开Azure-2024轨迹进行评估，相比vLLM基线，BalanceRoute显著降低了平均DP不平衡程度，提升了端到端服务吞吐量。

Conclusion: BalanceRoute通过创新的在线路由算法有效解决了LLM推理服务中的DP负载均衡瓶颈，在实际生产环境中展现了优异的性能提升。

Abstract: Data-parallel (DP) load balancing has emerged as a first-order bottleneck in large-scale LLM serving. When a model is sharded across devices via tensor parallelism (TP) or expert parallelism (EP) and replicated across many DP workers, every decode step ends in a synchronization barrier whose latency is set by the most heavily loaded worker; even modest persistent imbalance across DP workers compounds, step after step, into a substantial fraction of wasted compute. The problem is hard for reasons specific to LLM decoding: assignments are sticky (migrating KV caches has a high cost), per-request loads grow over time, arrivals are non-stationary, and the router must decide within a sub-100\,ms decode budget over hundreds of waiting requests and tens of workers. We present \textbf{BalanceRoute}, a family of practical online routing algorithms that target this bottleneck. The first, \textbf{BR-0}, requires no prediction infrastructure and uses a piecewise-linear F-score that captures the sharp asymmetry between admissions that fill safe margin and those that overflow into the envelope; a two-stage decomposition keeps per-step cost compatible with millisecond-scale scheduling. The second, \textbf{BR-H}, generalizes BR-0 with a short, constant lookahead $H$ and a lightweight termination-classifier interface, extending the F-score to a horizon-discounted form. We deploy BalanceRoute on a 144-NPU cluster and evaluate against vLLM baselines on both a proprietary production trace and the public Azure-2024 trace. Across both workloads, BalanceRoute substantially reduces average DP imbalance and improves end-to-end serving throughput.

</details>


### [76] [TACO: A Toolsuite for the Verification of Threshold Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06118)
*Paul Eichler,Tom Baumeister,Mouhammad Sakr,Mahboubeh Kalateh Dowlati,Marcus Völp,Swen Jacobs*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: TACO是一个用于开发与自动验证故障容错阈值型分布式算法的工具套件，通过整合多种模型检验方法与半判定程序，并采用模块化可扩展架构，为该领域算法的形式化验证与开发提供了综合性支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 故障容错和基于阈值的分布式算法在安全关键系统中应用广泛，但这类算法的正确性验证通常面临状态空间爆炸等挑战，因此需要专门的自动化工具来支持其模型检验和验证工作。

Method: TACO工具套件实现了三种阈值自动机的模型检验方法以覆盖已知可判定的片段领域，同时实现两种半判定程序以处理超出可判定范围的情况。此外，该工具套件采用模块化架构设计，便于扩展和二次开发。

Result: 通过实验评估验证了所实现算法的有效性，同时该工具套件具有模块化、可扩展和文档完善等特性，为阈值自动机相关算法和工具的开发提供了良好基础。

Conclusion: TACO作为模块化、可扩展且文档完善的工具套件，能够有效支持阈值自动机的算法开发和模型验证工作，为故障容错和阈值型分布式算法的设计与验证提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: We present TACO, a toolsuite for the development and automatic verification of fault-tolerant and threshold-based distributed algorithms. Our toolsuite implements three approaches for model checking threshold automata in different decidable fragments known from the literature and two semi-decision procedures going beyond these decidable fragments. Moreover, TACO is a modular, extensible, and well-documented framework for developing algorithms and tools for threshold automata. We present important features, give an overview of the implemented algorithms, and evaluate their performance experimentally.

</details>


### [77] [ResiHP: Taming LLM Training Failures with Dynamic Hybrid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06374)
*Tenghui Ma,Jihu Guo,Wei Gao,Sitian Lu,Zhisheng Ye,Hanjing Wang,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ResiHP is a resilient training system for hybrid parallel LLM training that accurately detects hardware failures through a workload-aware predictor and dynamically adapts parallelism configurations to maintain training efficiency at scale.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Large-scale LLM training across tens of thousands of GPUs suffers from performance skew caused by hardware failures on individual devices. Existing resilient systems fail to account for sequence length variability in datasets and device performance skew under hybrid parallelism, leading to spurious fail-slow detections and inefficient failure mitigation through individual adaptations.

Method: 1. Workload-aware execution time predictor to disentangle failures from legitimate iteration time fluctuations (e.g., caused by sequence length variability), enabling accurate online failure detection. 2. Dynamic scheduler that adaptively adjusts parallelism group sizes, model partitioning, and workload scheduling policies based on detected failures to maintain training efficiency.

Result: ResiHP improves training throughput by 1.04-4.39× compared with state-of-the-art resilient training systems under diverse failure scenarios in a 256-GPU cluster.

Conclusion: ResiHP provides a robust solution for resilient LLM training at scale by combining accurate failure detection with fine-grained adaptation strategies, achieving significant performance improvements under diverse failure scenarios.

Abstract: Hybrid parallelism underpins large-scale LLM training across tens of thousands of GPUs. At such scale, hardware failures on individual devices lead to performance skew across devices, diminishing overall training efficiency. Existing resilient systems overlook sequence length variability in datasets and device performance skew under hybrid parallelism. As a result, (1) iteration time fluctuations induced by sequence length variability can trigger spurious fail-slow detections, and (2) failures are mitigated through individual adaptations in hybrid parallelism, leading to unnecessary detection overhead and inefficient resilient training. To respond, this paper presents ResiHP, a resilient system that enables robust failure detection and fine-grained adaptation for hybrid parallel training. First, we develop a Detector to accurately identify failures. In particular, it employs a workload-aware execution time predictor that disentangles failures from iteration time fluctuations while remaining lightweight for online detection. Second, we design a Scheduler that dynamically adapts parallelism group sizes, model partitioning, and workload scheduling policies to improve training efficiency under failures. Experiments show that ResiHP improves training throughput by 1.04-4.39$\times$ compared with state-of-the-art resilient training systems under diverse failure scenarios in a 256-GPU cluster.

</details>


### [78] [ADELIA: Automatic Differentiation for Efficient Laplace Inference Approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06392)
*Afif Boudaoud,Lisa Gaedke-Merzhäuser,Alexandros Nikolaos Ziogas,Vincent Maillou,Alexandru Calotoiu,Marcin Copik,Håvard Rue,Mathieu Luisier,Torsten Hoefler*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ADELIA通过反向模式自动微分替代有限差分，结合结构化稀疏优化和多GPU并行，在保持计算精度的同时实现显著加速和节能，为大规模时空贝叶斯推断提供高效解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有INLA实现依赖有限差分（FD）近似导数，需2d+1次评估，计算量随超参数维度d增长，在固定能耗预算下限制了时间收敛性。

Method: 提出ADELIA框架，采用反向模式自动微分（AD）计算精确梯度（与维度d无关），并设计结构化稀疏多GPU反向传播，充分利用模型稀疏性特征。

Result: ADELIA在10个基准模型（含实际空气污染监测）上实现单梯度4.2-7.9倍加速，在含190万潜变量的大规模模型上可靠收敛（FD方法难以处理），即使扩展至16-32 GPU匹配时钟时间，FD仍需5-8倍更多能耗。

Conclusion: ADELIA是首个利用自动微分实现结构化稀疏优化的INLA框架，在大规模实际问题中表现优异，验证了AD方法替代有限差分的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal Bayesian inference drives environmental and health sciences using latent Gaussian models. Integrated Nested Laplace Approximations (INLA) enable inference for these models at HPC scale but rely on derivative-based optimization over $d$ hyperparameters. State-of-the-art INLA implementations approximate derivatives via central finite differences (FD), requiring $2d{+}1$ evaluations. These evaluations are embarrassingly parallel, but total work and energy grow with $d$, limiting time-to-solution under fixed budgets. Reverse-mode automatic differentiation (AD) computes exact gradients independently of $d$, but its efficient application to INLA's structured-sparse kernels is an open challenge. We present ADELIA, the first AD-enabled INLA implementation with a structure-exploiting multi-GPU backward pass leveraging model sparsity. We evaluate ADELIA on ten benchmark models, including real-world air-pollution monitoring. We achieve $4.2$--$7.9\times$ per-gradient speedups and reliable convergence on production-scale models with up to 1.9M latent variables, where FD struggles. Even when scaled to 16--32 GPUs to match ADELIA's wall-clock time, FD consumes $5$--$8\times$ more energy.

</details>


### [79] [ROSE: Rollout On Serving GPUs via Cooperative Elasticity for Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06534)
*Wei Gao,Yuheng Zhao,Dilxat Muhtar,Dakai An,Xuchun Shang,Tianyuan Wu,Lunxi Cao,Shaopan Xiong,Weixun Wang,Ju Huang,Teng Ma,Siran Yang,Jiamang Wang,Lin Qu,Bo Zheng,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ROSE是一个协作式资源弹性后训练系统，通过挖掘 serving 集群中的空闲GPU资源来加速agentic强化学习的rollouts执行，包含SLO安全共服务执行器、跨集群权重同步引擎和弹性rollout调度器三个核心组件，实验显示吞吐量提升1.20-3.31倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic RL训练受限于长尾rollouts和多轮环境交互，静态GPU配置无法高效适配：过度配置造成资源浪费，配置不足则导致争用和训练减速。同时观察到生产环境serving集群通常保留大量GPU算力和显存余量，可用于协作式弹性扩展。

Method: 提出ROSE系统，包含三个组件：(1) SLO安全共服务执行器——通过高效GPU内存和计算共享提升rollout吞吐量的同时保证SLO；(2) 跨集群权重同步引擎——利用权重分片和稀疏性实现快速权重同步；(3) 弹性rollout调度器——动态配置协作容量并智能路由轨迹rollouts。

Result: 在多种模型规模和集群规模下，ROSE相比现有固定资源和弹性基线方法实现1.20-3.31倍的端到端吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: 通过协作式弹性机制，ROSE成功复用了serving集群的闲置GPU资源，在保证服务SLO的前提下有效加速agentic RL训练，为大规模语言模型后训练提供了一种高效的资源利用范式。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a key driver for improving the multi-step reasoning and tool-use capabilities of LLMs. However, its efficiency is bottlenecked by long-tail rollouts with multi-turn environment interactions, making static GPU provisioning a poor fit: overprovisioning wastes GPUs on stragglers, while underprovisioning increases contention and slows training.
  We observe that production serving clusters routinely leave substantial GPU compute and memory headroom. Based on this observation, we argue for cooperative elasticity: opportunistically repurposing underutilized serving GPUs to execute rollouts. Realizing cooperative elasticity is non-trivial because it must preserve serving Service Level Objectives (SLOs) under bursty traffic and minimize communication overhead. To address these challenges, we present ROSE, a cooperative, resource-elastic post-training system that safely harvests idle compute and memory on serving GPUs to accelerate agentic RL rollouts. ROSE consists of three components: (1) an SLO-safe co-serving executor that improves rollout throughput while preserving serving SLOs through efficient GPU memory and compute sharing; (2) a cross-cluster weight transfer engine that leverages weight shards and sparsity for fast weight synchronization across clusters; and (3) an elastic rollout scheduler that dynamically provisions cooperative capacity and routes trajectory rollouts across dedicated rollout GPUs and opportunistic serving GPUs. Experiments across multiple model sizes and cluster scales show that ROSE improves average end-to-end throughput by 1.20-3.31 x compared with state-of-the-art resource-fixed and elastic baselines.

</details>


### [80] [CCL-Bench 1.0: A Trace-Based Benchmark for LLM Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06544)
*Eric Ding,Byungsoo Oh,Bhaskar Kataria,Kaiwen Guo,Jelena Gvero,Abhishek Vijaya Kumar,Arjun Devraj,Lindsey Bowen,Atharv Sonwane,Emaad Manzoor,Rachee Singh*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CCL-Bench是一个基于追踪的基准测试工具，通过记录可重用的执行轨迹、工作负载卡片和启动脚本，解决了现有LLM基础设施评估基准仅提供端到端数字而无法解释性能差异原因的问题。该工具能够计算细粒度的计算、内存和通信效率指标，揭示了三个传统摘要统计基准无法支持的发现：计算-通信重叠与训练步时间的关系、TPU与GPU互联带宽扩展性的差异、以及不同训练框架间可达3倍的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM基础设施评估基准存在根本性缺陷：仅发布少量端到端数字，无法解释为何某一配置优于另一配置。LLM工作负载的性能涉及硬件加速器、互联带宽、软件框架、并行策略和通信库等复杂配置空间，需要更深入的诊断能力。

Method: 提出CCL-Bench，一个基于追踪的基准测试框架。每个数据点包含执行轨迹、YAML工作负载卡片和启动脚本。开发了社区可扩展的工具包，用于计算细粒度的计算、内存和通信效率指标。

Result: 揭示三个传统基准无法支持的发现：(i)更高的计算-通信重叠可能与更长的训练步时间同时出现，揭示低效的并行化选择；(ii)在中小规模工作负载上，TPU互联带宽翻倍带来的端到端步时间改善远大于GPU互联带宽翻倍；(iii)在相同硬件上，一个训练框架的最优配置可能比另一个框架的最优配置慢3倍。

Conclusion: CCL-Bench证明了基于追踪的详细基准测试对于理解和优化LLM基础设施性能至关重要，能够揭示配置空间中的深层效率问题，而这是传统摘要统计基准无法提供的。

Abstract: Evaluative claims about LLM infrastructure -- ``workload X is fastest on hardware Y with software Z'' -- depend on a complex configuration space spanning hardware accelerators, interconnect bandwidth, software frameworks, parallelism plans, and communication libraries. Current infrastructure evaluation benchmarks publish a small set of end-to-end numbers that do not explain why one configuration outperforms another. We present CCL-Bench, a trace-based benchmark that addresses the limitations of existing benchmarks by recording reusable evidence for every ML workload. Each contributed data point in CCL-Bench packages an execution trace, a YAML workload card, and the launch scripts. We have developed a community-extensible toolkit to compute fine-grained compute, memory, and communication efficiency metrics from this evidence. Using CCL-Bench, we surface three claims that summary-statistic benchmarks cannot support: (i) higher compute-communication overlap can coincide with longer training step time and reveal inefficient parallelization choices, (ii) doubling TPU interconnect bandwidth yields a much higher end-to-end improvement in step time than doubling GPU interconnect bandwidth on small and medium workloads, and (iii) the best-tuned configuration on one training framework can run up to 3$\times$ slower than the best-tuned configuration on a peer framework on identical hardware.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [81] [Nationwide EHR-Based Chronic Rhinosinusitis Prediction Using Demographic-Stratified Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05213)
*Sicong Chang,Yidan Shen,Justina Varghese,Akshay R Prabhakar,Sebastian Guadarrama-Sistos-Vazquez,Jiefu Chen,Masayoshi Takashima,Omar G. Ahmed,Renjie Hu,Xin Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用All of Us项目全国电子健康记录数据，通过混合特征选择流程和亚组分层建模，实现慢性鼻窦炎早期预测，AUC为0.8461，为初级保健风险分层提供支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性鼻窦炎是一种常见且异质性的炎症性疾病，症状表现与过敏性鼻炎等常见疾病重叠，异质性表型掩盖风险模式，且既往预测研究依赖单机构队列，降低了人群层面的泛化能力。

Method: 采用All of Us研究项目的全国性纵向电子健康记录数据，利用两年诊断前病史预测慢性鼻窦炎；开发混合特征选择流程（结合基于患病率的统计筛选与基于模型的重要性排序），将约11万个候选代码压缩为100个可解释特征；在6个成人性别和生命阶段亚组中训练分层模型并进行亚组特定超参数调优。

Result: 模型整体AUC达到0.8461，相比最佳基线模型判别能力提升0.0168。

Conclusion: 研究表明，常规收集的电子健康记录数据可支持人群代表性的慢性鼻窦炎风险分层，并为初级保健中的早期分诊和转诊优先级排序提供信息指导。

Abstract: Chronic rhinosinusitis (CRS) is a common heterogeneous inflammatory disorder that causes substantial morbidity and healthcare costs. CRS is difficult to identify early from routine encounters, as symptom presentations overlap with common conditions such as allergic rhinitis, and heterogeneous phenotypes further obscure risk patterns. Prior predictive studies often rely on single-institutional cohorts , which reduce population-level generalizability. To overcome this, we leveraged nationwide longitudinal EHR data from the \textit{All of Us} Research Program to predict CRS diagnosis using two years of pre-diagnostic history. To address extreme feature sparsity and dimensionality in coded EHR data, we implemented a hybrid feature-selection pipeline that combines prevalence-based statistical screening with model-based importance ranking, compressing approximately 110,000 candidate codes into 100 interpretable features. To capture demographic heterogeneity, we trained demographic stratified models across six adult sex and life-stage subgroups with subgroup-specific hyperparameter tuning. Our framework achieved an overall AUC of 0.8461, improving discrimination by 0.0168 over the best baseline. These results demonstrate that routinely collected EHR data may support population-representative CRS risk stratification and inform earlier triage and referral prioritization in primary care.

</details>


### [82] [SAT: Sequential Agent Tuning for Coordinator Free Plug and Play Multi-LLM Training with Monotonic Improvement Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05216)
*Yi Xie,Yangyang Xu,Yi Fan,Bo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAT（Sequential Agent Tuning）框架，通过因子化策略和分块坐标更新实现多智能体协同训练无需中央控制器，同时提供单调改进保证和可插拔不变性理论。12B参数的3智能体团队超越32B单模型，验证了多智能体协作的高效性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance but are prohibitively expensive to deploy. Using teams of smaller LLMs could match or surpass single large models, but jointly updating multiple agents causes compounding distribution shifts, making coordination and training stability difficult.

Method: Sequential Agent Tuning (SAT) represents the team as a factorized policy and employs block-coordinate updates over agents, enabling scalable, decentralized training without a central controller. Key innovations include: (1) sequence-aware, on-policy advantage estimator that conditions on the evolving team policy, and (2) per-agent KL trust regions that isolate occupancy drift.

Result: Empirically, a team of three 4B agents (12B total) trained with SAT surpasses Qwen3-32B on AIME24/25 benchmarks by 3.9% on average. Plug-and-play validation by swapping in two 8B agents boosts composite score by 10.4%.

Conclusion: SAT provides monotonic improvement guarantee and plug-and-play invariance, enabling any agent to be upgraded to a stronger model without retraining the team, with formal performance bound guarantees.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) with a large number of parameters achieve strong performance but are often prohibitively expensive to deploy. Recent work explores using teams of smaller, more efficient LLMs that collectively match or even outperform a single large model. However, jointly updating multiple agents introduces compounding distribution shifts, making coordination and stability during training difficult. We address this by introducing Sequential Agent Tuning (SAT), a coordinator-free training paradigm. SAT represents the team as a factorized policy and employs block-coordinate updates over agents, enabling scalable, decentralized training without a central controller. Specifically, we develop a sequence-aware, on-policy advantage estimator that conditions on the evolving team policy, coupled with per-agent KL trust regions that isolate occupancy drift. Theoretically, this framework provides two critical guarantees. First, it ensures monotonic improvement, stabilizing the training process. Second, it establishes provable plug-and-play invariance: any agent can be upgraded to a stronger model without retraining the rest of the team, with a formal guarantee that the performance bound improves. Empirically, a team of three 4B agents (12B total) trained with SAT surpasses the much larger Qwen3-32B on AIME24/25 benchmarks by 3.9\% on average. We validate our plug-and-play theory by swapping in two 8B agents, which boosts the composite score by 10.4\%. We provide code and appendix of proof at https://github.com/Yydc/SAT-AAMAS

</details>


### [83] [Horizon-Constrained Rashomon Sets for Chaotic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05218)
*Gauri Kale,Rahul Vishwakarma,Holly Diamond,Ava Hedayatipour,Amin Rezaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究首次将混沌理论与预测多重性相结合，提出视界约束Rashomon集框架，揭示混沌系统中模型多重性随预测视界指数收缩的规律，并通过Lyapunov加权度量及决策对齐选择算法，在合成与真实混沌系统（Lorenz-96、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky、风电、交通、天气）验证了决策质量提升18-34%，为安全关键混沌领域的机器学习部署提供理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测多重性和混沌动力学是机器学习中的两个独立发展的基本挑战，尽管两者存在概念联系却长期未被沟通。该研究旨在建立两者间的理论桥梁，以指导安全关键混沌领域的机器学习部署。

Method: 1. 引入视界约束Rashomon集框架，通过最大Lyapunov指数表征模型多重性随预测视界的指数收缩规律；
2. 提出Lyapunov加权度量，为预测分歧提供更紧的边界估计；
3. 开发决策对齐选择算法，优先考虑下游效用而非单纯预测精度。实验涵盖合成混沌系统（Lorenz-96、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky）及真实应用场景（风电、交通、气象）。

Result: 证明了有效Rashomon集随预测提前期的指数收缩规律，收缩速率由最大Lyapunov指数决定；决策对齐选择算法在保持竞争力的预测性能同时提升决策质量18-34%。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了混沌理论与预测多重性之间的首个严格联系，为安全关键型混沌领域的机器学习部署提供了原则性指导。

实验结果表明，框架在混沌系统预测中实现了18-34%的决策质量提升，同时保持了具有竞争力的预测性能。

Abstract: Predictive multiplicity and chaotic dynamics represent two fundamental challenges in machine learning that have evolved independently despite their conceptual connections. We bridge this gap by introducing horizon-constrained Rashomon sets, a theoretical framework that characterizes how model multiplicity evolves with prediction horizon in chaotic systems. Unlike static prediction tasks where the Rashomon set remains fixed, chaos induces exponential divergence among initially similar models, fundamentally transforming the nature of predictive equivalence. We prove that the effective Rashomon set contracts exponentially with lead time at a rate determined by the maximum Lyapunov exponent and introduce Lyapunov-weighted metrics that provide tighter bounds on predictive disagreement. Leveraging these insights, we develop decision-aligned selection algorithms that choose among near-optimal models based on downstream utility rather than forecast accuracy alone. Extensive experiments on synthetic chaotic systems (Lorenz-96, Kuramoto-Sivashinsky) and real-world applications (wind power, traffic, weather) demonstrate that our framework improves decision quality by 18-34\% while maintaining competitive predictive performance. This work establishes the first rigorous connection between chaos theory and predictive multiplicity, providing principled guidance for deploying machine learning in safety-critical chaotic domains.

</details>


### [84] [MidSteer: Optimal Affine Framework for Steering Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05220)
*Tatiana Gaintseva,Andrew Stepanov,Ziquan Liu,Martin Benning,Gregory Slabaugh,Jiankang Deng,Ismail Elezi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper formalizes the theory of concept steering by linking it to affine concept erasure methods. The authors demonstrate that standard steering approaches are special cases of LEACE, propose theoretically grounded LEACE-Switch for concept switching, and introduce MidSteer as a generalized framework enabling minimal-disturbance concept manipulation across various models and modalities.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Steering intermediate representations in generative models is a powerful post-deployment alignment strategy, but lacks comprehensive theoretical foundations.

Method: The paper: (1) establishes mathematical link between steering and affine concept erasure, proving standard approaches are special cases of LEACE; (2) formulates LEACE-Switch for principled concept switching with optimality characterization; (3) introduces MidSteer (Minimal Disturbance concept Steering) as a generalized framework that relaxes assumptions to enable directed, minimal-disturbance transformations.

Result: MidSteer demonstrates favorable performance across diverse tasks, modalities, and architectures, including vision diffusion models and large language models.

Conclusion: The paper provides a comprehensive theoretical framework for concept steering, demonstrating that MidSteer serves as a versatile solution for concept manipulation across various models and tasks.

Abstract: Steering intermediate representations has emerged as a powerful strategy for controlling generative models, particularly in post-deployment alignment and safety settings. However, despite its empirical success, it currently lacks a comprehensive theoretical framework. In this paper, we bridge this gap by formalizing the theory of concept steering. First, we establish a link between steering and affine concept erasure, proving that the standard approach for removing unwanted behaviors is a special case of LEACE (a closed-form method for affine erasure). Next, we formulate a principled theoretical framework for concept switching, LEACE-Switch, and characterize the assumptions under which it provides an optimal affine solution. Building on this analysis, we then introduce MidSteer (Minimal Disturbance concept Steering), a more general affine framework for concept manipulation that relaxes these assumptions and enables directed, minimal-disturbance transformations. We demonstrate that MidSteer performs favorably across a range of tasks, modalities, and architectures, including vision diffusion models and large language models.

</details>


### [85] [Adaptive Computation Depth via Learned Token Routing in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05222)
*Ahmed Abdelmuniem Abdalla Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Token-Selective Attention (TSA)，通过可学习的每Token门控机制动态控制Transformer层的更新操作，实现计算资源的自适应分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer架构对所有token应用相同数量的层，未考虑不同token的上下文难度差异，导致计算资源浪费。

Method: TSA在连续Transformer块之间的残差更新上引入轻量级两层MLP生成连续的停止概率，实现端到端可微分训练，仅增加1.7%参数开销且不改变基础架构。

Result: 实验表明：1) 无深度正则化(λ=0)时路由器仍跳过20% token层操作；2) 字符级语言建模任务节省14-23% TLOps，质量损失<0.5%；3) 相同效率下比Early Exit方法低0.7%验证损失；4) 学习到的路由可迁移至推理时的稀疏执行，实现真实加速。

Conclusion: TSA通过任务损失梯度自动学习难度感知的路由策略，在保持模型质量的同时显著提升计算效率，且学得的路由机制可直接应用于实际推理加速。

Abstract: Standard transformer architectures apply the same number of layers to every token regardless of contextual difficulty. We present Token-Selective Attention (TSA), a learned per-token gate on residual updates between consecutive transformer blocks. Each gate is a lightweight two-layer multi-layer perceptron (MLP) that produces a continuous halting probability, making the mechanism end-to-end differentiable with 1.7% parameter overhead and no changes to the base architecture. Notably, TSA learns difficulty-proportional routing without any explicit depth pressure: even at $λ=0$ (no depth regularisation), the task-loss gradient alone drives the router to skip 20% of token-layer operations. On character-level language modeling, TSA saved 14-23% of token-layer operations (TLOps) across Tiny-Shakespeare and enwik8 at <0.5% quality loss. At matched efficiency, TSA achieved 0.7% lower validation loss than early exit, and the learned routing transfers directly to inference-time sparse execution for real wall-clock speedup.

</details>


### [86] [Structural Instability of Feature Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05223)
*Yunpeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用于分析稀疏自编码器（SAE）中特征联合不稳定性的几何框架。通过将激活空间建模为高维稀疏锥流形，在球面字典模型下推导了由信号锥的高斯平均宽度（统计维度）表征的渐近组合崩塌阈值。研究表明，在高偏置机制下，ReLU整流将相关性诱导的微观方差波动转化为系统性漂移，并在组合下累积，产生类似"棘轮效应"的干扰增长。在CLEVR数据集上的结构化语义特征验证了预测的标度趋势，发现层次相关性加速了相对于随机基线的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性表示假设忽视了过完备字典中产生的非线性干扰效应。组合调控（同时激活不同语义潜在变量）的理论基础尚不完善。需要建立几何框架来理解在特征联合时的不稳定性问题。

Method: 1. 将激活空间建模为高维稀疏锥流形；2. 采用球面字典模型；3. 利用信号锥的高斯平均宽度（统计维度）推导渐近组合崩塌阈值；4. 分析高偏置机制下ReLU整流的累积效应。

Result: 推导出由统计维度表征的组合崩塌阈值；揭示了ReLU整流产生系统性漂移的机制（棘轮效应）；在CLEVR数据集验证了标度趋势，发现层次相关性加速了组合崩塌的发生。

Conclusion: 基于联合的调控方法存在几何约束，限制了可扩展性。组合机制需要显式管理干扰，而非简单依赖线性叠加。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for disentangling feature superposition in transformer-based architectures, enabling precise control via activation steering. However, the theoretical foundations of compositional steering -- the simultaneous activation of distinct semantic latents -- remain under-explored. The prevailing Linear Representation Hypothesis often abstracts away non-linear interference effects that arise in overcomplete dictionaries. We present a geometric framework for analyzing the instability of feature unions. Modeling the activation space as a high-dimensional sparse cone manifold, we derive an asymptotic compositional-collapse threshold under a spherical dictionary model, characterized by the Gaussian mean width (statistical dimension) of the signal cone. We further show that, in the high-bias regime, ReLU rectification converts microscopic correlation-induced variance fluctuations into a systematic drift that accumulates under composition, yielding interference growth consistent with a ratchet effect. We validate the predicted scaling trends on structured semantic features extracted from CLEVR, where hierarchical correlations accelerate the transition relative to random baselines. Together, our results highlight geometric constraints on the scalability of union-based steering and motivate composition mechanisms that explicitly manage interference beyond naive linear superposition.

</details>


### [87] [Channel-Level Semantic Perturbations: Unlearnable Examples for Diverse Training Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05224)
*Bo Wang,Jia Ni,Mengnan Zhao,Zhan Qin,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了不可学习样本(UEs)在预训练-微调(PF)范式下的行为，发现冻结预训练权重会削弱UEs有效性，并提出浅层语义伪装(SSC)方法，通过限制生成过程在语义有效子空间内来绕过语义抑制，在多种训练范式下保持数据不可学习性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UEs研究仅关注从零训练场景，而预训练-微调范式在实践中广泛采用，导致UEs在真实应用中的有效性未被充分验证，存在显著研究空白。

Method: (1) 对比分析UEs在不同训练范式下的表现；(2) 提出"语义过滤"机制解释冻结预训练权重削弱UEs的原因；(3) 设计Shallow Semantic Camouflage (SSC)方法，将扰动生成限制在语义有效子空间内，绕过语义抑制。

Result: 加载和冻结预训练权重显著削弱现有UEs方法的有效性；SSC方法在浅层冻结和语义聚焦预训练等挑战性训练范式下均能保持数据的不可学习性。

Conclusion: 预训练范式对UEs构成根本性挑战，但通过语义感知的扰动生成策略可有效克服此限制，为保护数据隐私提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: The unauthorized use of personal data in model training has emerged as a growing privacy threat. Unlearnable examples (UEs) address this issue by embedding imperceptible perturbations into benign examples to obstruct feature learning. However, existing studies mainly evaluate UEs under from-scratch training settings, leaving their behavior under the widely adopted pretraining-finetuning (PF) paradigm largely unexplored. In this work, we provide the first systematic investigation of unlearnable examples across diverse training paradigms. Our analysis reveals that loading and freezing pretrained weights significantly weakens the effectiveness of existing UEs methods. We further explain these findings through semantic filtering: while UEs tend to induce models to overfit non-semantic noise, thereby weakening their semantic extraction capabilities, under the PF paradigm, frozen shallow layers preserve data semantics, effectively filtering out distracting information like unlearnable noise. Guided by these insights, we propose a hierarchical deception strategy, Shallow Semantic Camouflage (SSC), that confines the generation process to a semantically valid subspace, aiming to bypass the semantic suppression introduced by pretrained weights. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently preserves data unlearnability even under challenging training paradigms, such as shallow-layer freezing and semantic-focused pretraining (SF-Pretrain), bridging the critical gap in pretrain-based unlearnable learning.

</details>


### [88] [Quantizing With Randomized Hadamard Transforms: Efficient Heuristic Now Proven](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06014)
*Ran Ben-Basat,William Kuszmaul,Michael Mitzenmacher,Amit Portnoy,Shay Vargaftik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了随机Hadamard变换(RHT)作为统一随机旋转(URR)实用替代的理论保证问题，证明两次RHT可实现单坐标边际分布的渐近高斯收敛，三次RHT可保证矢量量化所需的协方差衰减，并提出基于输入矩的自适应运行时优化方案，平衡了计算效率与理论性能保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一随机旋转(URR)是现代量化方法中的常用预处理步骤，但计算成本高；实践中常以随机Hadamard变换(RHT)替代，后者虽能保持正交性且可快速实现，但对最坏情况输入的逼近效果缺乏理论保证。

Method: 1. 理论分析：建立两次RHT后归一化旋转向量的单坐标边际分布与标准高斯分布之间的Kolmogorov距离和1-Wasserstein距离上界(O(d^(-1/2)))；2. 应用验证：将该界代入DRIVE和QUIC-FL压缩框架的分析；3. 矢量量化扩展：证明三次RHT组合可导致坐标协方差衰减，确保对统一随机旋转优化的任意固定维度矢量量化码本具有相同期望误差；4. 运行时自适应：提出O(d)时间复杂度的输入矩检查方法，动态选择RHT数量。

Result: 1. 两次RHT后任意固定坐标的边际分布与标准高斯分布的Kolmogorov距离和1-Wasserstein距离误差为O(d^(-1/2))；2. 两次RHT可使DRIVE和QUIC-FL的性能渐近匹配URR；3. 三次RHT才能保证矢量量化所需的坐标协方差衰减；4. 基于矩的自适应检查可实时优化RHT使用数量。

Conclusion: 理论分析与实验验证表明，两次Hadamard变换可作为统一随机旋转的实用替代方案，在梯度压缩(DRIVE、QUIC-FL)等场景下性能渐近匹配；三次变换可保证矢量量化场景下的相关性衰减；基于矩的自适应检查机制可进一步优化实际部署性能，平衡计算效率与理论保证。

Abstract: Uniform random rotations (URRs) are a common preprocessing step in modern quantization approaches used for gradient compression, inference acceleration, KV-cache compression, model weight quantization, and approximate nearest-neighbor search in vector databases. In practice, URRs are often replaced by randomized Hadamard transforms (RHTs), which preserve orthogonality while admitting fast implementations. The remaining issue is the performance for worst-case inputs. With a URR, each coordinate is individually distributed as a shifted beta distribution, which converges to a Gaussian distribution in high dimensions. Generally, one RHT is not suitable in the worst case, as individual coordinates can be far from these distributions. We show that after composing two RHTs on any $d$-sized input vector, the marginal distribution of every fixed coordinate of the normalized rotated vector is within $O(d^{-1/2})$ of a standard Gaussian both in Kolmogorov distance and in $1$-Wasserstein distance. We then plug these bounds into the analyses of modern compression schemes, namely DRIVE and QUIC-FL, and show that two RHTs achieve performance that asymptotically matches URRs.
  However, we show that two RHTs may not be sufficient for Vector Quantization (VQ), which often requires weak correlation across fixed-size blocks of coordinates (as opposed to only marginal distribution convergence for single coordinates). We prove that a composition of three RHTs leads to decaying coordinate covariance. This ensures that any fixed, bounded, multi-dimensional VQ codebook optimized for URRs has the same expected error when using three RHTs, up to an additive term that vanishes with the dimension.
  Finally, because practical inputs are rarely adversarial, we propose a linear-time ${O}(d)$ check on the input's moments to dynamically adapt the number of RHTs used at runtime to improve performance.

</details>


### [89] [MACS: Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling for Efficient Multimodal MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05225)
*Bo Li,Chuan Wu,shaolin Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对MoE MLLMs在Expert Parallelism推理中的straggler effect问题，提出训练无关的MACS框架，通过 Entropy-Weighted Load 和 Dynamic Modality-Adaptive Capacity 两种机制分别解决信息异构性和模态动态性问题，实验表明该方法在多个多模态基准上显著优于现有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE MLLMs在Expert Parallelism推理中存在严重的效率瓶颈（straggler effect），且现有基于token数量的负载均衡方法无法应对多模态场景下的独特挑战：信息异构性（冗余视觉token与关键token被同等对待）和模态动态性（不同任务中视觉-文本比例差异导致资源错配）。

Method: 提出MACS（Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling）框架，包含两个核心机制：1）Entropy-Weighted Load机制：利用熵值量化视觉token的语义重要性；2）Dynamic Modality-Adaptive Capacity机制：根据输入的实时模态组成动态分配专家资源。

Result: 实验结果表明，MACS在多个多模态基准测试上显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: MACS为MoE MLLMs在EP推理中的高效部署提供了新颖且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts Multimodal Large Language Models (MoE MLLMs) suffer from a significant efficiency bottleneck during Expert Parallelism (EP) inference due to the straggler effect. This issue is worsened in the multimodal context, as existing token-count-based load balancing methods fail to address two unique challenges: (1) Information Heterogeneity, where numerous redundant visual tokens are treated equally to semantically critical ones, and (2) Modality Dynamics, where varying visual to text ratios across tasks lead to resource misallocation. To address these challenges, we propose MACS (Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling), a training-free inference framework. Specifically, MACS introduces an Entropy-Weighted Load mechanism to quantify the semantic value of visual tokens, addressing information heterogeneity. Additionally, the Dynamic Modality-Adaptive Capacity mechanism allocates expert resources based on the real-time modal composition of the input. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MACS significantly outperforms existing methods on various multimodal benchmarks, providing a novel and robust solution for the efficient deployment of MoE MLLMs in EP inference.

</details>


### [90] [Contrastive Identification and Generation in the Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06211)
*Xiaoyu Li,Andi Han,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: In the classical identification in the limit model of Gold [1967], a stream of positive examples is presented round by round, and the learner must eventually recover the target hypothesis. Recently, Kleinberg and Mullainathan [2024] introduced generation in the limit, where the learner instead must eventually output novel elements of the target's support. Both lines of work focus on positive-only or fully labeled data. Yet many natural supervision signals are inherently relational rather than singleton, which encode relationships between examples rather than labels of individual ones. We initiate the study of contrastive identification and generation in the limit, where the learner observes a contrastive presentation of data: a stream of unordered pairs $\{x,y\}$ satisfying $h(x)\ne h(y)$ for an unknown target binary hypothesis $h$, but which element is positive is hidden from the learner. We first present three results in the noiseless setting: an exact characterization of contrastive identifiable classes (a one-line geometric refinement of Angluin [1980]'s tell-tale condition), a combinatorial dimension called contrastive closure dimension (a contrasitive analogue of the closure dimension in Raman et al. [2025]) and exactly characterizing uniform contrastive generation with tight sample complexity, and a strict hierarchy in which contrastive generation and text identification are mutually incomparable. We then prove a sharp reversal under finite adversarial corruption: there exist classes identifiable from contrastive pairs under any finite corruption budget by a single budget-independent algorithm, yet not identifiable from positive examples under even one corrupted observation. The unifying technical object is the common crossing graph, which encodes pairwise ambiguity, family-level generation obstructions, and corruption defects in a single coverage-and-incidence language.

</details>


### [91] [LLMSpace: Carbon Footprint Modeling for Large Language Model Inference on LEO Satellites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05615)
*Lei Jiang,Adrian Ildefonso,Daniel Loveless,Fan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LLMSpace，首个用于低地球轨道（LEO）AI卫星上进行LLM推理的碳建模框架，兼顾运营碳排放和嵌入碳排放，考虑辐射加固硬件和LLM工作负载特性，揭示可持续太空LLM推理的关键权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）推理产生快速增长的能源需求和碳排放，形成新兴的能源与碳危机。太阳能供电的AI赋能LEO卫星被提出以减轻地面电力消耗，但其全生命周期碳足迹因发射排放、卫星制造和辐射加固硬件需求而难以评估。

Method: LLMSpace框架联合建模运营碳排放和嵌入碳排放、卫星外围子系统、辐射加固加速器和存储器，以及LLM特定工作负载特征（如prefill-decode行为和token生成）。使用真实卫星和GPU配置进行评估。

Result: LLMSpace揭示了碳足迹、推理延迟、硬件设计和运营寿命之间的关键权衡。

Conclusion: 该框架为实现可持续的太空LLM推理提供了重要分析工具，可指导未来空间AI系统的设计决策。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) impose rapidly growing energy demands, creating an emerging energy and carbon crisis driven by large-scale inference. Solar-powered, AI-enabled low Earth orbit (LEO) satellites have been proposed to mitigate terrestrial electricity consumption, but their lifecycle carbon footprint remains poorly understood due to launch emissions, satellite manufacturing, and radiation-hardened hardware requirements. This paper presents \textit{LLMSpace}, the first carbon modeling framework for LLM inference on AI-enabled LEO satellites. LLMSpace jointly models operational and embodied carbon, peripheral subsystems, radiation-hardened accelerators and memories, and LLM-specific workload characteristics such as prefill-decode behavior and token generation. Using realistic satellite and GPU configurations, LLMSpace reveals key trade-offs among carbon footprint, inference latency, hardware design, and operational lifetime for sustainable space-based LLM inference. Source code: https://github.com/UnchartedRLab/LLMSpace.

</details>


### [92] [Recursive Agent Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06639)
*Apurva Gandhi,Satyaki Chakraborty,Xiangjun Wang,Aviral Kumar,Graham Neubig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出递归智能体优化(RAO)，一种训练递归智能体的强化学习方法。递归智能体能自主生成子任务并委托给自身的新实例，实现推理时扩展。RAO教智能体何时以及如何进行委托和通信，使智能体能够突破上下文窗口限制，通过分治策略泛化到更困难的任务，并获得更低的wall-clock时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在实际应用中面临上下文窗口限制和复杂任务处理能力的挑战。传统的单智能体系统在处理超出其上下文窗口的任务或泛化到更困难问题时存在明显瓶颈，需要一种能够在推理时自然扩展的方法来突破这些限制。

Method: RAO采用强化学习方法训练递归智能体。递归智能体能够将复杂任务分解并委托给自身的新实例，实现递归调用。文章的核心贡献在于：1) 设计了递归推理机制，使智能体能在推理时通过分治策略自然扩展；2) 训练模型学会何时委派任务以及如何与子智能体通信协作。

Result: 实验结果表明，经过RAO训练的递归智能体具有以下优势：1) 训练效率更高；2) 能够处理超出模型上下文窗口限制的任务；3) 能够泛化到比训练任务难度更高的任务；4) 相比单智能体系统具有更低的wall-clock时间成本。

Conclusion: 递归智能体通过分治策略和自主委托机制，为突破大语言模型的上下文限制和提升任务处理能力提供了一条有效途径。RAO方法成功训练了智能体合理利用递归推理的能力，为构建更高效、更通用的人工智能系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: We introduce Recursive Agent Optimization (RAO), a reinforcement learning approach for training recursive agents: agents that can spawn and delegate sub-tasks to new instantiations of themselves recursively. Recursive agents implement an inference-time scaling algorithm that naturally allows agents to scale to longer contexts and generalize to more difficult problems via divide-and-conquer. RAO provides a method to train models to best take advantage of such recursive inference, teaching agents when and how to delegate and communicate. We find that recursive agents trained in this way enjoy better training efficiency, can scale to tasks that go beyond the model's context window, generalize to tasks much harder than the ones the agent was trained on, and can enjoy reduced wall-clock time compared to single-agent systems.

</details>


### [93] [Internalizing Outcome Supervision into Process Supervision: A New Paradigm for Reinforcement Learning for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05226)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Sibo wang,Huiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对强化学习推理中反馈稀疏和信用分配困难的问题，提出监督内化方法，使模型能够自动从失败轨迹中提取过程级学习信号，实现细粒度策略优化，区别于外部提供过程监督的传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决强化学习推理中的信用分配难题。现有方法依赖结果级奖励进行序列级优化，难以精确分配信用；而外部构建的过程监督成本高且难以扩展。

Method: 提出监督内化方法：通过识别、纠正和重用失败推理轨迹，自动提取过程级学习信号。

Result: 实现仅在结果级监督下进行细粒度策略优化，为强化学习推理中的细粒度信用分配开辟了新路径。

Conclusion: 提出监督内化方法，使模型能够通过识别、纠正和重用失败推理轨迹来自动提取过程级学习信号，实现仅需结果监督下的细粒度策略优化。

Abstract: The central challenge of reinforcement learning for reasoning lies not only in the sparsity of outcome-level supervision, but more fundamentally in how to transform feedback provided only at the end of a sequence into fine-grained learning signals that can guide intermediate reasoning steps. Existing approaches either rely on outcome-level rewards for sequence-level optimization, which makes precise credit assignment difficult, or depend on externally constructed process supervision, which is costly and difficult to scale sustainably. To address this, we propose a new perspective: reinforcement learning for reasoning can be understood as the problem of internalizing outcome supervision into process supervision. From this perspective, we introduce a supervision-internalization method for reinforcement learning for reasoning, enabling the model to automatically extract process-level learning signals through identifying, correcting, and reusing failed reasoning trajectories, thereby achieving finer-grained policy optimization under outcome-only supervision. We further abstract this idea into a new training paradigm, in which the model continually generates and refines its own internal process supervision during reinforcement learning, opening a new path for fine-grained credit assignment in reinforcement learning for reasoning that differs from externally provided process supervision.

</details>


### [94] [Rethinking Data Curation in LLM Training: Online Reweighting Offers Better Generalization than Offline Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05227)
*Wanru Zhao,Yihong Chen,Yuzhi Tang,Wentao Ma,Shengchao Hu,Shell Xu Hu,Alex Iacob,Abhinav Mehrotra,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ADAPT框架，将数据筛选从离线范式转变为在线动态重加权问题，通过自适应学习率调整样本重要性，实现隐式课程学习，在保持数据多样性的同时提升模型泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前离线数据筛选方法与训练过程解耦，存在工程开销大、对模型/任务变化敏感的问题；同时硬过滤或重采样会改变数据量，牺牲数据多样性并损害泛化能力。

Method: ADAPT采用基于相似性的质量信号引导的自适应样本级学习率，在训练过程中动态调整样本重要性，实现隐式课程学习，逐步将模型关注点从粗粒度模式转移到细粒度语义区分。

Result: 在指令微调和大规模预训练任务中，ADAPT一致性地优于离线选择/混合方法及先前的在线方法，在相同FLOPs条件下实现了更强的跨基准泛化能力。

Conclusion: ADAPT通过在线动态重加权范式有效解决了离线数据筛选的固有缺陷，无需改变训练样本数量即可实现更强的跨基准泛化能力，证明了在线课程学习在LLM训练中的有效性。

Abstract: Data curation is a critical yet under-explored area in large language model (LLM) training. Existing methods, such as data selection and mixing, operate in an offline paradigm, detaching themselves from training. This separation introduces engineering overhead and makes the curation brittle: the entire pipeline must be re-run under model/task shifts. Moreover, offline methods alter data size through hard filtering or resampling, often sacrificing data diversity and harming generalization. We propose to rethink data curation as an online reweighting problem, where sample importance is dynamically adjusted during training via loss weighting rather than static pre-processing. Specifically, we introduce ADAPT (Adaptive Data reweighting for Pretraining and FineTuning), a dynamic online framework that reweights training samples with adaptive per-sample learning rates guided by similarity-based quality signals, without changing the number of training samples. Unlike offline methods that enforce a static data distribution, ADAPT acts as an implicit curriculum learner, progressively shifting focus from coarse-grained patterns to fine-grained semantic distinctions as the model evolves. Experiments on both instruction tuning and large-scale pretraining show that ADAPT consistently outperforms offline selection/mixing and prior online methods, achieving stronger cross-benchmark generalization under equal FLOPs.

</details>


### [95] [OpenG2G: A Simulation Platform for AI Datacenter-Grid Runtime Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05519)
*Jae-Won Chung,Zhirui Liang,Yanyong Mao,Jiasi Chen,Mosharaf Chowdhury,Vladimir Dvorkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OpenG2G是一个用于模拟AI数据中心与电网运行时协调的仿真平台，旨在解决AI数据中心用电量激增导致的电网容量和可靠性挑战。该平台支持多种控制范式（经典、优化和基于学习的控制器），并量化AI模型和部署选择对数据中心灵活性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI计算需求增长和新数据中心建设给电网带来容量和可靠性挑战，导致新数据中心互联延迟多年，制约AI发展。数据中心可通过实时调整工作负载来响应电网信号，提供快速电力灵活性，但需要有效的协调策略。

Method: 构建OpenG2G仿真平台，采用模块化可扩展架构：1) 基于生产级AI服务真实测量数据的数据中心后端；2) 基于高保真电网仿真器的电网后端；3) 连接二者的通用控制器接口。用户可实现和比较各种控制范式（经典、优化、基于学习的控制器）。

Result: OpenG2G能够回答多种协调问题，包括量化AI模型和部署选择对数据中心灵活性和协调结果的影响。通过现实电网场景和AI工作负载验证了平台的有用性。

Conclusion: OpenG2G的模块化设计使其成为评估AI数据中心与电网协调策略的有效工具，为设计有效的协调机制提供了实验基础。

Abstract: AI's growing compute demand and new datacenter buildouts present major capacity and reliability challenges for the electricity grid, leading to multi-year interconnection delays for new datacenters and bottlenecking AI growth. To ease this strain, datacenters increasingly offer rapid power flexibility in response to grid signals, where the datacenter can increase or decrease its power consumption by adapting its workload in real time.
  In order to understand the impact of large datacenters on the grid and to facilitate the design of effective coordination strategies, we build OpenG2G, a simulation platform for AI datacenter-grid runtime coordination. We show that OpenG2G is capable of answering a wide range of coordination questions by allowing users to implement and compare various control paradigms (including classic, optimization, and learning-based controllers), and quantify how AI model and deployment choices affect datacenter flexibility and coordination outcomes. This versatility is enabled by OpenG2G's modular and extensible architecture: a datacenter backend driven by real measurements of production-grade AI services, a grid backend built on high-fidelity grid simulators, and a generic controller interface that closes the loop between them. We describe the design of OpenG2G and demonstrate its usefulness through realistic grid scenarios and AI workloads.

</details>


### [96] [Evolutionary fine tuning of quantized convolution-based deep learning models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05228)
*Marcin Pietroń*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于进化策略的量化优化方法，通过在每次迭代中微调部分权重的量化状态来改善预训练模型的量化效率，无需重新训练即可提升量化模型准确率，在多种主流架构上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在物联网、移动设备、自动驾驶等资源受限场景中面临内存占用和计算复杂度的挑战。尽管量化是常用的压缩技术，但传统的最近邻量化（nearest neighbor rounding）无法保证量化后模型达到最优准确率。

Method: 使用进化策略作为优化方法，在每次迭代中随机改变小部分权重的量化状态值，通过选择、变异等遗传算子寻找更优的量化配置。

Result: 实验表明，在适当的算子和参数设置下，进化策略能够快速提升预训练量化模型的准确率，验证了方法在图像分类（VGG、ResNet）、目标检测及自编码器架构上的有效性。

Conclusion: 进化策略通过在每次迭代中微调少量权重到不同的量化状态，能够快速提升量化模型的准确率，证明了该方法在多种架构（VGG、ResNet、Autoencoder）上的有效性。

Abstract: Deep learning models are the most efficient models in many machine learning tasks. The main disadvantage when using them in IoT, mobile devices, independent autonomous or real-time systems is their complexity and memory size. Therefore, much research has concentrated on compression techniques of deep learning architectures. One of the most popular technique is quantization. In most of the works, the quantization is done based on the nearest neighbour quantization technique. This work focuses on improving the quantization efficiency in pretrained and quantized models. This approach has the potential to improve the final accuracy of quantized models. The main postulate of the work is that final quantization states of the network based on nearest neighbour rounding does not guarantee optimal accuracy. In the presented work, the evolution strategy is used as an optimization approach. The evolution in each iteration changes the values of the small percentage of weights. It shifts theirs values to different quantization states. The work shows that proposed evolution with an appropriate set of operators and parameters can fast improve the accuracy of the quantized models. The results are presented for popular architectures such as VGG and Resnet for image classification and detection. Additionally, simulations were carried out for the autoencoder architecture.

</details>


### [97] [Attribution-Guided Continual Learning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05285)
*Yazheng Liu,Yuxuan Wan,Rui Xu,Xi Zhang,Sihong Xie,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种归因引导的持续微调框架，通过估计Transformer层中任务特定的参数重要性分数来调制梯度，从而在保持旧任务性能的同时学习新任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在持续学习中常遭受灾难性遗忘：顺序学习新任务后，在早期任务上表现下降。现有方法（数据回放、参数冻结、正则化）缺乏对LLM内部知识分布的语义感知，无法区分应保留或更新的参数。

Method: 提出归因引导的持续微调框架。具体步骤：1) 估计每个Transformer层中任务特定的逐元素参数重要性；2) 利用这些重要性分数调制梯度；3) 对旧任务重要的参数给予较小更新，对新任务相关性低的参数保持可塑性。

Result: 在持续学习基准测试上的实验表明，该方法始终优于基线方法，在更好地保留旧任务性能的同时，保持了新任务上的竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过参数重要性感知的梯度调制，该框架有效缓解了LLM的灾难性遗忘问题，兼顾了旧知识的保留和新知识的学习。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often suffer from catastrophic forgetting in continual learning: after learning new tasks sequentially, they perform worse on earlier tasks. Existing methods mitigate catastrophic forgetting by data replay, parameter freezing, or regularization. However, these methods lack semantic awareness of internal knowledge distribution in LLMs. As a result, they cannot distinguish parameters that should be preserved or updated. We propose an attribution-guided continual fine-tuning framework for LLMs. Our method estimates task-specific, element-wise parameter importance in each Transformer layer and uses these scores to modulate gradients. Parameters important to previous tasks receive smaller updates, while less relevant ones remain plastic for learning new tasks. Experiments on continual learning benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms baselines, achieving better retention of old tasks while maintaining competitive performance on new tasks.

</details>


### [98] [Graph Normalization: Fast Binarizing Dynamics for Differentiable MWIS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05330)
*Laurent Guigues*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Graph Normalization (GN) is a new dynamical system that provides a differentiable, guaranteed-convergent approximation for NP-hard Maximum Weight Independent Set problems. By combining Majorization-Minimization optimization with evolutionary game theory, GN achieves near-optimal solutions on million-edge graphs in seconds, enabling applications in deep learning architectures and constrained optimization across AI, computer vision, and computational biology.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Maximum Weight Independent Set (MWIS) is NP-hard but encompasses critical combinatorial problems: optimal assignment, scheduling, set packing, and MAP inference. Existing methods like Belief Propagation lack guaranteed convergence guarantees, creating need for a differentiable approximation engine.

Method: GN is a dynamical system on graphs using exact Majorization-Minimization steps with quasi-Newton descent. It is proven equivalent to Replicator Dynamics of a nonlinear evolutionary game where vertices compete for independent set inclusion. For Assignment Problems, it acts as a generalized Sinkhorn algorithm.

Result: GN guarantees convergence to binary indicator of Maximum Independent Set. It establishes a weighted Motzkin-Straus theorem showing MISes are in bijection with local minima of a quadratic form. Following Fisher's Fundamental Theorem, average fitness equals MWIS objective and strictly increases. On real-world benchmarks with up to 1M edges, GN achieves solutions within 1% of best known results within seconds on CPU.

Conclusion: Graph Normalization (GN) provides a principled, differentiable approach to NP-hard MWIS problems. It guarantees convergence to Maximum Independent Set, enables applications in deep learning (sparse attention, pruning, MoE), and supports end-to-end learning in computer vision, computational biology, and resource allocation.

Abstract: We introduce Graph Normalization (GN), a principled dynamical system on graphs that serves as a differentiable approximation engine for the NP-hard Maximum Weight Independent Set (MWIS) problem. MWIS encompasses many combinatorial challenges, including optimal assignment, scheduling, set packing, and MAP inference in discrete Markov Random Fields. Unlike Belief Propagation, we prove GN always converges to a binary indicator of a Maximum Independent Set. GN realizes a fast quasi-Newton descent through an exact Majorization-Minimization step, systematically improving the MWIS relaxed primal objective. We establish an equivalence between GN and the Replicator Dynamics of a nonlinear evolutionary game, where vertices compete for inclusion in an independent set. While a non-potential game, the GN game follows Fisher's Fundamental Theorem of Natural Selection, where the average fitness equals the MWIS primal objective and strictly increases. This connection leads to a weighted extension of the Motzkin-Straus theorem, showing MISes are in bijection with the local minima of a quadratic form over a tilted simplex. For the Assignment Problem, GN acts as a variant of the Sinkhorn algorithm that naturally converges to a hard assignment while generalizing to arbitrary constraint graphs. We demonstrate GN's performance as a fast binarization engine for the state-of-the-art Bregman-Sinkhorn relaxed MWIS solver. On real-world benchmarks with up to 1M edges, GN identifies solutions within 1% of the best known results in seconds on a CPU. GN opens new avenues for deep learning architectures requiring differentiable, "hard" decisions under constraints, with applications in structured sparse attention, dynamic network pruning, and Mixture-of-Experts. Beyond core AI, the GN framework enables end-to-end learning of constrained optimization in computer vision, computational biology, and resource allocation.

</details>


### [99] [Feature Starvation as Geometric Instability in Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05341)
*Faris Chaudhry,Keisuke Yano,Anthea Monod*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文针对大语言模型可解释性分析中的稀疏自编码器(SAE)存在的特征饥饿问题和收缩偏差，提出自适应弹性网SAE(AEN-SAEs)，通过结合$\ell_2$结构项和自适应$\ell_1$重加权实现完全可微的架构，理论上证明具有Lipschitz连续性和全局特征支撑恢复能力，实验表明可缓解特征饥饿且保持竞争力重建能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准$\ell_1$正则化SAE存在两大根本性问题：(1)特征饥饿(死神经元)，(2)收缩偏差。现有方法依赖计算昂贵的启发式重采样和不可微硬掩码方法。论文认为特征饥饿并非数据多样性不足的简单经验问题，而是过完备字典的优化-几何结构病理：$\ell_1$诱导的稀疏编码映射不稳定，与浅层摊销编码器根本性不对齐。

Method: 提出AEN-SAEs，结合两种机制：(1)$\ell_2$结构项——强制凸性和Lipschitz稳定性；(2)自适应$\ell_1$重加权——消除收缩偏差并抑制虚假特征。两者协同控制诱导多面体几何的曲率和交互结构，实现完全可微架构，无需辅助启发式方法。

Result: 理论上证明AEN-SAEs可获得Lipschitz连续的稀疏编码映射，并在温和假设下恢复全局特征支撑。实验上，在合成环境和真实LLM(Pythia 70M, Llama 3.1 8B)上验证了方法可缓解特征饥饿，同时保持具有竞争力的重建能力。

Conclusion: AEN-SAEs通过经典稀疏回归理论为SAE提供了更坚实的优化几何基础，证明了$\ell_1$正则化的问题源于其诱导的多面体几何不稳定，而非简单的超参数调节问题。该工作为LLM可解释性提供了更鲁棒的工具。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are used to disentangle the dense, polysemantic internal representations of large language models (LLMs) into interpretable, monosemantic concepts. However, standard $\ell_1$-regularized SAEs suffer from feature starvation (dead neurons) and shrinkage bias, often requiring computationally expensive heuristic resampling and nondifferentiable hard-masking methods to bypass these challenges. We argue that feature starvation is not merely an empirical artifact of poor data diversity, but a fundamental optimization-geometric pathology of overcomplete dictionaries: the $\ell_1$-induced sparse coding map is unstable and fundamentally misaligned with shallow, amortized encoders. To address this structural instability, we introduce adaptive elastic net SAEs (AEN-SAEs), a fully differentiable architecture grounded in classical sparse regression. AEN-SAEs combine an $\ell_2$ structural term that enforces strong convexity and Lipschitz stability with adaptive $\ell_1$ reweighting that eliminates shrinkage bias and suppresses spurious features, thereby jointly controlling the curvature and interaction structure of the induced polyhedral geometry. Theoretically, we show that AEN-SAEs yield a Lipschitz-continuous sparse coding map and recover the global feature support under mild assumptions. Empirically, across synthetic settings and LLMs (Pythia 70M, Llama 3.1 8B), AEN-SAEs mitigate feature starvation without auxiliary heuristics while maintaining competitive reconstruction abilities.

</details>


### [100] [Balancing Stability and Plasticity in Sequentially Trained Early-Exiting Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05358)
*Alaa Zniber,Ouassim Karrakchou,Mounir Ghogho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper tackles catastrophic interference in sequential training of early-exiting neural networks by applying continual learning techniques (EWC and LwF), enabling new exits to specialize while preserving previously learned knowledge, achieving better accuracy-speed trade-offs.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sequential training of early-exit networks causes catastrophic interference — newly added exits degrade the performance of previously trained classifiers, limiting the practical benefit of adaptive inference.

Method: Two strategies: (1) EWC-based weight protection for critical parameters important to previously trained exits; (2) LwF-based output distribution preservation to maintain earlier exits' knowledge. Both address the stability-plasticity trade-off from continual learning.

Result: Experimental validation on standard benchmarks demonstrates consistent improvements in early-exit performance, with higher accuracy over baseline sequential training and significant speedups at low computational budgets.

Conclusion: Proposed approaches consistently outperform existing sequential training methods for early-exit networks, achieving both higher accuracy and significant inference speedups under low computational budgets.

Abstract: Early-exiting neural networks enable adaptive inference by allowing inputs to exit at intermediate classifiers, reducing computation for easy samples while maintaining high accuracy. In practice, exits can be trained sequentially by incrementally adding them to a shared backbone; however, this sequential training can cause newly introduced exits to interfere with previously learned ones, degrading the performance of earlier classifiers. We address this problem by retaining the knowledge embedded in existing exits while allowing new ones to specialize. We propose two alternative approaches that operate at different levels of the model. The first constrains learning by protecting parameters that are important for previously trained exits, while the second preserves the output distributions of earlier exits as the network adapts. These alternatives directly reflect the stability-plasticity trade-off studied in continual learning. Accordingly, we leverage \textit{Elastic Weight Consolidation} to constrain critical weights and \textit{Learning without Forgetting} to preserve output distributions. Experiments on standard benchmarks show that our approaches consistently improve early-exit performance, achieving higher accuracy over existing sequential training methods and significant performance speedups at low computational budgets.

</details>


### [101] [SPADE: Faster Drug Discovery by Learning from Sparse Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05370)
*Rahul Nandakumar,Ben Fauber,Deepayan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPADE是一种高效的药物发现算法，通过创新的配体选择策略，仅需约40次测试即可发现10个高质量配体。在性能上超越深度学习和贝叶斯优化方法，样本效率提升7%-32%，且评分速度比竞争对手快10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物发现中候选配体的通过率极低（<5%），且需要能够处理尚无先验数据的新颖蛋白质。要求在尽可能少的测试中迭代选择并测试候选配体。

Method: 提出了名为SPADE的配体选择算法，通过仅约40次测试即可找到10个高质量配体。采用新颖的配体选择方法，结合学习稀疏数据的能力。

Result: SPADE在一对一比较中优于深度学习和贝叶斯优化方法，覆盖更多蛋白质，中位数提升达7%-32%的样本效率。评分候选药物的速度比最接近的竞争对手快10倍。

Conclusion: SPADE算法在药物发现中展现出卓越的性能，是处理新颖蛋白质（无先验数据）的高效选择方法。

Abstract: Drug discovery seeks molecules (ligands) that bind strongly and selectively to a target protein. However, fewer than 5% of candidate ligands pass the bar for even the early stages of drug discovery. Furthermore, we want methods that work for novel proteins for which we have no prior data. Starting from scratch, we have to iteratively select and test candidate ligands such that we find enough ligands of the desired quality in as few tests as possible. Our proposed algorithm, named SPADE, introduces a novel approach to ligand selection that requires only 40 tests on average to find 10 high-quality ligands. In one-vs-one comparisons, SPADE outperforms deep learning and Bayesian optimization methods on more proteins, achieving median improvements of 7%-32% in sample efficiency. SPADE is also 10x faster than its closest competitor at scoring candidate drugs. Dataset and code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SPADE_Fast_Drug_Discovery_by_Learning_from_Sparse_Data-F028/README.md

</details>


### [102] [Conditional Diffusion Under Linear Constraints: Langevin Mixing and Information-Theoretic Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05387)
*Ahmad Aghapour,Erhan Bayraktar,Asaf Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究扩散模型在线性逆问题（inpainting、超分辨率）中的零样本条件采样问题。通过score函数的法向-正交分解，发现现有投影方法仅保证测量一致性但未正确分配概率质量，提出误差上界与条件互信息相关的信息论框架，并设计了投影Langevin初始化+引导去噪的新方法，实验验证其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于投影的采样器仅通过修正观测分量来保证测量一致性，但这不足以确定可行集上概率质量的正确分布，导致条件样本存在偏差。

Method: 1) 提出score函数的法向-正切分解框架，将观测方向和切向方向分离分析；2) 证明了用无条件正切score替代条件score的误差上界与条件互信息相关；3) 将误差分解为初始化误差和路径score不匹配误差；4) 设计了投影Langevin初始化结合引导反向去噪的采样流程。

Result: 实验表明，所提方法在inpainting和super-resolution任务上优于强投影基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文通过正切-法向分解理论，为扩散模型在逆问题中的条件采样提供了信息论框架，证明了误差上界与观测-未观测分量间的条件互信息相关。基于此理论，提出了先进行投影Langevin初始化、再进行引导反向去噪的采样策略，在inpainting和超分辨率任务上显著优于现有投影方法。

Abstract: We study zero-shot conditional sampling with pretrained diffusion models for linear inverse problems, including inpainting and super-resolution. In these problems, the observation determines only part of the unknown signal. The remaining degrees of freedom must be sampled according to the correct conditional data distribution. Existing projection-based samplers enforce measurement consistency by correcting the observed component during reverse diffusion. However, measurement consistency alone does not determine how probability mass should be distributed along the feasible set, and this can lead to biased conditional samples.
  We analyze this issue through a normal--tangent decomposition of the score function. For Gaussian noising, the observed-direction score is exactly determined by the measurement; only the tangent conditional score is unknown. We prove that the error from replacing this score by the unconditional tangent score is upper bounded by a dimension-free conditional mutual information between observed and unobserved components. This gives an information-theoretic decomposition into initialization and pathwise score-mismatch errors. Motivated by the theory, we propose a projected-Langevin initialization followed by guided reverse denoising, which outperforms a strong projection-based baseline in inpainting and super-resolution experiments.

</details>


### [103] [Two-Stage Learned Decomposition for Scalable Routing on Multigraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05389)
*Filip Rydin,Morteza Haghir Chehreghani,Balázs Kulcsár*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NEPF方法解决多图中车辆路径问题的可扩展性瓶颈，通过策略分解和分层强化学习，在保持解质量的前提下大幅提升计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经方法处理车辆路径问题（VRP）局限于欧几里得设置或简单图；针对多图（并行边表示不同权衡选项）的方法存在严重的可扩展性问题。

Method: 提出节点-边策略因子化（NEPF）方法，将路由策略分解为节点排序阶段和边选择阶段。引入预编码边聚合方案和非自回归边选择架构，采用分层强化学习进行联合训练。

Result: 在六个VRP变体上的实验表明，NEPF在解决方案质量上达到或超越最先进水平，同时训练和推理速度显著提升。

Conclusion: NEPF通过将路由策略分解为节点排序和边选择两个阶段，有效解决了多图VRP中的可扩展性问题。该方法在保持竞争性解决方案质量的同时，显著提升了训练和推理速度。

Abstract: Most neural methods for Vehicle Routing Problems (VRPs) are limited to Euclidean settings or simple graphs. In this work, we instead consider multigraphs, where parallel edges represent distinct travel options with varying trade-offs (e.g., distance vs time). Few methods are designed for such formulations and those that do exist face major scalability issues. We mitigate these scalability issues via a Node-Edge Policy Factorization (NEPF) approach, which splits the routing policy into a node permutation stage and an edge selection stage. To enable the decomposition, we introduce a pre-encoding edge aggregation scheme and a non-autoregressive architecture for the edge stage, as well as a hierarchical reinforcement learning method to train the stages jointly. Our experiments across six VRP variants demonstrate that NEPF matches or outperforms the state-of-the-art in terms of solution quality, while being significantly faster in training and inference.

</details>


### [104] [Differentiable Parameter Optimization for DAEs with State-Dependent Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05395)
*Ion Matei,Maksym Zhenirovskyy,Anthony Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带状态依赖事件的分流代数方程(DAEs)的可微分参数优化问题，提出两种互补的梯度计算策略：自动微分穿越仿真法和离散伴随方法，并对比了两种方法在梯度解释、事件处理、实现复杂度和局部有效性方面的特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DAEs with state-dependent events广泛应用于受代数方程约束且存在模式切换、碰撞或状态重初始化等事件的系统。现有梯度学习方法面临三大挑战：(1)代数变量隐式定义；(2)事件时间依赖参数；(3)重置映射引入不连续性。这些挑战使得基于梯度的参数学习变得困难。

Method: 本文将学习问题建模为带DAE动力学、代数约束、监护方程和重置映射的约束最小二乘问题。方法一：自动微分穿越仿真法——在向量场内求解代数变量，利用隐函数定理求导，通过分段可微积分处理事件。方法二：显式离散伴随法——将前向仿真表示为事件分裂残差系统，通过求解光滑段和事件残差的拉格朗日乘子计算梯度。

Result: 本文澄清了伴随方法中的残差项本质上是等式约束而非启发式惩罚；两种方法均为前向仿真选择的事件路径提供梯度，且在固定事件排序和横向监护交叉条件下有效。

Conclusion: 两种方法在梯度解释、事件处理、实现复杂度和局部有效性方面具有互补特性，为DAE系统参数学习提供了理论统一的框架和实践指导。

Abstract: Differential-algebraic equations (DAEs) with state-dependent events arise in systems whose continuous dynamics are constrained by algebraic equations and interrupted by mode changes, switching logic, impacts, or state reinitializations. Gradient-based parameter learning for such systems is challenging because algebraic variables are implicitly defined, event times depend on the parameters, and reset maps introduce discontinuities. This paper studies differentiable parameter optimization for semi-explicit DAEs with events. We formulate the learning problem as a constrained least-squares problem with DAE dynamics, algebraic constraints, guard equations, and reset maps. We then develop two complementary gradient-computation strategies. The first is an automatic-differentiation-through-simulation method that solves algebraic variables inside the vector field, differentiates the algebraic solve using the implicit function theorem, and handles events through segmented differentiable integration. The second is an explicit discrete-adjoint method that represents the forward simulation as an event-split residual system and computes gradients by solving for the Lagrange multipliers of smooth-segment and event residuals. The formulation clarifies that residual terms in the adjoint method are equality constraints, not heuristic penalties. We compare the two approaches in terms of gradient interpretation, event-time handling, implementation complexity, and local validity. Both methods provide gradients for the event path selected by the forward simulation and are valid under fixed event ordering and transversal guard crossings.

</details>


### [105] [Information Theoretic Adversarial Training of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05415)
*Yiwei Zhang,Jeremiah Birrell,Reza Ebrahimi,Rouzbeh Behnia,Jason Pacheco,Elisa Bertino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WARDEN是一种针对LLM的分布式鲁棒对抗训练框架，通过f-散度模糊集动态重加权对抗样本，优化最坏情况损失，在保持模型效用的同时有效降低对抗攻击成功率，具备可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM即使经过对齐和安全训练，仍易受对抗提示攻击，现有对抗训练方法计算开销大且难以扩展，而基于梯度的连续对抗训练方法（CAT、CAPO）虽更高效，但缺乏对训练分布不确定性的显式建模。

Method: 提出WARDEN框架，通过f-散度模糊集围绕经验训练分布动态重加权对抗样本，优化经验分布散度球内的最坏情况对抗损失；在KL散度下利用凸对偶公式，目标简化为log-sum-exp形式，通过动力学参数控制重加权强度。

Result: 在多个LLM和攻击设置下，WARDEN显著降低攻击成功率，计算成本和模型效用与CAT、CAPO、MixAT基线方法相当。

Conclusion: WARDEN通过信息论优化目标，在多个LLM和攻击设置下显著降低攻击成功率，同时计算成本和模型效用与现有基线方法相当，是一种可扩展的鲁棒对齐实用方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to adversarial prompting despite advances in alignment and safety, often exhibiting harmful behaviors under novel attack strategies. While adversarial training can improve robustness, existing approaches are computationally expensive and difficult to scale. Recent continuous adversarial training methods, such as Continuous adversarial training (CAT) and Continuous Adversarial Preference Optimization (CAPO), address this challenge by leveraging gradient-based perturbations in the embedding space, enabling more efficient and expressive attacks. Building on this paradigm, we propose WARDEN, a distributionally robust adversarial training framework for LLMs that dynamically reweights adversarial examples through an f -divergence ambiguity set around the empirical training distribution. Our method optimizes the worst-case adversarial loss within a divergence ball around the empirical data distribution, automatically emphasizing harder adversarial examples. Using the convex dual formulation, the objective reduces to a log-sum-exp form under the KL divergence, with a dynamical parameter controlling the strength of reweighting. This study leads to a new class of information-theoretic objectives that significantly reduce attack success rates while maintaining model utility. Across multiple LLMs and attack settings, WARDEN substantially reduces attack success rates with computational and utility costs comparable to CAT-, CAPO-, and MixAT-based baselines, making it a practical approach for scalable robust alignment.

</details>


### [106] [Active Learning for Conditional Generative Compressed Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05435)
*Alexander DeLise,Nick Dexter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于条件生成模型（如Stable Diffusion）的压缩感知问题，分析了提示词（prompt）在采样分布设计和恢复模型定义中的双重作用。对于ReLU和Lipschitz条件生成器，证明了提示词匹配的Christoffel采样可保持近优复杂度，而提示词错配会产生显式的兼容性惩罚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成压缩感知利用预训练生成器的值域作为结构化信号恢复的非线性模型，但现有方法未充分考虑条件信息对采样和恢复的双重影响。如何系统性地利用文本提示词同时优化测量采样分布和恢复模型仍是一个开放问题。

Method: 提出条件生成压缩感知框架，将提示词的作用解耦为两部分：(1) 用于设计采样分布的提示词；(2) 用于定义恢复模型的提示词。基于Christoffel测量矩阵理论，推导了ReLU和Lipschitz条件生成器的稳定恢复界。

Result: 理论证明：(1) 提示词匹配的Christoffel采样保持与现有近优生成压缩感知理论相同的Christoffel复杂度常数；(2) 提示词错配会产生显式的兼容性惩罚（compatibility penalty）。实验表明Stable Diffusion的提示词能够有意义地重塑Christoffel采样分布。

Conclusion: 提示词应被视为设计变量，其对感知、近似和恢复具有不同的影响。该框架为条件生成压缩感知提供了理论基础，表明通过精心设计提示词可优化压缩成像系统的性能。

Abstract: Generative compressed sensing uses the range of a pretrained generator as a nonlinear model for recovering structured signals from limited measurements. We study a conditional version of this problem for image recovery from subsampled Fourier measurements using prompt-conditioned generative models. Our framework separates two roles of conditioning: the prompt used to design the sampling distribution and the prompt used to define the recovery model. For ReLU and Lipschitz conditional generators, we prove stable recovery bounds showing that prompt-matched Christoffel sampling retains the same Christoffel complexity constant as existing near-optimal generative compressed sensing theory, while prompt mismatch incurs an explicit compatibility penalty. Experiments with Stable Diffusion show that prompts meaningfully reshape Christoffel sampling distributions and influence image recovery. Overall, our results suggest that prompts should be treated as design variables with distinct effects on sensing, approximation, and recovery.

</details>


### [107] [On Semantic Loss Fine-Tuning Approach for Preventing Model Collapse in Causal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05438)
*Pratik Deshmukh,Atirek Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 标准微调导致因果推理模型崩溃，本文提出语义损失函数解决此问题，证明语义约束对 transformer 稳定因果推理至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 模型在因果推理任务上进行标准微调会导致灾难性模型崩溃，模型学到"总是预测 Yes 或 No"的捷径解，而非真正的因果推理能力。

Method: 提出基于图约束的语义损失函数及动态 λ 调度策略，在 200,000+ 评估样本、五个模型变体上验证，包括 1,000 个对抗性结构推理样本。

Result: 语义损失方法在传递性任务达 70.4%、d-分离任务达 68.6% 准确率（比崩溃基线提升 42.7%），对抗评估中保持 67-70% 而崩溃模型降至 43-71%。

Conclusion: 语义损失函数对于 transformer 模型的稳定因果推理是必要而非可选的，标准微调方法会导致模型崩溃。
