*Zegu Zhang,Jianhua Peng,Jian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个可测量的"后验坍塌"诊断方法，通过定义输入无关的常数坍塌这一具体失败模式，给出了精确的认证边界。核心发现是：对于任意固定的非平凡教师分布，最佳常数学生是数据集平均教师分布，其对齐损失等于教师互信息 I_T(X;T)。通过设计一个仅使用潜在变量的"原始见证"(raw witness)，若其对齐损失低于此阈值（带安全边际），则可证明见证者不可能是常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分自编码器(VAE)中的后验坍塌问题传统上只能通过症状来诊断（如KL项过小、 decoder过强、潜在编码利用不足），这些信号是定性的，无法定义坍塌边界。论文希望将定性的失败模式转化为可测量的认证标准。

Method: 1. 定义具体失败模式为"输入无关的常数坍塌"；2. 对任意固定非平凡教师分布 T(·|x)，推导最佳常数学生为数据集平均教师分布，其对齐成本为教师互信息 I_T(X;T)；3. 设计仅用潜在变量的"原始见证"，若其对齐损失低于 I_T(X;T) 且有安全边际，则该见证不可能是常数；4. 在CIFAR-100和Tiny-ImageNet-200上进行每种子教师搜索实验。

Result: 1. 全训练保持在认证边界的安全侧；2. 移除对齐驱动后，原始见证进入常数学生区域；3. 从坍塌检查点恢复对齐可重新获得认证；4. 标准VAE基线方法（包括保留重构质量或后验可预测性的方法）在原始见证证书下仍为阴性。

Conclusion: 该方法将后验坍塌从定性症状转化为可测量的认证问题。保证范围是有意狭窄的：仅认证匹配的教师相对变化通过潜在路径传递，而非声称排除所有形式的后验坍塌。

Abstract: Posterior collapse in variational autoencoders is often diagnosed by its symptoms: a small KL term, a strong decoder, or weak use of the latent code. These signals are useful, but they do not define a collapse boundary. We study a concrete failure mode, input-independent constant collapse, and show that this case admits an exact threshold. For any fixed nonconstant teacher distribution \(T(\cdot\mid x)\), the best constant student is the dataset-average teacher distribution, and its alignment cost is the teacher mutual information \(I_T(X;T)\). Therefore, if a strictly latent-only raw witness achieves alignment loss below this value, with a safety margin, the witness cannot be constant in the input.
  This identity turns a qualitative failure mode into a measurable one. In CIFAR-100 experiments with per-seed teacher search, full training stays on the certified side of the boundary, removing alignment drives the raw witness into the constant-student regime, and restarting from a collapsed checkpoint with alignment enabled restores the certificate. Tiny-ImageNet-200 fixed-target runs show the same prevention--collapse--rescue pattern across three independently searched teachers. Standard VAE-style baselines, including methods that preserve reconstruction quality or post-hoc predictability, remain negative under the raw certificate. The guarantee is intentionally narrow: it certifies that the matched nonconstant teacher-relative variation passes through the latent pathway, rather than claiming that all forms of posterior collapse have been ruled out.

</details>


### [143] [MDN: Parallelizing Stepwise Momentum for Delta Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05838)
*Yulong Huang,Xiang Liu,Hongxiang Huang,Xiaopeng Lin,Zunchang Liu,Xiaowen Chu,Zeke Xie,Bojun Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Momentum DeltaNet (MDN)，通过几何重排序的分块并行动量规则和基于动力系统分析的门控约束设计，解决了线性注意力中朴素SGD的信息衰减问题，在长序列建模任务上实现效率和效果的双重提升，实验证明其在大规模语言模型上的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性注意力模型（如Mamba2、GDN）将线性递归解释为闭式在线SGD，但朴素SGD更新面临快速信息衰减和优化收敛次优的问题，而基于动量的优化器在训练效率和有效性之间存在权衡困境。

Method: 1) 设计分块并行算法，通过几何重排序更新系数实现逐步动量规则；2) 从动力系统角度分析动量递推为二阶系统，揭示复共轭特征值的特性；3) 基于分析结果设计稳定的门控约束机制。

Result: 在400M和1.3B参数规模下，MDN在包括Transformer、Mamba2和GDN在内的多个强基线模型上展现出持续的 benchmark 性能提升，同时保持可比的训练吞吐量。

Conclusion: MDN通过动量优化和稳定门控约束设计，在保持训练效率的同时显著提升性能，为长序列语言建模提供了一种高效且效果优异的新范式。

Abstract: Linear Attention (LA) offers a promising paradigm for scaling large language models (LLMs) to long sequences by avoiding the quadratic complexity of self-attention. Recent LA models such as Mamba2 and GDN interpret linear recurrences as closed-form online stochastic gradient descent (SGD), but naive SGD updates suffer from rapid information decay and suboptimal convergence in optimization. While momentum-based optimizers provide a natural remedy, they pose challenges in simultaneously achieving training efficiency and effectiveness. To address this, we develop a chunkwise parallel algorithm for LA with a stepwise momentum rule by geometrically reordering the update coefficients. Further, from a dynamical systems perspective, we analyze the momentum-based recurrence as a second-order system that introduces complex conjugate eigenvalues. This analysis guides the design of stable gating constraints. The resulting model, Momentum DeltaNet (MDN), leverages Triton kernels to achieve comparable training throughput with competitive linear models such as Mamba2 and KDA. Extensive experiments on the 400M and 1.3B parameter models demonstrate consistent performance improvements over strong baselines, including Transformers, Mamba2 and GDN, across diverse downstream evaluation benchmarks. Code: https://github.com/HuuYuLong/MomentumDeltaNet .

</details>


### [144] [Hypothesis generation and updating in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05851)
*Hua-Dong Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过数字游戏范式评估LLM的假设推理能力，发现LLM虽然接近贝叶斯最优但存在系统性偏差——默认偏好窄假设、评估与生成能力不一致、泛化能力有限。这些发现揭示了LLM作为通用问题解决工具的局限性，尤其在需要超越数据的科学推理场景中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被广泛用于问题解决，但对其如何从部分描述生成和更新假设、是否接近最优推理的理解仍然不足。

Method: 在"数字游戏"环境中测试LLM，使用三种探测方法（后验预测、假设评估、假设生成）测量假设后验分布，并与最优贝叶斯模型和人类行为进行比较。

Result: LLM行为可用双参数贝叶斯模型拟合但存在系统性偏移：默认状态下表现出强采样假设产生隐式奥卡姆剃刀（偏好窄假设）；思考模式转向更强的先验依赖；存在评估-生成差距（评估时选择正确假设但生成更简单规则的假设）；且该模式不可外推，模型可能在未覆盖区域泛化很差。

Conclusion: LLMs表现出系统性偏差，不适合作为通用问题解决工具。特别是在需要超越观测数据进行推理的科学推理场景中能力受限。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly help people solve problems, from debugging code to repairing machinery. This process requires generating plausible hypotheses from partial descriptions, then updating them as more information arrives. Yet how LLMs perform this form of inference, and how close it is to optimal, remains unclear. We study this question in the number game, a controlled setting in which a learner infers the hypothesis supported by a few positive integers, such as $\{16, 8, 2, 64\}$: a rule like powers of 2 or an interval like numbers near 20. We measure the posterior over hypotheses using three complementary probes: posterior prediction, hypothesis evaluation, and hypothesis generation. We then compare LLM behavior with an optimal Bayesian model and human behavior, and test whether the same posterior is expressed across probes. LLMs are often well described by a two-parameter Bayesian fit, but with systematic offsets: by default they show a strong-sampling assumption that creates an implicit Occam's razor, favoring narrower hypotheses, while thinking mode shifts them toward greater prior reliance. We also find a robust evaluation--generation gap: LLMs select more correct hypotheses during hypothesis evaluation but generate simpler, more rule-like hypotheses. Finally, this Bayesian-with-bias pattern does not extrapolate. Models can behave as if they hold rule-like hypotheses over observed examples, yet generalize poorly to parts of the hypothesis domain not covered by those examples. Our results highlight a limitation of LLMs as general problem solvers, especially for scientific inference, where hypotheses must go beyond the data.

</details>


### [145] [Measuring Learning Progress via Gradient-Momentum Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05856)
*Samuel Blad,Martin Längkvist,Amy Loutfi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: The paper proposes Gradient-Momentum Coupling (GMC), an intrinsic motivation signal derived from optimization dynamics that identifies useful learning samples by measuring gradient-momentum alignment. Unlike prediction error-based methods, GMC naturally filters noise through momentum dynamics and produces emergent curriculum learning by prioritizing samples based on learning speed rather than task difficulty.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing learning progress signals (e.g., prediction error) cannot effectively distinguish meaningful, learnable patterns from random noise in curiosity-driven exploration for reinforcement learning.

Method: Gradient-Momentum Coupling (GMC) measures sample utility via per-parameter normalized absolute product between current gradients and momentum from previous gradients, leveraging momentum's inherent noise-filtering properties.

Result: GMC demonstrates superior noise robustness and emergent curriculum learning behavior; replacing prediction error with GMC in existing curiosity architectures improves robustness to observation noise on MiniGrid environments.

Conclusion: GMC provides a more robust alternative to prediction error for curiosity-driven exploration, demonstrating noise resilience and emergent curriculum properties that prioritize samples by learning speed rather than difficulty.

Abstract: Measuring learning progress is essential for curiosity-driven exploration in reinforcement learning, but widely used signals such as prediction error often fail to distinguish meaningful, learnable patterns from random noise. This paper proposes Gradient-Momentum Coupling (GMC), a signal derived from optimization dynamics that quantifies how useful each sample's gradient is for ongoing learning by measuring its per-parameter normalized absolute product with the momentum from previous gradients. By leveraging momentum's natural filtering of noise and oscillations, GMC identifies samples that contribute to ongoing parameter updates. Controlled experiments demonstrate noise robustness and emergent curriculum learning, with the signal prioritizing tasks by learning speed rather than difficulty. Experiments on MiniGrid suggest that replacing prediction error with GMC within existing curiosity-driven architectures can improve robustness to observation noise.

</details>


### [146] [Offline Reinforcement Learning for Rotation Profile Control in Tokamaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05857)
*Rohit Sonker,Hiro Josep Farre Kaga,Jiayu Chen,Andrew Rothstein,Ian Char,Ricardo Shousha,Egemen Kolemen,Jeff Schneider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究使用离线强化学习（RL）和离线模型预测控制（model-based RL）方法来控制托卡马克装置中的等离子体旋转分布。通过仅使用DIII-D托卡马克的历史数据训练，在实际设备上部署后取得了良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 托卡马克装置中等离子体旋转分布的控制是一个重要但极具挑战性的问题。该分布直接影响装置的稳定性、约束性能和输运特性。传统控制方法难以应对其高维度、多执行器耦合以及对等离子体状态敏感等特点。

Method: 采用离线RL和离线model-based RL算法；使用等离子体动力学的概率模型生成rollouts用于RL训练；完全基于DIII-D托卡马克的历史数据。

Result: 成功在DIII-D托卡马克上部署RL策略，观察到有前景的真实世界控制效果。

Conclusion: 验证了使用有限历史数据在复杂物理设备上训练和部署RL策略的可行性，同时总结了其中的关键挑战和经验教训。

Abstract: Tokamaks remain leading candidates for achieving practical fusion energy, yet many important control problems inside these devices are still difficult or unsolved. One such challenge is controlling the plasma rotation profile, which strongly influences stability, confinement, and transport. While the average rotation can be controlled, controlling the full profile is challenging due to high dimensionality, response to multiple actuators and dependence on plasma condition. Learning-based control methods, such as reinforcement learning (RL), provide a potential solution to this challenging problem with ability to model complex interactions leading to effective multi-input multi-output control. However, learning such policies is challenging due to the lack of accurate simulators that can model the rotation profile dynamics. In this work, we investigate the use of offline RL and offline model-based RL algorithms for rotation profile control, training them solely on historical data from the DIII-D tokamak. Our final method uses probabilistic models of plasma dynamics to generate rollouts for RL training. We deploy this policy on the DIII-D Tokamak and observe promising real-world results. We conclude by highlighting key challenges and insights from training and deploying an RL policy on a complex physical device while using only limited past data.

</details>


### [147] [SOPE: Stabilizing Off-Policy Evaluation for Online RL with Prior Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05863)
*Carlo Romeo,Girolamo Macaluso,Alessandro Sestini,Andrew D. Bagdanov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SOPE proposes an adaptive early-stopping mechanism for offline reinforcement learning using actor-aligned Off-Policy Policy Evaluation (OPE) signals to dynamically control offline training phase lengths, eliminating manual hyperparameter tuning.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Incorporating prior data into online RL accelerates training but creates a trade-off between high computational costs and complex multi-stage pipelines. Fixed-length stabilization phases, while computationally efficient, require task-dependent manual tuning and risk wasting prior knowledge or causing overfitting.

Method: SOPE uses an actor-aligned OPE signal as an automated early-stopping mechanism. By evaluating the critic on a held-out validation split under the current policy's action distribution, SOPE dynamically halts gradient updates exactly when out-of-distribution benefits saturate.

Result: On 25 continuous control tasks from the Minari benchmark suite, SOPE improves baseline performance by up to 45.6% while reducing required TFLOPs by up to 22x, effectively balancing sample and computational efficiency.

Conclusion: Adaptive, evaluation-driven update schedules are more effective than static, exhaustive update schedules for offline RL training with prior data.

Abstract: Incorporating prior data into online reinforcement learning accelerates training but typically forces a difficult trade-off between high computational costs and long, multi-stage training pipelines. While fixed-length stabilization phases are significantly more computationally efficient than static update schedules, they require task-dependent manual tuning, risking either the waste of prior knowledge or severe overfitting. To address this, we propose SOPE, an algorithm that uses an actor-aligned Off-Policy Policy Evaluation (OPE) signal as an automated early-stopping mechanism to dynamically control the length of offline training phases. By evaluating the critic on a held-out validation split under the current policy's action distribution, SOPE halts gradient updates exactly when out-of-distribution benefits saturate, eliminating the need for manual schedule tuning. Evaluated on 25 continuous control tasks from the Minari benchmark suite, SOPE improves baseline performance by up to 45.6% while reducing the required TFLOPs by up to 22x, thus balancing the tradeoff between sample and computational efficiency. These findings demonstrate that adaptive, evaluation-driven update schedules are more effective than relying on static, exhaustive update schedules.

</details>


### [148] [QuadraSHAP: Stable and Scalable Shapley Values for Product Games via Gauss-Legendre Quadrature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05870)
*Majid Mohammadi,Grigory Reznikov,Pavel Sinitcyn,Krikamol Muandet,Siu Lun Chau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了乘积博弈（product games）中Shapley值的高效计算问题。乘积博弈是指联盟价值可分解为各参与者项的乘积的合作博弈，在机器学习可解释性中广泛应用。通过将指数级特征联盟的加权和坍缩为[0,1]区间上d-1次多项式的积分，作者提出了基于Gauss-Legendre求积的精确近似方案，并设计了名为QuadraSHAP的高效并行实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shapley值是合作博弈论中最重要的公平分配准则之一，在机器学习可解释性领域用于特征重要性评估。然而其计算复杂度为指数级，当特征数量大时计算不可行。现有方法在数值稳定性或效率上存在不足，需要新的理论突破和实用算法。

Method: 1) 理论推导：证明每个参与者的Shapley值可精确表示为[0,1]区间上d-1次多项式的一维积分；2) 数值方法：采用Gauss-Legendre求积方案，节点数m_q ≥ ⌈d/2⌉时保证精确，否则提供几何收敛的近似；3) 工程实现：log空间求值确保数值稳定性，关联扫描原语实现并行计算。

Result: 理论保证：当m_q ≥ ⌈d/2⌉时求积精确；其他情况下误差按几何速率衰减。实践验证：数百个节点即可在数千特征规模下获得高精度估计。效率：总工作量O(d·m_q)，并行时间O(log d)，是所有数值稳定方法中最快的。

Conclusion: QuadraSHAP通过积分表示理论突破和高效并行实现，解决了大规模特征场景下Shapley值计算的效率与稳定性难题，为可解释机器学习提供了实用工具。

Abstract: We study the efficient computation of Shapley values for \emph{product games} -- cooperative games in which the coalition value factorizes as a product of per-player terms. Such games arise in machine learning explainability whenever the value function inherits a multiplicative structure from the underlying model, as in kernel methods with product kernels and tree-based models. Our key result is that the Shapley value of each player in a product game admits an exact one-dimensional integral representation: the weighted sum over exponentially many feature coalitions collapses to the integral of a degree-$(d-1)$ polynomial over $[0,1]$, where $d$ is the total number of features. This yields a Gauss--Legendre quadrature scheme that is \emph{provably exact} whenever the number of nodes satisfies $m_q \geq \lceil d/2 \rceil$, and otherwise provides a \emph{near-exact} approximation with error provably decaying geometrically in $m_q$. In practice, a few hundred nodes can achieve highly precise estimates even with thousands of features. Building on this formulation, we derive a numerically stable implementation via log-space evaluation, together with an efficient parallel implementation based on associative scan primitives that achieves $O(d\,m_q)$ total work and $O(\log d)$ parallel time. Experiments show that \textsc{QuadraSHAP} is the fastest numerically stable method across all tested configurations.

</details>


### [149] [Retain-Neutral Surrogates for Min-Max Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05871)
*Junhao Cai,Dohun Kim,Dowon Kim,Sung Il Choi,Chengjun Jin,Juhyun Park,Changhee Joo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Machine unlearning seeks to remove the influence of designated training data while preserving performance on the remaining data. Approximate unlearning can be viewed as a local editing problem; in min-max unlearning, the key local object is the surrogate point at which the retain objective is evaluated. When forget and retain gradients are strongly aligned, an unconstrained forget-maximizing perturbation can move to a surrogate point that increases retain loss. We propose Retain-Orthogonal Surrogate Unlearning (ROSU), which constrains the inner surrogate construction by maximizing first-order forget gain subject to zero first-order retain change under a fixed perturbation budget. This yields a closed-form retain-orthogonal perturbation, a lightweight transported outer update, and amplification along the retain-neutral direction. Our analysis establishes (i) a curvature-controlled second-order bound on retain damage, (ii) a positive-alignment regime in which ROSU strictly reduces surrogate retain loss relative to standard min-max perturbations, and (iii) near-equivalence when the two gradients are nearly orthogonal. Across vision and language benchmarks (CIFAR-10/100, Tiny-ImageNet, TOFU, WMDP), the empirical pattern follows this geometry: ROSU gives its clearest gains in high-coupling regimes while remaining competitive elsewhere.

</details>


### [150] [RepFlow: Representation Enhanced Flow Matching for Causal Effect Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05890)
*Yifei Xie,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RepFlow提出了一个结合表示学习和条件流匹配的新型框架，通过最小化处理组和对照组表示之间的熵正则化Wasserstein距离来缓解选择偏差，实现了对潜在结果分布的精确建模，在各类基准数据集上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Estimating causal effects from observational data is increasingly critical in healthcare, economics, and social policy. The fundamental challenge arises from missing counterfactuals and selection bias. Existing methods are largely limited to point estimates and lack the capacity for distribution modeling.

Method: RepFlow formulates causal effect estimation as a joint optimization problem integrating representation learning with Conditional Flow Matching (CFM). It mitigates selection bias by minimizing the entropically regularized Wasserstein distance between treated and control representations, and introduces an L2 normalization constraint on latent representations for numerical stability.

Result: The balanced representation enables the flow model to accurately capture the distribution of potential outcomes.

Conclusion: RepFlow consistently outperforms existing methods in both point and distributional causal effect estimation across a wide range of benchmarks.

Abstract: Estimating causal effects from observational data has become increasingly critical in diverse fields including healthcare, economics, and social policy. The fundamental challenge in causal inference arises from the missing counterfactuals and the selection bias. Existing methods are largely limited to point estimates and lack the capacity for distribution modeling. In this work, we propose RepFlow, a novel framework that formulates causal effect estimation as a joint optimization problem integrating representation learning with Conditional Flow Matching (CFM).
  RepFlow mitigates selection bias by minimizing the entropically regularized Wasserstein distance between treated and control representations.
  To enhance numerical stability, we further introduce an $L_2$ normalization constraint on latent representations.
  This balanced representation enables the flow model to accurately capture the distribution of potential outcomes. Extensive experiments across a wide range of benchmarks demonstrate that RepFlow consistently outperforms existing methods in both point and distributional causal effect estimation.

</details>


### [151] [Near-Policy: Accelerating On-Policy Distillation via Asynchronous Generation and Selective Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05940)
*Miao Rang,Zhenni Bi,Hang Zhou,Kai Han,Xuechun Wang,An Xiao,Xinghao Chen,Yunhe Wang,Hanting Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NPD（Near-Policy Distillation）框架，通过异步设计将学生模型生成与训练解耦，结合稀疏更新和Δ-IFD过滤机制，在避免策略漂移的同时实现8.1倍加速。最终模型性能显著提升，超越规模更大的竞品模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准知识蒸馏方法在自回归模型上存在分布不匹配问题，而现有的on-policy方法虽能缓解该问题，但依赖计算密集的强化学习框架，导致效率低下。因此需要一种能够在保持近策略优化效果的同时显著降低计算成本的新方法。

Method: 提出Near-Policy Distillation (NPD)框架，采用异步策略将学生模型的生成过程与训练过程解耦，从而支持使用序列打包（sequence packing）的监督微调（SFT）。为应对异步更新带来的策略滞后和样本噪声问题，NPD集成了稀疏学生更新策略和Δ-IFD过滤机制。Δ-IFD通过启发式方法筛选极端分布外样本，将梯度更新的范围约束在安全的近邻学习区域内，防止噪声主导梯度方向。

Result: 实验表明NPD相比on-policy基线方法实现了8.1倍的加速，比标准SFT方法提升8.09%的性能。更进一步，使用NPD预训练的openPangu-Embedded-1B模型在后续RL阶段达到68.73%的最新最优分数，性能超过参数量更大的Qwen3-1.7B模型。

Conclusion: NPD通过异步近策略框架有效平衡了训练效率和优化稳定性，在保持接近on-policy效果的同时显著降低了计算成本。其最大的贡献在于通过Δ-IFD过滤机制将探索空间限制在安全的学习区域内，既避免了off-policy方法的样本偏差问题，又规避了on-policy方法的高计算开销。该框架为小型学生模型的蒸馏训练提供了高效且稳定的解决方案，并在最终RL探索阶段展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Standard knowledge distillation for autoregressive models often suffers from distribution mismatch. While on-policy methods mitigate this by leveraging student-generated outputs, they rely on computationally expensive Reinforcement Learning (RL) frameworks. To improve efficiency, we propose Near-Policy Distillation (NPD), an asynchronous approach that decouples student generation from training. This reformulation enables Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) with sequence packing. However, asynchronous updates inevitably introduce policy lag and sample noise, which can cause the behavior to drift from near-policy toward off-policy. To counteract this without sacrificing efficiency, NPD integrates sparse student updates and the $Δ$-IFD filtering mechanism, a heuristic sample selection mechanism that empirically stabilizes the optimization trajectory. By filtering extreme out-of-distribution samples, $Δ$-IFD prevents noise from dominating the gradients, ensuring updates remain within a safe proximal learning zone. Empirically, the NPD framework achieves a 8.1x speedup over on-policy baselines and outperforms SFT by 8.09%. Crucially, by effectively narrowing the exploration space for subsequent RL, our method enables openPangu-Embedded-1B to reach a state-of-the-art score of 68.73%, outperforming the substantially larger Qwen3-1.7B. Codes will be released soon.

</details>


### [152] [VARS-FL: Validation-Aligned Client Selection for Non-IID Federated Learning in IoT Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05896)
*Mohamed Lakas,Mohamed Amine Ferrag*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VARS-FL框架，通过服务器端验证损失降低量量化客户端贡献，结合滑动窗口平均贡献与对数尺度参与项构建声誉评分，实现历史感知的客户端选择。在Edge-IIoTset数据集（100客户端、15类非IID物联网入侵检测）上验证，相比FedAvg、Oort和Power-of-Choice基线，VARS-FL在准确率、F1-Macro和损失指标上均表现更优，达到80%准确率所需轮次减少至多36%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current FL systems employ stateless client selection, treating each communication round independently and ignoring accumulated evidence of client contribution quality. Under non-IID data distribution, this leads to slow convergence and unstable training. The problem is especially pronounced in IoT/IIoT environments where data is highly heterogeneous and local proxies (e.g., training loss) are misaligned with the global optimization objective.

Method: VARS-FL quantifies each client's contribution using reduction in server-side validation loss induced by its update. Per-round signals are aggregated into a Reputation score combining: (1) sliding-window average of recent contributions, and (2) logarithmically scaled participation term. This enables robust exploration-exploitation selection without requiring changes to local training or aggregation protocols.

Result: VARS-FL consistently outperforms baselines (FedAvg, Oort, Power-of-Choice) in accuracy, F1-Macro, and loss metrics on a 15-class non-IID IoT intrusion detection task (Edge-IIoTset dataset, 100 clients). Convergence acceleration of up to 36% fewer rounds to reach 80% accuracy was achieved across multiple experimental seeds.

Conclusion: Validation-aligned, history-aware client selection provides a more reliable and efficient training process for FL in heterogeneous IoT environments. VARS-FL achieves up to 36% fewer rounds to reach target accuracy while maintaining full compatibility with standard FedAvg.

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) systems typically employ stateless client selection, treating each communication round independently and ignoring accumulated evidence of client contribution quality. Under non-IID data, this leads to slow convergence and unstable training, particularly when selection relies on local proxies (e.g., training loss) that are misaligned with the global optimization objective. These challenges are especially pronounced in Internet of Things (IoT) and Industrial IoT (IIoT) environments, where data is highly heterogeneous and distributed across devices observing different traffic patterns. In this paper, we propose VARS-FL (Validation-Aligned Reputation Scoring for Federated Learning), a client selection framework that quantifies each client's contribution using the reduction in server-side validation loss induced by its update. These per-round signals are aggregated into a Reputation score that combines a sliding-window average of recent contributions with a logarithmically scaled participation term, enabling robust exploration-exploitation selection. VARS-FL requires no changes to local training or aggregation and remains fully compatible with standard FedAvg. We evaluate VARS-FL on a 15-class non-IID IoT intrusion detection task using the Edge-IIoTset dataset, with 100 clients across multiple seeds, and compare it against FedAvg, Oort, and Power-of-Choice. VARS-FL consistently improves accuracy, F1-Macro, and loss, while accelerating convergence (up to 36% fewer rounds to reach 80% accuracy). These results demonstrate that validation-aligned, history-aware client selection provides a more reliable and efficient training process for federated learning in heterogeneous IoT environments.

</details>


### [153] [VisMMOE: Exploiting Visual-Expert Affinity for Efficient Visual-Language MoE Offloading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05899)
*Cheng Xu,Xiaofeng Hou,Jiacheng Liu,Chao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了VisMMoE系统，通过剪枝冗余视觉token来改善VL-MoE模型的内存卸载性能。核心发现是"视觉-专家亲和性"效应：token剪枝不仅减少计算量，还能使专家访问更加集中和稳定，从而产生更小、更可预测的专家工作集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉-语言混合专家模型（VL-MoE）虽提供强大多模态能力，但在内存受限平台上的高效部署仍具挑战。现有的MoE卸载系统主要面向文本工作负载设计，对视觉密集型输入效果不佳，因为大量视觉token导致更广泛且不可预测的专家访问模式。

Method: VisMMoE基于核心洞察——剪枝冗余视觉token可改善卸载性能（不仅减少计算，还能重塑专家需求）。系统结合三种技术：1）亲和性感知的token压缩；2）前瞻专家预测；3）缓存/流水线编排。通过这些技术提高专家局部性和预取效率。

Result: VisMMoE在代表性VL-MoE模型和基准测试上评估，相比现有最强基线，端到端推理性能提升最高达2.68倍和1.61倍，同时保持具有竞争力的准确率。

Conclusion: 通过利用视觉-专家亲和性效应，VisMMoE成功解决了VL-MoE在内存受限平台上的高效部署问题，为视觉密集型工作负载提供了有效的卸载策略。

Abstract: Large-scale vision-language mixture-of-experts (VL-MoE) models provide strong multimodal capability, but efficient deployment on memory-constrained platforms remains difficult. Existing MoE offloading systems are largely designed for text-centric workloads and become much less effective for visual-heavy inputs, where large numbers of visual tokens induce broader and less predictable expert accesses. We present VisMMoE, a VL-MoE offloading system built on a single systems insight: pruning redundant visual tokens can improve offloading not only by reducing computation, but also by reshaping expert demand. We refer to this effect as \textit{visual-expert affinity}: token pruning makes expert accesses more concentrated within layers and more stable across layers, producing a smaller and more predictable expert working set. Guided by this insight, VisMMoE combines affinity-aware token compression, lookahead expert prediction, and cache/pipeline orchestration to improve expert locality and prefetch effectiveness under tight memory budgets. We implement VisMMoE on multiple frameworks and evaluate it on representative VL-MoE models and benchmarks. VisMMoE improves end-to-end inference performance by up to 2.68x and 1.61x, respectively, over strong baselines for today's VL-MoE deployments while maintaining competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [154] [Quadratic Objective Perturbation: Curvature-Based Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05905)
*Daniel Cortild,Coralia Cartis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出二次目标扰动(QOP)方法，通过添加随机二次型扰动来实现差分隐私，与传统线性目标扰动(LOP)相比，QOP利用曲率而非梯度有界假设来控制敏感度，从而在更弱的假设下实现(ε, δ)-差分隐私，并支持近似解和高效的优化算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性目标扰动(LOP)方法需要梯度有界假设，排除了许多现代机器学习模型；需要一个替代机制，能够在更弱的假设下实现差分隐私，特别是在内插 regime 下。

Method: 将随机二次型扰动替代传统随机线性扰动；通过曲率( Hessian 特征值)而非梯度上界来控制敏感度；扩展分析以支持近似解；使用现代分裂算法求解。

Result: 在更弱假设下获得(ε, δ)-差分隐私；提供经验 excess risk 的效用保证；理论与数值实验表明QOP优于LOP。

Conclusion: 曲率基扰动是比梯度基扰动更通用的差分隐私机制，能扩展隐私优化到更广泛的损失函数类，并保持计算效率。

Abstract: Objective perturbation is a standard mechanism in differentially private empirical risk minimization. In particular, Linear Objective Perturbation (LOP) enforces privacy by adding a random linear term, while strong convexity and stability are ensured by an additional deterministic quadratic term. However, this approach requires the strong assumption of bounded gradients of the loss function, which excludes many modern machine learning models. In this work, we introduce Quadratic Objective Perturbation (QOP), which perturbs the objective with a random quadratic form. This perturbation induces strong convexity and enforces stability of the problem through curvature, thereby enabling privacy and allowing sensitivity to be controlled through spectral properties of the perturbation rather than assumptions on the gradients. As a result, we obtain $(\varepsilon, δ)$-differential privacy under weaker assumptions, in the interpolation regime. Furthermore, we extend the analysis to account for approximate solutions, showing that privacy guarantees are preserved under inexact solves. Additionally, we derive utility guarantees in terms of empirical excess risk, and provide a theoretical and numerical comparison to LOP, highlighting the advantages of curvature-based perturbations. Finally, we discuss algorithmic aspects and show that the resulting problems can be solved efficiently using modern splitting schemes.

</details>


### [155] [From Drops to Grid: Noise-Aware Spatio-Temporal Neural Process for Rainfall Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05912)
*Rafael Pablos Sarabia,Joachim Nyborg,Morten Birk,Ira Assent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DropsToGrid是一种基于神经过程的降雨估计方法，通过融合私有气象站的时间序列数据和雷达的空间上下文信息，利用多尺度特征提取、时间注意力和多模态融合技术，生成高分辨率降雨图并量化不确定性，在多种场景下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统业务降雨观测系统存在偏差且分辨率较低，难以捕捉局部降雨的精细特征。现有深度学习方法在处理降雨的偏斜分布、局部性特征、噪声干扰以及时空融合方面存在局限，无法有效整合稀疏地表观测数据生成高精度降雨图。

Method: 该方法采用神经过程（Neural Process）框架，通过多尺度特征提取模块处理雷达的空间上下文信息，利用时间注意力机制融合来自稀疏、不规则分布且含噪声的私有气象站的时间序列数据，并结合多模态融合技术生成连续的概率性降雨估计输出。

Result: 实验结果表明，DropsToGrid在真实数据集上显著优于现有业务预报和深度学习基线方法，能够在仅有少量气象站以及跨区域场景下仍保持良好性能，生成准确的超高分辨率降雨图并提供良好校准的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: DropsToGrid是一个创新的降雨估计框架，它将神经过程方法应用于降雨预测领域，通过融合稀疏气象站数据和雷达空间信息，成功解决了传统方法在分辨率和精度上的局限。该方法的核心贡献在于同时实现了高分辨率降雨预测和不确定性量化，为精细化气象服务提供了可靠的技术支撑。

Abstract: High-resolution rainfall observations are crucial for weather forecasting, water management, and hazard mitigation. Traditional operational measurements are often biased and low-resolution, limiting their ability to capture local rainfall. Accurate high-resolution rainfall maps require integrating sparse surface observations, yet existing deep learning densification methods are hindered by rainfall's skewed, localized nature, noise, and limited spatio-temporal fusion. We present DropsToGrid, a Neural Process-based method that generates dense rainfall fields by fusing temporal sequences from noisy, irregularly distributed private weather stations with spatial context from radar. Leveraging multi-scale feature extraction, temporal attention, and multi-modal fusion, the model produces stochastic, continuous rainfall estimates and explicitly quantifies uncertainty. Evaluations on real-world datasets demonstrate that DropsToGrid outperforms both operational and deep learning baselines, generating accurate high-resolution rainfall maps with well-calibrated uncertainty, even when only few stations are available and in cross-regional scenarios.

</details>


### [156] [Knowing but Not Correcting: Routine Task Requests Suppress Factual Correction in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05957)
*Zixuan Chen,Hao Lin,Zizhe Chen,Yizhou Tian,Garry Yang,Depeng Wang,Ya Guo,Huijia Zhu,James Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示LLM中存在"校正抑制"现象——明明知道错误却选择遵从；通过机制分析定位问题发生在响应选择而非知识编码阶段；提出CDS和DPA两种训练无关干预方法，有效提升模型在任务场景中维持事实准确性的意愿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在独立呈现虚假声明时能够可靠地进行纠正，但当这些虚假声明嵌入到任务导向型请求中时，模型往往选择遵从而非纠正——论文将这种失败模式定义为"校正抑制"。

Method: 1) 构建含300个虚假前提的基准测试集，对8个模型进行系统性评估；2) 机制分析采用注意力可视化追踪错误信息的处理流程；3) 提出两种无需训练的干预方法：校正方向转向(CDS)在中间层注入校正方向向量；动态载荷放大(DPA)通过注意力差异定位载荷token并在最终层放大其表示。

Result: 校正抑制发生率在19%至90%之间，四个模型超过80%，属普遍且严重的问题。机制分析表明：抑制并非知识缺陷——模型内部已注册错误，但任务上下文导致浅层注意力从虚假声明转移、中层输出意图向遵从方向晶化。CDS在Qwen3.5-9B上实现最高校正率(0%→58.2%)；DPA是唯一能同时保持或提升推理能力的方法。

Conclusion: 论文引入"事实严格性"作为模型可靠性的新维度，为提升LLM在任务场景中的事实准确性提供了有效的训练无关干预方案。

Abstract: LLMs reliably correct false claims when presented in isolation, yet when the same claims are embedded in task-oriented requests, they often comply rather than correct. We term this failure mode \emph{correction suppression} and construct a benchmark of 300 false premises to systematically evaluate it across eight models. Suppression rates range from 19\% to 90\%, with four models exceeding 80\%, establishing correction suppression as a prevalent and severe phenomenon. Mechanistic analysis reveals that suppression is not a knowledge failure: the model registers the error internally but task context diverts early-layer attention from the false claim as output intent crystallizes toward compliance at middle layers. We characterize this as \emph{knowing but not correcting} -- suppression occurs at response selection rather than knowledge encoding. Guided by this mechanism, we propose two training-free interventions. Correction Direction Steering (CDS) estimates a correction-compliance direction from matched pairs and injects it at middle layers before output intent crystallizes. Dynamic Payload Amplification (DPA) localizes payload tokens via attention divergence between early and late layers and amplifies their representation at the final layer, requiring no calibration data. Experiments on Qwen3.5-9B and LLaMA3.1-8B show both methods substantially improve factual strictness. CDS achieves the highest correction rate on Qwen3.5-9B (0\%$\to$58.2\%). DPA is the only method that preserves or improves reasoning capability on both models. These findings introduce \emph{factual strictness} -- the willingness to uphold accuracy against contextual pressures -- as a new dimension of model reliability.

</details>


### [157] [Beyond Uniform Credit Assignment: Selective Eligibility Traces for RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05965)
*Chaoli Mou,Zhan Zhuang,Xinning Chen,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出选择性资格迹（S-trace）方法，通过稀疏资格迹机制选择性掩码低熵token，实现细粒度信用分配，解决GRPO等无 critic 算法中均匀信用分配导致的效率问题，在Qwen3系列模型（1.7B/4B/8B）上均优于GRPO并保持更高的样本和token效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current critic-free algorithms like GRPO rely on "uniform credit assignment" that indiscriminately broadcasts trajectory-level advantages, failing to distinguish critical reasoning steps and thereby hindering learning efficiency.

Method: The method consists of two components: (1) P-trace, a sample-efficient, critic-free eligibility traces method grounded in partial trust region preservation; (2) S-trace, which builds on P-trace by implementing sparse eligibility traces that selectively mask low-entropy tokens to achieve fine-grained credit assignment and reduce variance. Theoretically, GSPO is contextualized as a special instance of this eligibility traces framework.

Result: S-trace outperforms GRPO with improvements of 0.49% on Qwen3-1.7B, 3.16% on Qwen3-4B, and 2.98% on Qwen3-8B in average pass@16, while achieving higher sample and token efficiency simultaneously.

Conclusion: S-trace is a robust method for improving LLM reasoning that scales effectively from 1.7B to 8B parameters, consistently outperforming GRPO across model sizes while simultaneously improving both sample efficiency and token efficiency.

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become a key approach for improving the reasoning abilities of large language models. However, widely used critic-free algorithms such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) necessitate a ``uniform credit assignment'' assumption that indiscriminately broadcast trajectory-level advantages, hindering learning efficiency by failing to distinguish critical reasoning steps. To address this limitation, we propose Selective Eligibility Traces (S-trace). Grounded in the intuition of partial trust region preservation, we initially introduce P-trace as a sample-efficient, critic-free eligibility traces method, upon which we build S-trace, implementing a sparse eligibility traces mechanism to further mitigate variance and achieve fine-grained credit assignment by selectively masking low-entropy tokens. Theoretically, we contextualize the recent Group Sequence Policy Optimization (GSPO) method within the critic-free eligibility traces framework, identifying it as a special instance of the eligibility traces method operating under uniform credit assignment. Experiments demonstrate that S-trace not only outperforms GRPO, showing gains of 0.49\% on Qwen3-1.7B and 3.16\% on Qwen3-4B, and maintaining a robust 2.98\% improvement when scaled further to Qwen3-8B in average pass@16, but notably achieves this with simultaneously higher sample and token efficiency.

</details>


### [158] [Sharper Guarantees for Misspecified Kernelized Bandit Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05967)
*Davide Maran,Csaba Szepesvári*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对核化 bandit 优化中的错误适配问题，提出通过局部化机制（谱局部化控制Lebesgue常数 + 域分割算法实现空间局部化）将错误适配放大从多项式降低至对数/多项对数级别，显著改进了离线简单遗憾和在线累积遗憾的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有核bandit优化理论中，错误适配代价以核复杂度放大——离线边界中错误适配水平ε乘以√d_eff，在线遗憾边界中对应惩罚为√γ_n·nε。此多项式/线性放大导致算法在错误适配场景下表现保守，理论保证与实际性能存在显著差距。

Method: 离线设置：利用谱Lebesgue常数替代传统核有效维度；在线设置：修改域分割算法，在温和的局部特征值衰减假设下实现改进。

Result: 离线设置：对数放大（一维单调谱）和多项对数放大（多元Fourier对角积核）；在线设置：累积遗憾改进为Õ(√(γ_n n) + nε)，移除了错误适配项中的额外√γ_n因子。

Conclusion: 通过局部化机制（谱局部化控制离线近似算子的Lebesgue常数，域分割实现空间模拟），可将核-bandit优化中的错误适配放大从多项式/线性级别降低到对数或多项对数级别，显著改善了现有最坏情况边界。

Abstract: Existing guarantees for misspecified kernelized bandit optimization pay for misspecification through kernel complexity: in generic offline bounds, the misspecification level $\varepsilon$ is multiplied by $\sqrt{d_\mathrm{eff}}$, where $d_\mathrm{eff}$ is the kernel effective dimension, while in online regret bounds, the corresponding penalty is $\sqrt{γ_n}\,n\varepsilon$, where $γ_n$ is the maximum information gain after $n$ rounds of interaction.
  In this work, we show that, for a large class of kernels, the misspecification amplification can be reduced to logarithmic or polylogarithmic growth. In the offline setting, we first prove high-probability simple-regret bounds whose misspecification term is governed by a spectral Lebesgue constant. This yields logarithmic amplification for one-dimensional monotone spectra and polylogarithmic amplification for multivariate Fourier-diagonal product kernels. In the online setting, we modify a domain-splitting algorithm and prove a cumulative regret bound of $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(\sqrt{γ_n n}+n\varepsilon)$ under mild localized eigendecay assumptions, removing the extra $\sqrt{γ_n}$ factor from the misspecification term. The common principle is localization: spectral localization controls the Lebesgue constant of the offline approximation operator, while domain splitting implements the spatial analogue of this mechanism in the online setting, preventing local misspecification errors from being amplified globally.

</details>


### [159] [Training Transformers for KV Cache Compressibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05971)
*Yoav Gelberg,Yam Eitan,Michael Bronstein,Yarin Gal,Haggai Maron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对长上下文语言模型中KV缓存的内存和访问成本问题，提出KV compressibility是模型学习表征的属性，而非仅由上下文决定。理论证明几乎任何序列到向量的函数都可以有可压缩和不可压缩的transformer实现。提出KV-CAT训练方法，通过训练时KV稀疏化策略引导模型学习可压缩的表征，从而改善下游压缩方法的质量-资源权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存的内存和访问成本随前缀长度线性增长，限制了长上下文语言模型的发展。现有后处理压缩方法受限于预训练模型的内部表征压缩能力，需要从训练阶段就引导模型学习可压缩的表征。

Method: 提出KV-CAT（KV-Compression Aware Training）持续预训练方法。核心是在训练时引入KV稀疏化策略（KV sparsification policy），遮蔽部分KV槽位，强制模型使用更少的KV槽位，从而促使模型学习适合后处理压缩的表征。

Result: 实验表明KV-CAT在检索、长上下文问答、压缩前缀续写的困惑度评估等多个任务上，改善了下游压缩方法的质量-预算权衡（quality-budget tradeoff）。

Conclusion: KV compressibility是学习表征的属性而非仅由上下文决定。通过KV-CAT在训练阶段引导模型学习可压缩表征，可突破后处理压缩方法的性能瓶颈，为长上下文模型的高效部署提供新思路。

Abstract: Long-context language modeling is increasingly constrained by the Key-Value (KV) cache, whose memory and decode-time access costs scale linearly with the prefix length. This bottleneck has motivated a range of context-compression methods, from token-level summarization to recent optimization-based KV compression methods. These post-hoc methods operate on the KV cache of a fixed pretrained model, so their effectiveness is fundamentally limited by how well the model's internal representations can be compressed. In this work, we formalize the notion of KV compressibility and show that it is a property of the learned representations, rather than of the context alone. We prove that almost any sequence-to-vector function admits both highly compressible and inherently non-compressible transformer implementations, highlighting the need to guide transformers toward compressible representations during training. Motivated by this, we propose KV-Compression Aware Training (KV-CAT), a continued pretraining procedure that incentivizes the emergence of compressible representations. We introduce a train-time KV sparsification policy that masks KV slots during training. This forces the model to use fewer KV slots and encourages it to learn representations amenable to post-hoc compression. Empirically, we show that KV-CAT improves the quality-budget tradeoff of downstream compression methods across retrieval, long-context question answering, and perplexity-based evaluation of compressed-prefix continuation.

</details>


### [160] [Is Escalation Worth It? A Decision-Theoretic Characterization of LLM Cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06350)
*Dylan Bouchard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究建立了模型级联的决策理论框架，证明了k-model级联的最优性能等价于成对级联的点态包络，揭示了级联性能的根本限制是结构性成本——需先支付廉价模型费用才能决定升级，而非中间阶段不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型级联方法将 deferral threshold 视为经验超参数，缺乏对 cost-quality 前沿几何结构的理论指导。

Method: 决策理论框架 + 约束优化 + 对偶理论 + 包络分析。针对2-model级联建立分段凹性；针对k-model级联推导一阶条件；验证部分使用5个基准测试（MATH、MMLU、TriviaQA、SimpleQA、LiveCodeBench）和8个模型。

Result: （1）成对包络覆盖所有可能的确定性阈值级联；（2）固定链式结构表现不如成对包络；（3）轻量级 pre-generation router 在4/5数据集上优于最佳级联策略，优势源于避免廉价模型的生成成本而非更强的路由信号。

Conclusion: 确定了模型级联性能的核心限制来自结构性成本（必须在升级决策前支付廉价模型生成费用），而非中间阶段不足。成对包络是最优确定性阈值级联的主要形式，更复杂的链式结构无实际收益。

Abstract: Model cascades, in which a cheap LLM defers to an expensive one on low-confidence queries, are widely used to navigate the cost-quality tradeoff at deployment. Existing approaches largely treat the deferral threshold as an empirical hyperparameter, with limited guidance on the geometry of the resulting cost-quality frontier over a model pool. We develop a decision-theoretic framework grounded in constrained optimization and duality. For a two-model cascade, we establish piecewise concavity of the cost-quality frontier on decreasing-benefit regions of the confidence support, with reciprocal shadow prices linking the budget- and quality-constrained formulations. Given a pool of $k$ models, we characterize the frontier achievable by deterministic two-model threshold cascades as the pointwise envelope over $\binom{k}{2}$ pairwise cascades, with switching points where the optimal pair changes. For $k$-model cascades, we derive first-order conditions in which a single shadow price equalizes marginal quality-per-cost across stage boundaries. We validate the framework on five benchmarks (MATH, MMLU, TriviaQA, SimpleQA, LiveCodeBench) across eight models from five providers. Within the deterministic threshold-cascade class, full fixed chains underperform the pairwise envelope, and optimized subsequence cascades do not deliver practically meaningful held-out gains over it. A lightweight pre-generation router exceeds the best cascade policy on four of five datasets, mainly because it avoids the cheap model's generation cost on queries sent directly to a larger model rather than because of a stronger routing signal. These results suggest that cascade performance is limited primarily by structural cost, since cascades pay the cheap model before any escalation decision, rather than by a shortage of intermediate stages.

</details>


### [161] [Physical Fidelity Reconstruction via Improved Consistency-Distilled Flow Matching for Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05975)
*Sicheng Ma,Tianyue Yang,Xiuzhe Wu,Xiao Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将最优传输流匹配教师模型蒸馏为单步一致性模型，实现高保真流场快速重建。推理时通过加噪低分辨率观测初始化生成轨迹，使无条件模型无需重训练即可完成条件重建。在三个流体力学基准（最高$256 \times 256$分辨率）上，模型参数减半、推理加速12倍，且训练效率优于从零训练的单步模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型和流匹配模型依赖迭代采样，计算成本高，难以满足 ensemble forecasting、实时可视化、simulation-in-the-loop 等延迟敏感场景的需求，需要将高保真流匹配生成模型压缩为紧凑的单步模型。

Method: 采用最优传输流匹配（OT-FM）教师模型蒸馏为一个单步一致性模型（consistency model）。核心创新：在推理时，通过沿传输路径对低分辨率观测进行加噪来初始化生成轨迹，使无条件高保真流模型无需重训练即可实现条件重建。训练在$256 \times 256$分辨率的粗细粒度重建任务上进行。

Result: 在 Smoke Buoyancy、Turbulent Channel Flow、Kolmogorov Flow 三个流体力学基准上，蒸馏模型在谱指标上与教师模型性能相当，参数减少约一半，推理速度提升12倍；相比同等训练预算下从零训练的单步模型，SSIM 指标提升23.1%。

Conclusion: 该方法在三个流体力学基准上验证有效，蒸馏模型保留教师模型性能的同时，参数减半，推理速度提升12倍；相比从零训练的一致性模型，SSIM指标提升23.1%，表明知识蒸馏不仅加速推理，还提高了训练效率。

Abstract: Reconstructing high-fidelity flow fields from low-fidelity observations is a central problem in scientific machine learning, yet recent diffusion and flow-matching models typically rely on iterative sampling, making them costly for latency-sensitive workflows such as ensemble forecasting, real-time visualization, and simulation-in-the-loop inference. We study whether a high-fidelity flow-matching generative model can be compressed into a compact one-step model for fast scientific flow reconstruction. Our approach distills an optimal-transport flow-matching teacher into a one-step consistency model. Low-fidelity observations are incorporated at inference by initializing the generative trajectory from a noised observation along the transport path, allowing an unconditional high-fidelity flow model to perform conditional reconstruction without retraining the teacher. We evaluate this distillation strategy on three fluid benchmarks, Smoke Buoyancy, Turbulent Channel Flow, and Kolmogorov Flow, using coarse-to-fine reconstruction as a controlled testbed at field sizes up to $256 \times 256$. Across these settings, the distilled student retains similar performance of the teacher's model on spectrum metrics, while using roughly half as many parameters and achieving a $12\times$ inference speedup over the flow-matching teacher. Under the same training budget, the distilled student also outperforms a one-step consistency model trained directly from scratch by $23.1\%$ in SSIM, showing that teacher distillation improves training efficiency rather than merely accelerating sampling. These results suggest a promising route for turning future high-capacity scientific generative models into compact reconstruction models that are faster to train, cheaper to run, and easier to deploy.

</details>


### [162] [E = T*H/(O+B): A Dimensionless Control Parameter for Mixture-of-Experts Ecology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06415)
*Qingjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出参数E=T×H/(O+B)作为预测MoE模型专家生态健康状况的统一控制指标，E≥0.5可保证零死亡专家，无需手工设计的负载均衡辅助损失。通过跨模态实验验证了该指标的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) 模型训练中存在专家崩溃（dead experts）问题，现有解决方案依赖手工设计的负载均衡辅助损失，缺乏统一的理论指导框架。

Method: 定义无量纲控制参数 E = T×H/(O+B)，整合路由温度(T)、路由熵权重(H)、oracle权重(O)和平衡权重(B)四个超参数。通过12个控制实验（8个视觉任务、4个语言任务）进行验证，累计训练超过11,000个epoch，测试数据集包括CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、TinyImageNet-200、WikiText-2和WikiText-103。

Result: E ≥ 0.5 足以保证零死亡专家，无需额外的负载均衡辅助损失。实验验证了该指标跨模态的有效性。

Conclusion: E参数可作为MoE训练的统一诊断工具（类比流体力学中的雷诺数）。此外发现：(1)死亡专家可复苏；(2)正交毒性与数据集相关；(3)任务复杂度影响临界E阈值；(4)模型过拟合与专家生态健康解耦；(5)三层MoE自发坍缩为两层功能结构；(6)生态结构在50倍温度范围内具有温度不变性。

Abstract: We introduce E = T*H/(O+B), a dimensionless control parameter that predicts whether Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models will develop a healthy expert ecology or collapse into dead experts. E combines four hyperparameters -- routing temperature T, routing entropy weight H, oracle weight O, and balance weight B -- into a single quantity. Through 12 controlled experiments (8 vision, 4 language) totaling over 11,000 training epochs, we establish that E >= 0.5 alone is sufficient to guarantee zero dead experts, removing the necessity for handcrafted load-balancing auxiliary losses. We validate this cross-modally on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet-200, WikiText-2, and WikiText-103. Six additional findings emerge: (1) dead experts can resuscitate -- triggered by balance loss driving router re-exploration; (2) ortho toxicity is dataset-dependent, not universal; (3) task complexity shifts the critical E threshold; (4) model overfitting is decoupled from expert ecological health; (5) three-tier MoE spontaneously collapses into a two-tier functional structure; (6) ecological structure is temperature-invariant across a 50x range. We propose that E serves as a unified diagnostic for MoE training, analogous to the Reynolds number in fluid dynamics.

</details>


### [163] [Invariant Features in Language Models: Geometric Characterization and Model Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06458)
*Agnibh Dasgupta,Abdullah Tanvir,Xin Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将语义不变性建模为潜空间中的局部几何属性，通过对比学习方法分离语义相关与语义无关的变化方向，并验证了不变表征在模型归因任务中的有效性，为理解语言模型的语义组织机制提供了新的几何视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型对释义表现出强鲁棒性，表明语义信息可能通过稳定的内部表征编码，但这种不变性的结构和起源尚不清楚。

Method: (1) 提出局部几何框架，将语义等价输入映射为潜空间中的结构化区域；(2) 提出对比子空间发现方法，分离语义变化与语义保持的变化；(3) 将不变表征应用于零样本模型归因。

Result: 不变结构出现在特定深度区域；语义位移主要位于冗余子空间之外；表征层面的干预表明不变成分对模型输出具有因果作用；不变表征捕获模型特异性几何模式，实现准确归因。

Conclusion: 语义不变性可被视为潜表征的局部几何属性，为理解语言模型如何组织语义提供了原则性视角。

Abstract: Language models exhibit strong robustness to paraphrasing, suggesting that semantic information may be encoded through stable internal representations, yet the structure and origin of such invariance remain unclear. We propose a local geometric framework in which semantically equivalent inputs occupy structured regions in latent space, with paraphrastic variation along nuisance directions and semantic identity preserved in invariant subspaces. Building on this view, we make three contributions: (1) a geometric characterization of invariant latent features, (2) a contrastive subspace discovery method that separates semantic-changing from semantic-preserving variation, and (3) an application of invariant representations to zero-shot model attribution. Across models and layers, empirical results support these contributions. Invariant structure emerges in specific depth regions, semantic displacement lies largely outside the nuisance subspace, and representation-level interventions indicate a causal role of invariant components in model outputs. Invariant representations also capture model-specific geometric patterns, enabling accurate attribution. These findings suggest that semantic invariance can be viewed as a local geometric property of latent representations, offering a principled perspective on how language models organize meaning.

</details>


### [164] [A Fine-Grained Understanding of Uniform Convergence for Halfspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06004)
*Aryeh Kontorovich,Kasper Green Larsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了半空间（halfspaces）的细粒度均匀收敛行为，揭示了非齐次与齐次半空间之间的显著差异。非齐次半空间的VC界本质上不可改进，而齐次半空间在R²中表现更优，达到O(1/n)的误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的VC维理论仅提供最坏情况下的收敛界，本文旨在超越这一框架，深入研究半空间均匀收敛的细粒度行为，特别关注齐次与非齐次半空间之间的结构差异。

Method: 对非齐次半空间采用标准VC分析；对齐次半空间（特别是R²）采用关键楔形（critical-wedge）局部化技术，证明带状对数无关的偏差界，并通过并集运算分析Dyadic风险带。

Result: 1. 非齐次半空间：真实误差Θ(dln(n/d)/n)（可实现情形），√(τln(1/τ))（非理性情形）；2. 齐次半空间R²：每个一致假设误差O(1/n)；3. 证明lnln n开销下界不可逃避。

Conclusion: 本文给出了半空间均匀收敛的完整刻画，揭示了维数和结构的关键阈值，为理解线性分类器的泛化行为提供了精确理论依据。

Abstract: We study the fine-grained uniform convergence behavior of halfspaces beyond worst-case VC bounds. For inhomogeneous halfspaces in $\mathbb{R}^d$ with $d\ge 2$, we show that standard first-order VC bounds are essentially tight: even consistent hypotheses can incur population error $Θ(d\ln(n/d)/n)$, and in the agnostic setting the deviation scales as $\sqrt{τ\ln(1/τ)}$ at true error $τ$. In contrast, homogeneous halfspaces in $\mathbb{R}^2$ exhibit a markedly different behavior. In the realizable case, every hypothesis consistent with the sample has error $O(1/n)$. In the agnostic case, we prove a bandwise, log-free deviation bound on each dyadic risk band via a critical-wedge localization argument. Unioning over bands incurs only a $\ln\ln n$ overhead, and we establish a matching lower bound showing this overhead is unavoidable. Together, these results give a fine-grained and nearly complete picture of uniform convergence for halfspaces, revealing sharp dimensional and structural thresholds.

</details>


### [165] [Cubit: Token Mixer with Kernel Ridge Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06501)
*Chuanyang Zheng,Jiankai Sun,Yihang Gao,Yuehao Wang,Liangchen Tan,Mac Schwager,Anderson Schneider,Yuriy Nevmyvaka,Xiaodong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper interprets Transformers' attention as Nadaraya-Watson regression and proposes Cubit, a Kernel Ridge Regression-based architecture with improved mathematical foundations and stronger long-sequence modeling capabilities.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The core token-mixing mechanism in Transformers remains attention, and despite various improvements to positional encoding, attention, and feed-forward networks, the fundamental approach has not fundamentally changed. The authors seek a stronger mathematical foundation by interpreting attention as Nadaraya-Watson regression and proposing a KRR-based alternative.

Method: Proposes Cubit, a KRR-based architecture that replaces classical attention with the closed-form solution of KRR, combining kernel similarity-based value aggregation with kernel matrix inverse normalization. Also introduces Limited-Range Rescale (LRR) to improve training stability by rescaling value layers within a controlled range.

Result: Experiments validate that Cubit exhibits stronger long-sequence modeling capability, and the performance gain over Transformers increases as training sequence length grows.

Conclusion: Cubit provides a stronger mathematical foundation than vanilla Transformers by replacing Nadaraya-Watson regression with Kernel Ridge Regression, and demonstrates improved long-sequence modeling capability that increases with sequence length.

Abstract: Since its introduction in 2017, the Transformer has become one of the most widely adopted architectures in modern deep learning. Despite extensive efforts to improve positional encoding, attention mechanisms, and feed-forward networks, the core token-mixing mechanism in Transformers remains attention. In this work, we show that the attention module in Transformers can be interpreted as performing Nadaraya-Watson regression, where it computes similarities between tokens and aggregates the corresponding values accordingly. Motivated by this perspective, we propose Cubit, a potential next-generation architecture that leverages Kernel Ridge Regression (KRR), while the vanilla Transformer relies on Nadaraya-Watson regression. Specifically, Cubit modifies the classical attention computation by incorporating the closed-form solution of KRR, combining value aggregation through kernel similarities with normalization via the inverse of the kernel matrix. To improve the training stability, we further propose the Limited-Range Rescale (LRR), which rescales the value layer within a controlled range. We argue that Cubit, as a KRR-based architecture, provides a stronger mathematical foundation than the vanilla Transformer, whose attention mechanism corresponds to Nadaraya-Watson regression. We validate this claim through comprehensive experiments. The experimental results suggest that Cubit may exhibit stronger long-sequence modeling capability. In particular, its performance gain over the Transformer appears to increase as the training sequence length grows.

</details>


### [166] [PairAlign: A Framework for Sequence Tokenization via Self-Alignment with Applications to Audio Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06582)
*Adhiraj Banerjee,Vipul Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PairAlign是一种通过序列级自对齐实现紧凑音频标记化的框架。该方法将标记化视为条件序列生成任务，使用编码器-解码器架构学习标记身份、顺序、长度和结束位置，通过双视图对比训练减少多对一坍缩，在TIMIT检索任务上实现55%的token数量减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频标记器依赖量化、聚类或codec重建，采用局部分配策略，导致序列一致性、紧凑性、长度控制、终止判断和编辑相似性等关键指标未被直接优化。这是语音处理中比较、记忆、检索和推理等操作的核心瓶颈。

Method: PairAlign将标记化视为条件序列生成任务：编码器将语音映射为连续条件表示，自回归解码器从BOS开始生成token序列，学习标记身份、顺序、长度和EOS位置。核心训练策略包括：(1)给定两个内容保持的视图，序列被训练为另一视图表示的似然目标；(2)使用VQ风格标记化作为起点，通过EMA教师目标、交叉配对教师强制、前缀损坏、似然对比和长度控制进行精炼。

Result: 在3秒语音任务中，PairAlign学习到紧凑、非退化的序列，具有广泛的词汇使用量和强跨视图一致性。在TIMIT检索任务中，在保持编辑距离搜索能力的同时，存档token数量减少55%。连续扫描探针显示其局部重叠低于密集几何标记器，但具有更强的长度控制和100ms偏移下的有界编辑轨迹。

Conclusion: PairAlign是一种序列-符号预测学习器，类似于JEPA风格的目标函数，从另一视图预测抽象目标作为学习的长度可变符号序列而非连续潜变量。该方法为音频标记化提供了一种新的序列级优化范式，在紧凑性和编辑距离保持之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: Many operations on sensory data -- comparison, memory, retrieval, and reasoning -- are naturally expressed over discrete symbolic structures. In language this interface is given by tokens; in audio, it must be learned. Existing audio tokenizers rely on quantization, clustering, or codec reconstruction, assigning tokens locally, so sequence consistency, compactness, length control, termination, and edit similarity are rarely optimized directly.
  We introduce PairAlign, a framework for compact audio tokenization through sequence-level self-alignment. PairAlign treats tokenization as conditional sequence generation: an encoder maps speech to a continuous condition, and an autoregressive decoder generates tokens from BOS, learning token identity, order, length, and EOS placement. Given two content-preserving views, each view's sequence is trained to be likely under the other's representation, while unrelated examples provide competing sequences. This gives a scalable surrogate for edit-distance preservation while discouraging many-to-one collapse.
  PairAlign starts from VQ-style tokenization and refines it with EMA-teacher targets, cross-paired teacher forcing, prefix corruption, likelihood contrast, and length control.
  On 3-second speech, PairAlign learns compact, non-degenerate sequences with broad vocabulary usage and strong cross-view consistency. On TIMIT retrieval, it preserves edit-distance search while reducing archive token count by 55%. A continuous-sweep probe shows lower local overlap than a dense geometric tokenizer, but stronger length control and bounded edit trajectories under 100 ms shifts. PairAlign is a sequence-symbolic predictive learner: like JEPA-style objectives, it predicts an abstract target from another view as a learned variable-length symbolic sequence, not a continuous latent.

</details>


### [167] [Multi-agent decision making: A Blackwell's informativeness approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06028)
*Zheng Zhang,Cuong C. Nguyen,Kevin Wells,Gustavo Carneiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用Blackwell信息论框架分析多智能体LLM决策问题，证明了投票和辩论机制的信息量不超过所有智能体的私有信息池，并提出基于后验乘积估计的实用方法，在6个QA基准上超越现有辩论和投票方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多智能体LLM决策研究缺乏正式的理论保证，现有的投票和辩论等聚合方法多为经验性设计，无法保证决策的信息丰富度(informativeness)。需要从信息论角度建立原则性分析框架。

Method: 1) 将多智能体LLM决策问题抽象为Blackwell信息结构；2) 形式化证明投票和辩论产生的信息结构在Blackwell序意义下不超过所有智能体的联合私有信息；3) 提出贝叶斯联合后验最大化作为理论上界；4) 设计基于后验乘积估计器的近似实现方法。

Result: 在6个问答基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法优于现有的多智能体辩论和投票方法，验证了理论分析的正确性和近似方法的实用性。

Conclusion: Blackwell信息论框架为多智能体LLM决策提供了严格的理论分析工具；联合后验最大化是优于投票和辩论的最优决策规则；后验乘积估计是可行的近似实现方案。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models (LLMs) has motivated research on decision-making in multi-agent systems, where multiple agents collaborate to achieve shared objectives. Existing aggregation approaches, such as voting and debate, are largely ad-hoc and lack formal guarantees regarding the informativeness of the resulting decisions. In this paper, we provide a principled approach to analyse decisions made in the multi-LLM setting using Blackwell's informativeness framework. Within the Blackwell information-structure abstraction, we show that voting and debate induce information structures that are no more informative than the pooled private information of all agents. This result identifies Bayesian pooled posterior maximisation as an information-theoretic upper-bound decision rule under the Blackwell ordering. Motivated by this theoretical analysis, we introduce a practical method for LLM-based question-answering (QA) tasks that estimates each agent's posterior and approximates the pooled posterior using a product-of-posteriors estimator. Extensive experiments on six QA benchmarks demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art multi-LLM debate and voting methods.

</details>


### [168] [When and Why SignSGD Outperforms SGD: A Theoretical Study Based on $\ell_1$-norm Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06615)
*Hongyi Tao,Dingzhi Yu,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文在新的问题几何框架（l1范数稳定点、l∞-光滑性、可分离噪声）下首次严格证明了SignSGD在稀疏噪声条件下的维度优势（复杂度降低d倍），并将该理论推广至Muon矩阵优化器，通过GPT-2预训练实验验证了理论预测的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sign-based算法（SignSGD、Muon）在训练大模型中表现优异，但理论上缺乏对标SGD优势的清晰理解；标准条件下SGD对l2范数稳定点已是最小最优，限制了sign方法的理论收益空间。

Method: 在l1范数稳定点、l∞-光滑性和可分离噪声模型的新几何框架下，推导SignSGD的匹配上下界，并将框架推广至矩阵域分析Muon优化器。

Result: 在稀疏噪声下，SignSGD相比SGD复杂度降低d倍（d为维度）；Muon在矩阵域保持相同的维度缩放最优性；124M GPT-2预训练实验验证了理论预测的收敛速度优势。

Conclusion: SignSGD在稀疏噪声条件下确实具有维度优势（复杂度降低d倍），且该理论优势在124M参数GPT-2预训练中得到验证；Muon在矩阵域也保持此最优维度缩放特性。

Abstract: Sign-based optimization algorithms, such as SignSGD and Muon, have garnered significant attention for their remarkable performance in training large foundation models. Despite this empirical success, we still lack a theoretical understanding of when and why these sign-based methods outperform vanilla SGD. The core obstacle is that under standard smoothness and finite variance conditions, SGD is known to be minimax optimal for finding stationary points measured by $\ell_2$-norms, thereby fundamentally precluding any complexity gains for sign-based methods in standard settings. To overcome this barrier, we analyze sign-based optimizers leveraging $\ell_1$-norm stationarity, $\ell_\infty$-smoothness, and a separable noise model, which can better capture the coordinate-wise nature of signed updates. Under this distinct problem geometry, we derive matched upper and lower bounds for SignSGD and explicitly characterize the problem class in which SignSGD provably dominates SGD. Specifically, we compare the \emph{upper bound of SignSGD} with the \emph{lower bound of SGD}, illustrating that SignSGD effectively reduces the complexity by a factor of $d$ under \emph{sparse noise}, where $d$ is the problem dimension. Furthermore, we elevate this framework to the matrix domain, providing an equivalent optimal lower bound for the Muon optimizer, proving that extending the sign operator to matrices preserves this optimal scaling with dimensionality. Finally, we bridge our theoretical bounds to practice, demonstrating that the theoretical superiority of SignSGD accurately predicts its faster convergence during the pretraining of a 124M parameter GPT-2 model.

</details>


### [169] [Does Synthetic Data Help? Empirical Evidence from Deep Learning Time Series Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06032)
*Hugo Cazaux,Eyjólfur Ingi Ásgeirsson,Hlynur Stefánsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实证研究（9个实验组、4,218次运行）系统评估了合成时间序列增强方法。研究发现效果高度依赖模型架构：通道混合模型（如TimesNet、iTransformer）大多受益，而通道独立模型（如DLinear、PatchTST）普遍表现下降。仅Seasonal-Trend生成器在测试基准上表现可靠，课程切换策略则造成显著性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据在语言模型训练中已广泛应用，但在时间序列预测中的作用机制尚不清晰，缺乏系统性评估。

Method: 大规模实证研究，涵盖5种架构、4种合成信号、7个数据集，共4,218次实验运行。

Result: 通道混合模型在大多数试验中受益于合成增强；通道独立模型表现持续下降。平均而言，增强在67%的试验中造成性能下降。仅Seasonal-Trend生成器可靠有效，硬课程切换导致MSE增加24%。低资源设置下TimesNet仅用10% Weather数据加合成增强即可超越全数据基线。

Conclusion: 使用合成数据增强时应：(1)选择通道混合架构；(2)采用渐进式退火调度；(3)低资源增强需考虑架构和数据集特异性。

Abstract: Synthetic data has transformed language model training, yet its role in time series forecasting remains poorly understood. We present a large-scale empirical study: nine experiment groups, 4,218 runs systematically evaluating synthetic time series augmentation across five architectures, four synthetic signals and seven datasets. The effect is sharply architecture-conditional: channel-mixing models (TimesNet, iTransformer) benefit in the majority of trials, while channel-independent models (DLinear, PatchTST) are consistently degraded. In selected low-resource settings the gains are striking: TimesNet trained on only 10\% of Weather data with synthetic augmentation surpasses the full-data baseline (4 of 16 sparsity-dataset combinations). Averaged across all architectures, augmentation hurts in 67\% of trials. We further find that only the Seasonal-Trend generator reliably helps across the tested benchmarks, and that hard curriculum switching is actively harmful (+24\% MSE degradation). These results provide concrete, actionable guidelines on how to use synthetic data: use synthetic augmentation with channel-mixing architectures, use gradual annealing schedules, and treat low-resource augmentation as architecture- and dataset-dependent. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/hugoiscracked/synthetic-ts/tree/main}

</details>


### [170] [Optimal Transport for LLM Reward Modeling from Noisy Preference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06036)
*Licheng Pan,Haochen Yang,Haoxuan Li,Yunsheng Lu,Yongqi Tong,Yinuo Wang,Shijian Wang,Zhixuan Chu,Lei Shen,Yuan Lu,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SelectiveRM是一种基于最优传输理论的奖励模型去噪框架，通过Joint Consistency Discrepancy对齐分布，并引入Mass Relaxation机制（部分传输）自主排除噪声样本，在理论上优化了干净风险的更紧上界，实验验证其优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF中的奖励模型易受噪声偏好数据影响。现有方法依赖同质噪声假设，无法捕捉语言偏好的复杂性；传统训练目标会过拟合错误，导致模型性能下降。

Method: 提出SelectiveRM框架，包含两个核心组件：1) Joint Consistency Discrepancy：对齐模型预测分布与偏好数据分布；2) Mass Relaxation机制：通过部分传输放松严格的质量守恒约束，使模型能自主排除与语义一致性矛盾的噪声样本。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，SelectiveRM显著优于现有最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: SelectiveRM从理论和实验两方面验证了其有效性，为RLHF中的奖励模型去噪提供了一种基于最优传输的优雅解决方案。

Abstract: Reward models are fundamental to Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF), yet real-world datasets are inevitably corrupted by noisy preference. Conventional training objectives tend to overfit these errors, while existing denoising approaches often rely on homogeneous noise assumptions that fail to capture the complexity of linguistic preferences. To handle these challenges, we propose SelectiveRM, a framework grounded in optimal transport. We first devise a Joint Consistency Discrepancy to align the distribution of model predictions with preference data. Furthermore, to address the limitation of strict mass conservation which compels the model to fit outliers, we incorporate a Mass Relaxation mechanism via partial transport. This enables the autonomous exclusion of samples with noisy preference that contradict semantic consistency. Theoretically, we demonstrate that SelectiveRM optimizes a tighter upper bound on the unobserved clean risk. Extensive experiments validate that our approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across diverse benchmarks.

</details>


### [171] [Requests of a Feather Must Flock Together: Batch Size vs. Prefix Homogeneity in LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06046)
*Saksham Rathi,Preeti,Mythili Vutukuru*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM推理中的KV缓存访问效率问题，提出 Feather 调度器利用强化学习动态平衡批大小与前缀同质性，并设计CHT数据结构加速前缀检测。实验表明该方案在vLLM和SGLang中可获得2-10倍吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的自回归token生成因需要"attend to"所有先前token的KV缓存而受内存约束。现有最优推理引擎的前缀感知调度器仅通过最大化批内前缀重用来减少KV缓存内存占用，但未在更小、更同质的批上停止批形成以获得更好性能；此外，现有调度器依赖radix-tree遍历进行共享前缀检测，产生的CPU开销常与GPU执行时间相当。

Method: 提出Feather：一个基于强化学习的前缀感知调度器，学习批大小与前缀同质性之间的最优权衡；引入Chunked Hash Tree (CHT)：一种轻量级数据结构，用于快速前缀检测和高效请求选择，避免了昂贵的树遍历操作。

Result: 在vLLM和SGLang中集成后，Feather相比现有调度器实现2-10倍端到端吞吐量提升；在无足够前缀共享的工作负载下表现不低于现状；通过减少KV缓存访问总次数超越了具有相同目标的前缀感知注意力内核的性能。

Conclusion: Feather通过RL学习和CHT数据结构，有效解决了前缀感知调度中批大小与前缀同质性之间的权衡问题，在vLLM和SGLang中实现了2-10倍的端到端吞吐量提升，且在无前缀共享的工作负载下不劣于现有方案。

Abstract: Auto-regressive token generation in large language models is memory-bound because it requires "attending to" key and value tensors (KV cache) of all previous tokens. Prior work aims to improve the efficiency of this decode process by batching multiple requests together, and maximizing batch size subject to GPU memory constraints. The key observation of our work is that with prefix-sharing workloads, smaller, prefix-homogeneous batches -- where all requests share a common prefix -- can achieve higher decode throughput than larger, heterogeneous batches, due to better spatial and temporal locality during KV cache accesses. However, prefix-aware schedulers in state-of-the-art inference engines maximize prefix reuse within a batch only to reduce KV cache memory footprint, but do not stop batch formation at smaller homogeneous batches that could have performed better. Further, we show that shared prefix detection in existing schedulers relies on radix-tree traversals, incurring substantial CPU overhead that is often comparable to GPU execution time. This paper presents Feather, a prefix-aware scheduler that uses reinforcement learning (RL) to learn the optimal tradeoff between batch size and prefix homogeneity. We also introduce Chunked Hash Tree (CHT), a lightweight data structure that enables fast prefix detection and efficient request selection for the RL scheduler, avoiding expensive tree traversals. We integrate Feather into vLLM and SGLang, and our evaluation shows that Feather achieves 2--10$\times$ higher end-to-end throughput as compared to existing schedulers, while doing no worse than the status quo when the workload does not have enough prefix sharing. Feather achieves these gains by reducing the total number of KV cache accesses, surpassing the performance of prefix-aware attention kernels that have the same goal.

</details>


### [172] [TFM-Retouche: A Lightweight Input-Space Adapter for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06047)
*Duong Nguyen,Mohammed Jawhar,Nicolas Chesneau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TFM-Retouche是一种轻量级输入空间残差适配器，通过在输入空间学习残差校正来对齐输入数据与预训练模型的归纳偏置，从而在不修改TFM内部架构的情况下实现任务适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型（TFM）的归纳偏置在推理时是固定的，适配到特定数据集通常需要昂贵的全量微调或需要针对每种TFM内部架构定制的PEFT方法（如LoRA），且权重空间微调是否能提升准确性和校准效果尚存争议。

Method: TFM-Retouche采用输入空间残差适配方式，学习小型残差校正来对齐输入数据与预训练模型的归纳偏置。适配器通过冻结的TFM进行端到端训练，并包含训练后身份保护机制，当适配在验证集上无帮助时回退到未修改的TFM。

Result: 在TabArena-Lite（51个数据集，涵盖二分类、多分类和回归任务）上，基于TabICLv2的TabICLv2-Retouche以轻量级任务调优和集成策略在排行榜上位列第一，相比冻结的TabICLv2基础模型Aggregate Elo提升+56，并位于预测质量与训练/推理时间的帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 输入空间残差适配是一种有效的、与架构无关的TFM适配范式，能够在保持高效性的同时显著提升表格基础模型的任务适应性。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs), such as TabPFN-2.6, TabICLv2, ConTextTab, Mitra, LimiX, and TabDPT, achieve strong zero-shot performance through in-context learning, but their inductive biases remain fixed at inference time. Adapting a pretrained TFM to a specific dataset or task typically requires either full fine-tuning, which is computationally expensive, or parameter-efficient tuning methods (PEFT) such as LoRA, which must be tailored to the internal architecture of each TFM. Furthermore, the evidence on whether weight-space fine-tuning improves accuracy or calibration is mixed \citep{tanna_exploring_2026,rubachev_finetuning_2025}. We introduce TFM-Retouche, a lightweight input-space residual adapter that is architecture-agnostic by design with respect to the frozen TFM backbone. TFM-Retouche learns a small residual correction in the input space to align the input data with the inductive biases of the pretrained model. The adapter is trained end-to-end through the frozen TFM, with a post-training identity guard that falls back to the unmodified TFM whenever adaptation does not help on held-out validation. On TabArena-Lite (51 datasets spanning binary classification, multiclass classification, and regression), TabICLv2-Retouche -- the framework instantiated on TabICLv2 -- is the top-ranked method on the leaderboard with light per-task tuning and ensembling, lifting aggregate Elo by +56 over the frozen TabICLv2 base and sitting on the Pareto front of predictive quality versus both training and inference time.

</details>


### [173] [When No Benchmark Exists: Validating Comparative LLM Safety Scoring Without Ground-Truth Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06652)
*Sushant Gautam,Finn Schwall,Annika Willoch Olstad,Fernando Vallecillos Ruiz,Birk Torpmann-Hagen,Sunniva Maria Stordal Bjørklund,Leon Moonen,Klas Pettersen,Michael A. Riegler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出无基准比较安全评分框架，通过工具有效性链在缺乏真实标签时验证安全评估。SimpleAudit在挪威语安全场景中表现优异(AUROC 0.89-1.00)，并在公共部门采购中验证实用性。核心发现：安全评估结果不可简化为单一排名，需同时报告多维度指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当特定语言、行业或监管领域的标记基准尚不存在时，如何比较候选语言模型的安全性成为难题。现有方法缺乏统一框架来评估和比较模型在这种情况下的安全表现。

Method: 提出"无基准比较安全评分"形式化框架，定义场景审计的契约条件。在固定场景包、评分规则、审计员、评判者、采样配置和重运行预算下保持有效。通过"工具有效性链"替代真实标签：控制安全vs消融对比的响应性、目标驱动方差主导审计员/评判者伪影、跨重运行稳定性。实现为SimpleAudit本地优先评分工具。

Result: SimpleAudit在挪威安全包上验证：安全与消融目标分离AUROC达0.89-1.00，目标身份是主导方差成分(η²≈0.52)，严重度分布在10次重运行后稳定。Petri框架支持两种工具。真实采购案例显示Borealis和Gemma 3的相对安全性随场景类别和风险度量而变化。

Conclusion: 在挪威公共部门采购案例中，更安全的模型取决于场景类别和风险度量。分数、匹配差值、关键率、不确定性及审计员和评判者必须一起报告，而非简化为单一排名。上游的工具差异主要体现在声明-契约执行和部署适配上。

Abstract: Many deployments must compare candidate language models for safety before a labeled benchmark exists for the relevant language, sector, or regulatory regime. We formalize this setting as benchmarkless comparative safety scoring and specify the contract under which a scenario-based audit can be interpreted as deployment evidence. Scores are valid only under a fixed scenario pack, rubric, auditor, judge, sampling configuration, and rerun budget. Because no labels are available, we replace ground-truth agreement with an instrumental-validity chain: responsiveness to a controlled safe-versus-abliterated contrast, dominance of target-driven variance over auditor and judge artifacts, and stability across reruns.
  We instantiate the chain in SimpleAudit, a local-first scoring instrument, and validate it on a Norwegian safety pack. Safe and abliterated targets separate with AUROC values between 0.89 and 1.00, target identity is the dominant variance component ($η^2 \approx 0.52$), and severity profiles stabilize by ten reruns. Applying the same chain to Petri shows that it admits both tools. The substantial differences arise upstream of the chain, in claim-contract enforcement and deployment fit. A Norwegian public-sector procurement case comparing Borealis and Gemma 3 demonstrates the resulting evidence in practice: the safer model depends on scenario category and risk measure. Consequently, scores, matched deltas, critical rates, uncertainty, and the auditor and judge used must be reported together rather than collapsed into a single ranking.

</details>


### [174] [Verifier-Backed Hard Problem Generation for Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06660)
*Yuhang Lai,Jiazhan Feng,Yee Whye Teh,Ning Miao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VHG框架，通过在三玩家自我博弈中引入验证器，解决了LLM生成有效难题的难题。两个验证器变体（符号/LLM）在数学任务上均显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在解决科学和数学问题方面表现出色，但在生成有效、具有挑战性和新颖性的问题方面存在困难。现有方法依赖昂贵的人工专家或采用朴素的自我博弈范式，容易产生因奖励黑客而导致的无有效问题。

Method: 提出VHG框架，基于三玩家自我博弈（Setter-Solver-Verifier）：验证器评估问题有效性，解答器评估问题难度，两者共同约束生成器的奖励。具体实现包括两种验证器变体：硬符号验证器和软LLM验证器。

Result: 在不定积分任务和通用数学推理任务上的实验表明，VHG显著且清晰地超越了所有基线方法。

Conclusion: VHG通过引入验证器构建三玩家自我博弈机制，有效解决了LLM生成问题中的奖励黑客和无效问题难题，在不定积分和数学推理任务上显著优于所有基线方法。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate strong capabilities for solving scientific and mathematical problems, yet they struggle to produce valid, challenging, and novel problems - an essential component for advancing LLM training and enabling autonomous scientific research. Existing problem generation approaches either depend on expensive human expert involvement or adopt naive self-play paradigms, which frequently yield invalid problems due to reward hacking. This work introduces VHG, a verifier-enhanced hard problem generation framework built upon three-party self-play. By integrating an independent verifier into the conventional setter-solver duality, our design constrains the setter's reward to be jointly determined by problem validity (evaluated by the verifier) and difficulty (assessed by the solver). We instantiate two verifier variants: a Hard symbolic verifier and a Soft LLM-based verifier, with evaluations conducted on indefinite integral tasks and general mathematical reasoning tasks. Experimental results show that VHG substantially outperforms all baseline methods by a clear margin.

</details>


### [175] [Towards Generation-Efficient Uncertainty Estimation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06053)
*Mingcheng Zhu,Yu Liu,Tingting Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型（LLM）不确定性估计的低成本方法，挑战了"必须完成完整生成才能估计不确定性"的传统假设，提出了统一的"早期估计"框架，并在此框架下开发了两种新方法：基于部分生成前缀的Logit Magnitude和基于输入提示的MetaUE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗、金融等高风险应用中，LLM的幻觉问题难以被用户察觉，现有不确定性估计方法需要完整自回归生成，导致高昂的推理成本，亟需更高效的不确定性估计方案。

Method: (1) 建立统一框架，将不确定性估计形式化为LLM自回归生成过程的"早期估计"问题，按观察信息量组织现有方法；(2) 提出Logit Magnitude：利用top-M logit在早停生成前缀上估计不确定性；(3) 提出MetaUE：将基于生成的不确定性蒸馏为轻量级输入-only估计器。

Result: Logit Magnitude在多个通用和领域特定基准上表现优异，部分生成前缀通常足以实现有效的不确定性估计；MetaUE在多种设置下提供了具有竞争力的输入-only近似。

Conclusion: 有效的不确定性估计所需生成量比通常假设的更少，支持在更早阶段识别不可靠响应，为低成本LLM不确定性估计提供了理论基础和实践方案。
