Abstract: Uncertainty estimation is important for deploying LLMs in high-stakes applications such as healthcare and finance, where hallucinations can appear fluent and plausible while being factually incorrect, making it difficult for users to judge whether an output should be trusted. Existing methods require one or more full autoregressive generations to estimate uncertainty, which introduces substantial inference cost and often delays uncertainty assessment. In this paper, we investigate whether effective uncertainty estimation can be achieved with partial generation or even input-only information. Specifically, we first develop a unified framework that formulates uncertainty estimation as an early estimation problem over the autoregressive generation process of LLMs. This framework organises existing and proposed estimators by the information they observe, ranging from multi-generation to input-only prediction, and clarifies the performance-cost trade-off underlying different uncertainty estimation methods. Building on this view, we study two largely underexplored low-cost settings: estimating uncertainty with part of the generation, and predicting uncertainty from the input prompt. We propose Logit Magnitude, which uses top-M logit evidence to estimate uncertainty from an early-stopped generation prefix, and MetaUE, which distils generation-based uncertainty into a lightweight input-only estimator trained with uncertainty scores. Extensive experiments on general and domain-specific benchmarks show that Logit Magnitude achieves strong performance, and partial generations of LLMs are often sufficient for effective uncertainty estimation. MetaUE further provides a competitive input-only approximation in several settings. These findings suggest that effective uncertainty estimation requires less generation than commonly assumed, enabling unreliable responses to be identified earlier.

</details>


### [176] [Towards Self-Explainable Document Visual Question Answering with Chain-of-Explanation Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06058)
*Kjetil Indrehus,Adrian Duric,Changkyu Choi,Ali Ramezani-Kebrya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoExVQA是一种自解释的文档视觉问答框架，通过链式解释设计实现可验证的推理过程。模型首先识别问题相关证据，再定位答案区域，最后仅从定位区域解码答案。该方法在PFL-DocVQA上实现SotA可解释DocVQA性能，ANLS提升12%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DocVQA模型将问题相关证据与答案定位耦合，以黑盒方式运作，缺乏验证预测如何依赖视觉证据的手段。这限制了模型的可解释性和可信度。

Method: 提出CoExVQA框架，采用链式解释(Chain-of-Explanation)设计：(1)识别问题相关证据；(2)显式定位答案区域；(3)仅从定位区域解码答案。预测过程支持跨模态的直接检验和验证。

Result: 在PFL-DocVQA数据集上，将解码限制于定位证据后，达到可解释DocVQA的SotA性能，ANLS指标较当前可解释基线提升12%。

Conclusion: CoExVQA通过链式解释机制提供透明、可验证的预测，使推理过程可直接跨模态检验。

Abstract: Document Visual Question Answering (DocVQA) requires vision-language models to reason not only about what information in a document is relevant to a question, but also where the answer is grounded on the page. Existing DocVQA models entangle question-relevant evidence and answer localization and operate largely as black boxes, offering limited means to verify how predictions depend on visual evidence. We propose CoExVQA, a self-explainable DocVQA framework with a grounded reasoning process through a chain-of-explanation design. CoExVQA first identifies question-relevant evidence, then explicitly localizes the answer region, and finally decodes the answer exclusively from the grounded region. Prediction via CoExVQA's chain-of-explanation enables direct inspection and verification of the reasoning process across modalities. Empirical results show that restricting decoding to grounded evidence achieves SotA explainable DocVQA performance on PFL-DocVQA, improving ANLS by 12% over the current explainable baselines while providing transparent and verifiable predictions.

</details>


### [177] [Geometry-Aware Simplicial Message Passing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06061)
*Elena Xinyi Wang,Bastian Rieck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GSWL测试，将坐标信息融入单纯复形的颜色细化，解决了传统WL测试无法区分几何差异的问题，建立了几何感知消息传递方案表达能力上界，并与ECT结合提供了完整的几何表达能力特征和逼近框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WL测试及其单纯扩展(SWL)无法捕捉几何信息，无法区分具有相同连通性但不同嵌入的网格。

Method: 提出几何单纯Weisfeiler-Lehman (GSWL)测试，将顶点坐标融入颜色细化过程中。

Result: 证明了几何感知单纯消息传递方案的表达能力被GSWL上界限定；在任何固定有限族的几何单纯复形上，GSWL的区分能力可被这些方案匹配。

Conclusion: GSWL测试与ECT结合，为几何单纯复形提供了几何表达能力特征和逼近框架。实验验证了从组合到几何感知模型的清晰层次结构。

Abstract: The Weisfeiler--Lehman (WL) test and its simplicial extension (SWL) characterize the combinatorial expressivity of message passing networks, but they are blind to geometry, i.e., meshes with identical connectivity but different embeddings are indistinguishable. We introduce the Geometric Simplicial Weisfeiler--Lehman (GSWL) test, which incorporates vertex coordinates into color refinement for geometric simplicial complexes. In addition, we show that (i) the expressivity of geometry-aware simplicial message passing schemes is bounded above by GSWL, and (ii) that there exist parameters such that the discriminating power of GSWL is matched by these schemes on any fixed finite family of geometric simplicial complexes. Combined with the Euler Characteristic Transform (ECT), a complete invariant for geometric simplicial complexes, this yields a geometric expressivity characterization together with an approximation framework. Experiments on synthetic and mesh datasets serve to validate our theory, showing a clear hierarchy from combinatorial to geometry-aware models.

</details>


### [178] [Causal Reinforcement Learning for Complex Card Games: A Magic The Gathering Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06066)
*Cristiano da Costa Cunha,Ajmal Mian,Tim French,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MTG-Causal-RL是一个基于Magic: The Gathering的Gymnasium基准，包含3077维部分观测、478动作的掩码离散动作空间、5种竞争标准原型、3种奖励方案，以及一个手动的战略变量结构因果模型(SCM)。该基准将因果变量、SCM预测的干预效果和每因子信用追踪作为第一类指标，首次将因果归因、跨原型迁移和政策审计作为核心评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果强化学习缺乏基准测试，无法同时满足以下复杂系统的需求：序列决策、隐藏信息、大规模掩码动作空间、以及明确的因果结构。

Method: 提出CGFA-PPO（因果图-分解优势PPO）作为参考因果智能体，使用胜率的SCM父节点作为因子对齐的评论家目标，并引入干预校准损失。

Result: 掩码PPO和CGFA-PPO达到具有竞争力的同分布胜率并显著超越随机基线；因子校准轨迹和留一法迁移差距揭示了标量胜率无法呈现的诊断结构。

Conclusion: MTG-Causal-RL为因果RL、世界模型和LLM-agent研究提供了一个共享测试平台，能够同时研究：掩码动作空间下的因果归因、跨原型结构迁移、以及基于SCM的政策可审计性。

Abstract: Causal reinforcement learning (RL) lacks benchmarks for complex systems that combine sequential decision making, hidden information, large masked action spaces, and explicit causal structure. We introduce MTG-Causal-RL, a Gymnasium benchmark built on Magic: The Gathering with a 3,077-dimensional partial observation, a 478-action masked discrete action space, five competitive Standard archetypes, three reward schemes, and a hand-specified Structural Causal Model (SCM) over strategic variables. Every episode exposes causal variables, SCM-predicted intervention effects, and per-factor credit traces, making causal credit assignment, leave-one-out cross-archetype transfer, and policy auditability first-class metrics. We adapt a panel of reference baselines: random, heuristic, masked PPO, a causal-world-model PPO variant, and an architecture-matched scalar control. We propose Causal Graph-Factored Advantage PPO (CGFA-PPO) as a reference causal agent that uses SCM parents of win probability as factor-aligned critic targets with an intervention-calibration loss. All comparisons use paired seeds, paired-bootstrap confidence intervals, and Holm-Bonferroni correction within pre-registered families. Masked PPO and CGFA-PPO reach competitive in-distribution win rates and exceed the random baseline; per-factor calibration trajectories and leave-one-out transfer gaps expose diagnostic structure that scalar win rate alone cannot. We release the benchmark, reference-baseline results, and full evaluation protocol openly. By coupling a strategically rich, partially observed domain with an explicit causal interface and statistical protocol, MTG-Causal-RL gives causal-RL, world-model, and LLM-agent research a shared testbed for questions current benchmarks cannot pose together: causal credit assignment under masked action spaces, structural transfer across archetypes, and SCM-grounded policy auditability.

</details>


### [179] [Normalized Architectures are Natively 4-Bit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06067)
*Maxim Fishman,Brian Chmiel,Ron Banner,Daniel Soudry,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: nGPT通过将权重和隐藏表示约束到单位超球面，增强了模型对低精度算术的鲁棒性，无需Hadamard变换或按张量缩放等干预即可实现稳定的NVFP4训练，在1.2B dense模型和高达3B/30B参数的MoE模型上验证有效，且随着隐藏维度增长优势递增。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练大语言模型需要4位精度以提高效率，但标准架构在低精度下需要额外干预（如Hadamard变换和逐张量缩放）来维持模型质量。

Method: 提出nGPT架构，将权重和隐藏表示约束到单位超球面，利用点积在不同架构中的信号累积差异实现鲁棒性。

Result: 在1.2B dense模型和混合（Mamba-Transformer）MoE模型（最高3B/30B参数）上验证了稳定端到端NVFP4训练，无需额外干预。

Conclusion: nGPT的超球面约束增强了元素乘积间的弱正相关性，使信号在隐藏维度上constructively累积，而噪声继续平均消散，从而获得更高的有效信噪比和更平坦的损失景观。该效应随隐藏维度增长而增强，暗示在大规模场景下优势更大。

Abstract: Training large language models at 4-bit precision is critical for efficiency. We show that nGPT, an architecture that constrains weights and hidden representations to the unit hypersphere, is inherently more robust to low-precision arithmetic. This removes the need for interventions-such as applying random Hadamard transforms and performing per-tensor scaling calculations-to preserve model quality, and it enables stable end-to-end NVFP4 training. We validate this approach on both a 1.2B dense model and hybrid (Mamba-Transformer) MoE models of up to 3B/30B parameters. We trace this robustness to the dot product: while quantization noise remains largely uncorrelated in both standard and normalized architectures, the signal behaves differently. In nGPT, the hypersphere constraint enhances weak positive correlations among the element-wise products, leading to a constructive accumulation of the signal across the hidden dimension while the noise continues to average out. This yields a higher effective signal-to-noise ratio and a flatter loss landscape, with the effect strengthening as the hidden dimension grows, suggesting increasing advantages at scale. A reference implementation is available at https://github.com/anonymous452026/ngpt-nvfp4

</details>


### [180] [Understanding diffusion models requires rethinking (again) generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06077)
*Pierre Marion,Yu-Han Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文认为理解扩散模型的泛化需要超越经典统计学习理论和监督学习中的"良性过拟合"范式的新理论框架。扩散模型中记忆化与泛化是不相容的——完全记忆训练集的模型只能生成复制品而非新数据。文章呼吁研究重心从解释"扩散模型为何不记忆化"转向探究"模型在前记忆化阶段实际学到了什么"，并通过CIFAR-10数据集进行实证研究来支持这一立场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典统计学习理论和监督学习中发展出的"良性过拟合"范式无法有效解释扩散模型的泛化现象。在扩散模型中，对训练数据的记忆化与生成新样本本质上是不相容的，这要求建立全新的理论框架来理解其泛化机制。

Method: 对CIFAR-10数据集上训练的扩散模型进行实证研究，将发现归纳为具体的开放性问题。

Result: 尽管记忆化与生成新数据在理论上不相容，但实际扩散模型仍表现出泛化能力。研究揭示了容量限制、优化过程的隐式正则化以及架构归纳偏置等因素可能共同作用于此现象。

Conclusion: 领域研究应从解释"扩散模型为何不记忆化"转向探究"模型在前记忆化阶段实际学习什么"。需建立新理论框架，并通过实证研究提出关键开放问题以推动对该领域的理解。

Abstract: This position paper argues that understanding generalization in diffusion models requires fundamentally new theoretical frameworks that go beyond both classical statistical learning theory and the benign overfitting paradigm developed for supervised learning. In diffusion models, unlike in supervised learning, memorization of training data and generalization to novel samples are incompatible: a model that has fully memorized its training set generates copies rather than novel data. Several theoretical explanations for why practical diffusion models nevertheless generalize have been proposed, based on capacity limitations, implicit regularization from optimization, or architectural inductive biases, but their interactions remain unclear. We argue that the field should pivot from explaining why the diffusion models do not memorize to investigating what the model actually learns during pre-memorization phase. To highlight our stance, we conduct empirical study of diffusion models trained on CIFAR-10, and we distill the findings into concrete open questions that we believe are key to improve understanding of generalization in diffusion models.

</details>


### [181] [Fast Gauss-Newton for Multiclass Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06081)
*Mikalai Korbit,Mario Zanon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将多类softmax交叉熵的广义Gauss-Newton(GGN)精确分解为true-vs-rest项和类内协方差项，提出Fast Gauss-Newton(FGN)仅保留前者作为正定下近似。FGN在二分类时精确，且可通过共轭梯度法高效求解，实验验证其在竞品质量集中或阻尼较大时表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多类softmax交叉熵中，完整的广义Gauss-Newton(GGN)曲率通过softmax协方差耦合所有输出logits，随着类别数增长，曲率向量乘积的缩放变得更加困难。

Method: 1) 将多类GGN精确分解为true-vs-rest项和类内协方差项；2) FGN仅保留前者，drop掉后者作为近似；3) 利用FGN曲率结构，将阻尼更新转化为等价的白化行空间系统；4) 使用共轭梯度法通过Jacobian-vector和vector-Jacobian乘积进行无矩阵求解。

Result: FGN在竞品质量集中或阻尼较大时最接近完整softmax GGN；随着被丢弃的类内协方差增大而偏离。通过针对机制实验和固定特征多类头的评估验证了分解预测。

Conclusion: FGN提供了一个实用的近似方案，在竞品质量集中或阻尼较大时效果最佳。该方法通过矩阵-free的共轭梯度求解，使得高类别数场景下的二阶优化变得可行。

Abstract: In multiclass softmax cross-entropy, the full generalized Gauss-Newton (GGN) curvature couples all output logits through the softmax covariance, making curvature-vector products harder to scale as the number of classes grows. We show that the standard multiclass GGN can be decomposed exactly into a true-vs-rest term and a positive semidefinite within-competitor covariance term. Fast Gauss-Newton (FGN) retains the first term and drops the second, yielding a positive semidefinite under-approximation of the multiclass GGN that is exact for binary classification. The derivation uses an exact true-vs-rest scalar-margin representation of softmax cross-entropy: the loss and gradient are unchanged, and the approximation enters only at the curvature level. Exploiting the FGN curvature structure, the damped update can be written as an equivalent whitened row-space system with one row per mini-batch example. We solve this system matrix-free by conjugate gradient using Jacobian-vector and vector-Jacobian products of the scalar margin map. Targeted mechanism experiments and an evaluation on a fixed-feature multiclass head support the predictions from the decomposition: FGN stays closest to the full softmax GGN when competitor mass is concentrated or damping is large, and deviates as the dropped within-competitor covariance grows.

</details>


### [182] [Beyond Autoregressive RTG: Conditioning via Injection Outside Sequential Modeling in Decision Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06104)
*Yongyi Wang,Hanyu Liu,Lingfeng Li,Bozhou Chen,Ang Li,Qirui Zheng,Xionghui Yang,Chucai Wang,Wenxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SlimDT通过将RTG信息从自回归token流中解耦并注入到状态表示中，将序列长度缩短三分之一，在D4RL基准上超越标准Decision Transformer并达到SOTA性能，实现计算效率与任务性能的双重提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策转换器（DT）将离线强化学习形式化为自回归序列建模，通过从回报待到（RTG）、状态和动作的token序列中预测动作取得了良好效果。然而，RTG是一个标量，概括了未来奖励信息，其信息量远少于典型的状态或动作向量，却消耗相同计算资源。更糟的是，Transformer的自注意力成本随序列长度呈二次增长，将RTG作为独立token会产生不必要的计算开销。

Method: SlimDT将RTG从自回归序列中移除，改为在序列建模步骤之前将RTG信息注入到状态表示中，使Transformer仅需处理紧凑的（状态，动作）序列，从而将序列长度缩短至原来的三分之一。

Result: 序列长度减少三分之一，推理效率直接提升。在D4RL基准测试中，SlimDT在多种任务上超越标准DT，并达到与现有最先进方法相当的性能水平。

Conclusion: SlimDT通过将稀疏的条件信号（RTG）与信息丰富的序列（状态-动作）解耦，实现了计算效率提升和任务性能提高的双重收益。SlimDT在D4RL基准测试中超越了标准DT，并达到了与现有最先进方法相当的性能水平。

Abstract: Decision Transformer (DT) formulates offline reinforcement learning as autoregressive sequence modeling, achieving promising results by predicting actions from a sequence of Return-to-Go (RTG), state, and action tokens. However, RTG is a scalar that summarizes future rewards, containing far less information than typical state or action vectors, yet it consumes the same computational budget per token. Worse, the self-attention cost of Transformers grows quadratically with sequence length, so including RTG as a separate token adds unnecessary overhead. We propose SlimDT, which removes RTG from the autoregressive sequence. Instead, we inject RTG information into the state representations before the sequential modeling step, allowing the Transformer to process only a compact (state, action) sequence. This reduces the sequence length by one-third, directly improving inference efficiency. On the D4RL benchmark, SlimDT surpasses standard DT across various tasks and achieves performance comparable to existing state-of-the-art methods. Decoupling a sparse conditioning signal from an information-rich sequence thus yields both computational gains and higher task performance.

</details>


### [183] [Matrix-Valued Optimism is Matrix-Valued Augmentation: Additive Hybrid Designs for Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06141)
*Jiayi Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了增广拉格朗日与乐观原始对偶方法在矩阵值校正层面的等价性，提出加性原理并利用设计自由度推导出闭式混合校正规则，实验验证了混合策略的有效性，但指出该方法在强病态问题上的固有局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增广拉格朗日方法和乐观原始对偶方法通过不同机制稳定等式约束优化：前者增加约束依赖的原始曲率，后者增加对偶记忆。现有关于标量参数等价性的工作需要推广到矩阵情形以统一理解并利用设计自由度。

Method: 通过将标量参数等价性推广到矩阵值校正情形，证明了对称矩阵参数的理想原始轨迹仅取决于校正矩阵的总和而非其在两通道间的分配方式，从而暴露了设计自由度。结合局部谱权重的矩阵校正分裂与步长选择方法，推导了闭式混合规则。

Result: 实验表明混合设计策略优于纯增广和纯乐观端点方法，能紧密跟踪网格搜索混合预言器，在轻度至中度病态条件下与一阶原始对偶基线方法具有竞争力。局限性在于精确抵消需要随约束雅可比矩阵病态性增加而增大矩阵校正量。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了增广拉格朗日与乐观原始对偶方法在矩阵值校正上的等价性框架，并提出了实用的混合设计规则，在约束雅可比矩阵病态程度为轻度至中度时具有良好的实用价值，但精确抵消能力随病态性增加而下降。

Abstract: Augmented Lagrangian and optimistic primal--dual methods stabilize equality-constrained optimization through seemingly different mechanisms: the former adds constraint-dependent primal curvature, while the latter adds dual memory. Recent work has shown that these mechanisms are equivalent for scalar parameters. We extend this equivalence to matrix-valued correction. We prove an additivity principle: for symmetric matrix parameters, the ideal primal trajectory depends only on the summed correction matrix, not on how it is split between augmented and optimistic channels. This exposes a design freedom: algebraically equivalent decompositions can have different finite-step feasibility because augmented correction affects primal curvature, whereas optimistic correction affects the scale of the dual memory correction. We formulate the resulting step-size-limited design problem and derive a closed-form hybrid rule that selects a matrix correction, splits it between the two channels, and chooses primal and dual steps using local spectral weights. Experiments on nonlinear equality-constrained problems with controlled constraint-Jacobian conditioning show that the hybrid design improves over pure augmented and pure optimistic endpoints, closely tracks a grid-search hybrid oracle, and is competitive with first-order primal--dual baselines under mild-to-moderate ill-conditioning. The experiments also identify the expected limitation: exact cancellation requires increasingly large matrix corrections as the constraint Jacobian becomes ill-conditioned.

</details>


### [184] [Unifying Goal-Conditioned RL and Unsupervised Skill Learning via Control-Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06145)
*Alireza Modirshanechi,Benjamin Eysenbach,Peter Dayan,Eric Schulz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: The paper theoretically unifies goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) and mutual information skill learning (MISL) under control maximization, shows that three canonical GCRL formulations are inequivalent but share a goal-sensitivity interpretation, and proves that MISL objectives are bounded by formulation-specific downstream goal-sensitivities, providing theoretical foundation and practical guidance for RL pretraining.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Despite empirical success of unsupervised pretraining in GCRL, its theoretical foundations are poorly understood. It remains unclear why skills learned through MISL should support goal-reaching, complicated by the fact that both GCRL and MISL are umbrella terms with distinct criteria and objectives.

Method: Theoretical analysis unifying GCRL and MISL as instances of control maximization, proving inequivalence of three canonical GCRL formulations, and establishing bounds between MISL objectives and formulation-specific downstream goal-sensitivities.

Result: Three canonical GCRL formulations are fundamentally inequivalent (can induce incompatible optimal policies in the same environment). However, they share a common interpretation: well-performing goal-conditioned policies have goal-sensitive future trajectories. MISL objectives are bounded by formulation-specific downstream goal-sensitivities, establishing precise correspondence between MISL methods and downstream GCRL tasks.

Conclusion: MISL objectives can be matched to specific downstream GCRL tasks based on goal-sensitivity bounds, providing practical guidance for selecting pretraining objectives for specific downstream tasks.

Abstract: Unsupervised pretraining has driven empirical advances in goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL), but its theoretical foundations remain poorly understood. In particular, an influential class of methods, mutual information skill learning (MISL), discovers behaviorally diverse skills that can later be used for downstream goal-reaching. However, it remains a theoretical mystery why skills learned through MISL should support goal-reaching. A subtle challenge is that both GCRL and MISL are umbrella terms: different GCRL tasks use distinct criteria for measuring goal-reaching performance, while different MISL methods optimize distinct notions of behavioral diversity. We address this challenge and unify GCRL and MISL as instances of control maximization. We identify three canonical GCRL formulations and prove that they are fundamentally inequivalent: they can induce incompatible optimal policies even in the same environment. Nevertheless, they all share a common interpretation: a well-performing goal-conditioned policy is one whose future trajectory is highly sensitive to the commanded goal, with the precise notion of sensitivity determined by the GCRL formulation. Noting that MISL objectives can be understood as measures of skill-sensitivity akin to goal-sensitivity, we show that MISL objectives are bounded by formulation-specific downstream goal-sensitivities. These bounds establish a precise correspondence between MISL methods and downstream GCRL tasks: for every GCRL formulation, there exists a matching MISL objective for which more diverse skills afford greater downstream goal sensitivity. Our results thus lay a theoretical foundation for RL pretraining and have important practical implications, such as suggesting which pretraining objectives to use when a user cares about a specific class of downstream tasks.

</details>


### [185] [AdaGamma: State-Dependent Discounting for Temporal Adaptation in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06149)
*Yaomin Wang,Jianting Pan,Ran Tian,Xiaoyang Li,Yu Zhang,Hengle Qin,Tianshu YU*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaGamma，一种在深度行动者-评论家框架中实现状态依赖折扣的实用方法。通过回报一致性目标防止退化，结合SAC和PPO验证了在连续控制任务和真实物流场景中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度强化学习方法使用固定折扣因子，无法适应不同状态的最优规划时域。状态依赖折扣虽具概念吸引力，但直接实现会导致训练不稳定和TD-error崩溃。

Method: AdaGamma提出了一种实用的深度行动者-评论家方法，核心创新包括：(1) 学习状态依赖的折扣函数；(2) 引入回报一致性目标作为正则化手段，防止目标操纵导致退化；(3) 理论分析了状态依赖折扣诱导的Bellman算子的基本适定性条件。

Result: AdaGamma成功集成到SAC和PPO框架中，在连续控制基准测试中取得一致改进，并在京东物流平台在线A/B测试中获得统计显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: AdaGamma通过将状态依赖折扣与回报一致性目标相结合，在深度强化学习中实现了一种既灵活又稳定的折扣机制。该方法在理论上完善了变折扣Bellman算子的适定性分析，在实践上验证了其在连续控制基准和真实物流平台上的有效性，为深度RL中的时域规划提供了一种新思路。

Abstract: The discount factor in reinforcement learning controls both the effective planning horizon and the strength of bootstrapping, yet most deep RL methods use a single fixed value across all states. While state-dependent discounting is conceptually appealing, naive deep actor--critic implementations can become unstable and degenerate toward TD-error collapse. We propose AdaGamma, a practical deep actor--critic method for state-dependent discounting that learns a state-dependent discount function together with a return-consistency objective to regularize the induced backup structure. On the theory side, we analyze the Bellman operator induced by state-dependent discounting and establish its basic well-posedness properties under suitable conditions. Empirically, AdaGamma integrates into both SAC and PPO, yielding consistent improvements on continuous-control benchmarks, and achieves statistically significant gains in an online A/B test on the JD Logistics platform. These results suggest that state-dependent discounting can be made effective in deep RL when coupled with a return-consistency objective that prevents degenerate target manipulation.

</details>


### [186] [Entropy-Regularized Adjoint Matching for Offline RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06156)
*Abdelghani Ghanem,Mounir Ghogho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ME-AM是一种将最大熵对抗匹配与混合行为先验相结合的离线强化学习框架，通过Mirror Descent熵最大化缓解流行度偏差，同时扩展几何支撑以实现对分布外高奖励区域的探索，在保持流匹配模型表达性的同时解决了传统方法的核心限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QAM方法虽通过连续伴随方法稳定策略优化，但存在两个根本性限制：流行度偏差（popularity bias）会压制低密度区域的高奖励动作，以及支撑绑定（support binding）限制了对离分布区域的探索。现有残差高斯策略等解决方案会重新引入单峰分布的表达性瓶颈。

Method: ME-AM提出两个核心机制：(1) 基于Mirror Descent的熵最大化目标，通过对抗流行度偏差使策略能够从低密度高奖励区域提取最优动作；(2) Mixture Behavior Prior（混合行为先验），在数学上扩展了流向量的几何支撑，使模型能够探索分布外的高奖励区域，同时保持生成向量场的绝对连续性。

Result: 实验表明，ME-AM在多种稀疏奖励连续控制环境中达到或超越当前最优方法的性能。

Conclusion: ME-AM通过将最大熵原则与混合行为先验相结合，在不牺牲流匹配模型表达性的前提下，有效解决了离线强化学习中的流行度偏差和支撑绑定问题。该框架在稀疏奖励连续控制环境中展现出具有竞争力的性能，为将生成式流模型应用于离线RL提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Integrating expressive generative policies, such as flow-matching models, into offline reinforcement learning (RL) allows agents to capture complex, multi-modal behaviors. While Q-learning with Adjoint Matching (QAM) stabilizes policy optimization via the continuous adjoint method, it remains inherently bound to the fixed behavior distribution. This dependence induces a \textit{popularity bias} that can suppress high-reward actions in low-density regions, and creates a \textit{support binding} that restricts off-manifold exploration. Existing workarounds, such as appending \textit{residual} Gaussian policies, often re-introduce the expressivity bottlenecks associated with unimodal distributions. In this work, we propose \textit{Maximum Entropy Adjoint Matching} (ME-AM), a unified framework that addresses these limitations within the continuous flow formulation. ME-AM incorporates two mechanisms: (1) a Mirror Descent entropy maximization objective that mitigates the popularity bias to facilitate the extraction of optimal policies from offline datasets, and (2) a \textit{Mixture Behavior Prior} that mathematically broadens the geometric support to encompass out-of-distribution high-reward regions. By exploring this extended geometry, ME-AM identifies robust actions while preserving the absolute continuity of the generative vector field. Empirically, ME-AM demonstrates competitive or superior performance compared to prior state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods across a diverse suite of sparse-reward continuous control environments.

</details>


### [187] [In-Context Black-Box Optimization with Unreliable Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06187)
*Nicolas Samuel Blumer,Julien Martinelli,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究反馈辅助的上下文黑盒优化（FICBO），通过预训练Transformer模型结合历史观测和辅助反馈信号来改进候选点选择，并在合成和真实任务上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒优化中辅助反馈可能存在偏差或误导性，现有方法难以跨任务泛化且假设优化历史是唯一信号。本文旨在构建能感知多源反馈并自适应评估其可靠性的优化框架。

Method: 提出结构化反馈先验建模不同反馈源在访问性、相关性和失真程度上的差异，基于此预训练反馈感知Transformer；测试时通过对比观测目标值与辅助信号在上下文中估计源可靠性。

Result: FICBO在合成和真实任务中有效利用有价值反馈，同时对弱误导性源保持鲁棒，性能优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 该方法为反馈-informed优化提供了可解释的跨任务适应框架，模型能习得对测试时反馈源的感知能力。

Abstract: Black-box optimization in science and engineering often comes with side information: experts, simulators, pretrained predictors, or heuristics can suggest which candidates look promising. This information can accelerate search, but it can also be biased, input-dependent, or misleading. Feedback-aware BO methods typically handle one task at a time, limiting their ability to generalize over multiple sources of feedback. In-context optimizers address cross-task adaptation, but usually assume that optimization history is the only available signal at test time. We study feedback-informed in-context black-box optimization (FICBO), where a pretrained optimizer conditions on both the observed history and cheap auxiliary feedback for the current candidate set. We introduce a structured feedback prior that models how feedback sources vary in their access, relevance, and distortion relative to the true objective, and use it to pretrain a feedback-aware transformer. At test time, the model estimates source reliability in context by comparing observed objective values with auxiliary signals, improving query selection. On synthetic and real-world tasks, FICBO effectively exploits informative feedback while remaining robust to weak or misleading sources, improving over other baselines. Empirical investigations further illustrate how the model perceives test-time sources, offering insights into its interpretability and decision-making process.

</details>


### [188] [Constrained Contextual Bandits with Adversarial Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06190)
*Dhruv Sarkar,Abhishek Sinha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究预算受限的对抗性上下文老虎机问题，在继续设置下同时控制遗憾值和预算约束违反。基于SquareCB框架提出模块化框架，利用在线回归预言机将约束问题规约为标准无约束问题，在对抗性上下文场景下获得优于先前工作的理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要针对随机上下文场景，而在实际应用中上下文可能具有对抗性；需要同时控制累积成本约束违反程度。

Method: 基于SquareCB框架，提出模块化框架，利用在线回归预言机(online regression oracles)，将约束问题规约为标准无约束上下文老虎机问题，通过自适应定义的替代奖励函数实现。

Result: 对于更一般的对抗性上下文获得改进的理论保证，并提供高效算法和简洁透明的分析。

Conclusion: 实验验证部分未在摘要中体现

Abstract: We study budget-constrained contextual bandits with adversarial contexts, where each action yields a random reward and incurs a random cost. We adopt the standard realizability assumption: conditioned on the observed context, rewards and costs are drawn independently from fixed distributions whose expectations belong to known function classes. We focus on the continuing setting, in which the algorithm operates over the entire horizon even after the budget for cumulative cost is exhausted. In this setting, the objective is to simultaneously control regret and the violation of the budget constraint. Building on the seminal $\mathsf{SquareCB}$ framework of Foster et al. [2018], we propose a simple and modular framework that leverages online regression oracles to reduce the constrained problem to a standard unconstrained contextual bandit problem with adaptively defined surrogate reward functions. In contrast to prior works, which focus on stochastic contexts, our reduction yields improved guarantees for more general adversarial contexts, together with an efficient algorithm with a compact and transparent analysis.

</details>


### [189] [Bandit Learning in General Open Multi-agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06202)
*Mengfan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了开放系统中的多臂老虎机学习问题，其中智能体可以随时进入和离开系统。文章提出了一个统一的问题框架，通过引入"预训练度"、"稳定性"和"全局动态遗憾"等新概念来刻画系统特性，并开发了具有可证明收敛保证的全局UCB算法。研究表明，遗憾主要由新智能体的预训练度线性决定，在稳定系统下由最优臂识别时间和智能体模式主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放系统老虎机研究存在结构性假设过强、与实际不符的问题。开放系统中智能体的进出行为导致内生非平稳性、信息累积模式复杂、遗憾尺度随时间范围增长，这些挑战需要新的学习范式来应对。

Method: 1) 构建统一开放系统老虎机问题框架，包含异构奖励和一般智能体模式；2) 提出三个核心概念：预训练度（衡量新智能体进入时携带的信息量）、稳定性（衡量新智能体对系统的冲击程度）、全局动态遗憾（比较所有活跃智能体累积期望奖励与时变最优臂的差距；3) 开发认证的全局UCB学习算法。

Result: 遗憾上界显示：进入不确定性通过预训练度线性影响遗憾；在稳定机制下，遗憾由识别持久最优臂所需时间及智能体模式共同决定。通过hard实例的下界证明了这些依赖关系的紧性。

Conclusion: 本文为开放系统老虎机学习提供了理论基础，证明了预训练度和稳定性是决定遗憾的关键因素，且相关依赖关系具有最优性。

Abstract: Recent developments in digital platforms have highlighted the prevalence of open systems, where agents can arrive and depart over time. While bandit learning in open systems has recently received initial attention, existing work imposes structural assumptions that are frequently violated in practice. A learning paradigm for general open systems creates fresh challenges: newly arriving agents induce endogenous non-stationarity; agent patterns determine how quickly information accumulates; and new agents make regret scale further with the time horizon. To this end, we formulate a unified open-system bandit problem with general dynamics, including heterogeneous rewards and general agent patterns. We introduce new concepts to capture the inherent complexities: the \emph{pre-training degree} of new agents quantifies how much information an agent carries upon entry, \emph{stability} measures the impact of new agents on the system, and \emph{global dynamic regret} compares the cumulative expected reward of all active agents with that of the varying optimal arms. We develop certified global-UCB learning methodologies with provable guarantees. Our regret bounds reveal that entry uncertainty enters linearly via the pre-training degree, while in stable regimes, regret is governed by the time needed to identify a persistent optimal arm, as well as by the agent patterns. We further show that these dependencies are tight via lower bounds in hard instances.

</details>


### [190] [Playing the network backward: A Game Theoretic Attribution Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06212)
*Jakob Paul Zimmermann,Jim Berend,Georg Loho,Sebastian Lapuschkin,Wojciech Samek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: A unified game-theoretic framework for backward attribution methods in neural networks, recasting gradient/LRP methods as game equilibria integrals, enabling principled design of attribution rules with improved performance on ViT-B/16.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Backward attribution methods (gradients, LRP, transformer-specific rules) lack a shared framework to compare underlying backward calculations, making systematic analysis and improvement difficult.

Method: Recasts backward attribution as a two-player game on an extended network graph (building on ReLU Net Game). Derives gradients and full alpha-beta-LRP family as integrals over game trajectories under specific equilibria. Specifies explanation properties (localization focus, noise robustness, stable attention routing) as game-theoretic concepts (policy regularization, risk aversion, extended action sets), translating them into novel adaptations of backward rules.

Result: Attribution maps become projections of trajectory distributions. The framework enables principled derivation of existing methods and specification-driven design of new backward rules with validated performance improvements on vision transformers.

Conclusion: A novel adaptation of alpha-beta-LRP (selected from the game-theoretic framework) outperforms prior transformer-specific backward methods across all considered localisation metrics on ViT-B/16, demonstrating the practical value of the proposed unified framework.

Abstract: Attribution methods explain which input features drive a model's prediction, making them central to model debugging and mechanistic interpretability. Yet backward attribution methods, including gradients, LRP, and transformer-specific rules, lack a shared framework in which to compare the underlying backward calculations. We introduce such a framework by recasting backward attribution as a two-player game on an extended network graph, building on Gaubert and Vlassopoulos' ReLU Net Game. Gradients and the full alpha-beta-LRP family arise as integrals over game trajectories under specific equilibria, so attribution maps become projections of trajectory distributions rather than the primary object. Desired explanation properties, such as localisation focus, robustness to input noise, or stable attention routing, can be specified as game-theoretic concepts, including policy regularization, risk aversion, and extended action sets, and translate directly into novel adaptations of the well-known backward rules. On ViT-B/16, one such selected adaptation of alpha-beta-LRP outperforms prior transformer-specific backward methods across all considered localisation metrics.

</details>


### [191] [Memory Inception: Latent-Space KV Cache Manipulation for Steering LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06225)
*Andy Zeyi Liu,Michael Zhang,Ilana Greenberg,Adam Alnasser,Lucas Baker,John Sous*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Memory Inception (MI) 是一种无需训练的 LLM 引导方法，通过在选定层插入文本衍生的 KV 银行实现潜在注意力空间引导，兼顾提示引导的强控制力和激活引导的紧凑性，在人格引导、可更新指导和结构化推理任务上表现优异，KV 存储减少达 118 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 引导方法存在固有缺陷：指令提示虽控制力强但逐层缓存导致长对话臃肿；激活引导虽紧凑但控制力弱且不支持结构化提醒。

Method: MI 将引导重新定义为选择性 KV 分配问题，仅在模型路由到的位置注入潜在槽位，在选定层插入文本衍生的 KV 银行，而非在整个提示缓存中物化提醒内容。

Result: 人格引导任务达到最佳控制-漂移权衡；可更新指导支持对话中行为切换而不重写可见转录；在 HARDMath 和 PHYSICS 的 10/12 主题×模式单元上超越可见提示；KV 存储减少最高达 118 倍。

Conclusion: MI 特别适用于引导内容需持久化、结构化或保留在可见提示中成本高昂的场景。

Abstract: Steering large language models (LLMs) is usually done by either instruction prompting or activation steering. Prompting often gives strong control, but caches guidance tokens at every layer and can clutter long interactions; activation steering is compact but typically weaker and does not support large structured reminders. We introduce memory inception (MI), a training-free method that steers in latent attention space by inserting text-derived key-value (KV) banks only at selected layers. Rather than materializing reminder content throughout the prompt cache, MI treats steering as selective KV allocation, injecting latent slots only where the model routes to them. On matched personality-steering tasks, MI gives the best overall control--drift trade-off, remaining competitive with prompting while consistently outperforming CAA. On updateable guidance, MI supports mid-conversation behavior shifts without rewriting the visible transcript, achieving the highest post-shift alignment on Qwen3. On structured reasoning, MI outperforms visible prompting on HARDMath and PHYSICS (10/12 subject$\times$mode cells), serving as proxies for structured reasoning in verifiable domains, while cutting content-matched KV storage by up to 118$\times$. These results position MI as a powerful steering method when guidance is persistent, structured, or expensive to keep in the visible transcript.

</details>


### [192] [Soft Deterministic Policy Gradient with Gaussian Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06228)
*Hyunjun Na,Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Deterministic policy gradient (DPG) is widely utilized for continuous control; however, it inherently relies on the differentiability of the critic with respect to the action during policy updates. This assumption is violated in practical control problems involving sparse or discrete rewards, leading to ill-defined policy gradients and unstable learning. To address these challenges, we propose a principled alternative based on a smoothed Bellman equation formulated via Gaussian smoothing. Specifically, we define a novel action-value function based on a smoothed Bellman equation and derive the soft deterministic policy gradient (Soft-DPG). Our formulation eliminates explicit dependence on critic action-gradients and ensures that the gradient remains well-defined even for non-smooth Q-functions. We instantiate this framework into a deep reinforcement learning algorithm, which we call soft deep deterministic policy gradient (Soft DDPG). Empirical evaluations on standard continuous control benchmarks and their discretized-reward variants show that Soft DDPG remains competitive in dense-reward settings and provides clear gains in most discretized-reward environments, where standard DDPG is more sensitive to irregular critic landscapes.

</details>


### [193] [Band Together: Untargeted Adversarial Training with Multimodal Coordination against Evasion-based Promotion Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06238)
*Guanmeng Xian,Ning Yang,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态推荐系统中规避式推广攻击的防御问题，发现并解决了跨模态梯度不匹配的挑战，提出UAT-MC方法通过梯度对齐协调视觉和文本模态的扰动，在提升鲁棒性的同时兼顾推荐准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法主要针对单模态设置和投毒式威胁，对规避式威胁的研究不足。多模态推荐系统在多用户推广场景下存在跨模态梯度不匹配问题：视觉和文本扰动因不同用户群体占主导而被优化在不一致方向上，导致攻击有效性降低，并使鲁棒训练低估了最坏情况风险。

Method: 提出了无目标对抗训练与多模态协调方法（UAT-MC）。该方法将所有商品视为潜在攻击目标以应对规避性攻击中目标未知的挑战，并引入梯度对齐机制显式纠正视觉和文本模态的梯度不一致，实现跨模态同步扰动，从而最大化对抗强度以进行鲁棒训练。

Result: 大量实验表明，UAT-MC在显著提升对推广攻击的鲁棒性的同时，在防御-准确率权衡中保持了可接受的推荐性能。

Conclusion: UAT-MC通过梯度对齐机制协调多模态扰动，有效解决了交叉模态梯度不匹配问题，显著提升了模型对规避性推广攻击的鲁棒性，同时在防御-准确率权衡中保持了可接受的推荐性能。

Abstract: Multimodal recommender systems exploit visual and textual signals to alleviate data sparsity, but this also makes them more vulnerable to evasion-based promotion attacks. Existing defenses are largely limited to single-modal settings and mainly focus on poisoning-based threats, leaving evasion-based threats underexplored. In this work, we first identify a cross-modal gradient mismatch under the multi-user promotion setting, where visual and textual perturbations are optimized in inconsistent directions due to the dominance of distinct user groups. This phenomenon dilutes the attack effectiveness and leads robust training to underestimate worst-case risks. To address this issue, we propose Untargeted Adversarial Training with Multimodal Coordination (UAT-MC). UAT-MC tackles the challenge of unknown targeted items in evasion-based attacks (as opposed to poisoning-based attacks) by treating all items as potential targets, and introduces a gradient alignment mechanism to explicitly correct this mismatch. This design ensures synchronized perturbations across modalities, thereby maximizing adversarial strength for robust training. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UAT-MC significantly improves robustness against promotion attacks while maintaining acceptable recommendation performance under the defense-accuracy trade-off. Code is available at https://github.com/gmXian/UAT-MC.

</details>


### [194] [When Graph Language Models Go Beyond Memorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06239)
*Masatsugu Yamada,Mahito Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了图语言模型是否真正学习到结构化规律还是仅记忆训练图，提出了一种结合频繁子图挖掘、图级自举基线和三级频率分层的诊断协议，在5个TU基准上的实验表明：大规模模型确实获得了超越记忆的结构化对齐，主要体现在高频模式；但低频模式覆盖仍然不足；这种模式在不同图序列化方式下均成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图语言模型领域缺乏有效区分"真正学习结构规律"与"仅记忆训练图"的方法，现有聚合保真度指标无法解决此问题，导致对模型能力的高估风险。

Method: 设计了校准诊断协议，包含三个核心组件：频繁子图挖掘、图级自举基线（模拟纯记忆场景）和三级频率分层；采用两种图序列化方式（DFS编码和动作序列）；在novel-only子集上进行固定样本分析。

Result: 小规模时模型表现与自举基线难以区分（主要记忆）；大规模（375万图）时逐字记忆急剧下降但排名相关性保持高位；高频模式被良好复现，稀有模式仍覆盖不足；两种序列化方式均呈现相同的尺度依赖交叉现象。

Conclusion: 图语言模型在大规模时可获得超越记忆的结构化规律，主要体现在高频 regime；低频模式的欠覆盖问题随规模增加仅轻微改善；该诊断框架有效且具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: It remains unclear whether graph language models learn structural regularities or merely memorize training graphs; this cannot be resolved by current aggregate fidelity metrics alone. We develop a calibrated diagnostic protocol that combines frequent subgraph mining, a graph-level bootstrap baseline, and three-level frequency stratification to disentangle memorization from structural alignment. Using this framework, we show that graph language models can acquire structural regularities beyond memorization at scale, primarily in the high-frequency regime. This is supported by the following empirical evidence: On five TU benchmarks, LLaMA-style graph language models reach high subgraph-rank correlation, yet their alignment is matched or exceeded by the memorization bootstrap in most cases. At small scale, under our bootstrap diagnostic, fidelity is largely indistinguishable from verbatim recall. In contrast, at large scale with 3.75M graphs, verbatim memorization drops sharply while rank correlation remains near ceiling. Crucially, in a separate fixed-subsample analysis, frequent subgraph mining restricted to the novel-only subset closely tracks the corresponding all-generation Spearman correlation, providing evidence that the alignment is not driven solely by verbatim recall. Across all scales, high-frequency patterns are well reproduced, while rare patterns remain poorly covered, and this deficit narrows only marginally as capacity increases. We observe the same scale-dependent crossover under two distinct graph serializations (canonical DFS code and action sequences), providing evidence of robustness in our analysis.

</details>


### [195] [Cumulative-Goodness Free-Riding in Forward-Forward Networks: Real, Repairable, but Not Accuracy-Dominant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06240)
*Amirhossein Yousefiramandi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文识别并形式化了Forward-Forward训练中的层免费搭车现象，通过三种局部修复方案有效解决了该优化病理，但实验表明该问题并非限制FF训练可达到准确率的主导因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在累积善良度变体中，后面的层可能继承前面层已部分完成的分类任务，导致层间任务分配不均，文章将这种现象形式化为"层免费搭车"（layer free-riding）问题。

Method: 在softplus FF准则下形式化了层间自由骑乘现象；研究了三种局部修复方案（逐块、难度门控、深度缩放），这些方案不依赖反向传播梯度即可恢复当前层的分离度量。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上深层分离统计获得4×-45×的显著提升，而精度变化小于1个百分点；Tiny ImageNet验证了层健康诊断与最终精度之间的定性差距；校准实验表明架构和数据增强选择对最终精度的影响大于训练规则修改。

Conclusion: 累积的"免费搭车"现象是一个真实存在且可修复的优化病理；然而，对于所研究的FF训练规则、架构和数据集而言，它并非限制可达到准确率的主导因素。

Abstract: Forward-Forward (FF) training allows each layer to learn from a local goodness criterion. In cumulative-goodness variants, however, later layers can inherit a task that earlier layers have already partially separated. We formalize this phenomenon as layer free-riding: under the softplus FF criterion, the class-discrimination gradient reaching block $d$ decays exponentially with the positive margin accumulated by preceding blocks. We then study three local remedies -- per-block, hardness-gated, and depth-scaled -- that recover current-layer separation measures without relying on backpropagated gradients. On CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, these remedies dramatically improve layer-separation statistics, with $4\times$--$45\times$ gains in deeper layers, while changing accuracy by less than one percentage point for non-degenerate training procedures. Tiny ImageNet provides a tougher cross-dataset check for our selected block-wise configuration and reveals the same qualitative gap between layer-health diagnostics and final accuracy. Calibration experiments further show that architecture and augmentation choices have a larger effect on final accuracy than the training-rule modifications studied here. Cumulative free-riding is therefore a real and repairable optimization pathology. Nonetheless, for the FF training rules, architectures, and datasets we study, it is not the dominant factor limiting achievable accuracy.

</details>


### [196] [A Note on TurboQuant and the Earlier DRIVE/EDEN Line of Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18555)
*Ran Ben-Basat,Yaniv Ben-Itzhak,Gal Mendelson,Michael Mitzenmacher,Amit Portnoy,Shay Vargaftik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇技术说明（technical note），澄清了TurboQuant与早期工作DRIVE和EDEN之间的关系。TurboQuant可视为EDEN的特例或次优版本，实验表明EDEN在所有测试场景下均优于TurboQuant。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清TurboQuant与EDEN/DRIVE之间的关系，纠正TurboQuant论文中可能存在的误解。EDEN的作者认为TurboQuant的工作并未充分认识到其与EDEN的继承关系，且TurboQuant的方法是EDEN的次优特例。

Method: 通过理论分析和实验验证进行比较。理论上分析TurboQuant_mse是固定S=1的EDEN特殊情况，TurboQuant_prod的三个次优来源（固定S=1、残差量化MSE差、直接量化优于链式量化）。实验上在TurboQuant论文的所有精度实验上进行对比。

Result: 有偏EDEN（优化S）比TurboQuant_mse更准确；无偏EDEN比TurboQuant_prod显著更准确（常超过1 bit，如2-bit EDEN优于3-bit TurboQuant_prod）；EDEN在所有测试设置中均优于TurboQuant。

Conclusion: TurboQuant是EDEN的次优特例，其提出的方法并未超越EDEN。TurboQuant_mse在大维度下趋近于有偏EDEN；TurboQuant_prod在三个维度上均次优于直接使用b-bit EDEN无偏量化。

Abstract: This note clarifies the relationship between the recent TurboQuant work and the earlier DRIVE (NeurIPS 2021) and EDEN (ICML 2022) schemes. DRIVE is a 1-bit quantizer that EDEN extended to any $b>0$ bits per coordinate; we refer to them collectively as EDEN.
  First, TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$ is a special case of EDEN obtained by fixing EDEN's scalar scale parameter to $S=1$. EDEN supports both biased and unbiased quantization, each optimized by a different $S$ (chosen via methods described in the EDEN works). The fixed choice $S=1$ used by TurboQuant is generally suboptimal, although the optimal $S$ for biased EDEN converges to $1$ as the dimension grows; accordingly TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$ approaches EDEN's behavior for large $d$.
  Second, TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$ combines a biased $(b-1)$-bit EDEN step with an unbiased 1-bit QJL quantization of the residual. It is suboptimal in three ways: (1) its $(b-1)$-bit step uses the suboptimal $S=1$; (2) its 1-bit unbiased residual quantization has worse MSE than (unbiased) 1-bit EDEN; (3) chaining a biased $(b-1)$-bit step with a 1-bit unbiased residual step is inferior to unbiasedly quantizing the input directly with $b$-bit EDEN.
  Third, some of the analysis in the TurboQuant work mirrors that of the EDEN works: both exploit the connection between random rotations and the shifted Beta distribution, use the Lloyd-Max algorithm, and note that Randomized Hadamard Transforms can replace uniform random rotations.
  Experiments support these claims: biased EDEN (with optimized $S$) is more accurate than TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$, and unbiased EDEN is markedly more accurate than TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$, often by more than a bit (e.g., 2-bit EDEN beats 3-bit TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$). We also repeat all accuracy experiments from the TurboQuant paper, showing that EDEN outperforms it in every setup we have tried.

</details>


### [197] [Structure-Preserving Gaussian Processes Via Discrete Euler-Lagrange Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06246)
*Jan-Hendrik Ewering,Kathrin Flaßkamp,Niklas Wahlström,Thomas B. Schön,Thomas Seel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出拉格朗日高斯过程（LGPs），一种利用离散强制欧拉-拉格朗日方程进行概率化、样本高效的动力学学习方法。该方法通过保持拉格朗日-达朗贝尔原理的几何结构来避免能量漂移，实现稳定的长期预测。核心创新在于可直接从离散位置数据学习，无需速度或动量信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动力学学习方法通常无法保证物理一致性，易产生能量漂移，导致长期预测不稳定。此外，许多实际场景（如动作捕捉、视觉伺服）仅提供位置测量数据，无法获取速度或动量信息，而现有方法大多依赖完整的状态信息。

Method: 1. 基于强制欧拉-拉格朗日方程构建线性算子，用于高斯过程条件化；2. 采用变分离散化格式保持几何结构；3. 在无外力情况下严格保持拉格朗日-达朗贝尔原理的几何结构；4. 实现无需速度/动量信息的纯位置数据学习。

Result: 在多种合成场景和真实世界案例（包括具有滞回特性的软体机器人）中进行验证，结果表明LGPs仅从稀疏位置数据即可学习到物理一致的动力学模型，具备不确定性量化能力，并实现稳定的长期预测。

Conclusion: LGPs成功结合了几何力学与概率学习，实现了样本高效、物理一致的未知动力学学习，扩展了高斯过程在仅位置测量场景中的应用，为机器人控制与仿真提供了可靠的数据驱动方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose Lagrangian Gaussian Processes (LGPs) for probabilistic and data-efficient learning of dynamics via discrete forced Euler-Lagrange equations. Importantly, the geometric structure of the Lagrange-d'Alembert principle, which governs the motion of dynamical systems, is preserved by construction in the absence of external forces. This allows learning physically consistent models that overcome erroneous drift in the system's energy, thereby providing stable long-term predictions. At the core of our approach lie linear operators for Gaussian process conditioning, constructed from discrete forced Euler-Lagrange equations and variational discretization schemes. Thereby and unlike prior work, the method enables learning dynamics from discrete position snapshots, i.e., without access to a system's velocities or momenta. This is particularly relevant for a large class of practical scenarios where only position measurements are available, for instance, in motion capture or visual servoing applications. We demonstrate the data-efficiency and generalization capabilities of the LGPs in various synthetic and real-world case studies, including a real-world soft robot with hysteresis. The experimental results underscore that the LGPs learn physically consistent dynamics with uncertainty quantification solely from sparse positional data and enable stable long-term predictions.

</details>


### [198] [The Role of Node Features in Graph Pooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06250)
*Jan von Pichowski,Alžbeta Hrabošová,Ingo Scholtes,Christopher Blöcker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究揭示图池化效果不佳的根本原因：节点特征与图拓扑的对齐程度是池化有效性的关键条件，当该条件得到满足时，池化可显著提升图分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图池化在图分类任务中的实证效果往往不如预期，表现与标准WL-1表达性GNN相比增益有限或不一致，因此需要理解造成这一差距的根本原因。

Method: 通过分析节点特征与图拓扑之间的交互作用及其对池化目标的影响，形式化定义了有效池化所需的节点特征条件，并提出了一种量化评估特征质量的指标。

Result: 实验验证表明，当节点特征与图拓扑结构良好对齐时，池化操作能够带来性能提升，并证明了所提出的特征质量要求在识别适合池化的数据集方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，当节点特征质量满足要求时，图池化能够有效提升图分类任务的性能；该工作为识别适合使用池化操作的数据集提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Graph pooling is commonly applied in graph classification, yet its empirical gains over standard WL-1 expressive GNNs are often marginal or inconsistent. We study this gap by analysing the interaction between node features and graph topology and their effect on pooling objectives. Our analysis reveals that pooling operators require node features that are well-aligned with the graph's topology -- a condition often overlooked and not guaranteed in empirical networks. We formalise fundamental requirements for node features to enable effective pooling, and introduce a quantitative measure of feature quality. Our empirical evaluation shows that, when these requirements are satisfied, pooling can be beneficial and improve performance on appropriate datasets.

</details>


### [199] [Trade-off Functions for DP-SGD with Subsampling based on Random Shuffling: Tight Upper and Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06259)
*Marten van Dijk,Murat Bilgehan Ertan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在$f$-DP框架下对基于随机洗牌的差分隐私随机梯度下降（DP-SGD）进行了紧 trade-off 分析。不同于泊松子采样的隐式公式，随机洗牌可获得透明可解释的闭式界。基于Berry-Esseen定理的紧界在常数因子内最优，并可组合用于多轮训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Poisson子采样的$f$-DP分析只能通过机器计算得到非透明的结果，而随机洗牌机制可能具有更透明、更可解释的分析形式。本文旨在建立随机洗牌DP-SGD的紧trade-off函数界限。

Method: 1. 在$f$-DP框架下分析随机洗牌机制；2. 使用Berry-Esseen定理推导闭式边界；3. 引入基于大数定律推广的新证明技术处理渐近行为。

Result: 1. 在$\sigma \geq \sqrt{3/\ln M}$ regime下获得紧闭式trade-off界；2. 具体参数示例：$\delta=1/100$、$\sigma=1$时，$M\approx1.14\times10^6$、$N\approx1.14\times10^7$；3. 多轮组合时$\delta$呈线性依赖$E$；4. 渐近极限结果：当$E=c_M^2M$且$c_M\to0$时，$f^{\otimes E}(a)\to1-a$（对角极限）。

Conclusion: 本文提供了随机洗牌DP-SGD的透明紧界，揭示了与Poisson子采样不同的渐近行为，为实际隐私保护机器学习提供理论支撑。

Abstract: We derive a tight analysis of the trade-off function for Differentially Private Stochastic Gradient Descent (DP-SGD) with subsampling based on random shuffling within the $f$-DP framework. Our analysis covers the regime $σ\geq \sqrt{3/\ln M}$, where $σ$ is the noise multiplier and $M$ is the number of rounds within a single epoch. Unlike $f$-DP analyses for Poisson subsampling, which yield non-closed implicit formulas that can be machine computed but are non-transparent, random shuffling admits a tight analysis yielding transparent and interpretable closed-form bounds. Our concrete bounds, derived via the Berry-Esseen theorem, are tight up to constant factors within the proof framework. We demonstrate worked parameter settings for a single epoch ($E=1$) with a corresponding trade-off function $\geq 1-a-δ$, that is, only $δ$ below the ideal random guessing diagonal $1-a$: For $δ= 1/100$ and $σ= 1$, roughly $M \approx 1.14\times 10^6$ rounds and $N \approx 1.14\times 10^7$ training samples suffice to achieve meaningful differential privacy. This is in contrast to recent negative results for the regime $σ\leq 1/\sqrt{2 \ln M}$. Our concrete bounds can be composed over multiple epochs leading to $δ$ having a linear in $E$ dependency, which restricts $E=O(\sqrt{M})$. To go beyond Berry--Esseen, we introduce a new proof technique based on a generalization of the law of large numbers that yields an asymptotic random guessing diagonal-limit result: if $E=c_M^2M$ with $c_M\to 0$, then the $E$-fold composed trade-off function satisfies $f^{\otimes E}(a)\to 1-a$ uniformly in $a\in[0,1]$ with $δ$ having only an $O(\sqrt{E})$ dependency. We compare this asymptotic regime with the corresponding Poisson subsampling asymptotic, and highlight the characterization of explicit convergence rates as an open question.

</details>


### [200] [Beyond Rigid Alignment: Graph Federated Learning via Dual Manifold Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06260)
*Wentao Yu,Bo Han,Jie Yang,Chen Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedGMC通过引入双重流形校准机制解决图联邦学习中的语义异质性和结构异质性问题，无需强制参数对齐，而是通过语义锚点和结构模板构建全局流形来引导局部流形校准，在11个同质和异质图数据集上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦图学习方法通过强制模型参数或原型对齐来处理异质性，但这依赖于限制性的全局线性假设，严重压缩了客户端的个性化表示空间，无法保留多样化的局部图分布。

Method: 提出联邦图流形校准(FedGMC)框架，包含双重流形校准机制：1) 语义校准：服务器通过等距语义锚点构建几何最优语义流形，指导局部语义流形校准；2) 结构校准：服务器通过构建全局结构模板指导局部结构流形校准；最后服务器动态聚合局部流形来细化全局流形。

Result: 在11个同质和异质图数据集上的实验表明，FedGMC有效平衡了全局共性和局部个性化需求，显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: FedGMC通过统一流形视角处理语义和结构异质性，为联邦图学习提供了一种更灵活且有效的个性化协作学习范式。

Abstract: Graph Federated Learning (GFL) enables collaborative representation learning across distributed subgraphs while preserving privacy. However, heterogeneity remains a critical challenge, as subgraphs across clients typically differ significantly in both semantics and structures. Existing methods address heterogeneity by enforcing the rigid alignment of model parameters or prototypes between clients and the server. However, these alignments implicitly rely on a restrictive global linearity assumption that summarizes local data distributions using a single and globally consistent representation space. This severely compresses the personalized representation space of clients and fails to preserve diverse local graph distributions. To overcome these limitations, we propose Federated Graph Manifold Calibration (FedGMC), a novel paradigm that tackles semantic heterogeneity and structural heterogeneity from a unified manifold perspective. Instead of enforcing rigid alignment, FedGMC introduces a dual manifold calibration mechanism that preserves global commonalities while maximizing the personalized representation space of local clients. Specifically, for semantic heterogeneity, the server constructs a geometrically optimal semantic manifold via equidistant semantic anchors, so as to guide the calibration of local semantic manifolds. For structural heterogeneity, the server constructs a global structural manifold by building global structural templates, so as to guide the calibration of local structural manifolds. Finally, the server dynamically refines both global semantic manifolds and structural manifolds by aggregating local manifolds. Extensive experiments on eleven homophilic and heterophilic graphs demonstrate that FedGMC effectively balances global commonality and local personalization, thereby significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baseline methods.

</details>


### [201] [Inference-Time Refinement Closes the Synthetic-Real Gap in Tabular Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06261)
*Eugenio Lomurno,Filippo Balzarini,Francesco Benelle,Francesca Pia Panaccione,Matteo Matteucci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TARDIS，一种无需重新训练即可细化预训练表格扩散模型的推理时框架。该方法通过双向Chamfer细化(BCR)模式，在15个基准上显著超越真实数据和backbone模型，推理时间仅需1-80分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前扩散模型在合成表格数据上接近但很少超越真实数据效用。现有工作仅在训练时通过架构改进和规模化来缩小差距，推理时细化这一替代方案尚未被探索。

Method: TARDIS是一个推理时细化框架，在冻结的预训练backbone上运行。通过TPE搜索score-level引导空间，每轮试验通过网格搜索优化后验样本选择器和软标签蒸馏步骤。核心编码了双向Chamfer细化(BCR)模式：对称Chamfer函数通过score级梯度和批次排序两种方式最小化。

Result: 在15个二分类、多分类和回归基准上，TARDIS相比真实数据训练的模型中位数提升+8.6%（95%CI [+3.3, +16.4]，Wilcoxon p=0.016，11/15严格获胜），相比TabDiff backbone在所有15个数据集上均提升（平均+12.9%，p<10^-4），同时保持流形保真度、多样性和样本级隐私。

Conclusion: TARDIS通过BCR模式在15个基准数据集上取得显著改进，中位数提升+8.6%，推理时间仅需1-80分钟，匹配真实数据流形保真度、多样性和隐私性。

Abstract: Diffusion-based generators set the current state of the art for synthetic tabular data. These methods approach but rarely exceed real-data utility, and closing this synthetic-real gap has so far been pursued exclusively at training time, via architectural advances, scaling, and retraining of monolithic generators. The inference-time alternative, i.e., refining the outputs of a pre-trained backbone with parameters left untouched, has remained largely unexplored for tabular synthesis. We introduce TARDIS (Tabular generation through Refinement, Distillation, and Inference-time Sampling), an inference-time refinement framework that operates on a frozen pre-trained backbone, configured per dataset by a Tree-structured Parzen Estimator search over score-level guidance during reverse diffusion, with each trial's objective set by an inner grid search over post-hoc sample selectors and an optional soft-label distillation step. The search space encodes a single mathematical pattern we name Bidirectional Chamfer Refinement (BCR): the symmetric Chamfer functional between synthetic and real samples is minimized both continuously, via a score-level gradient, and discretely, via batch-ranking post-generation. The per-dataset search recovers BCR-aligned configurations on most datasets, evidence for BCR as the dominant refinement pattern. Across 15 binary, multiclass, and regression benchmarks TARDIS achieves a median +8.6% downstream-task improvement over models trained on real data (95% CI [+3.3, +16.4], Wilcoxon p=0.016, 11/15 strict wins) and improves over the TabDiff backbone on all 15 datasets (mean +12.9%, p<10^-4), matching the backbone on manifold fidelity, diversity, and sample-level privacy. Inference-time refinement of a pre-trained tabular diffusion backbone reaches and exceeds real-data utility in 1 to 80 minutes on a single consumer-grade GPU.

</details>


### [202] [Can Attribution Predict Risk? From Multi-View Attribution to Planning Risk Signals in End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06264)
*Le Yang,Ruoyu Chen,Haijun Liu,Jiawei Liang,ShangQuan Sun,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种层级归因框架用于端到端自动驾驶规划的可解释性和风险识别，通过粗到细区域搜索和三个归因统计量实现规划风险预测，在多个数据集上验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶模型决策不透明、风险难定位，现有方法与规划过程脱耦，无法揭示视觉证据。归因方法应用于多视角连续轨迹预测存在独特挑战。

Method: 提出层级归因框架，采用L2一致性目标的粗到细区域归因策略，提取归因熵、相机内空间方差、跨相机基尼系数三个统计量作为风险预测信号。

Result: 在BridgeAD、UniAD、GenAD上验证，Spearman相关性达0.30±0.07，碰撞检测AUROC达0.77±0.04，具备良好泛化性和方法稳定性。

Conclusion: 该研究首次将视觉归因与端到端自动驾驶规划的风险识别相结合，通过层级归因框架和统计指标实现了可解释的规划风险预测，具备良好泛化性和稳定性。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving models generate future trajectories from multi-view inputs, improving system integration but introducing opaque decisions and hard-to-localize risks. Existing methods either rely on auxiliary monitoring models or generate textual explanations, but are decoupled from the planning process and fail to reveal the visual evidence underlying trajectory generation. While attribution offers a direct alternative, planning differs from image classification by taking six-view camera images as input and predicting continuous multi-step trajectories, requiring attribution to capture both critical views and regions and their influence on outputs. Moreover, whether attribution maps can support risk identification remains underexplored. To address this, we propose a hierarchical attribution framework for end-to-end planning. Specifically, using L2 consistency with the original trajectory as the objective, we design a coarse-to-fine region attribution strategy that searches candidate regions across the full six-view input and refines attribution within them. We further extract three attribution statistics as predictive signals for planning risk, including attribution entropy to measure how concentrated the planner's reliance is over the joint visual space, within-camera spatial variance to characterize how spread out the attribution is within each view, and cross-camera Gini coefficient to quantify how unevenly attribution is distributed across the six cameras. Experiments on BridgeAD, UniAD, and GenAD show that these statistics correlate with planning risk, achieving Spearman correlations of $0.30 \pm 0.07$ with trajectory error and AUROC of $0.77 \pm 0.04$ for collision detection. The signal generalizes to held-out scenes with negligible degradation and remains stable under an alternative attribution baseline.

</details>


### [203] [A Flow Matching Algorithm for Many-Shot Adaptation to Unseen Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06272)
*Tyler Ingebrand,Ruihan Zhao,Kushagra Gupta,David Fridovich-Keil,Sandeep P. Chinchali,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FP-FM是一种无需在推理时额外训练即可从目标分布生成样本的算法，通过学习基函数来表示训练分布的速度场，并通过对新分布进行最小二乘投影来实现快速适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式建模虽在自然语言条件图像生成等任务上取得成功，但如何从样本数据点自适应模型仍缺乏充分探索。现有的条件生成方法通常需要额外的训练过程或复杂的条件编码，难以高效地适应新的目标分布。

Method: FP-FM学习基函数来跨越一组训练分布对应的速度场，然后通过简单的最小二乘投影计算将生成过程适应到新分布。该方法还提出了多种变体，通过丰富系数计算（如让系数依赖时间）来在表达力和计算成本之间取得权衡。

Result: 在合成数据集和基于图像的数据集上，FP-FM相比基线方法取得了显著提升的精确率和召回率，尤其在未见过的分布上表现突出。

Conclusion: FP-FM能够高效地从多样化目标分布生成样本，无需在推理时进行额外训练，在分布适应性和生成质量方面展现出强大优势。

Abstract: While generative modeling has achieved remarkable success on tasks like natural language-conditioned image generation, enabling model adaptation from example data points remains a relatively underexplored and challenging problem. To this end, we propose Function Projection for Flow Matching (FP-FM), an algorithm that directly conditions generation on samples from the target distribution. FP-FM learns basis functions to span the velocity fields corresponding to a set of training distributions, and adapts to new distributions by computing a simple least-squares projection onto this basis. This enables efficient generation of samples from diverse target distributions without additional training at inference time. We further introduce multiple variants of FP-FM that provide a trade-off in expressivity and compute by enriching the coefficient calculation, e.g., by making the coefficients dependent on time. FP-FM achieves greatly improved precision and recall relative to baselines across synthetic and image-based datasets, with especially strong gains on unseen distributions.

</details>


### [204] [When Labels Have Structure: Improving Image Classification with Hierarchy-Aware Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06274)
*April Chan,Davide D'Ascenzo,Sebastiano Cultrera di Montesano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HACE是一种层次感知的交叉熵损失函数，通过预测聚合和祖先标签平滑两个组件将类层次结构融入分类损失，在多个数据集和架构上验证了其有效性，显著优于标准交叉熵损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准交叉熵损失对所有错误分类一视同仁，忽略了类层次结构中编码的语义距离，导致模型无法利用类别间的语义层级关系。

Method: HACE包含两个核心组件：1) 预测聚合：将模型概率向上传播，使父节点累积子节点的置信度；2) 祖先标签平滑：将真实标签的监督信号沿从叶节点到根节点的路径分布。

Result: 端到端训练：18个架构-数据集组合中15个提升，平均提升4.66%；线性探测：DINOv2-Large冻结特征上在三个数据集均超越所有基线方法，平均提升2.18%。

Conclusion: HACE作为标准交叉熵的直接替代方案，在层次化分类任务中表现稳健，验证了将类层次结构显式纳入损失函数的有效性。

Abstract: Standard cross-entropy is the default classification loss across virtually all of machine learning, yet it treats all misclassifications equally, ignoring the semantic distances that a class hierarchy encodes. We propose Hierarchy-Aware Cross-Entropy (HACE), a drop-in replacement for standard cross-entropy that incorporates a known class hierarchy directly into the loss. HACE combines two components: prediction aggregation, which propagates the model's probability mass upward through the class hierarchy to ensure that parent nodes accumulate the confidence of their children; and ancestral label smoothing, which distributes the ground-truth signal along the path from the true class to the root. We evaluate HACE on CIFAR-100, FGVC Aircraft, and NABirds in two regimes: end-to-end training across six architectures spanning convolutional and attention-based designs, and linear probing on frozen DINOv2-Large features. In end-to-end training, HACE improves accuracy over standard cross-entropy in 15 out of 18 architecture--dataset pairs, with a mean gain of 4.66\%. In linear probing on frozen DINOv2-Large features, HACE outperforms all competing methods on all three datasets, with a mean improvement of 2.18\% over the next best baseline.

</details>


### [205] [PACE: Prune-And-Compress Ensemble Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06278)
*Fabian Akkerman,Julien Ferry,Théo Guyard,Thibaut Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PACE通过两阶段策略解决大型集成模型的部署和可解释性问题：第一阶段主动生成新弱学习器以增强多样性，第二阶段对增强后的集成进行剪枝。该方法在性能和忠实度控制上都优于现有的剪枝和压缩方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ensemble models achieve state-of-the-art performance but require aggregating many weak learners, which hinders deployment, interpretability, and downstream tasks like robustness verification.

Method: Two-phase strategy: (1) First, actively generate new learners via a theoretically grounded procedure to enhance diversity; (2) Then perform pruning on the enriched ensemble. Both phases allow fine control on faithfulness to the original ensemble.

Result: Outperforms prior pruning and compression methods while offering principled control of faithfulness guarantees.

Conclusion: PACE offers principled control of faithfulness guarantees to the original ensemble.

Abstract: Ensemble models achieve state-of-the-art performance on prediction tasks, but usually require aggregating a large number of weak learners. This can hinder deployment, interpretability, and downstream tasks such as robustness verification. Remedies to this issue fall into two main camps: pruning, which discards redundant learners, and compression, which generates new ones from scratch. We introduce PACE, a framework that interleaves these paradigms in a two-phase strategy. First, new learners are actively generated via a theoretically grounded procedure to enhance the diversity of the initial ensemble. When no more relevant learners can be found, a second phase of pruning is performed on this enriched ensemble. During both operations, PACE allows fine control on the faithfulness to the original ensemble. Experiments show that our method outperforms prior pruning and compression methods while offering principled control of faithfulness guarantees.

</details>


### [206] [Attributions All the Way Down? The Metagame of Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06295)
*Hubert Baniecki,Przemyslaw Biecek,Fabian Fumagalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Metagame框架，通过将归因方法本身建模为合作博弈并计算Shapley值，量化特征之间的二阶交互效应。理论证明了归因可层次分解为元归因，经验验证了其在语言模型、视觉-语言编码器和扩散模型中的应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性方法主要关注特征对模型输出的直接（一阶）影响，但缺乏对特征之间交互效应的量化分析框架。研究者需要理解一个特征的归因如何受到其他特征的影响，即二阶交互效应。

Method: 将归因方法本身视为合作博弈，计算其Shapley值来定义元归因（meta-attribution）φ_{j→i}(f)，量化特征j对特征i归因值的有向影响。

Result: 理论上证明归因可层次分解为元归因，元归因是现有交互指数的有向扩展。应用上在指令微调语言模型、视觉-语言编码器和多模态扩散Transformer三个场景验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: Metagame提供了一个统一的框架来理解和量化模型解释中的二阶交互效应，兼具理论基础和实用价值。

Abstract: We introduce the metagame, a conceptual framework for quantifying second-order interaction effects of model explanations. For any first-order attribution $φ(f)$ explaining a model $f$, we measure the directional influence of feature $j$ on the attribution of feature $i$, denoted as meta-attribution $\varphi_{j \to i}(f)$, by treating the attribution method itself as a cooperative game and computing its Shapley value. Theoretically, we prove that attributions hierarchically decompose into meta-attributions, and establish these as directional extensions of existing interaction indices. Empirically, we demonstrate that the metagame delivers insights across diverse interpretability applications: (i) quantifying token interactions in instruction-tuned language models, (ii) explaining cross-modal similarity in vision-language encoders, and (iii) interpreting text-to-image concepts in multimodal diffusion transformers.

</details>


### [207] [Molecules Meet Language: Confound-Aware Representation Learning and Chemical Property Steering in Transformer-VAE Latent Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06303)
*Zakaria Elabid,Jan Andrzejewski,Bartosz Brzoza,Attila Cangi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究针对分子生成模型潜空间中属性可预测性的来源问题，提出了一种基于线性/非线性探针和混淆感知评估的方法，用于区分化学信号与SELFIES表征引入的伪像。研究表明化学意义的导向在不清晰的分子潜空间中可以涌现，但必须通过解码分子验证和混杂因素控制来确保其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子生成模型通常假设潜空间具有有意义的几何结构，但观察到的属性可预测性可能反映的是序列层面的捷径（shortcuts）而非真正的化学组织，因此需要区分化学信号与SELFIES表征引入的伪像。

Method: 1. 训练无监督的自回归Transformer-VAE模型，使用SELFIES分子表征；2. 冻结模型权重，拟合从RDKit描述符到潜空间的线性探针；3. 使用探针权重作为全局导向候选方向；4. 引入基于残差化、混杂方向对齐分析和分子解码遍历的混淆感知评估框架；5. 进一步探索非线性探针和局部潜梯度。

Result: 发现SELFIES长度、分支token、环token和token熵在潜空间中高度编码；通过混淆感知评估验证了cLogP、FractionCSP3、HeavyAtomCount、TPSA、BertzCT和HBA属性的稳健单调导向；非线性探针表明部分属性适合全局方向导向，而另一些属性更适合局部潜梯度描述。

Conclusion: 在分子生成模型的潜空间中，化学意义的导向可以从不清晰的条件中涌现，但必须通过对解码分子的验证和对表征层面混杂因素的控制来确保其有效性。

Abstract: Molecular generative models often assume meaningful latent geometry, but apparent property predictability can reflect sequence-level shortcuts rather than chemical organization. We study this issue in an unsupervised autoregressive Transformer-VAE trained on SELFIES. After training, we freeze the model, fit linear probes to RDKit descriptors, and use the probe weights as candidate global steering directions. To separate chemical signal from SELFIES artifacts, we introduce a confound-aware evaluation based on residualization, confound-direction alignment analysis, and decoded-molecule traversal. This is necessary because SELFIES length, branch tokens, ring tokens, and token entropy are strongly encoded in the latent space. Under this confound-aware evaluation, we find robust monotonic steering for cLogP, FractionCSP3, HeavyAtomCount, TPSA, BertzCT, and HBA. Nonlinear probes further show that some properties admit stable global directions, while others are better described by local latent gradients. Overall, our results show that chemically meaningful steering can emerge in entangled molecular latent spaces, but only when validated through decoded molecules and controlled for representation-level confounds.

</details>


### [208] [Perceive, Route and Modulate: Dynamic Pattern Recalibration for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06310)
*Siru Zhong,Zhao Meng,Haohuan Fu,Haoyang Li,Qingsong Wen,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出动态模式重校准(DPR)机制，通过"感知-路由-调制"管道在token级别实现时间感知的自适应调整，克服现有深度预测模型中固定权重矩阵导致的静态响应问题。该方法可作为轻量级适配器集成到多种架构中，验证了动态重校准相比大规模参数扩展的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current deep forecasting models rely on fixed weight matrices applied uniformly to all temporal tokens, creating a "static pattern response". Models settle into a compromised average and cannot adapt to continuously shifting local dynamics in real-world time series.

Method: DPR employs a "Perceive-Route-Modulate" pipeline: (1) Perceive - generates context-aware perception signals; (2) Route - computes soft-routing distribution over learned basis of adaptive response patterns; (3) Modulate - produces time-aware modulation vectors applied to hidden states via residual Hadamard product. The mechanism is backbone-agnostic, enabling integration as a lightweight adapter across diverse architectures.

Result: As a backbone-agnostic adapter, DPR enhances forecasting performance across diverse architectures with minimal overhead. As a standalone model (DPRNet), it achieves competitive performance across 12 benchmarks, demonstrating that dynamic recalibration can effectively substitute for macroscopic parameter scaling.

Conclusion: Dynamic recalibration through token-level adaptation offers an alternative paradigm to macroscopic parameter scaling, achieving comparable performance with greater efficiency. DPR represents a general solution to a fundamental bottleneck in deep forecasting architectures.

Abstract: Local temporal patterns in real-world time series continuously shift, rendering globally shared transformations suboptimal. Current deep forecasting models, despite their scale and complexity, rely on fixed weight matrices applied uniformly to all temporal tokens. This creates a static pattern response: models settle into a compromised average, unable to adapt to changing local dynamics. We introduce Dynamic Pattern Recalibration (DPR), a backbone-agnostic mechanism that resolves this via token-level recalibration. Through a lightweight "Perceive-Route-Modulate" pipeline, DPR computes a soft-routing distribution over a learned basis of adaptive response patterns, generating a time-aware modulation vector that recalibrates hidden states via a residual Hadamard product. As a backbone-agnostic adapter, DPR enhances forecasting across diverse architectures with minimal overhead, confirming it addresses a general bottleneck. As a minimalist standalone model, DPRNet achieves competitive performance across 12 benchmarks, validating dynamic recalibration against macroscopic parameter scaling.

</details>


### [209] [When Does $\ell_2$-Boosting Overfit Benignly? High-Dimensional Risk Asymptotics and the $\ell_1$ Implicit Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06314)
*Ye Su,Jian Li,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究高维数据中连续时间$\ell_2$-Boosting在$\ell_1$隐式偏差下的良性过拟合现象。通过结合凸高斯极小极大定理与双侧截断高斯矩的渐近展开，解析求解了非光滑$\ell_1$插值器。结果表明，在纯噪声模型下，过拟合风险以对数速率$Θ(σ^2/\log(p/n))$衰减，慢于$\ell_2$几何中的线性衰减。通过分析boosting流的非光滑次梯度动力学，提出了无调参的早停规则，可在有界$\ell_1$路径条件下恢复Lasso基本不等式并达到极小极大最优速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 良性过拟合（benign overfitting）在$\ell_2$几何下已有良好表征，但在$\ell_1$隐式偏差（如贪婪集成）的条件下行为尚不明确。主要障碍在于坐标选择阈值的非线性耦合，使得标准谱分解工具失效。

Method: 1. 连续时间$\ell_2$-Boosting（过参数化特征$p$与样本数$n$）；2. 耦合凸高斯极小极大定理（CGMT）与双侧截断高斯矩的精密渐近展开；3. 分析boosting流的非光滑次梯度动力学以设计早停规则。

Result: 1. **纯噪声模型**：贪婪选择将噪声局部化到稀疏活跃集，过剩方差以对数速率$Θ(σ^2/\log(p/n))$衰减（良性过拟合失败于线性速率）；2. **尖峰-各向同性设计**（$k^*$个头部特征值，$r_2=p-k^*$尾部维度）：当$r_2 \gg n$时风险趋于零，但仍为对数速率$Θ(σ^2/\log(r_2/n))$，慢于$\ell_2$几何的线性衰减；3. **早停规则**：在有界$\ell_1$路径条件下可恢复Lasso基本不等式，达到$\ell_1$有界信号的极小极大最优经验预测速率。

Conclusion: $\ell_1$几何下的局部化机制导致风险对数衰减（而非$\ell_2$的线性衰减），但通过适当的早停可克服收敛慢的问题。该框架为理解梯度提升等贪婪算法的隐式正则化提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Benign overfitting is well-characterized in $\ell_2$ geometries, but its behavior under the $\ell_1$ implicit bias of greedy ensembles remains challenging. The analytical barrier stems from the non-linear coupling of coordinate selection thresholds, which invalidates standard spectral resolvent tools. To isolate this algorithmic bias, we characterize the high-dimensional risk of continuous-time $\ell_2$-Boosting over $p$ features and $n$ samples. By coupling the Convex Gaussian Minimax Theorem with delicate asymptotic expansions of double-sided truncated Gaussian moments, we analytically resolve the non-smooth $\ell_1$ interpolant. Under an isotropic pure-noise model, we prove that benign overfitting fails at the linear rate: greedy selection localizes noise into sparse active sets, and the excess variance decays at a logarithmic rate $Θ(σ^2/\log(p/n))$ for noise variance $σ^2$. We remark that while this localization mechanism should persist in the presence of signals, the exact signal-noise decomposition remains an open problem. For spiked-isotropic designs with $k^*$ head eigenvalues and $r_2 = p - k^*$ tail dimensions, the risk converges to zero when $r_{2} \gg n$, but only at a logarithmic rate $Θ(σ^2/\log(r_2/n))$, which is slower than the linear decay observed in $\ell_2$ geometries. To avoid this slow convergence, we analyze the non-smooth subdifferential dynamics of the boosting flow. This yields a tuning-free early stopping rule that, under a bounded $\ell_1$-path condition, recovers the Lasso basic inequality and attains the minimax-optimal empirical prediction rate for $\ell_1$-bounded signals.

</details>


### [210] [Pro-KLShampoo: Projected KL-Shampoo with Whitening Recovered by Orthogonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06316)
*Ruotong Sun,Ermin Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现KL-Shampoo的Kronecker预条件子具有"尖峰-平坦"特征值分布结构，利用这一结构将Kronecker因子预条件与正交化方法统一，结合两种技术路线优势，提出了更高效的Pro-KLShampoo优化器，在多个LLM预训练规模上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM预训练优化器主要采用两类矩阵结构利用方法：基于KL散度最小化的显式Kronecker因子预条件（如KL-Shampoo）和基于梯度动量正交化的方法（如Muon）。这两条路线通常独立发展，缺乏统一的理论框架和实践结合。

Method: 利用KL-Shampoo Kronecker预条件子的"尖峰-平坦"(spike-and-flat)特征值结构，将预条件子限制为：追踪r维子空间上保留完整谱结构，其余n-r方向使用共享特征值的参数族，并应用正交化处理。通过数学恒等式证明该正交化可恢复完整KL-Shampoo预条件子的代数形式，实现两条技术路线的统一。

Result: 在GPT-2 124M/350M和LLaMA 134M/450M四个预训练规模上，Pro-KLShampoo在验证损失、峰值GPU内存和达到特定损失水平的 wallclock time 三个维度上均一致优于KL-Shampoo，且在测试的所有子空间秩下均成立。

Conclusion: Pro-KLShampoo通过统一KL-Shampoo与正交化两条技术路线，在GPT-2和LLaMA四个预训练规模上均实现了验证损失、峰值GPU内存和达到特定损失水平所需时间的全面提升，是一种实用且高效的LLM预训练优化器。
