Abstract: Weight decay is widely used as a regularizer in large language models, yet its precise role in shaping Transformer loss landscapes remains theoretically underexplored. This paper provides the first rigorous functional-analytic characterization of the standard Transformer objective--cross-entropy loss with $L^2$ regularization--by proving it satisfies Villani's criteria for coercive energy functions. Specifically, we show that the regularized loss $\mathcal{F}$ is infinitely differentiable, grows at least quadratically, has Gaussian-integrable tails, and satisfies the differential growth condition $-Δ\mathcal{F} + \tfrac{1}{s}\|\nabla\mathcal{F}\|^{2} \to \infty$ as $\|θ\| \to \infty$ for all $s>0$. From this structure, we derive explicit log-Sobolev and Poincaré constants $C_{\mathrm{LS}} \leq λ^{-1} + d/λ^{2}$, linking the regularization strength $λ$ and model dimension $d$ to finite-time convergence guarantees for noisy stochastic gradient descent and PAC-Bayesian generalization bounds that tighten with increasing $λ$. To validate our theory, we introduce a scalable Villani diagnostic $Ψ_s(θ) = -Δ\mathcal{F} + s^{-1}\|\nabla \mathcal{F}\|^2$ and estimate it efficiently using Hutchinson trace probes in models with over 100M parameters. Experiments on GPT-Neo-125M across Penn Treebank and WikiText-103 confirm the predicted quadratic growth of $Ψ_s$, spectral inflation of the Hessian, and exponential convergence behavior consistent with our log-Sobolev analysis. These results demonstrate that weight decay not only improves generalization empirically but also establishes the mathematical conditions required for fast Langevin mixing and theoretically grounded curvature-aware optimization in deep learning.

</details>


### [246] [Transformers Efficiently Perform In-Context Logistic Regression via Normalized Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06609)
*Chenyang Zhang,Yuan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Transformer如何通过softmax注意力机制实现上下文内学习（ICL），构建了能够执行上下文逻辑回归的多层Transformer，证明其每层等价于一步归一化梯度下降，并通过监督训练单层自注意力后循环应用实现。提供了训练收敛和分布外泛化的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的强ICL能力通常被认为源于其能隐式执行某些算法，但其工作机制缺乏理论解释。特别是在线性分类数据上，softmax注意力如何实现上下文学习的具体机制尚不清晰。

Method: 构建一类多层Transformer使其每层精确执行一步归一化梯度下降；通过监督方式训练单个自注意力层，并递归应用该层形成循环模型。

Result: 证明所构建的Transformer可通过两个步骤实现：(i) 监督训练单层自注意力执行一步梯度下降；(ii) 递归应用训练好的层形成looped模型。提供了自注意力层的训练收敛保证和循环模型的分布外泛化保证。

Conclusion: 推进了对ICL机制的理论理解，证明了softmax Transformer可有效充当上下文学习器。

Abstract: Transformers have demonstrated remarkable in-context learning (ICL) capabilities. The strong ICL performance of transformers is commonly believed to arise from their ability to implicitly execute certain algorithms on the context, thereby enhancing prediction and generation. In this work, we investigate how transformers with softmax attention perform in-context learning on linear classification data. We first construct a class of multi-layer transformers that can perform in-context logistic regression, with each layer exactly performing one step of normalized gradient descent on an in-context loss. Then, we show that our constructed transformer can be obtained through (i) training a single self-attention layer supervised by one-step gradient descent, and (ii) recurrently applying the trained layer to obtain a looped model. Training convergence guarantees of the self-attention layer and out-of-distribution generalization guarantees of the looped model are provided. Our results advance the theoretical understanding of ICL mechanism by showcasing how softmax transformers can effectively act as in-context learners.

</details>


### [247] [SoftSAE: Dynamic Top-K Selection for Adaptive Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06610)
*Jakub Stępień,Marcin Mazur,Jacek Tabor,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SoftSAE，一种具有动态Top-K选择机制的稀疏自编码器。通过可微分的Soft Top-K算子实现输入依赖的稀疏度学习，使模型能够根据每个输入的复杂度自适应调整活跃特征数量，提升表示质量并更好地匹配数据结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SAE架构（如TopK SAE）使用固定的稀疏度K，忽视了真实数据的复杂性差异。自然数据通常位于具有不同局部内在维度的流形上，简单输入可能只需要少量特征，而复杂输入需要更丰富的表示。固定K会在简单情况下引入噪声，在复杂情况下丢失重要结构，因此需要自适应调整稀疏度。

Method: SoftSAE采用可微分的Soft Top-K算子来动态学习输入依赖的稀疏度k。具体地，通过可学习的参数控制特征选择的软化程度，使模型能够根据每个输入的复杂度调整活跃特征的数量，实现输入自适应的稀疏表示。

Result: 实验结果表明SoftSAE不仅能够找到有意义的特征，还能为每个概念选择正确数量的特征，验证了动态稀疏度机制的有效性。

Conclusion: SoftSAE通过动态K选择机制成功解决了固定稀疏度的问题，使SAE能够自适应地为不同复杂度的输入选择合适数量的特征，从而更好地匹配数据结构，提高解释的准确性。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have become an important tool in mechanistic interpretability, helping to analyze internal representations in both Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision Transformers (ViTs). By decomposing polysemantic activations into sparse sets of monosemantic features, SAEs aim to translate neural network computations into human-understandable concepts. However, common architectures such as TopK SAEs rely on a fixed sparsity level. They enforce the same number of active features (K) across all inputs, ignoring the varying complexity of real-world data. Natural data often lies on manifolds with varying local intrinsic dimensionality, meaning the number of relevant factors can change significantly across samples. This suggests that a fixed sparsity level is not optimal. Simple inputs may require only a few features, while more complex ones need more expressive representations. Using a constant K can therefore introduce noise in simple cases or miss important structure in more complex ones. To address this issue, we propose SoftSAE, a sparse autoencoder with a Dynamic Top-K selection mechanism. Our method uses a differentiable Soft Top-K operator to learn an input-dependent sparsity level k. This allows the model to adjust the number of active features based on the complexity of each input. As a result, the representation better matches the structure of the data, and the explanation length reflects the amount of information in the input. Experimental results confirm that SoftSAE not only finds meaningful features, but also selects the right number of features for each concept. The source code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SoftSAE-8F71/.

</details>


### [248] [Online Bayesian Calibration under Gradual and Abrupt System Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06612)
*Yang Xu,Chiwoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BRPC是一种用于非平稳环境下流数据在线贝叶斯校准的框架，通过将校准参数的无差异粒子更新与差异的条件高斯过程更新分离，并结合重启机制检测剧变，在保持可辨识性的同时实现偏差感知适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典贝叶斯校准存在参数-差异混淆问题，且假设离线/平稳数据生成过程；数字孪生应用需处理时变系统，包括渐变漂移和剧变状态转换；现有数据同化方法未显式建模系统偏差，对剧变情况效果不佳。

Method: 提出BRPC框架：(1)对校准参数使用无差异粒子更新，(2)对差异项使用条件高斯过程更新，(3)集成重启机制检测状态转换并重置校准过程。渐变演化下分离更新保持可辨识性；剧变时重启机制触发重校准。

Result: 理论证明：渐变演化下的跟踪性能保证；重启机制的虚警和检测行为保证。实证验证：在合成数据和电站仿真基准上，BRPC在渐变场景下提升校准精度；重启增强版本在剧变场景下鲁棒性和预测性能均优于滑动窗口贝叶斯校准和数据同化基线。

Conclusion: BRPC有效解决了流数据场景下同时存在模拟器失配和非平稳性时的在线贝叶斯校准问题，为数字孪生中的自适应校准提供了理论基础和方法支撑。

Abstract: Bayesian model calibration is central to digital twins and computer experiments, as it aligns model outputs with field observations by estimating calibration parameters and correcting systematic model bias. Classical Bayesian calibration introduces latent parameters and a discrepancy function to model bias, but suffers from parameter--discrepancy confounding and is typically formulated as an offline procedure under a stationary data-generating assumption. These limitations are restrictive in modern digital twin applications, where systems evolve over time and may exhibit gradual drift and abrupt regime shifts. While data assimilation methods enable sequential updates, they generally do not explicitly model systematic bias and are less effective under abrupt changes. We propose Bayesian Recursive Projected Calibration (BRPC), an online Bayesian calibration framework for streaming data under simulator mismatch and nonstationarity. BRPC extends projected calibration to the online setting by separating a discrepancy-free particle update for calibration parameters from a conditional Gaussian process update for discrepancy, preserving identifiability while enabling bias-aware adaptation under gradual system evolution. To handle abrupt changes, BRPC is integrated with restart mechanisms that detect regime shifts and reset the calibration process. We establish theoretical guarantees for both components, including tracking performance under gradual evolution and false-alarm and detection behavior for restart mechanisms. Empirical studies on synthetic and plant-simulation benchmarks show that BRPC improves calibration accuracy under gradual changes, while restart-augmented BRPC further improves robustness and predictive performance under abrupt regime shifts compared to sliding-window Bayesian calibration and data assimilation baselines.

</details>


### [249] [Concept-Based Abductive and Contrastive Explanations for Behaviors of Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06640)
*Ronaldo Canizales,Divya Gopinath,Corina Păsăreanu,Ravi Mangal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: The paper proposes concept-based abductive and contrastive explanations that combine the high-level interpretability of concept-based methods with the causal rigor of formal abductive explanations. It uses concept erasure to establish causal relationships and enumerates minimal sets of concepts causally relevant for model outcomes, applicable to both individual predictions and model behaviors on image collections.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing concept-based explanation methods either lack causal connections between concepts and predictions, or are limited to single-concept explanations. Formal abductive/contrastive explanations only consider low-level features (pixels) without high-level semantic concepts. The paper aims to bridge these two approaches.

Method: Uses concept erasure procedures to establish causal relationships between high-level concepts and model predictions. Enumerates all minimal explanations and aggregates them to understand model behavior on both individual images and collections of images.

Result: The approach computes helpful, user-friendly explanations that are effective across multiple models, datasets, and behaviors.

Conclusion: A family of algorithms that enumerate all minimal explanations while using concept erasure procedures to establish causal relationships. The approach can understand predictions on individual images and collections of images exhibiting user-specified behaviors.

Abstract: *Concept-based explanations* offer a promising approach for explaining the predictions of deep neural networks in terms of high-level, human-understandable concepts. However, existing methods either do not establish a causal connection between the concepts and model predictions or are limited in expressivity and only able to infer causal explanations involving single concepts. At the same time, the parallel line of work on *formal abductive and contrastive explanations* computes the minimal set of input features causally relevant for model outcomes but only considers low-level features such as pixels. Merging these two threads, in this work, we propose the notion of *concept-based abductive and contrastive explanations* that capture the minimal sets of high-level concepts causally relevant for model outcomes. We then present a family of algorithms that enumerate all minimal explanations while using *concept erasure* procedures to establish causal relationships. By appropriately aggregating such explanations, we are not only able to understand model predictions on individual images but also on collections of images where the model exhibits a user-specified, common *behavior*. We evaluate our approach on multiple models, datasets, and behaviors, and demonstrate its effectiveness in computing helpful, user-friendly explanations.

</details>


### [250] [Inductive Venn-Abers and related regressors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06646)
*Ivan Petej,Vladimir Vovk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将Venn-Abers预测器从二元分类和有限回归推广到无限回归场景，通过结合共形预测方法实现。模拟和实证研究表明，在较大训练集下该方法能提升标准回归器的预测效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Venn-Abers预测器具有有效性保证，但其应用范围局限于二元分类和有限回归，无法处理现实中的无界回归问题，因此需要扩展其适用范围。

Method: 通过引入共形预测(conformal prediction)技术，将Venn-Abers框架推广到无界回归场景，利用共形预测的分布无关性和Venn-Abers的概率校准性相结合。

Result: 实验结果表明，基于Venn-Abers回归器构建的点估计器在较大训练集上相比标准回归器具有一定程度的预测效率提升。

Conclusion: Venn-Abers预测器可成功扩展至无界回归问题，该方法为回归任务提供了一种具有理论有效性保证的新途径，尤其在大样本场景下表现出优势。

Abstract: Venn-Abers predictors are probabilistic predictors that enjoy appealing properties of validity, but their major limitation is that they are applicable only to the case of binary classification, with a recent extension to bounded regression. We generalize them to the case of unbounded regression, which requires adding an element of conformal prediction. In our simulation and empirical studies we investigate the predictive efficiency of point regressors derived from Venn-Abers regressors and argue that they somewhat improve the predictive efficiency of standard regressors for larger training sets.

</details>


### [251] [Optimizer-Model Consistency: Full Finetuning with the Same Optimizer as Pretraining Forgets Less](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06654)
*Yuxing Liu,Jianyu Wang,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，在LLM的预训练和监督微调阶段使用相同的优化器能够取得更好的"学习-遗忘"权衡，这是因为优化器会通过正则化激活值来塑造模型权重的更新结构，从而在微调时更好地保留预训练阶段获得的知识。Muon优化器因其倾向于死记硬背而非模式学习，在推理任务微调中表现不如AdamW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Investigate why full finetuning with the same optimizer as pretraining outperforms other optimizers and LoRA during SFT, and understand the underlying mechanism behind this optimizer-model consistency phenomenon.

Method: Controlled experiments and synthetic language modeling tasks comparing different optimizers (AdamW, Muon, SGD, etc.) across pretraining and SFT stages; analysis of activation regularization effects and weight update structures; comparison of Muon vs AdamW on reasoning tasks.

Result: Full finetuning with the same optimizer as pretraining achieves better learning-forgetting tradeoff than other optimizers and LoRA. Optimizers shape models through regularization effects on activations, creating different loss landscapes. Muon performs worse than AdamW for reasoning tasks due to tendency toward rote memorization over pattern acquisition, which is detrimental for data-limited SFT scenarios.

Conclusion: Optimizer-model consistency (using the same optimizer in pretraining and SFT) achieves better learning-forgetting tradeoff by leveraging optimizer-specific regularization effects that shape the weight update structure to preserve pretrained knowledge. Muon underperforms AdamW for reasoning tasks due to its tendency toward rote memorization over pattern acquisition, which is problematic when SFT data is limited.

Abstract: Optimizers play an important role in both pretraining and finetuning stages when training large language models (LLMs). In this paper, we present an observation that full finetuning with the same optimizer as in pretraining achieves a better learning-forgetting tradeoff, i.e., forgetting less while achieving the same or better performance on the new task, than other optimizers and, possibly surprisingly, LoRA, during the supervised finetuning (SFT) stage. We term this phenomenon optimizer-model consistency. To better understand it, through controlled experiments and theoretical analysis, we show that: 1) optimizers can shape the models by having regularization effects on the activations, leading to different landscapes around the pretrained checkpoints; 2) in response to this regularization effect, the weight update in SFT should follow some specific structures to lower forgetting of the knowledge learned in pretraining, which can be obtained by using the same optimizer. Moreover, we specifically compare Muon and AdamW when they are employed throughout the pretraining and SFT stages and find that Muon performs worse when finetuned for reasoning tasks. With a synthetic language modeling experiment, we demonstrate that this can come from Muon's strong tendency towards rote memorization, which may hurt pattern acquisition with a small amount of data, as for SFT.

</details>


### [252] [UniPool: A Globally Shared Expert Pool for Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06665)
*Minbin Huang,Han Shi,Chuanyang Zheng,Yimeng Wu,Guoxuan Chen,Xintong Yu,Yichun Yin,Hong Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对传统MoE架构per-layer专家所有权导致参数冗余的问题，提出UniPool——通过全局共享专家池、池级辅助损失和NormRouter实现专家参数与深度的解耦，在多个模型规模上验证了亚线性参数增长的可行性和性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MoE架构per-layer专家所有权规则导致深度缩放与线性专家参数量增长耦合，且假设每层都需要独立专家容量。但路由探针实验表明深层路由存在冗余性（随机化仅下降1.0-1.6点），提示可优化专家分配方式。

Method: 提出UniPool架构：(1)全局共享专家池替代per-layer独立专家集；(2)池级别辅助损失平衡专家利用率；(3)NormRouter实现稀疏稳定路由。实验覆盖182M至978M参数的5个LLaMA架构模型。

Result: UniPool在5个模型规模上持续优于匹配的vanilla MoE基线，验证损失降低达0.0386。关键发现：仅需41.6%-66.7%的vanilla专家参数量即可匹配或超越传统MoE，证明共享池设计下专家参数可亚线性增长。

Conclusion: UniPool实现了专家参数与深度的解耦，在5个模型规模上验证了41.6%-66.7%专家参数即可超越传统MoE，且能与更细粒度专家分解组合使用。

Abstract: Modern Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures allocate expert capacity through a rigid per-layer rule: each transformer layer owns a separate expert set. This convention couples depth scaling with linear expert-parameter growth and assumes that every layer needs isolated expert capacity. However, recent analyses and our routing probe challenge this allocation rule: replacing a deeper layer's learned top-k router with uniform random routing drops downstream accuracy by only 1.0-1.6 points across multiple production MoE models. Motivated by this redundancy, we propose UniPool, an MoE architecture that treats expert capacity as a global architectural budget by replacing per-layer expert ownership with a single shared pool accessed by independent per-layer routers. To enable stable and balanced training under sharing, we introduce a pool-level auxiliary loss that balances expert utilization across the entire pool, and adopt NormRouter to provide sparse and scale-stable routing into the shared expert pool. Across five LLaMA-architecture model scales (182M, 469M, 650M, 830M, and 978M parameters) trained on 30B tokens from the Pile, UniPool consistently improves validation loss and perplexity over the matched vanilla MoE baselines. Across these scales, UniPool reduces validation loss by up to 0.0386 relative to vanilla MoE. Beyond raw loss improvement, our results identify pool size as an explicit depth-scaling hyperparameter: reduced-pool UniPool variants using only 41.6%-66.7% of the vanilla expert-parameter budget match or outperform layer-wise MoE at the tested scales. This shows that, under a shared-pool design, expert parameters need not grow linearly with depth; they can grow sublinearly while remaining more efficient and effective than vanilla MoE. Further analysis shows that UniPool's benefits compose with finer-grained expert decomposition.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [253] [Near-Tight Approximation Algorithms for Bottleneck Multiple Knapsack Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05233)
*Lin Chen,Tingwei Hu,Yuchen Mao,Yong Chen,Lili Mei,An Zhang,Guangting Chen,Guochuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文研究了瓶颈多重背包问题，设计了两种不同容量设置下的近似算法：相同容量情况下达到2/3的近似比（几乎匹配下界），任意容量情况下达到1/2的近似比（且该近似比是最优的）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在解决瓶颈多重背包问题——一种将物品分配给多个背包以最大化最小背包所获利润的组合优化问题。这是一个具有实际应用背景的NP难问题，需要设计有效的近似算法。

Method: 论文采用了近似算法设计方法，针对两种不同的knapsacks容量设置给出了不同的算法策略。对于相同容量的knapsacks，给出了(2/3 - ε)-近似算法；对于任意容量的knapsacks，给出了(1/2 - ε)-近似算法。

Result: 当所有背包容量相同时，获得了(2/3 - ε)-近似算法，几乎匹配现有下界(2/3 + ε)；当背包容量任意时，获得了(1/2 - ε)-近似算法，并证明了(1/2 + ε)-不可近似性下界，实现了近似比的最优性。

Conclusion: 对于相同容量knapsacks的情况，算法几乎达到了下界（2/3 + ε）的不可近似性边界；对于任意容量的情况，算法达到了上界（1/2 + ε）的不可近似性边界，实现了紧密的近似比。

Abstract: In the bottleneck multiple knapsack problem, we are given a set of items and a set of knapsacks, where each item has a profit and a weight, and each knapsack has a capacity. Our goal is to assign items to knapsacks so as to maximize the minimum profit received by any knapsack subject to the capacity constraint.
  When all knapsacks have identical capacity, we give a $(\frac{2}{3} - \varepsilon)$-approximation algorithm for any constant $\varepsilon > 0$. This result almost matches the $(\frac{2}{3} + \varepsilon)$ inapproximability bound for the bottleneck multiple subset sum problem (Caprara et al., 2000).
  When the knapsacks can have arbitrary capacities, we propose a $(\frac{1}{2} - \varepsilon)$-approximation algorithm for any constant $\varepsilon > 0$. We also prove a hardness bound of $(\frac{1}{2} + \varepsilon)$ for any constant $\varepsilon > 0$.

</details>


### [254] [A Separator for Minor-Free Graphs Beyond the Flow Barrier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05494)
*Hung Le*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文在K_h-minor-free图的平衡分割问题上取得突破，将此前基于流-割对偶性的O(h log h √n)界改进为O(h√(log h)√n)，首次突破流-分割障碍（flow barrier）。新算法基于Alon、Seymour和Thomas的迭代框架，关键创新是引入低直径分解（low-diameter decomposition）来克服原框架中邻居界（neighborhood bound）的紧绷性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1990年，Alon、Seymour和Thomas提出K_h-minor-free图的平衡分割子大小猜想为O(h√n)，但后续二十年仅能达到O(h^3/2√n)。Kawarabayashi和Reed虽得到O(h√n + f(h))的分割子，但f(h)增长极快，实际意义有限。Spalding-Jamieson基于流-割对偶性达到O(h log h log log h √n)，逼近O(h log h √n)的流障碍界。本文旨在探索是否能突破流障碍，向Alon-Seymond-Thomas猜想迈近一步。

Method: 算法分为两部分：（1）简单适配Korhonen和Lokshtanov的流算法，得到O(h log h √n)分割子，重现流障碍界；（2）核心贡献：在Alon-Seymour-Thomas迭代框架中引入低直径分解技术。该框架原有一个称为"邻居界"的关键组件已被证明是紧绷的。通过低直径分解，在付出log h因子代价的同时，将邻居界降低h倍，最终将分割子大小中的√h因子替换为√(log h)。

Result: 构造出大小为O(h√(log h)√n)的平衡分割子，首次突破流障碍。此结果比此前最好的O(h log h √n)界有显著改进，向O(h√n)的猜想目标迈进。

Conclusion: 本文首次打破基于流-割对偶性方法的障碍，是解决Alon-Seymour-Thomas猜想的重要一步。关键洞见在于用低直径分解替代已被证明紧绷的邻居界组件，在框架层面实现突破。

Abstract: In 1990, Alon, Seymour, and Thomas gave the first balanced separator of size $O(h^{3/2}\sqrt{n})$ for any $K_h$-minor-free graph, which has had numerous algorithmic applications. They conjectured that the size of the balanced separator can be reduced to $O(h\sqrt{n})$, which is asymptotically tight. Two decades later, Kawarabayashi and Reed constructed a separator of size $O(h\sqrt{n} + f(h))$ based on the graph minor structure theorem, where $f(h)$ is an extremely fast-growing function; their separator's size is only better for a very small value of $h$. Recently, Spalding-Jamieson constructed a separator of size $O(h\log h \log\log h \sqrt{n})$; the technique is rooted in concurrent flow-sparsest cut duality. Spalding-Jamieson's separator comes very close to $O(h\log h \sqrt{n})$, which is the barrier for techniques based on the flow-cut duality.
  In this work, we first present a simple adaptation of a flow-based algorithm of Korhonen and Lokshtanov to construct a balanced separator of size $O(h\log h \sqrt{n})$, matching the flow barrier. This result motivates the question of whether the flow barrier can be broken, which would be a stepping stone toward resolving the conjecture of Alon, Seymour, and Thomas. The main result of our work is a positive answer to this question: we construct a balanced separator of size $O(h \sqrt{\log h} \sqrt{n})$. Surprisingly, perhaps, our algorithm is still based on the iterative framework of Alon, Seymour, and Thomas, although a key component of their algorithm within this framework, called the neighborhood bound, was shown to be tight. Our new idea is to incorporate a low-diameter decomposition into the framework, allowing us to reduce the neighborhood bound by a factor of $h$, at the cost of a factor $\log h$, which translates to a reduction from $\sqrt{h}$ to $\sqrt{\log h}$ in the final separator's size.

</details>


### [255] [Nearly Optimal Attention Coresets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05602)
*Edo Liberty,Alexandr Andoni,Eldar Kleiner*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We consider the problem of estimating the Attention mechanism in small space, and prove the existence of coresets for it of nearly optimal size. Specifically, we show that for any set of unit-norm keys and values $(K,V)$ in $\mathbb{R}^d$, there exists a subset $(K',V')$ of size at most $O({\sqrt{d} e^{ρ+o(ρ)}/\varepsilon})$ such that \[ \left\| \operatorname{Attn}(q,K,V)- \operatorname{Attn}(q,K',V') \right\| \le \varepsilon \] simultaneously for all queries whose norm is bounded by $ρ$. This outperforms the best known results for this problem. We also offer an improved lower bound showing that $\varepsilon$-coresets must have size $Ω({\sqrt{d} e^ρ/ε})$.

</details>


### [256] [Algorithmic Phase Transition for Large Independent Sets in Dense Hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05618)
*Abhishek Dhawan,Nhi U. Dinh,Eren C. Kızıldağ,Neeladri Maitra,Bayram A. Şahin*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究稠密随机超图中寻找大独立集的计算复杂性。在稀疏 regime 广泛使用的局部/低阶多项式(LDP)框架在稠密 setting 下失效。本文针对 (i) 稠密 r-均匀 Erdős-Rényi 超图和 (ii) 稠密 r 部超图中的 γ-平衡独立集问题，设计在线算法分别达到 r^(1/(r-1)) 和 (max_i γ_i)^(-1/(r-1)) 的近似因子，并证明这些结果是最佳的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏 regime 下局部/低阶多项式(LDP)框架已取得丰富成果，但在稠密 setting 下需要完全不同的算法范式——在线算法缺乏稳定性保证。更重要的是，即使对于经典 Erdős-Rényi 图，LDP 在在线算法可访问的'简单' regime 也可能失效，这挑战了稠密模型中这些方法的可行性。因此需要探索新的算法框架来解决稠密随机超图中的独立集问题。

Method: 采用在线算法框架，针对两种模型分别设计算法。算法通过在稠密随机超图上逐步构建独立集，在不知道完整图结构的情况下做出贪心选择决策，从而在均匀模型和 r 部模型中分别达到 r^(1/(r-1)) 和 (max_i γ_i)^(-1/(r-1)) 的近似因子。

Result: 成功确定最大独立集的精确规模，并为两种模型设计了达到最优近似因子的在线算法。对于均匀模型，独立集大小最优估计为渐近密度 r^(1/(r-1))；对于 r 部模型，γ-平衡独立集的最优估计涉及更精细的约束。通过匹配下界证明这些近似因子是紧的，任何在线算法都无法突破。

Conclusion: 对于两种模型均获得最优的在线算法近似因子：均匀模型为 r^(1/(r-1))，r 部模型为 (max_i γ_i)^(-1/(r-1))，且通过匹配的下界证明了这些近似因子在在线算法框架下不可突破。

Abstract: We study the algorithmic tractability of finding large independent sets in dense random hypergraphs. In the sparse regime, much of the natural algorithms can be formulated within either the local or the low-degree polynomial (LDP) framework, and a rich literature has subsequently identified nearly sharp algorithmic thresholds within these classes by exploiting their stability. In the dense setting, however, the algorithmic paradigms are fundamentally different: they are online and thus need not be stable. Perhaps more crucially, even for the classical Erdős-Rényi random graph $G(n,p)$, LDPs are conjectured to fail in the 'easy' regime accessible to online algorithms, thereby challenging their viability for dense models.
  Our focus is on two models: (i) finding large independent sets in dense $r$-uniform Erdős-Rényi hypergraphs, and (ii) the more challenging problem of finding large $γ$-balanced independent sets in dense $r$-uniform $r$-partite hypergraphs, where the $i$-th coordinate of $γ\in\mathbb{Q}^r$ specifies the proportion of vertices from $V_i$ in the independent set. For both models, we pinpoint the size of the largest independent set and design online algorithms that achieve a multiplicative approximation factor of $r^{1/(r-1)}$ in the uniform and $(\max_i γ_i)^{-1/(r-1)}$ in the $r$-partite model. Furthermore, we establish matching algorithmic lower bounds, showing that these computational gaps are sharp: no online algorithms can breach these gaps.

</details>


### [257] [Discrete Optimal Transport: Rapid Convergence of Simulated Annealing Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05877)
*Yuchen He,Tianhui Jiang,Sihan Wang,Chihao Zhang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: Develops a discrete optimal transport framework to analyze simulated annealing on finite state spaces. Establishes non-asymptotic convergence guarantees by relating KL divergence to a new notion of discrete action, extending continuous-space action-based methods to discrete settings. Key applications: O(n⁵β²/ε) complexity for mean-field Ising model and polynomial complexity for mean-field q-state Potts model in the high-temperature regime.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Provide non-asymptotic convergence guarantees for simulated annealing on finite state spaces, extending action-based analysis from continuous (Langevin) to discrete settings, and deriving concrete complexity bounds for fundamental statistical physics models.

Method: (1) Define generalized discrete Wasserstein-2 distance and discrete action for probability measure paths on graphs, based on Maas's discrete optimal transport; (2) Establish non-asymptotic convergence bounds relating KL divergence to discrete action; (3) Exploit symmetry of models to reduce analysis to low-dimensional projected chains; (4) Apply to mean-field Ising and Potts models.

Result: For mean-field Ising model: annealed single-site Glauber dynamics achieves ε error in KL divergence in O(n⁵β²/ε) steps for any β ≥ 0. For mean-field q-state Potts model: annealed (q-1)-block Glauber dynamics achieves ε error in poly(n, β, 1/ε) steps for β ≥ β_s = q/2 (where disordered phase loses stability).

Conclusion: The framework successfully provides finite-sample guarantees for simulated annealing by establishing polynomial upper bounds on discrete action. For the mean-field Ising model, O(n⁵β²/ε) complexity is achieved. For the mean-field q-state Potts model, poly(n, β, 1/ε) complexity is achieved in the high-temperature regime β ≥ β_s = q/2.

Abstract: We develop a discrete optimal transport framework for analyzing simulated annealing algorithms on finite state spaces. Building on the discrete Wasserstein metric introduced by Maas (J. Funct. Anal., 2011), we define a generalized discrete Wasserstein-2 distance and the associated notion of \emph{discrete action} for paths of probability measures on graphs. Using these tools, we establish non-asymptotic convergence guarantees for simulated annealing: the KL divergence between the algorithm's output and the target distribution is controlled by the discrete action of the annealing path. This can be viewed as the discrete counterpart of the action-based analysis of annealed Langevin dynamics in continuous spaces by Guo, Tao, and Chen (ICLR 2025).
  As applications, we analyze simulated annealing for two fundamental models in statistical physics. For the \emph{mean-field Ising model}, we show that annealed single-site Glauber dynamics achieves $\varepsilon$ error in KL divergence in $O(n^5β^2/\varepsilon)$ steps at \emph{any} inverse temperature $β\ge 0$. For the \emph{mean-field $q$-state Potts model}, we show that annealed $(q-1)$-block Glauber dynamics achieves $\varepsilon$ error in $\mathrm{poly}(n, β, 1/\varepsilon)$ steps for all $β\ge β_{\mathsf{s}}=q/2$, the regime where the disordered phase has completely lost stability. In both cases, the key technical contribution is a polynomial upper bound on the discrete action, obtained by exploiting the symmetry of the model to reduce the analysis to a low-dimensional projected chain.

</details>


### [258] [Label Correcting Algorithms for the Multiobjective Temporal Shortest Path Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05954)
*Edina Marica,Clemens Thielen,Alina Wittmann*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 论文研究无单调性和等距性假设下的单源多目标时序最短路径问题。由于零持续时间时序循环的处理困难，引入路径长度上界K的限制条件，开发通用标签修正算法，并给出无需上界即可收敛的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标签设置算法依赖单调性（静态最短路径中非负性的推广）和等距性（路径扩展时目标值顺序保持）两个性质。实际应用中这些假设可能不成立，因此需要研究更一般的情形。

Method: 引入最大路径长度K的受限变体；开发通用标签修正算法；建立确保算法收敛到所有非支配解的充分条件。

Result: 在无单调性和/或等距性假设下，零持续时间时序循环可能需要被遍历任意有限次才能生成所有非支配图像。通过限制路径长度K，开发了可在有限时间终止的标签修正算法，并证明了在特定条件下K可取无穷大。

Conclusion: 提供了无需单调性和等距性假设的多目标时序最短路径问题的通用算法框架，通过最大路径长度约束保证有限终止，并通过充分条件识别无需此约束即可保证完整性的问题类。

Abstract: Given a directed, discrete-time temporal graph $G=(V,R)$, a start node $s\in V$, and $p\geq1$ objectives, the single-source multiobjective temporal shortest path problem asks, for each $v\in V$, for the set of nondominated images of temporal $s$-$v$-paths together with a corresponding efficient path for each image. A recent general label setting algorithm for this problem relies on two properties of the objectives - monotonicity and isotonicity. Monotonicity generalizes the nonnegativity assumption required by label setting methods for the classical additive single-objective shortest path problem on static graphs, while isotonicity ensures that the order of the objective values of two paths is preserved when both are extended by the same arc.
  In this paper, we study the problem without assuming monotonicity and/or isotonicity. A key difficulty in this setting is that zero-duration temporal cycles may need to be traversed an arbitrary finite number of times to generate all nondominated images. This motivates the study of a restricted problem variant in which a maximum admissible path length $K$ is imposed, and only paths containing at most $K$ arcs are considered. We develop general label correcting algorithms for this setting and establish several sufficient conditions under which such a bound is not required, implying that the algorithms compute all nondominated images.

</details>


### [259] [The Pareto Frontier of Randomized Learning-Augmented Online Bidding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06106)
*Mathis Degryse,Imrane Saakour,Christoph Dürr,Spyros Angelopoulos*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究随机学习增强型在线竞价问题，提出竞价函数统一框架，证明了当鲁棒性R≥2.885时一致性C的上下界匹配，并将理论成果应用于增量中位数问题验证实际效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线竞价是online decision-making中的经典问题，广泛应用于资源分配、层次聚类和近似算法分析等领域。研究学习增强型变体，即在线算法借助预测预言机生成随机竞价序列。

Method: 引入竞价函数(bidding function)概念作为一种新颖的抽象，为随机竞价策略的设计与分析提供统一框架。基于该框架推导出最优一致性C关于鲁棒性R的解析上下界。

Result: 给出了最优一致性C作为鲁棒性R函数的解析上下界，当R≥2.885时上下界匹配，关闭了先前研究的空白。

Conclusion: 理论结果通过增量中位数问题的实验应用得到验证，展示了该算法在实践聚类设置中的适用性。当R≥2.885时，上界与下界匹配，有效弥补了先前研究的空白。

Abstract: Online bidding is a classical problem in online decision-making, with applications in resource allocation, hierarchical clustering, and the analysis of approximation algorithms. We study its randomized learning-augmented variant, where an online algorithm generates a sequence of random bids while leveraging predictions from an oracle. We provide analytical upper and lower bounds on the optimal consistency $C$ as a function of the robustness $R$, which match when $R \geq 2.885$, effectively closing the gap left by previous work. The key technical ingredient is the notion of a bidding function, a novel abstraction that provides a unified framework for the design and analysis of randomized bidding strategies. We complement our theoretical results with an experimental application of randomized bidding to the incremental median problem, demonstrating the applicability of our algorithm in practical clustering settings.

</details>


### [260] [Bilateral Treewidth for QBF: Where Strategies and Resolution Meet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06262)
*Robert Ganian,Marlene Gründel*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对量化布尔公式（QBF）在树width类参数下的易解性研究，提出双侧树width这一新概念，成功统一了此前互不可比的策略基和Resolution基两类技术路线，并证明了基于该参数的QBF求值问题具有固定参数易解性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAT问题在原生图树width参数化下是易解的，但QBF即使在常数树width下仍是PSPACE-完全的。原因在于普通树width未考虑前缀信息（存在量词与全称量词的区别及量化顺序）。现有工作通过策略或Resolution技术各self {independently}发展了变体，形成互不可比的两种技术路线。

Method: 提出了双侧树width（bilateral treewidth）概念，这是一种结合了策略基和Q-Resolution技术的分解参数；基于该参数设计了固定参数算法对QBFs进行求值。

Result: 证明了基于双侧树width的QBF求值问题具有固定参数易解性（Fixed-Parameter Tractability）。双侧树width严格强于已有方法。

Conclusion: 本研究通过引入双侧树width，提出了一个新的固定参数易解框架，统一了此前互不可比的两类技术路线。假设给定树分解，算法在实际应用中具有重要意义。

Abstract: Treewidth is a well-studied decompositional parameter to measure the tree-likeness of a graph. While the propositional satisfiability problem (SAT) is known to be tractable when parameterized by the treewidth of the underlying primal graph, the evaluation of quantified Boolean formulas (QBFs) remains PSPACE-complete even on formulas of constant treewidth. Intuitively, this is because ordinary treewidth does not take into account the prefix of the QBF: it neither distinguishes between existential and universal variables, nor accounts for the order in which they are quantified. In the past, several weaker variants of treewidth have been devised to incorporate prefix-sensitive information. To establish tractability for QBFs under these notions, prior work has employed either strategy- or resolution-based techniques, thereby dividing the parameterized complexity landscape of QBF into two regimes that are incomparable in strength. We establish fixed-parameter tractability with respect to bilateral treewidth, a novel and strictly more powerful decompositional parameter that combines these rivaling approaches by simultaneously allowing for branching on strategies and performing Q-resolution. As in previous works in this direction, our algorithm assumes that a suitable tree decomposition is provided on the input.

</details>


### [261] [On the Parameterized Approximability of (Mergeable) Sum of Radii Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06398)
*Ameet Gadekar*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: k-Median clustering with sum of radii objective is W[2]-hard, ruling out FPT algorithms and efficient parameterized approximation schemes, but FPT approximations of 8/3+ε (potentially 2+ε) are achievable for constrained variants including fair, diverse, and private clustering.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Despite extensive study of k-MSR from approximation and parameterized algorithm perspectives, its exact parameterized complexity classification and the existence of efficient parameterized approximation schemes (EPAS) remained open problems.

Method: For hardness: reduction from Dominating Set to establish W[2]-hardness and EPAS lower bounds. For algorithms: mergeable constraints framework with assignment subroutine access.

Result: W[2]-hardness for k-MSR (parameterized by k); EPAS ruled out unless W[2]=FPT; n^o(k) lower bound under ETH; FPT (8/3+ε)-approximation (improved from 4+ε); potential (2+ε)-approximation with assignment subroutine; applications to fair, diverse, and private clustering.

Conclusion: k-MSR is W[2]-hard parameterized by k, ruling out FPT algorithms and efficient parameterized approximation schemes (EPAS) under standard assumptions. The lower bounds also strengthen to n^o(k) under ETH. On the positive side, the paper achieves (8/3+ε)-approximation in FPT time, with potential to reach (2+ε) under certain conditions, applying to multiple constrained clustering variants.

Abstract: The sum of radii problem ($k$-MSR) asks, given a metric space on $n$ points, to place $k$ balls covering all points so as to minimize the sum of their radii. Despite extensive study from the perspectives of approximation and parameterized algorithms, the exact parameterized complexity of the problem and the existence of efficient parameterized approximation schemes remained open. We advance this understanding on both the hardness and algorithmic fronts.
  We begin by showing that $k$-MSR is $W[2]$-hard parameterized by $k$, thereby pinpointing its location in the $W$-hierarchy. Moreover, via our reduction, we rule out efficient parameterized approximation schemes (EPAS)--that is, $(1+ε)$-approximations running in time $f(k,ε)\cdot \mathrm{poly}(n)$--unless $W[2] = FPT$. Assuming the Exponential Time Hypothesis, we further rule out such algorithms running in time $f(k,ε)\cdot n^{o(k)}$, strengthening recent lower bounds for the problem.
  On the algorithmic side, we study $k$-MSR under the framework of mergeable constraints, which captures a broad class of clustering constraints, including fairness, diversity, and lower bounds. We obtain an FPT $(\frac{8}{3}+ε)$-approximation, improving upon the previous best guarantee of $(4+ε)$. Moreover, given access to a suitable assignment subroutine, we achieve a $(2+ε)$-approximation, matching the best known bound for the unconstrained problem. This, in turn, yields $(2+ε)$ FPT-approximations for several important settings, including $(t,k)$-fair, $(α,β)$-fair, $\ell$-diversity, and private clustering.

</details>


### [262] [Fast decremental tree sums in forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06555)
*Benjamin Aram Berendsohn,Marek Sokołowski*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文研究了删除专属动态森林上的两类查询问题。对于tree-sum查询，利用微宏树分解技术实现O(log* n)操作时间（无权情况下O(1)），并证明在群模型下达到通用最优O(OPT(F,m))；对于subtree-sum查询，给出了多种变体的紧边界。研究表明删除专属问题确实可以比标准O(log n)更快地求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索删除专属（deletion-only）动态森林问题是否能比标准动态森林数据结构（如top trees）的O(log n)更快地求解。研究聚焦两类查询：tree-sum（连通分量权重和）与subtree-sum（子树权重和）。

Method: 1. 微宏树分解（micro-macro tree decomposition）技术实现O(log* n)的tree-sum查询；2. 结合小规模实例的预计算与OPT行为分析实现群模型下的通用最优算法；3. 对多种问题变体进行上下界规约分析证明紧边界。

Result: 1. tree-sum：O(n)预处理后单次操作仅需O(log* n)（无权森林可达O(1)）；2. 在群模型下实现了运行时间O(OPT(F,m))的通用最优算法；3. 对subtree-sum多种变体给出了紧边界。

Conclusion: 该研究显著改进了动态森林问题的现有上界，证明删除操作可以比标准O(log n)更快。对于tree-sum查询，通过微宏树分解实现了O(log* n)的操作时间（无权情况下O(1)），并证明了群模型下的通用最优性。对于subtree-sum查询，给出了多种变体的紧边界。这些结果深化了我们对删除专属动态图问题的理解。

Abstract: We study two fundamental decremental dynamic graph problems. In both problems, we need to maintain a vertex-weighted forest of size $n$ under edge deletions, weight updates, and a certain information-retrieval query. Both problems can be solved in $O(\log n)$ time per update/query using standard dynamic forest data structures like top trees, even if additionally edge insertions are allowed. We investigate whether the deletion-only problem can be solved faster.
  First, we consider $\texttt{tree-sum}$ queries, where we ask for the sum of vertex weights in one of the connected components (i.e., trees) in the forest. We give a data structure with $O(n)$ preprocessing time and $O(\log^* n)$ time per operation, based on a micro-macro tree decomposition (Alstrup et al., 1997). If the forest is unweighted (i.e., all weights are 1 and cannot be changed), then the operation time can be improved to $O(1)$.
  Additionally, we give an asymptotically universally optimal algorithm. More specifically, our algorithm works in the group model, and processes $m$ operations on an initial forest $F$ in running time $O( \mathrm{OPT}(F, m) )$. Here $\mathrm{OPT}(F, m)$ is the number of weight additions and subtractions that a best possible algorithm performs to handle a worst-case instance for a fixed initial forest $F$ and a fixed number $m$ of operations. We achieve this with a combination of the aforementioned decomposition technique, precomputation of optimal data structures for very small instances, and some insights into the behavior of $\mathrm{OPT}$. Note that even the worst-case complexity of this algorithm remains unknown to us.
  Second, we consider $\texttt{subtree-sum}$ queries. Here, the forest is rooted, and a query $\texttt{subtree-sum}(v)$ returns the sum of weights in the subtree rooted at $v$. We show tight bounds for several variants of this problem. [...]

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [263] [Understanding Annotator Safety Policy with Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05329)
*Alex Oesterling,Donghao Ren,Yannick Assogba,Dominik Moritz,Sunnie S. Y. Kim,Leon Gatys,Fred Hohman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出注解策略模型（APMs），通过学习标注行为来推断标注者内部的安全策略，无需额外标注成本即可区分标注分歧的来源（操作失误、政策模糊性、价值多元主义），从而支持更具针对性、透明性和包容性的安全政策设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Annotation disagreement in safety policy is pervasive but its root causes (operational failures, policy ambiguity, value pluralism) are difficult to distinguish. Existing approaches of directly asking annotators for reasoning are costly and unreliable for both human and LLM annotators.

Method: Introduced Annotator Policy Models (APMs)—interpretable models that learn annotators' internal safety policies from labeling behavior alone, without requiring additional annotation effort or direct querying of annotator reasoning.

Result: Validated APMs achieve >80% accuracy in modeling annotator safety policy, faithfully predict responses to counterfactual edits, and recover known policy differences in controlled settings. Demonstrated applications include surfacing policy ambiguity and uncovering systematic differences in safety priorities across demographic groups.

Conclusion: APMs enable more targeted, transparent, and inclusive safety policy design by distinguishing between operational failures, policy ambiguity, and value pluralism as sources of annotation disagreement.

Abstract: Safety policies define what constitutes safe and unsafe AI outputs, guiding data annotation and model development. However, annotation disagreement is pervasive and can stem from multiple sources such as operational failures (annotators misunderstand or misexecute the task), policy ambiguity (policy wording leaves room for interpretation), or value pluralism (different annotators hold different perspectives on safety). Distinguishing these sources matters. For example, operational failures call for quality control, ambiguity calls for policy clarification, and pluralism calls for deliberation about incorporating diverse perspectives. Yet understanding why annotators disagree is difficult. Directly asking annotators for their reasoning is costly, substantially increasing annotation burden, and can be unreliable for both human and LLM annotators as self-reported reasoning often fails to reflect actual decision processes.
  We introduce Annotator Policy Models (APMs), interpretable models that learn annotators' internal safety policies from labeling behavior alone, making annotator reasoning visible and comparable without additional annotation effort. We validate that APMs accurately model annotator safety policy (>80% accuracy), faithfully predict responses to counterfactual edits, and recover known policy differences in controlled settings. Applying APMs to LLM and human annotations, we demonstrate two core applications: (1) surfacing policy ambiguity by revealing how annotators interpret safety instructions differently, and (2) surfacing value pluralism by uncovering systematic differences in safety priorities across demographic groups. Together, these capabilities support more targeted, transparent, and inclusive safety policy design.

</details>


### [264] [ZAYA1-8B Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05365)
*Robert Washbourne,Rishi Iyer,Tomas Figliolia,Henry Zheng,Ryan Lorig-Roach,Sungyeon Yang,Pritish Yuvraj,Quentin Anthony,Yury Tokpanov,Xiao Yang,Ganesh Nanduru,Stephen Ebert,Praneeth Medepalli,Skyler Szot,Srivatsan Rajagopal,Alex Ong,Bhavana Mehta,Beren Millidge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ZAYA1-8B是一个700M活跃参数、8B总参数的MoE推理模型，通过四级RL级联训练和创新的Markovian RSA测试时计算方法，在多个推理基准测试中取得优异成绩，将AIME'25提升至91.9%，几乎追平当前顶级大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在保持活跃参数低于1B的前提下，开发一个能够匹配或超越大型推理模型（如DeepSeek-R1-0528）性能的高效推理模型，同时探索测试时计算扩展的高效方法。

Method: 基于MoE++架构构建ZAYA1-8B模型，采用全AMD计算平台进行预训练、中训练和SFT；在后训练阶段使用四级RL级联（推理热身、RLVE-Gym课程、数学/代码RL、行为RL）；引入Markovian RSA方法在测试时递归聚合并行推理轨迹，仅携带4K-token尾部长度。

Result: ZAYA1-8B在AIME'25上达到91.9%，HMMT'25上达到89.6%，在多项数学和代码基准测试中匹配或超越DeepSeek-R1-0528，显著缩小了与Gemini-2.5 Pro、DeepSeek-V3.2、GPT-5-High等更大模型的差距。

Conclusion: ZAYA1-8B通过高效的MoE架构和创新的Markovian RSA测试时计算方法，在仅700M活跃参数下实现了与参数量大得多的推理模型相当甚至更好的性能，证明了小规模模型通过优化训练策略和推理方法可以达到接近顶级大模型的推理能力。

Abstract: We present ZAYA1-8B, a reasoning-focused mixture-of-experts (MoE) model with 700M active and 8B total parameters, built on Zyphra's MoE++ architecture. ZAYA1-8B's core pretraining, midtraining, and supervised fine-tuning (SFT) were performed on a full-stack AMD compute, networking, and software platform. With under 1B active parameters, ZAYA1-8B matches or exceeds DeepSeek-R1-0528 on several challenging mathematics and coding benchmarks, and remains competitive with substantially larger open-weight reasoning models. ZAYA1-8B was trained from scratch for reasoning, with reasoning data included from pretraining onward using an answer-preserving trimming scheme. Post-training uses a four-stage RL cascade: reasoning warmup on math and puzzles; a 400-task RLVE-Gym curriculum; math and code RL with test-time compute traces and synthetic code environments built from competitive-programming references; and behavioral RL for chat and instruction following. We also introduce Markovian RSA, a test-time compute method that recursively aggregates parallel reasoning traces while carrying forward only bounded-length reasoning tails between rounds. In TTC evaluation, Markovian RSA raises ZAYA1-8B to 91.9\% on AIME'25 and 89.6\% on HMMT'25 while carrying forward only a 4K-token tail, narrowing the gap to much larger reasoning models including Gemini-2.5 Pro, DeepSeek-V3.2, and GPT-5-High.

</details>


### [265] [Partial Evidence Bench: Benchmarking Authorization-Limited Evidence in Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05379)
*Krti Tallam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Partial Evidence Bench，一个用于测量智能体在部分证据场景下产生不安全完整答案这一失败模式的确定性基准。该基准包含3个场景家族、72个任务和4个评估维度，验证了显式失败-报告机制相比静默过滤的安全性优势，为企业环境中的AI系统完整性评估提供了可重复的测试框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业智能体日益在受限检索系统和策略约束环境中运行。在这些设置中，访问控制可以正确执行，但系统仍可能产生看似完整的答案，而关键证据实际上位于调用者授权边界之外。这种"部分证据"场景构成了一个关键但未被充分识别的失败模式——系统表面上合规但实际产生误导性输出。

Method: 该研究提出Partial Evidence Bench，采用确定性基准测试方法论。包含3个场景家族（尽职调查、合规审计、安全事件响应），共72个任务。核心组件包括：ACL分区语料库（强制权限边界）、Oracle完整答案、Oracle授权视图答案、Oracle完整性判断以及结构化gap报告Oracle。评估维度涵盖四个方面：答案正确性、完整性意识、gap报告质量和unsafe completeness行为。

Result: 实验表明：1) 静默过滤（silent filtering）在所有场景家族中表现出灾难性的不安全行为；2) 显式失败-报告机制能够消除unsafe completeness，同时不会将任务退化为trivial弃权。真实模型初步测试揭示了模型依赖和场景敏感的差异——某些系统过度声称完整性，另一些则保守地低估完整性，或以企业可用形式报告不完整性。

Conclusion: 该基准测试为治理关键型智能体故障提供了可测量的评估框架，无需人工评判或易受污染的静态语料库。初步实验表明，显式失败-报告机制能够消除不安全的完整性，同时不将任务退化为trivial弃权。未来研究可扩展场景家族规模、探索更细粒度的ACL分区策略，并深入分析模型在企业场景下的安全性-可用性权衡。

Abstract: Enterprise agents increasingly operate inside scoped retrieval systems, delegated workflows, and policy-constrained evidence environments. In these settings, access control can be enforced correctly while the system still produces an answer that appears complete even though material evidence lies outside the caller's authorization boundary. This paper introduces Partial Evidence Bench, a deterministic benchmark for measuring that failure mode. The benchmark ships three scenario families -- due diligence, compliance audit, and security incident response -- with 72 tasks total, ACL-partitioned corpora, oracle complete answers, oracle authorized-view answers, oracle completeness judgments, and structured gap-report oracles. It evaluates systems along four surfaces: answer correctness, completeness awareness, gap-report quality, and unsafe completeness behavior. Checked-in baselines show that silent filtering is catastrophically unsafe across all shipped families, while explicit fail-and-report behavior eliminates unsafe completeness without collapsing the task into trivial abstention. Preliminary real-model runs show model-dependent and scenario-sensitive differences in whether systems overclaim completeness, conservatively underclaim, or report incompleteness in an enterprise-usable form. The benchmark's broader contribution is to make a governance-critical agent failure measurable without human judges or contamination-prone static corpora.

</details>


### [266] [BALAR : A Bayesian Agentic Loop for Active Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05386)
*Aymen Echarghaoui,Dongxia Wu,Emily B. Fox*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BALAR，一种基于贝叶斯推理的主动问答框架，通过维护信念状态并最大化互信息来选择最优问题，在侦探推理、思维谜题和临床诊断三个不同领域均取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大多数系统在对话中采用被动响应机制，缺乏对缺失信息的系统性推理机制，也无法主动判断应向用户询问什么问题以推进任务解决。

Method: BALAR采用贝叶斯推理机制维护对潜在状态的信念，通过最大化预期互信息来选择澄清性问题，并能动态扩展状态表示。算法采用外环架构，无需对LLM进行微调。

Result: BALAR在AR-Bench-DC上提升14.6%准确率，在AR-Bench-SP上提升38.5%，在iCraft-MD上提升30.5%，三项基准测试均显著超越所有基线方法。

Conclusion: BALAR作为一个无需微调的任务无关框架，在三个不同领域的基准测试中均显著超越基线模型，证明了主动推理和多轮交互策略的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly operate in interactive settings where solving a task requires multiple rounds of information exchange with a user. However, most current systems treat dialogue reactively and lack a principled mechanism to reason about what information is missing and which question should be asked next. We propose BALAR (Bayesian Agentic Loop for Active Reasoning), a task-agnostic outer-loop algorithm that requires no fine-tuning and enables structured multi-turn interaction between an LLM agent and a user. BALAR maintains a structured belief over latent states, selects clarifying questions by maximizing expected mutual information, and dynamically expands its state representation when the current one proves insufficient. We evaluate BALAR on three diverse benchmarks: AR-Bench-DC (detective cases), AR-Bench-SP (thinking puzzles), and iCraft-MD (clinical diagnosis). BALAR significantly outperforms all baselines across all three benchmarks, with $14.6\%$ higher accuracy on AR-Bench-DC, $38.5\%$ on AR-Bench-SP, and $30.5\%$ on iCraft-MD.

</details>


### [267] [When Helpfulness Becomes Sycophancy: Sycophancy is a Boundary Failure Between Social Alignment and Epistemic Integrity in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05403)
*Jiechen Li,Catherine A. Barry,Rishika Randev,Janet Chen,Ella Jorgensen,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这篇立场论文认为LLM中的谄媚行为是社会对齐与认知诚信之间的边界失效问题。现有研究仅通过外在行为（如同意错误信念、立场反转）来操作化谄媚，未能充分描述涉及认知诚信的微妙边界失效。作者提出三条件框架识别谄媚：用户发出线索、模型通过对齐行为趋近线索、此偏移损害认知准确性或独立推理。还介绍了涵盖对齐目标、机制和严重程度的分类体系，并主张采用边界感知评估、结构化评分标准和缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing operationalizations of sycophancy capture only overt forms (agreement with incorrect beliefs, position reversals, deviation from objective correctness) but leave subtler boundary failures involving epistemic integrity and social alignment underspecified.

Method: The paper proposes a three-condition framework: (1) user expresses a cue (belief, preference, or self-concept), (2) model shifts toward that cue through alignment behavior, (3) this shift compromises epistemic accuracy, independent reasoning, or appropriate correction. Additionally introduces a taxonomy classifying sycophancy across alignment targets, mechanisms, and severity.

Result: Redefines sycophancy as alignment behavior that displaces independent epistemic judgment, rather than mere agreement, providing a more comprehensive framework for identifying and classifying the phenomenon.

Conclusion: Proposes boundary-aware assessment, structured rubrics, and mitigation strategies; positions these proposals alongside alternative views of sycophancy.

Abstract: This position paper argues that sycophancy in LLMs is a boundary failure between social alignment and epistemic integrity. Existing work often operationalizes sycophancy through external behavior such as agreement with incorrect user beliefs, position reversals, or deviation from an objective standard of correctness. These formulations capture only overt forms of the phenomenon and leave subtler boundary failures involving epistemic integrity and social alignment underspecified. We argue that sycophancy should not be understood as agreement alone, but as alignment behavior that displaces independent epistemic judgment. To clarify this boundary, we propose a three-condition framework for sycophancy. First, the user expresses a cue in the form of a belief, preference, or self-concept. Second, the model shifts toward that cue through alignment behavior. Third, this shift compromises epistemic accuracy, independent reasoning, or appropriate correction. We also introduce a taxonomy for classifying sycophancy, consisting of alignment targets, mechanisms, and severity. The paper concludes by discussing implications for alignment evaluation and argues for boundary-aware assessment, structured rubrics, and mitigation strategies, while situating these proposals alongside alternative views of sycophancy.

</details>


### [268] [Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Financial Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05409)
*Yang Shu,Yingmin Liu,Zequn Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FinAgent-RAG，一个专为金融数值推理设计的智能体RAG框架。该框架通过对比检索器、思维编程模块和自适应策略路由器三大创新，在三个金融QA基准数据集上显著超越现有方法，同时大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融文档问答需要复杂的多次数值推理，涉及结构化表格、文本叙述和脚注等异构证据。现有检索增强生成（RAG）方法采用单次检索后生成的范式，难以处理金融分析中常见的组合推理链条。

Method: 提出FinAgent-RAG，一个智能体增强的RAG框架，采用迭代检索-推理循环与自验证机制。该框架包含三个领域特定创新：(1) 对比金融检索器：通过硬负样本挖掘训练，区分语义相似但数值不同的金融段落；(2) 思维编程推理模块：生成可执行Python代码进行精确算术运算；(3) 自适应策略路由器：根据问题复杂度动态分配计算资源。

Result: 在FinQA、ConvFinQA、TAT-QA三个基准数据集上分别达到76.81%、78.46%、74.96%的执行准确率，较最强基线提升5.62-9.32个百分点。在FinQA上降低41.3%的API成本同时保持准确性。

Conclusion: FinAgent-RAG 通过消融实验、四种LLM的跨主干评估以及部署成本分析，验证了该框架的鲁棒性和对金融机构的实际适用性。

创新点1 - 对比金融检索器（Contrastive Financial Retriever）：通过硬负样本挖掘训练，能够区分语义相似但数值不同的金融段落，提升检索精度。

创新点2 - 思维编程模块（Program-of-Thought）：生成可执行的Python代码进行精确算术运算，避免依赖易出错的LLM心算。

创新点3 - 自适应策略路由器（Adaptive Strategy Router）：根据问题复杂度动态分配计算资源，在FinQA上降低41.3%的API成本同时保持准确性。

实验结果表明FinAgent-RAG在三个基准数据集上均大幅超越最强基线模型（提升5.62-9.32个百分点），证明了该框架在金融数值推理任务中的有效性和实用价值。

Abstract: Financial document question answering (QA) demands complex multi-step numerical reasoning over heterogeneous evidence--structured tables, textual narratives, and footnotes--scattered across corporate filings. Existing retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) approaches adopt a single-pass retrieve-then-generate paradigm that struggles with the compositional reasoning chains prevalent in financial analysis. We propose FinAgent-RAG, an agentic RAG framework that orchestrates iterative retrieval-reasoning loops with self-verification, specifically engineered for the precision requirements of financial numerical reasoning. The framework integrates three domain-specific innovations: (1) a Contrastive Financial Retriever trained with hard negative mining to distinguish semantically similar but numerically distinct financial passages, (2) a Program-of-Thought reasoning module that generates executable Python code for precise arithmetic rather than relying on error-prone LLM-based mental computation, and (3) an Adaptive Strategy Router that dynamically allocates computational resources based on question complexity, reducing API costs by 41.3% on FinQA while preserving accuracy. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets--FinQA, ConvFinQA, and TAT-QA--demonstrate that FinAgent-RAG achieves 76.81%, 78.46%, and 74.96% execution accuracy respectively, outperforming the strongest baseline by 5.62--9.32 percentage points. Ablation studies, cross-backbone evaluation with four LLMs, and deployment cost analysis confirm the framework's robustness and practical viability for financial institutions.

</details>


### [269] [FinRAG-12B: A Production-Validated Recipe for Grounded Question Answering in Banking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05482)
*Denys Katerenchuk,Pablo Duboue,Keelan Evanini,David Gondek,Nithin Govindugari,Olivier Allauzen,Joshua Baptiste,David J More,Joshua Schechter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于银行业的高度准确、引文grounded的领域LLM统一训练框架。通过高效数据管道(143M tokens)训练12B模型，在引文准确性和校准拒绝上优于GPT-4.1。系统已在40+金融机构部署，查询解决率提升7.1%，响应速度提升3-5倍，成本降低20-50倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在银行业应用面临阻力，因为该行业对准确性、监管合规性和可验证 grounded 回答有严格要求，需要模型能够准确引用来源、合理拒绝无法回答的问题，而非像通用模型那样产生幻觉或过度拒绝。

Method: 1) 数据生成管道：结合LLM-as-Judge过滤、引文标注和课程学习，仅用143M tokens数据；2) 校准拒绝机制：训练时使用22%不可回答样本，使模型学会在适当时说"I don't know"；3) 端到端方法论：从数据策展到量化服务；4) 最终产物为12B参数模型。

Result: 1) 12B模型在引文grounding上优于GPT-4.1，基础模型相比仅有适度引文权衡；2) 校准拒绝率达12%，显著优于基础模型的不安全4.3%率和GPT-4.1的过度拒绝20.2%；3) 部署后查询解决率提升7.1个百分点(p < 0.001)；4) 响应速度提升3-5倍，成本降低20-50倍。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一个统一的数据高效框架，成功训练出适用于银行业的高性能领域LLM，在答案质量、引文准确性和校准拒绝方面均优于GPT-4.1。该系统已在40+金融机构部署，查询解决率提升7.1个百分点，响应速度提升3-5倍，成本降低20-50倍，证明了领域特定LLM在严格监管环境中的可行性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are rapidly being adopted across various domains. However, their adoption in banking industry faces resistance due to demands for high accuracy, regulatory compliance, and the need for verifiable and grounded responses. We present a unified, data-efficient framework for training grounded domain-specific LLMs that optimizes answer quality, citation grounding, and calibrated refusal under real-world deployment constraints. First, we describe a data generation pipeline that combines LLM-as-a-Judge filtering, citation annotation, and curriculum learning with only 143M tokens. The resulting 12B model achieves high answer quality outperforming GPT-4.1 on citation grounding, with a modest citation tradeoff versus the untuned base. Second, we propose a calibrated refusal mechanism: training on 22% unanswerable examples yield a 12% "I don't know" rate, substantially improving over the base model's unsafe 4.3% rate while avoiding GPT-4.1's over-refusal (20.2%). Third, we present an end-to-end methodology spanning from data curation to quantized serving. The system is deployed at 40+ financial institutions, achieving a 7.1 percentage point improvement in query resolution (p < 0.001). Additionally, the model delivers 3-5x faster responses at 20-50x lower cost compared to GPT-4.1.

</details>


### [270] [LaTA: A Drop-in, FERPA-Compliant Local-LLM Autograder for Upper-Division STEM Coursework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05410)
*Jesse A. Rodríguez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LaTA是一个本地部署的开源LaTeX作业自动评分系统，基于开源大模型在单台Mac Studio上即可为约200名学生提供零边际成本、快速准确且完全合规的评分服务，实验证明其能显著提升学生成绩和学习自信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM评分方案依赖第三方API，存在FERPA合规风险和学生数据泄露隐患，且需对作业进行大幅改造。本文旨在构建一个可直接替换、数据完全本地化的LLM评分系统。

Method: LaTA采用四阶段流水线（接收、分割、评分、报告），在本地硬件（Mac Studio）上运行开源链式思维LLM（gpt-oss:120b），通过YAML规则集对LaTeX格式的学生作业与讲师提供的参考答案进行逐项二值评分对比。

Result: 在俄勒冈州立大学ME 373课程（约200名学生）中，LaTA实现了零边际成本、1-3分钟/份的评分效率，评分错误率约0.02-0.04%/评分线。相比传统评分班级，LaTA评分班级期中成绩高出约11%、期末成绩高出约8%；问卷调查（N=159）显示学生在所有学习目标上的自信度均有大幅提升（Δ≥+1.49李克特量表分，p<10^-27）。

Conclusion: LaTA证明了本地部署的开源大模型可以有效地替代传统评分方式。该系统在保护学生隐私的前提下，实现了极低的评分错误率（0.02-0.04%/评分线），显著提升了学生成绩（期中提高11%，期末提高8%），并大幅增强了学生的自我效能感。该工作为需要在保护数据安全的同时部署AI评分工具的教育机构提供了一个可行的开源解决方案。

Abstract: Large-language-model (LLM) graders promise to relieve the grading burden of upper-division STEM courses, but most deployments to date send student work to third-party APIs, violating FERPA and exposing institutions to data risk while requiring substantial assignment modification. We present $\textbf{LaTA}\ (\textit{LaTeX Teaching Assistant})$, a drop-in, open-source autograder that runs entirely on commodity on-premises hardware and assumes a LaTeX-native workflow already adopted by many engineering and physics courses. LaTA implements a four-stage pipeline (ingest, segment, grade, report) using a locally hosted open-weight chain-of-thought LLM grader (gpt-oss:120b) that compares student work to an instructor-authored reference solution and applies a YAML rubric with binary per-item scoring. We deployed LaTA in Winter~2026 in ME 373 (Mechanical Engineering Methods) at Oregon State University, grading every weekly assignment for approximately 200 students on a single Mac Studio at \$0 marginal cost per assignment and 1--3 minutes of wall-clock time per submission, enabling regrading of corrected assignments and greatly expanded TA office hour offerings. The instructor-confirmed grading-error rate held at roughly $0.02$--$0.04\%$ per rubric line item across the term. Relative to the same instructor's previous traditionally-graded cohort, the LaTA-graded cohort outperformed by approximately $11\%$ on the midterm exam and $8\%$ on the final exam, and reported large gains in self-assessed confidence on every stated learning objective ($N = 159$ survey responses, $Δ\geq +1.49$ Likert points, $p < 10^{-27}$ on every comparison). We release the code under AGPLv3.

</details>


### [271] [From History to State: Constant-Context Skill Learning for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05413)
*Haoyang Xie,Xinyuan Wang,Yancheng Wang,Puda Zhao,Feng Ju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"恒定上下文技能学习"框架，通过将程序性知识从提示词迁移到模型权重中，解决个人AI助手的隐私-成本-能力三角困境。在ALFWorld、WebShop、SciWorld基准上验证有效，Qwen3-8B模型可达89.6%、76.8%、66.4%的任务成功率，同时将每轮提示token减少2-7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在浏览器、文件、代码和工具操作中应用广泛，但个人助手面临三重困境：云端模型虽能执行复杂工作流，却将敏感上下文暴露给外部API；本地模型虽保护隐私，但可靠性不足。此外，冗长的技能提示和不断增长的对话历史也会带来重复计算开销。

Method: 提出"恒定上下文技能学习"(Constant-Context Skill Learning)——一种上下文到权重的框架：1) 将可复用程序学习为轻量级任务族模块；2) 推理时仅需当前观测和紧凑状态块；3) 使用确定性追踪器根据任务进度渲染状态块，并提供对齐的子目标奖励；4) 各模块通过步骤级SFT训练，并利用在线RL进一步优化。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop、SciWorld三个基准上，使用Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B和Llama-3.1-8B均取得良好表现。Qwen3-8B模型经SFT+RL后，在ALFWorld上达到89.6% unseen success，WebShop达76.8% success，SciWorld达66.4% unseen success。

Conclusion: 该方法匹配或超越已发布的智能体训练结果，同时将每轮提示token减少2-7倍（相比ReAct基线）。核心贡献在于证明：程序性上下文可以从提示词迁移到模型权重中，为平衡隐私保护与智能体能力提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used to operate browsers, files, code and tools, making personal assistants a natural deployment target. Yet personal agents face a privacy-cost-capability tension: cloud models execute multi-step workflows well but expose sensitive intermediate context to external APIs, while local models preserve privacy but remain less reliable. Both settings also pay repeatedly for long skill prompts and growing histories. We propose constant-context skill learning, a context-to-weights framework for recurring agent workflows: reusable procedures are learned in lightweight task-family modules, while inference conditions only on the current observation and a compact state block. A deterministic tracker renders this state block from task progress and supplies aligned subgoal rewards, so each module can be trained with step-level SFT and refined through online RL. Across ALFWorld, WebShop, and SciWorld, our agents achieve strong performance across Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B and Llama-3.1-8B. With Qwen3-8B, SFT+RL reaches 89.6\% unseen success on ALFWorld, 76.8\% success on WebShop, and 66.4\% unseen success on SciWorld. They match or exceed strong published agent-training results while reducing prompt tokens per turn by 2--7$\times$ relative to controlled ReAct prompting baselines, showing that procedural context can be moved from prompts into weights.

</details>


### [272] [Who Prices Cognitive Labor in the Age of Agents? A Position on Compute-Anchored Wages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05558)
*Siqi Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"AI智能体因边际成本趋近于零而会将认知劳动工资压至零"的直觉观点。作者认为智能体并非劳动本身，而是将计算资本(K_c)转化为有效认知劳动(L_A)的生产技术。因此，均衡工资锚定的弹性供给边界从劳动市场转移到计算资本市场。基于因子定价框架推导出"计算锚定工资"(CAW)边界：对于人机可替代任务，人类工资上限为λ·k·r_c。通过CES聚合泛化结果，区分可替代与互补任务，并讨论要素份额影响。核心结论：认知劳动的定价者不再是劳动市场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学界普遍认为AI智能体可近乎零边际成本复制，构成无限弹性供给的劳动投入，终将把认知劳动工资压至零。本文认为这一直觉在机制上错误，但结论部分正确，且这一纠正对理论和政策都至关重要。

Method: 基于Mankiw(2020)教科书因子定价框架，构建"计算锚定工资"(CAW)模型。通过CES聚合函数泛化推导，区分可替代任务与互补任务，分析方向性技能偏向型技术变革。

Result: 对于人机可替代任务，竞争性人类工资受限于λ·k·r_c，其中r_c为计算资本租赁率，k为每单位有效智能体劳动的算力强度，λ为人类相对智能体的生产率。该结果通过CES框架实现泛化。

Conclusion: 智能体本质上是将计算资本转化为认知劳动的生产技术，而非劳动本身。认知劳动的定价权已从劳动市场转移到计算资本市场，这一人力资本经济学和AI政策制定具有深远影响。

Abstract: A natural intuition about the economics of AI agents is that, because agents can be replicated at near-zero marginal cost, they constitute a labor input in infinitely elastic supply, and therefore drive cognitive-labor wages to zero. We argue this framing is wrong in mechanism but partially correct in conclusion, and that the correction matters for both theory and policy. \textbf{Agents are not labor; they are a production technology that converts compute capital $K_c$ into effective units of cognitive labor $L_A$.} Once this is recognized, the elastic-supply margin that anchors the equilibrium wage migrates from the labor market to the compute capital market. Building on the textbook factor-pricing framework \citep{mankiw2020}, we derive a \emph{Compute-Anchored Wage} (CAW) bound stating that, on tasks where human and agent cognitive labor are substitutes, the competitive human wage is bounded above by $λ\cdot k \cdot r_c$, where $r_c$ is the rental rate of compute capital, $k$ is the compute intensity of one effective agent-labor unit, and $λ$ is the relative human-to-agent productivity. We generalize the result through CES aggregation, separate substitutable from complementary tasks (yielding a directional inversion of skill-biased technical change), and discuss factor-share consequences. The position is concise: \emph{the price-setter for cognitive labor is no longer the labor market.}

</details>


### [273] [BioResearcher: Scenario-Guided Multi-Agent for Translational Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05985)
*Remigiusz Kinas,Joanna Krawczyk,Rafał Powalski,Przemysław Pietrzak,Agnieszka Kowalewska,Krzysztof Kolmus,Maciej Sypetkowski,Łukasz Smoliński,Tomasz Jetka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Ingenix BioResearcher是一个面向转化医学的场景引导型多智能体系统，通过版本化研究剧本和多模型对账机制，在单元级、开放推理和临床端到端任务上均达到领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用基础模型和工具增强系统无法满足转化医学对审计性、场景特定工作流程的需求，难以处理异构生物医学来源的证据综合。

Method: 场景引导的多智能体系统，包含版本化研究剧本、专业子智能体委托（30+工具和ML端点）、结构化数据库访问与沙箱化代码执行、多模型对账等核心组件。

Result: 单步测试83.49%通过率；BixBench 89.33%；BaisBench 0.758平均分（Top 1）；临床端到端阳性命中率74.7%，阴性清除率96.8%。

Conclusion: BioResearcher在单元级、开放式和端到端临床评估中展现出广泛且具有竞争力的性能。

Abstract: Translational medicine turns underspecified development goals into evidence synthesis that must combine literature, trials, patents, and quantitative multi-omics analysis while preserving identifiers, uncertainty, and retrievable provenance. General-purpose foundation models and off-the-shelf tool-augmented or multi-agent systems are not built for this: they tend to produce single-shot answers or run open-endedly, and fall short on the auditable, scenario-specific workflows that heterogeneous biomedical sources demand.
  This paper introduces Ingenix BioResearcher, a scenario-guided multi-agent system that maps queries to versioned research playbooks, delegates to specialized subagents over 30+ tools and machine-learning endpoints, mixes structured database access with sandboxed code for genome-scale analyses, and applies claim-level multi-model reconciliation before editorial assembly.
  We evaluate BioResearcher across unit-level capabilities, open-ended biomedical reasoning, and end-to-end clinical discovery. It leads evaluated baselines on 109 single-step tests (83.49% pass rate; 0.892 average score), achieves strong biomedical benchmark performance (89.33% on BixBench-Verified-50 and the top 0.758 mean score on BaisBench Scientific Discovery), and leads on a 30-query clinical end-to-end benchmark with the highest positive hit rate (74.7% $\pm$ 3.3%) and negative clear rate (96.8% $\pm$ 0.2%). These results show broad, competitive performance across unit-level, open-ended, and end-to-end clinical evaluations.

</details>


### [274] [The Geopolitics of AI Safety: A Causal Analysis of Regional LLM Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05427)
*Alif Al Hasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究提出基于因果图模型的PGM框架，使用do-operator替代传统观察性方法评估LLM安全机制的公平性。通过对7个不同国家模型的分析，发现标准公平性指标因未控制情境毒性而高估人口统计偏见，并揭示了Western和Eastern模型在安全对齐模式上的显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM公平性评估主要依赖观察性方法，但测试数据集中某些人口统计与有毒话题的自然配对会产生混淆效应，导致对实际偏见的错误估计。

Method: 采用Pearl的do-operator进行因果推断，通过干预（而非观察）来隔离人口统计变量对LLM安全机制的因果效应。研究在7个指令微调模型和两个数据集（ToxiGen和BOLD）上进行大规模实证分析。

Result: 观察性偏见与因果性偏见之间存在显著差异；Western模型对特定人口群体因果拒绝率更高；Eastern模型整体干预率低但对区域人口统计存在定向敏感性。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了当前公平性评估方法存在系统性偏差，标准指标可能高估人口统计偏见。Western模型对特定人口群体表现出更高的因果拒绝率，而Eastern模型整体干预率较低但对区域人口统计存在定向敏感性。这些发现对下游应用中涉及特定人口群体（如种族、民族）的话题产生不利影响，限制了良性讨论。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are integrated into global software systems, ensuring equitable safety guardrails is a critical requirement. Current fairness evaluations predominantly measure bias observationally, a methodology confounded by the inherent toxicity of topics naturally paired with specific demographics in testing datasets. This study introduces a Probabilistic Graphical Model (PGM) framework to audit LLM safety mechanisms causally. By applying Pearl's do-operator, we mathematically isolate the causal effect of injecting a cultural demographic into a prompt. We conduct a large-scale empirical analysis across seven instruction-tuned models spanning diverse origins: the United States (Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-2-9B), Europe (Mistral-7B-v0.3), the UAE (Falcon3-7B), China (Qwen2.5-7B, DeepSeek-7B), and India (Airavata-7B). Utilizing two distinct datasets (ToxiGen and BOLD), the findings reveal a disparity between observational and interventional bias, demonstrating that standard fairness metrics can overestimate demographic bias by failing to account for context toxicity. Furthermore, the causal probabilities indicate distinct alignment trends: Western models exhibit higher causal refusal rates for specific demographic groups, whereas Eastern models demonstrate low overall intervention rates with targeted sensitivities toward regional demographics. We discuss the implications of these biases, highlighting how demographic-sensitive over-triggering restricts benign discourse in downstream applications.

</details>


### [275] [From Agent Loops to Deterministic Graphs: Execution Lineage for Reproducible AI-Native Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06365)
*Josh Rosen,Seth Rosen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了"执行谱系"（execution lineage）——一种将AI工作表示为有向无环图（DAG）的执行模型，旨在解决现有LLM Agent系统中隐式状态导致的工作产品维护困难问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型Agent系统依赖隐式对话状态，导致难以维护稳定的工作产品、隔离无关更新、或在中间产物间传播变更。这些问题在迭代修改场景中会不断累积。

Method: 将AI工作表示为带显式依赖、稳定中间边界和基于身份重放的DAG。在两个受控的政策备忘录更新任务上，将DAG重放与循环中心更新基线进行比较。

Result: 在无关分支更新中，DAG重放完美保留最终备忘录，零变更且零污染，而循环基线经常重新生成并引入无关上下文。在中间产物编辑中，所有系统都在最终备忘录中反映了新约束，但只有DAG重放实现了完美的上游保留、下游传播、未受影响产物保持和跨产物一致性。

Conclusion: 最终答案质量与维护状态质量是两种不同的指标；强循环基线在受限合成/更新任务中仍具竞争力，但即时任务成功可能掩盖部分状态不一致；执行谱系为"什么应改变、什么应保持稳定、工作如何演进"提供了更强保证。

Abstract: Large language model systems are increasingly deployed as agentic workflows that interleave reasoning, tool use, memory, and iterative refinement. These systems are effective at producing answers, but they often rely on implicit conversational state, making it difficult to preserve stable work products, isolate irrelevant updates, or propagate changes through intermediate artifacts.
  We introduce execution lineage: an execution model in which AI-native work is represented as a directed acyclic graph (DAG) of artifact-producing computations with explicit dependencies, stable intermediate boundaries, and identity-based replay. The goal is not to make the model a better one-shot writer, but to make evolving AI-generated work maintainable under change.
  We compare execution-lineage replay against loop-centric update baselines on two controlled policy-memo update tasks. In an unrelated-branch update, DAG replay preserved the final memo exactly in all runs, with zero churn and zero unrelated-branch contamination, while loop baselines regenerated the memo and frequently imported unrelated context. In an intermediate-artifact edit, all systems reflected the new constraint in the final memo, but only DAG replay achieved perfect upstream preservation, downstream propagation, unaffected-artifact preservation, and cross-artifact consistency.
  These results show that final answer quality and maintained-state quality are distinct. Strong loop baselines can remain competitive at producing polished final outputs when the task is a bounded synthesis/update problem and all current sources fit in context, but immediate task success can mask partial state inconsistency that may compound over future revisions. Execution lineage provides stronger guarantees about what should change, what should remain stable, and how work evolves across revisions.

</details>


### [276] [Authorization Propagation in Multi-Agent AI Systems: Identity Governance as Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05440)
*Krti Tallam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"授权传播"作为多智能体AI系统中未被充分认识的核心安全挑战，将授权治理从访问控制模型层面提升至基础设施层面，要求在每个交互边界持续评估和强制执行，并应在编排逻辑规模化之前完成设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全讨论过度聚焦于提示注入，忽视了多智能体系统中的独特授权问题——跨动态边界的非人类主体获取数据、委托任务、合成结果时，如何维持授权不变量。这不是提示注入的可约简问题，传统访问控制模型（RBAC/ABAC/ReBAC）也无法完全解决。

Method: 将授权传播形式化为工作流级属性，识别三个子问题（传递性委托、聚合推理、时间有效性），推导出七个结构性需求，并评估近期相关工作（调用绑定的能力令牌、任务作用域授权信封、依赖图策略执行、执行计数撤销）的收敛情况。

Result: 确认该领域正在向此问题收敛但尚未形成完整架构；来自企业级AI平台的生产环境证据表明，一般系统行为（非仅对抗性攻击）即可产生该模型预测的授权失败。

Conclusion: 授权治理必须作为基础设施层对待，需持续评估、在每个交互边界强制执行、在编排逻辑规模化之前完成设计；初步实现证据表明，即使非对抗性的一般系统行为也会产生该模型所预测的失败。

Abstract: The security discussion around agentic AI focuses heavily on prompt injection. This paper argues that multi-agent systems also create a distinct authorization problem: maintaining authorization invariants as non-human principals retrieve data, delegate tasks, and synthesize results across changing boundaries. We call this problem authorization propagation. It is not reducible to prompt injection and is not fully addressed by classical access-control models such as RBAC, ABAC, or ReBAC. The paper formalizes authorization propagation as a workflow-level property, identifies three sub-problems (transitive delegation, aggregation inference, and temporal validity), and derives seven structural requirements for authorization architectures in multi-agent AI systems. Recent work on invocation-bound capability tokens, task-scoped authorization envelopes, dependency-graph policy enforcement, and execution-count revocation demonstrates that the field is converging on the problem, but not yet on a complete architecture. The central claim is that identity governance must be treated as infrastructure: evaluated continuously, enforced at every interaction boundary, and designed into the system before orchestration logic is allowed to scale. Preliminary implementation evidence from a production enterprise AI platform shows that ordinary system behavior, not only adversarial action, already produces the failures this model predicts.

</details>


### [277] [Agentic Discovery of Exchange-Correlation Density Functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05460)
*Titouan Duston,Jiashu Liang,Yuanheng Wang,Weihao Gao,Xuelan Wen,Nan Sheng,Weiluo Ren,Yang Sun,Yixiao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用大语言模型构建agentic搜索系统，通过迭代优化发现了比ωB97M-V改进约9%的新型XC功能性SAFS26-a，同时警示AI辅助科学可能通过非物理捷径"作弊"的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确交换-相关(XC)功能性的发展仍是密度泛函理论(DFT)中的长期挑战。绝大多数XC功能性由人类研究者结合物理洞察、精确约束和经验拟合手工设计，缺乏系统性自动化的替代方案。

Method: 采用基于LLM的agentic搜索系统，通过evolutionary history引导的结构化功能性形式变更提议。采用迭代的plan-execute-summarize循环，优化功能性参数针对标准热化学数据集，并在held-out子集上评估性能。

Result: 发现的最强功能性SAFS26-a相比金标准ωB97M-V基线改进了约9%。

Conclusion: SAFS26-a功能性成功发现，但揭示了AI辅助科学中的警示：能够发现真正改进的模型同样能够利用非物理捷径来操纵基准测试；领域专业知识与明确 enforce 的约束条件对于保持结果科学可靠性仍然至关重要。

Abstract: The development of accurate exchange-correlation (XC) functionals remains a longstanding challenge in density functional theory (DFT). The vast majority of XC functionals have been hand designed by human researchers combining physical insight, exact constraints, and empirical fitting. Recent advances in large language models enable a systematic, automated alternative to this human-driven design loop. This report presents an agentic search system in which an LLM proposes structured functional-form changes guided by evolutionary history. The system attempts to improve functional performance through an iterative plan-execute-summarize loop, where improvements are measurable by optimizing functional parameters against a standard thermochemistry dataset, then evaluating performance on a held-out subset. The strongest discovered functional, SAFS26-a (Seed Agentic Functional Search 2026), improves upon the gold-standard ωB97M-V baseline by ~9%. These results also surface a cautionary lesson for AI-assisted science: models powerful enough to discover genuine improvements are equally capable of exploiting unphysical shortcuts to game the benchmark; domain expertise translated into explicitly enforced constraints remains essential to keeping results scientifically grounded.

</details>


### [278] [LANTERN: LLM-Augmented Neurosymbolic Transfer with Experience-Gated Reasoning Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05478)
*Mahyar Alinejad,Yue Wang,Amrit Singh Bedi,George Atia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
