### [348] [Event-Causal RAG: A Retrieval-Augmented Generation Framework for Long Video Reasoning in Complex Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06185)
*Peizheng Yan,Yu Zhao,Liang Xie,Juntong Qi,Mingming Wang,Erwei Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: We propose Event-Causal RAG, a lightweight retrieval-augmented framework for infinite long-video reasoning. Instead of fixed-length clips, we segment videos into semantic events represented as State-Event-State (SES) graphs, merged into an Event Knowledge Graph with dual-store memory supporting semantic and causal-topological retrieval, enabling efficient identification of relevant event causal chains for video foundation models.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current end-to-end video understanding methods are limited by O(n²) self-attention complexity; existing RAG approaches suffer from fragmented clip-level memory, weak temporal/causal structure modeling, and high storage/inference costs when processing ultra-long or infinite videos.

Method: Event-Causal RAG: segments streaming videos into semantic events, represents events as State-Event-State (SES) graphs, merges into global Event Knowledge Graph, dual-store memory (semantic + causal-topological retrieval), bidirectional retrieval strategy, integrates with video foundation model for answer generation.

Result: Consistently outperforms strong clip-based retrieval baselines and long-context video models, particularly on questions requiring multi-event integration and causal inference across long temporal gaps.

Conclusion: Event-Causal RAG demonstrates superior performance over clip-based retrieval baselines and long-context video models, with improved memory efficiency and robust streaming capabilities.

Abstract: Recent large vision-language models have achieved strong performance on short- and medium-length video understanding, yet they remain inadequate for ultra-long or even infinite video reasoning, where models must preserve coherent memory over extended durations and infer causal dependencies across temporally distant events. Existing end-to-end video understanding methods are fundamentally limited by the $O(n^2)$ complexity of self-attention, while recent retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) approaches still suffer from fragmented clip-level memory, weak modeling of temporal and causal structure, and high storage and online inference costs. We present Event-Causal RAG, a lightweight retrieval-augmented framework for infinite long-video reasoning. Instead of indexing fixed-length clips, our method segments streaming videos into semantically coherent events and represents each event as a structured State-Event-State (SES) graph, capturing the event together with its surrounding state transitions. These graphs are merged into a global Event Knowledge Graph and stored in a dual-store memory that supports both semantic matching and causal-topological retrieval. On top of this memory, we design a bidirectional retrieval strategy to efficiently identify the most relevant event causal chains and provide them, together with the associated video evidence, to a backbone video foundation model for answer generation. Experiments on long-video understanding benchmarks demonstrate that Event-Causal RAG consistently outperforms strong clip-based retrieval baselines and long-context video models, particularly on questions requiring multi-event integration and causal inference across long temporal gaps, while also achieving improved memory efficiency and robust streaming performance.

</details>


### [349] [Patch-Effect Graph Kernels for LLM Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06480)
*Ruben Fernandez-Boullon,David N. Olivieri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将激活 patching 的机械可解释性分析重构为图机器学习问题，通过 patch-effect 图表示和图核分析实现跨结构系统比较，发现在 GPT-2 Small 的 IOI 任务中局部边特征优于全局图描述符，并建立了对照基线以区分切片判别证据与因果电路 claims。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性研究通过激活 patching 识别因果电路，但跨多样化 prompt 和任务族扩展干预会产生高维、非结构化数据集，难以系统化比较。

Method: 提出将激活 patching 轮廓表示为模型组件上的 patch-effect 图，重构机械可解释性分析为图机器学习问题。引入三种图构建方法：基于因果中介的直接影响（CI）、偏相关（PC）和共影响（co-influence），并应用图核分析结构。在 GPT-2 Small 上使用 IOI 及其相关任务进行评估，通过 screened paired-patching 验证候选边，并设置 prompt-only 和 raw patch-effect 对照实验。

Result: Patch-effect 图保留了判别性结构信号；局部边槽特征比全局图形状描述符具有更高分类准确率；CI 和 PC 方法选择的候选边比随机或低秩候选具有更强的激活影响效应；图特征压缩了结构化 patching 信号，而 raw 张量和表面线索构成了强基线。

Conclusion: 本框架为比较 patching 衍生的结构提供了压缩与评估流程，在受控基线下区分了鲁棒的切片判别证据与更强的任务通用因果电路 claims，为机械可解释性研究提供了系统化的比较方法论。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability aims to reverse-engineer transformer computations by identifying causal circuits through activation patching. However, scaling these interventions across diverse prompts and task families produces high-dimensional, unstructured datasets that are difficult to compare systematically. We propose a framework that reframes mechanistic analysis as a graph machine-learning problem by representing activation-patching profiles as patch-effect graphs over model components. We introduce three graph-construction methods: direct-influence via causal mediation, partial-correlation, and co-influence and apply graph kernels to analyze the resulting structures. Evaluating this approach on GPT-2 Small using Indirect Object Identification (IOI) and related tasks, we find that patch-effect graphs preserve discriminative structural signals. Specifically, localized edge-slot features provide higher classification accuracy than global graph-shape descriptors. A screened paired-patching validation suggests that CI and PC selected candidate edges correspond to stronger activation-influence effects than random or low-rank candidates. Crucially, by evaluating these representations against rigorous prompt-only and raw patch-effect controls, we make the evidential scope of the benchmark explicit: graph features compress structured patching signal, while raw tensors and surface cues define strong baselines that any circuit-level claim should address. Ultimately, our framework provides a compression and evaluation pipeline for comparing patching-derived structures under controlled baselines, separating robust slice-discriminative evidence from stronger task-general causal-circuit claims.

</details>


### [350] [Systematic Evaluation of Large Language Models for Post-Discharge Clinical Action Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06191)
*Shivali Dalmia,Ananya Mantravadi,Prasanna Desikan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究系统评估了零样本/少样本LLM在临床动作提取任务上的表现，发现在二分类任务上LLM表现优异但在细粒度分类上仍落后于监督模型。研究揭示标注缺乏推理依据是当前评估的关键局限，建议构建包含推理逻辑的注释数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床文档复杂，安全关键的临床动作提取对过渡期和出院后患者安全至关重要。现有零样本/少样本LLM在此场景的能力尚未被系统性评估。

Method: 提出两阶段提取框架，通过阶段性提示策略将叙述性出院小结分解为细粒度、可操作的临床任务。框架在无任务特定微调且严格数据隐私约束下运行。

Result: 当代LLM在二值动作可执行性检测任务上达到或超越监督模型性能，但在细粒度多标签分类任务中，监督基线仍保持优势。定性错误分析发现多数失败源于模型推理与数据集标注规范之间的不一致，特别是隐含临床动作和刚性结构标注规则的情况。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在二分类任务上表现可媲美或超越监督模型，但在细粒度多标签分类上仍落后于监督基线。标注缺乏推理依据导致无法区分模型临床推理失败与标注规范不一致，建议构建包含"为什么标注"而不仅是"标注什么"的推理注释数据集，以正确评估模型的临床理解能力。

Abstract: The work in this paper evaluates zero-shot and few-shot large language models (LLMs) for safety-critical clinical action extraction using the CLIP discharge-note dataset, with particular emphasis on transitions of care and post-discharge patient safety. To manage the complexity of clinical documentation, we introduce a two-stage extraction framework that decomposes discharge notes, that are written in narrative form, into fine-grained, explicitly actionable clinical tasks through a staged prompting strategy. Our contributions include a systematic assessment of generative LLMs for clinical action extraction, a detailed comparison between general-purpose LLMs and task-specific supervised BERT-based models, and an analysis of annotation inconsistencies across different action categories. We show that contemporary LLMs achieve performance comparable to or exceeding supervised models on binary actionability detection, while supervised baselines retain a meaningful advantage on fine-grained multi-label category classification, despite the absence of task-specific fine-tuning and under strict data-privacy constraints. Qualitative error analysis reveals that many failures stem from misalignment between model reasoning and dataset annotation conventions, particularly in cases involving implicit clinical actions and rigid structural labeling rules. These results indicate that reported performance reflects model limitations due to lack of clinical reasoning, that is not captured by plain annotations. Labels without rationales make it impossible to distinguish clinical reasoning failures from annotation convention mismatches. Advancing clinical NLP requires reasoning-annotated datasets that document why specific spans are actionable, not merely which spans were labeled, enabling proper evaluation of model clinical understanding.

</details>


### [351] [Towards Annotation-Free Validation of MLLMs: A Vision-Language Logical Consistency Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06201)
*Ying Gu,Mei Chee Leong,Hui Li Tan,Shangbo Mao,Liyuan Li,Nancy Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VL-LCM框架，无需真实标注即可评估多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)的视觉-语言逻辑一致性，发现当前模型的逻辑一致性显著落后于准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统准确率评估可能奖励不合理的猜测，且在缺乏真实标注的新任务中不适用。

Method: 基于基本逻辑原理，定义视觉-语言逻辑一致性度量(VL-LCM)，在传统MC-VQA测试和无需真实标注的NaturalBench测试上评估11个来自4个主流家族的MLLMs。

Result: 尽管近期MLLMs准确率显著提升，但逻辑一致性严重滞后；VL-LCM与有标注指标具有良好相关性，且在无标注场景下同样适用。

Conclusion: 逻辑一致性可作为准确率和可靠性的双重指标，VL-LCM可用于MLLM选择、验证及新任务中的可靠答案验证。

Abstract: Dominant accuracy evaluation might reward unwarranted guessing of Large Language Models, and it might not be applicable to novel tasks for model validation without ground-truth (gt) annotation. Based on basic logic principle, we propose a novel framework to evaluate the vision-language logical consistency of MLLMs on both sufficient and necessary cause-effect relations. We define Vision-Language Logical Consistency Metric (VL-LCM) on traditional MC-VQA tests, and recent NaturalBench tests without the need for gt annotation. Through systematic experiments on representative VL benchmark MMMU and recent VL challenges like NaturalBench, we evaluated 11 recent open-source MLLMs from 4 frontier families. Our findings reveal that, despite significant progress of recent MLLMs on accuracy, logical consistency lags behind significantly. Extensive evaluations on the correlations of VL-LCM with metrics on gt, the reliability of LCM, and the relation of VL-LCM with response distribution justify the validity and applicability of VL-LCM even without gt annotation. Our findings suggest that, beyond accuracy, logical consistency could be employed for both accuracy and reliability. VL-LCM can also be employed for MLLM selection, validation, and reliable answer justification in novel tasks without gt annotation.

</details>


### [352] [SkillOS: Learning Skill Curation for Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06614)
*Siru Ouyang,Jun Yan,Yanfei Chen,Rujun Han,Zifeng Wang,Bhavana Dalvi Mishra,Rui Meng,Chun-Liang Li,Yizhu Jiao,Kaiwen Zha,Maohao Shen,Vishy Tirumalashetty,George Lee,Jiawei Han,Tomas Pfister,Chen-Yu Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillOS提出了一种基于经验驱动的强化学习训练方法，用于学习自进化智能体中的技能管理。核心思想是将智能体执行器（冻结）与可训练的技能管理器分离，通过组合奖励和基于技能依赖的任务流分组进行训练，使技能库随时间演化为结构化的Markdown文件，包含更高层次的元技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体多采用一次性问题解决模式，缺乏从历史交互中学习的能力。虽然已有技能复用研究，但现有方法存在局限：依赖人工技能管理、采用启发式操作规则、仅支持短时域技能操作。核心瓶颈在于难以从间接且延迟的反馈中学习复杂的长程管理策略。

Method: 1) 架构设计：将智能体分为冻结的执行器和可训练的技能管理器；2) 技能管理器维护外部SkillRepo，接收执行器的反馈；3) 训练策略：设计组合奖励函数，基于技能相关任务依赖对任务流进行分组，早期轨迹更新SkillRepo，后期相关任务评估更新效果；4) 时序差分更新机制，实现延迟反馈的学习。

Result: 在多轮智能体任务和单轮推理任务上，SkillOS在有效性和效率上均优于无记忆基线和强记忆基线。学习的技能管理器可泛化到不同执行器骨干和任务领域。

Conclusion: 研究表明，分离式设计（冻结执行器+可训练管理器）能有效学习复杂技能管理策略；技能库随时间演化为结构化Markdown文件，编码更高层次的元技能；基于任务依赖的分组训练方法为间接延迟反馈的学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly deployed to handle streaming tasks, yet they often remain one-off problem solvers that fail to learn from past interactions. Reusable skills distilled from experience provide a natural substrate for self-evolution, where high-quality skill curation serves as the key bottleneck. Existing approaches either rely on manual skill curation, prescribe heuristic skill operations, or train for short-horizon skill operations. However, they still struggle to learn complex long-term curation policies from indirect and delayed feedback. To tackle this challenge, we propose SkillOS, an experience-driven RL training recipe for learning skill curation in self-evolving agents. SkillOS pairs a frozen agent executor that retrieves and applies skills with a trainable skill curator that updates an external SkillRepo from accumulated experience. To provide learning signals for curation, we design composite rewards and train on grouped task streams based on skill-relevant task dependencies, where earlier trajectories update the SkillRepo, and later related tasks evaluate these updates. Across multi-turn agentic tasks and single-turn reasoning tasks, SkillOS consistently outperforms memory-free and strong memory-based baselines in both effectiveness and efficiency, with the learned skill curator generalizing across different executor backbones and task domains. Further analyses show that the learned curator produces more targeted skill use, while the skills in SkillRepo evolve into more richly structured Markdown files that encode higher-level meta-skills over time.

</details>


### [353] [Beyond Fixed Benchmarks and Worst-Case Attacks: Dynamic Boundary Evaluation for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06213)
*Haoxiang Wang,Da Yu,Huishuai Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出动态边界评估(DBE)方法，通过识别每个LLM在通过概率约0.5处的边界项目来解决固定基准测试的局限性。该方法包含校准题库、SGBS搜索算法和自适应评估协议三大组件，在安全、能力、真实性三个维度上实现了更广泛的模型覆盖和有效的能力比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM评估依赖固定基准测试，存在天花板效应和地板效应，无法准确捕捉不同模型间的能力差异。核心问题在于：现有评估方法对所有模型使用相同的测试项目集合，导致能力较强的模型快速达到饱和，而能力较弱的模型则立即触碰底线，从而掩盖了真实的能力差距。

Method: DBE使用"动态边界评估"方法，通过随机采样解码下逐提示通过概率约为0.5的边界点来定位每个模型的能力边界。具体包含三个核心组件：(i) 校准的题库，覆盖安全、能力、真实性三个维度；(ii) SGBS搜索算法，在仅API级别查询访问的条件下定位目标LLM的边界项目；(iii) 统一能力量表的评估协议，当目标模型超出题库覆盖范围时可自适应扩展评估集。

Result: DBE在四个类别（有害请求拒绝、过度拒绝、约束指令遵循、多轮谄媚抵抗）上验证了方法有效性。结果表明该评估覆盖了更广泛的模型范围而不出现饱和，同时保持了与现有数据集的兼容性。

Conclusion: DBE provides a scalable, adaptive evaluation framework that addresses the limitations of static benchmarks, enabling meaningful comparisons across a broader spectrum of LLMs while remaining compatible with existing datasets.

Abstract: Evaluating large language models (LLMs) today rests on fixed benchmarks that apply the same set of items to any model, producing ceiling and floor effects that mask capability gaps. We argue that the most informative evaluation signal lies at the boundary, where the per-prompt pass probability is near $0.5$ under random-sampling decoding, and propose Dynamic Boundary Evaluation (DBE), which actively locates each model's boundary and places it on a globally comparable difficulty scale. DBE delivers three artifacts: (i) a calibrated item bank covering safety, capability, and truthfulness, with per-item difficulty labels validated across $9$ reference LLMs; (ii) Skill-Guided Boundary Search (SGBS), a search algorithm that finds boundary items for a given target LLM using only API-level query access; and (iii) an evaluation protocol that places a new LLM on a unified ability scale and grows the evaluation set adaptively when the target falls outside the bank's coverage. We instantiate DBE on four categories spanning safety (harmful request refusal and over-refusal), capability (constrained instruction following), and truthfulness (multi-turn sycophancy resistance). The resulting evaluation covers a broader model spectrum without saturation while remaining compatible with existing datasets.

</details>


### [354] [MASPO: Joint Prompt Optimization for LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06623)
*Zhexuan Wang,Xuebo Liu,Li Wang,Zifei Shan,Yutong Wang,Zhenxi Song,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MASPO是一个用于自动迭代优化多智能体系统中提示的新框架，通过联合评估机制（评估提示对后续智能体成功的影响）和进化束搜索策略，在无需ground-truth标签的情况下弥合本地目标与全局目标的差距，实验证明其有效提升系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-based多智能体系统中，提示质量至关重要，但跨交互智能体联合优化提示面临挑战——本地优化目标与系统整体目标之间存在对齐问题，且缺乏有效的全局标注数据。

Method: MASPO采用联合评估机制评估提示，考察其对后续agent成功的影响；同时使用数据驱动的进化束搜索高效探索高维提示空间，通过迭代优化实现跨agent的提示协同调优。

Result: 在6个不同任务上的实验表明，MASPO持续优于现有最先进的提示优化方法，平均准确率提升2.9%。代码已开源。

Conclusion: MASPO通过联合评估机制弥合了本地交互与全局目标之间的差距，结合数据驱动的进化束搜索策略，在6个不同任务上实现了平均准确率提升2.9%，持续超越现有提示优化方法。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based Multi-agent systems (MAS) have shown promise in tackling complex collaborative tasks, where agents are typically orchestrated via role-specific prompts. While the quality of these prompts is pivotal, jointly optimizing them across interacting agents remains a non-trivial challenge, primarily due to the misalignment between local agent objectives and holistic system goals. To address this, we introduce MASPO, a novel framework designed to automatically and iteratively refine prompts across the entire system. A core innovation of MASPO is its joint evaluation mechanism, which assesses prompts not merely by their local validity, but by their capacity to facilitate downstream success for successor agents. This effectively bridges the gap between local interactions and global outcomes without relying on ground-truth labels. Furthermore, MASPO employs a data-driven evolutionary beam search to efficiently navigate the high-dimensional prompt space. Extensive empirical evaluations across 6 diverse tasks demonstrate that MASPO consistently outperforms state-of-the-art prompt optimization methods, achieving an average accuracy improvement of 2.9. We release our code at https://github.com/wangzx1219/MASPO.

</details>


### [355] [Proactive Instance Navigation with Comparative Judgment for Ambiguous User Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06223)
*Junhyuk Kwon,Seungjoon Lee,Hyejin Park,Kyle Min,Jungseul Ok*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ProCompNav框架，通过两阶段（候选池构建+比较判断）解决自然语言实例导航中的歧义问题。该方法通过二元问题一次剪枝所有不一致候选，将歧义解决从开放式目标描述重构为池级别区分性提问，在保持最小输入的同时提升成功率并降低响应长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两个缺陷：过早停止于首个合理候选而未充分探索替代方案；或虽收集多个候选但基于单个候选而非候选池进行属性提问。导致无法有效区分目标与干扰物，造成过早决策和冗长用户响应。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1)候选池构建；(2)通过比较判断识别目标。每轮提取分裂候选池的属性-值对，提出二元是/否问题，一次性剪枝所有不一致候选。

Result: 在CoIN-Bench上提升成功率；在TextNav上达到最优水平；大幅缩短响应长度。

Conclusion: ProCompNav在CoIN-Bench上以最小输入超过交互基线、以详细描述超过非交互基线，同时大幅降低响应长度；在TextNav上达到SOTA，表明比较判断方法对实例级导航具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Natural-language instance navigation becomes challenging when the initial user request does not uniquely specify the target instance. A practical agent should reduce the user's burden by actively asking only the information needed to distinguish the target from similar distractors, rather than requiring a detailed description upfront. Existing approaches often fall short of this goal: they may stop at the first plausible candidate before sufficiently exploring alternatives, or, even after collecting multiple candidates, ask about the target's attributes derived from individual candidates rather than questions selected to distinguish candidates in the pool. As a result, despite the dialogue, the agent may still fail to distinguish the target from distractors, leading to premature decisions and lengthy user responses. We propose Proactive Instance Navigation with Comparative Judgment (ProCompNav), a two-stage framework that first constructs a candidate pool and then identifies the target through comparative judgment. At each round, ProCompNav extracts an attribute-value pair that splits the current pool, asks a binary yes/no question, and prunes all inconsistent candidates at once. This reframes disambiguation from open-ended target description to pool-level discriminative questioning, where each question is chosen to narrow the candidate set. On CoIN-Bench, ProCompNav improves Success Rate over interactive baselines with the same minimal input and non-interactive baselines with detailed descriptions, while substantially reducing Response Length. ProCompNav also achieves state-of-the-art Success Rate on TextNav, suggesting that comparative judgment is broadly useful for instance-level navigation among similar distractors.

</details>


### [356] [Can RL Teach Long-Horizon Reasoning to LLMs? Expressiveness Is Key](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06638)
*Tianle Wang,Zhaoyang Wang,Guangchen Lan,Xinpeng Wei,Sipeng Zhang,Guanwen Qiu,Abulhair Saparov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ScaleLogic框架，通过合成逻辑推理环境研究强化学习在LLM推理中的规模法则，发现训练计算量与推理深度呈幂律关系（T∝D^γ），且逻辑表达力越强，γ指数越大（1.04→2.60），更表达性的训练设置能带来更好的下游迁移效果（+10.66分）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究RL训练如何随任务难度规模化的努力受限于缺乏可控、可规模化的评估环境。现有环境难以独立控制推理深度和逻辑复杂性两个维度。

Method: 提出ScaleLogic框架：1）独立控制推理深度（证明规划的范围）；2）控制底层逻辑的表达力（从简单蕴含→一阶逻辑+合取、析取、否定、全称量词）。使用该框架进行RL训练实验，测试多种RL方法、课程学习策略，并评估在数学和通用推理基准上的迁移效果。

Result: 1）训练计算量T与推理深度D呈幂律关系：T∝D^γ（R²>0.99）；2）γ随逻辑表达力单调递增（1.04→2.60）；3）更表达性的训练设置带来更大下游性能提升（+10.66分）和更高效的计算迁移；4）幂律关系在多种RL方法中均成立；5）课程学习显著提升规模效率。

Conclusion: 模型的训练内容（what）而非仅训练量（how much）决定下游迁移效果。逻辑表达力是影响RL规模化效率的关键因素，课程学习是提升效率的有效策略。这些发现为设计更高效的RL推理训练方案提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has been applied to improve large language model (LLM) reasoning, yet the systematic study of how training scales with task difficulty has been hampered by the lack of controlled, scalable environments. We introduce ScaleLogic, a synthetic logical reasoning framework that offers independent control over two axes of difficulty: the depth of the required proof planning (i.e., the horizon) and the expressiveness of the underlying logic. Our proposed framework supports a wide range of logics: from simple implication-only logic ("if-then") towards more expressive first-order reasoning with conjunction ("and"), disjunction ("or"), negation ("not"), and universal quantification ("for all"). Using this framework, we show that the RL training compute $T$ follows a power law with respect to reasoning depth $D$ ($T \propto D^γ$, $R^{2} > 0.99$), and that the scaling exponent $γ$ increases monotonically with logical expressiveness, from $1.04$ to $2.60$. On downstream mathematics and general reasoning benchmarks, more expressive training settings yield both larger performance gains (up to $+10.66$ points) and more compute-efficient transfer compared to less expressive settings, demonstrating that what a model is trained on, not just how much it is trained, shapes downstream transfer. We further show that the power-law relationship holds across multiple RL methods, and curriculum-based training substantially improves scaling efficiency.

</details>


### [357] [A Versatile AI Agent for Rare Disease Diagnosis and Risk Gene Prioritization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06226)
*Tianyu Liu,Wangjie Zheng,Rui Yang,Benny Kai Guo Loo,Hui Zhang,Jeffries Lauran,Jianlei Gu,Botao Yu,Weihao Xuan,Kexin Huang,Nan Liu,James Zou,Yonghui Jiang,Hua Xu,Hongyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Hygieia系统，通过多模态AI整合表型、基因和临床数据，采用知识增强路由器框架专门优化罕见病诊断，在基准测试中实现最优性能，临床验证显示相比医生诊断提升12%-60%，具有减轻临床工作量和辅助决策的实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罕见病的精准诊断面临重大挑战，当前诊断流程往往导致评估时间过长且准确率较低。

Method: Hygieia采用多模态AI智能体架构，整合表型特征、基因图谱和临床记录等多种数据源。其核心为基于路由器的知识增强框架，通过针对不同疾病类别定制诊断策略来缓解幻觉问题，并优先考虑罕见病的风险相关基因组因素，同时提供置信度评分以辅助临床决策。

Result: Hygieia在多个诊断基准上达到最优性能。与耶鲁医学院和杜克-国大医学院的临床专家合作验证表明：（1）相比医生诊断性能提升12%-60%；（2）在处理真实病例的医疗记录中表现出良好效果。

Conclusion: Hygieia可增强诊断准确性和可解释性，并显著减轻临床医生的工作量，展现出作为临床决策支持系统的潜力。

Abstract: Accurate and timely diagnosis is essential for effective treatment, particularly in the context of rare diseases. However, current diagnostic workflows often lead to prolonged assessment times and low accuracy. To address these limitations, we introduce Hygieia, a multi-modal AI agent system designed to support precision disease diagnosis by integrating diverse data sources, including phenotypic features, genetic profiles, and clinical records. Hygieia features a router-based and knowledge-enhanced framework that mitigates hallucination and tailors diagnostic strategies to different disease categories. Notably, it prioritizes risk-related genomic factors for rare diseases and provides confidence scores to assist clinical decision-making. We conducted a comprehensive evaluation demonstrating that Hygieia achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple diagnostic benchmarks. In collaboration with clinical experts from Yale School of Medicine and Duke-NUS Medical School, we further validated its practical utility by showing (1) Hygieia's superior diagnostic performance compared to physicians with an improvement from 12%-60% and (2) its effectiveness in assisting clinicians with medical records for handling real-world cases. Our findings indicate that Hygieia not only enhances diagnostic accuracy and interpretability but also significantly reduces clinician workload, highlighting its potential as a valuable tool in clinical decision support systems.

</details>


### [358] [Price of Fairness in Short-Term and Long-Term Algorithmic Selections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06227)
*Shahin Jabbari,Chen Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究算法决策中的公平性问题，探讨短期公平约束与长期 disparity 之间的张力，引入短期和长期群体公平性概念，通过公平价格(PoF)量化公平性与效用之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注静态场景下的公平性，但静态公平约束可能加剧长期 disparity。本文旨在解决这一张力，研究决策者在重复选择个体时短期效用与长期群体分布之间的平衡问题。

Method: 建立数学模型分析顺序选择问题；定义短期和长期群体公平性指标；引入公平价格(PoF)量化公平-效用权衡；使用合成数据和真实数据进行实证验证。

Result: 短期：最优和公平策略可被刻画，但PoF可能很大，即使群体分布几乎相同；长期：简单的投资策略可消除 disparity 并获得较低的PoF。

Conclusion: 短期公平约束可能带来较大效用损失，但通过适当的长期投资策略可以实现公平与效用的双赢，为算法公平性设计提供理论指导。

Abstract: Algorithmic decision-making in high-stakes settings can have profound impacts on individuals and populations. While much prior work studies fairness in static settings, recent results show that enforcing static fairness constraints may exacerbate long-run disparities. Motivated by this tension, we study a stylized sequential selection problem in which a decision-maker repeatedly selects individuals, affecting both immediate utility and the population distribution over time. We introduce notions of group fairness for both the short and long term and theoretically analyze the trade-off between fairness and utility via the Price of Fairness (PoF). We characterize optimal and fair policies in the short term and show that the PoF can be large even when group distributions are nearly identical. In contrast, we show that long-term disparities can vanish under simple investment policies that achieve a low PoF. We also empirically validate these theoretical observations using both synthetic and real datasets.

</details>


### [359] [Data Language Models: A New Foundation Model Class for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06290)
*Eda Erol,Giuliano Pezzoli,Ozer Cem Kelahmet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了表格数据的缺失基础模型——数据语言模型（DLM），以原生方式理解表格数据，无需序列化或预处理。Schema-1模型通过在230万+数据集上的预训练，在预测性能、缺失值填补和跨领域分类等任务上取得了最先进成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前所有表格AI方法（包括梯度提升树和最新的表格基础模型）都需要预处理管道才能处理数据，没有任何模型能够原生理解表格数据这一重要模态。而语言模型原生理解文本、视觉模型原生理解图像、音频模型原生理解音频，表格数据作为许多关键AI决策的基础模态，却缺乏对应的原生理解模型。

Method: 提出Data Language Model (DLM)范式，采用原生表格理解架构。Schema-1模型拥有140M参数，在超过230万个合成和真实世界的表格数据集上进行训练，直接从原始单元格值学习表格结构。

Result: Schema-1在标准行级预测基准测试中优于梯度提升集成、AutoML堆栈和评估的其他表格基础模型；在缺失值重建任务上，在各种条件下平均表现优于所有经典统计方法和前沿大语言模型；能够仅从原始单元格值识别任何未见数据集的行业部门分类，可靠地跨任何领域执行，这是先前任何表格模型都无法完成的任务。

Conclusion: Schema-1是首个能够原生理解表格数据的基础模型，无需任何序列化或预处理，可直接处理原始单元格值。该模型在传统预测任务上超越了梯度提升集成、AutoML和现有表格基础模型，并在缺失值重建任务上取得了比经典统计方法和大语言模型更低的重建误差，证明了理解数据自身分布几何结构比语言模型的世界知识对填补任务更有价值。此外，其首次实现了从原始数据中可靠识别任何领域未见数据集的行业部门分类能力。

Abstract: Every major data modality now has a foundation model that understands it natively: text has language models, images have vision models, audio has audio models. Tabular data, the modality on which many consequential real-world AI decisions are made, does not. Every approach to tabular AI today, from gradient-boosted trees to the latest tabular foundation models, requires a preprocessing pipeline before any model can consume the data. None of them understand tabular data as a modality. We introduce the Data Language Model (DLM), the missing foundation model for tabular data. A DLM understands tables the way a language model understands sentences: natively, without serialization or preprocessing, directly from raw cell values. It is the tabular data layer on which AI models, agents, and vertical AI applications can be built, eliminating the preprocessing pipelines that currently stand between raw data and every AI system that consumes it. We present Schema-1, the first DLM: a 140M parameter model trained on more than 2.3M synthetic and real-world tabular datasets. Schema-1 outperforms gradient-boosted ensembles, AutoML stacks, and the tabular foundation models we evaluate on established row-level prediction benchmarks. On missing value reconstruction it achieves lower reconstruction error than all classical statistical methods and frontier large language models on mean performance across conditions, establishing that structural understanding of a dataset's own distributional geometry is more useful for imputation than world knowledge encoded in language. It identifies the industry sector of any unseen dataset from raw cell values alone, reliably across any domain, a task no prior tabular model can perform. It is the native tabular understanding layer that has been missing from the AI stack.

</details>


### [360] [Mind the Gap? A Distributional Comparison of Real and Synthetic Priors for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06343)
*Alex O. Davies,Telmo de Menezes e Silva Filho,Nirav Ajmeri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Tabular foundation models are pre-trained on one of three classes of corpus: curated datasets drawn from benchmark repositories, tables harvested at scale from the web, or synthetic tables sampled from a parametric generative prior. Despite the centrality of pre-training data to model performance, little is known about how these corpora relate to one another in distribution, and the impact this has on downstream performance. In this work we take three canonical, archetypal datasets used to train tabular foundation models; the T4 dataset represents web-scraped corpora, the TabFM dataset curated tables from Kaggle, and the TabICL dataset as the only well-used synthetic prior with publicly available parameters. We characterise each corpus using aggregate features over whole tables, columns and correlations, and compare them using discriminator AUCs and k-NN coverage metrics. We find that the TabICL synthetic prior occupies a narrow region of the space of real tables, that this mismatch cannot be closed by optimising prior hyper-parameters across more than 86 thousand configurations, and that curated and web-scraped corpora are broadly interchangeable on a distributional level in feature space. Surprisingly, the distributional gap between synthetic pre-training data and real tables has a clearly detectable effect on performance under neither feature-based proximity measures or TabICL's own internal representations, suggesting that coverage of the real-data distribution is not the primary driver of TabICL's generalisation.

</details>


### [361] [More Than Can Be Said: A Benchmark and Framework for Pre-Question Scientific Ideation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06345)
*Jie Yu,Song Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出InciteResearch框架，旨在将研究者隐性的研究摩擦显性化、可检查化。核心创新在于：五维画像提取隐性需求、苏格拉底式追问链分布式执行、假设违背与必要性验证机制。配合首个跨科学模式的隐性与显性研究辅助基准TF-Bench验证效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI研究智能体假设用户已具备清晰可操作的研究问题，无法处理人类研究中常见的状态——"隐性摩擦"（即问题形成前的不对齐感）。这种研究起点与当前系统的假设存在根本性矛盾。

Method: (1) 五维研究者画像提取：从模糊甚至领域无关的输入中锚定特定摩擦点，构建结构化状态表征；(2) 隐含假设违背机制：最大化可行性-新颖性乘积，强制执行7阶段因果推导追溯；(3) 方法必要性验证：检查所提方法是否为重构洞见的必要推论；(4) TF-Bench基准：区分领域相关与领域无关的灵感，覆盖四种科学模式。

Result: 在TF-Bench上显著超越基于提示的基线：新颖性从3.671提升至4.250，影响力从3.806提升至4.397。将生成的研究提案从"重组式创新"转变为"架构式洞察"。

Conclusion: AI研究智能体应从"自动化执行工具"升级为"思维延伸伙伴"，通过将隐性摩擦显性化、假设违背机制化、方法必要性验证系统化，推动研究提案从"重组式创新"转向"架构式洞察"。

核心贡献：(1) 提出多智能体协作框架InciteResearch，模拟苏格拉底式追问链；(2) 构建首个跨四种科学模式的隐性与显性研究辅助基准TF-Bench；(3) 验证了可行性-新颖性乘积最大化的有效性（新颖性↑15.7%，影响力↑15.5%）。

Abstract: AI research agents have shown strong potential in automating literature search and manuscript refinement, yet most assume a clear and actionable initial input, operating only after a research question has been made explicit. In contrast, human research often begins with tacit friction, a sense of misalignment before a question can be formed. We introduce InciteResearch, a multi-agent framework designed to make a researcher's implicit understanding explicit, inspectable, and actionable. InciteResearch decomposes the logical chain of Socratic questioning and distributes it across the entire pipeline that: (1) Elicits a structured five-dimensional researcher profile state anchored by specific friction points from vague, even domain-unrelated inputs; (2) Violates hidden assumptions by maximizing the feasibility-novelty product with enforcing a 7-stage causal derivation trace; and (3) check whether the proposed method is a Necessary consequence of the reframed insight. We further introduce TF-Bench, the first benchmark for tacit-to-explicit research assistance that distinguishes domain-related from domain-unrelated inspirations across four scientific modes. On TF-Bench, InciteResearch achieves leapfrogging gains over a prompt-based baseline (novelty/impact from 3.671/3.806 to 4.250/4.397), shifting generated proposals from recombination to architectural insight. Our work demonstrates that AI can serve as an extension of thinking itself, rather than merely automating downstream execution.

</details>


### [362] [Prediction and Empowerment: A Theory of Agency through Bridge Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06346)
*Richard Csaky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文在确定性物理世界中研究部分可观测条件下的智能体问题，将感知和 actuation 建模为 agent 与环境之间的"桥接接口"，证明预测、压缩和 empowerment 之间存在分离，并探讨这对现代 AI 智能体设计和人机对齐的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在解决部分可观测环境下智能体理论的基础问题：在看似随机的确定性世界中（随机性源于初始条件、固定定律和外部噪声的不确定性），如何形式化理解预测、状态识别和控制能力之间的关系，并为人机对齐提供理论框架。

Method: 构建了一个统一的形式化框架：1) 将感知和 actuation 建模为 split between agent 控制参数和环境控制通道状态的桥接接口；2) 通过潜在微观状态上的先验和多对一的观测粗粒化，诱导出确定性 POMDP；3) 在此框架下进行理论证明；4) 进一步将框架特化为保信息量的比特串模型。

Result: 主要理论结果包括：1) 完美预测可通过识别目标相关的隐藏商空间或通过覆写控制使未来 action-determined 实现；2) 高 empowerment 本身不足以保证预测；3) 在可精炼接口和足够记忆下，动作条件的观测压缩过程可减少关于潜在商的后验不确定度；4) 比特串特化中，预测通过识别需要内部容量至少等于相关潜在熵，覆写控制需要终末端动作容量。

Conclusion: 对于现代 AI 智能体，设计原则应区分：隐藏状态识别、接口精炼、任务相关可控性和纯覆写/干扰控制。人机对齐部分是一个接口设计问题，涉及人类意图、智能体内部状态、外部工具和世界通道状态之间的桥接。论文以工作草稿形式发表，欢迎反馈。

Abstract: We study agency under partial observability in deterministic physical or simulated worlds, where apparent randomness arises from uncertainty over initial conditions, fixed law bits, and unrolled exogenous noise. We model sensing and actuation as bridge interfaces split between agent-controlled parameters and environment-controlled channel state, inducing a deterministic POMDP through a prior over latent microstates and many-to-one observation coarsening. Within this framework, we prove a separation between prediction, compression, and empowerment. Perfect prediction can be achieved either by identifying the hidden quotient relevant to the target family or by overwrite control that makes the future target action-determined; high empowerment alone is insufficient. Under refinable interfaces and sufficient memory, action-conditioned observation-compression progress reduces posterior uncertainty about the latent quotient, and when refinement requires steering world-side channel conditions, this creates target-conditioned interface empowerment. A bit-string specialization with a conserved information budget makes the resulting tradeoff explicit: prediction by identification requires internal capacity at least the relevant latent entropy, whereas overwrite control requires terminal action capacity over the controlled quotient. For modern AI agents, the results suggest a design principle rather than a theorem of inevitability: objectives should distinguish hidden-state identification, interface refinement, task-relevant controllability, and mere overwrite or distractor control. Human--AI alignment is partly an interface-design problem, where the relevant bridge is between human intent, agent internal state, external tools, and world-side channel conditions. This is a working draft: feedback and criticism is most welcome.

</details>


### [363] [Debiased Multimodal Personality Understanding through Dual Causal Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06371)
*Yangfu Zhu,Zitong Han,Nianwen Ning,Yuting Wei,Yuandong Wang,Hang Feng,Zhenzhou Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Multimodalpersonalityunderstandingplaysacriticalroleinhuman centered artificial intelligence. Previous work mainly focus on learn-ing rich multimodal representations for video personality under standing. However, they often suffer from potential harm caused by subject bias (e.g., observable age and unobservable mental states), as subjects originate from diverse demographic backgrounds. Learn ing such spurious associations between multimodal features and traits may lead to unfair personality understanding. In this work, weconstruct aStructural Causal Model (SCM)toanalyze theimpact of these biases from a causal perspective, and propose a novel Dual Causal Adjustment Network (DCAN) to mitigate the interference of subject attributes on personality understanding. Specifically, we design a Back-door Adjustment Causal Learning (BACL) module to block spurious correlations from observable demographic factors via a prototype-based confounder dictionary, and subsequently ap ply a Front-door Adjustment Causal Learning (FACL) module to ad dress latent and unobservable biases throughalearnedmediatordic tionary intervention, thereby achieving causal disentanglement of representations for deconfounded reasoning. Importantly, we con struct a Demographic-annotated Multimodal Student Personality (DMSP) dataset to support the analysis and discussion of fairness related factors. Extensive experiments on the benchmark dataset CFI-V2 and our DMSPdataset demonstrate that DCAN consistently improves prediction accuracy, reaching 92.11% and 92.90%, respec tively. Meanwhile, the improvementsinthefairnessmetricsofequal opportunity and demographic parity are 6.57% and 7.97% on CFI-V2, and 15.38% and 20.06% on the DMSP dataset. Our code and DMSP dataset are available at https://github.com/Sabrina-han/DCAN

</details>


### [364] [Rethinking Vacuity for OOD Detection in Evidential Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06382)
*Claire McNamara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了Evidential Deep Learning中基于空隙(Uncertainty Mass)的OOD检测指标对类别数K的敏感性：当ID和OOD的K值不同时，即使模型预测完全不变，AUROC/AUPR也可能被人为夸大（最高达0.68），这是一个重要的评估伪影，在因果语言模型的MCQA任务中同样存在此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空隙(Uncertainty Mass)作为EDL中OOD检测的指标，其计算依赖于类别数K，但当ID和OOD的K值不同时，该指标可能产生人为夸大的检测性能，需要揭示这一评估 artifacts。

Method: 实验对比分析不同K值下AUROC/AUPR指标的变化；区分标准EDL和IB-EDL两种方法。

Result: 当K值差异仅为1时，AUROC最多相差0.318-0.360，AUPR最多相差0.613-0.683，证明基于空隙的指标对K值变化极度敏感，存在评估假象。

Conclusion: 建议在EDL中采用非基于空隙的指标或确保ID和OOD的K值相等；需要更清晰定义MCQA场景下的ID和OOD。

Abstract: Vacuity, or Uncertainty Mass (UM), is commonly used as a metric to evaluate Out-of-Distribution (OOD) detection in Evidential Deep Learning (EDL). It generally involves dividing the number of classes ($K$) by the total strength of belief ($S$) of the model's predictions, where $S$ is derived from summing the Dirichlet parameters. As such, UM is sensitive to the cardinality of $K$. In particular, it is unlikely in practice that there is a linear relationship between $K$ and $S$ as $K$ and $S$ increase due to the nature of EDL (suppressing incorrectly assigned evidence). As a result, when comparing In Distribution (ID) and OOD results, it is important that $K_{\mathrm{ID}}$ and $K_{\mathrm{OOD}}$ are equal; something that is not always ensured in practice. We provide an empirical demonstration of how results for AUROC and AUPR can substantially differ when class cardinality between ID and OOD differs by 1, with AUROC differing by as much as 0.318 and AUPR by 0.613 for standard EDL, and AUROC by 0.360 and AUPR by 0.683 for IB-EDL. More concretely, our findings isolate an evaluation artefact: when K differs between ID and OOD, AUROC/AUPR can be artificially inflated without any change in model predictions. We further discuss the evaluation of EDL over causal language models using Multiple-Choice Question-Answer (MCQA) datasets and argue for clearer definitions of ID and OOD in this context. Our primary contribution is an empirical and theoretical demonstration that vacuity-based OOD detection in EDL-fine-tuned LLMs is highly sensitive to uncontrolled differences in evaluated class cardinality.

</details>


### [365] [Knowledge Graphs, the Missing Link in Agentic AI-based Formal Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06434)
*Vaisakh Naduvodi Viswambharan,Keerthan Kopparam Radhakrishna,Deepak Narayan Gadde,Aman Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have enabled workflows that generate SystemVerilog Assertions (SVAs) from natural-language specifications, with the potential to accelerate Formal Verification (FV). However, high-quality assertion synthesis remains challenging because specifications are often ambiguous or incomplete and critical micro-architectural details reside in the Register Transfer Level (RTL). Many existing approaches treat the specification and RTL as loosely structured text, which weakens specification-to-RTL grounding and leads to semantic mismatches and frequent syntax failures during formal parsing and elaboration. This work addresses these limitations with a verification-centric Knowledge Graph (KG) constructed from structured Intermediate Representations (IRs) extracted from the specification, RTL, and formal-tool feedback, including syntax diagnostics, Counterexamples (CEXs), and coverage reports. The KG links requirements, design hierarchy, signals, assumptions, and properties to provide traceable, design-grounded context for generation. A multi-agent workflow queries and updates this KG to generate SVAs and to drive three refinement loops: syntax repair guided by tool diagnostics, CEX-guided correction using trace links, and coverage-directed property augmentation. Evaluation across seven benchmark designs indicates that KG-based context retrieval improves specification-to-RTL grounding and consistently produces compilable SVAs with low syntax-repair overhead. The approach achieves formal coverage ranging from 78.5% to 99.4%, though convergence exhibits design dependence with complex temporal and arithmetic reasoning remaining challenging for current LLM capabilities.

</details>


### [366] [SCRuB: Social Concept Reasoning under Rubric-Based Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06444)
*Jamelle Watson-Daniels,Himaghna Bhattacharjee,Skyler Wang,Brandon Handoko,Antonio Li,Anaelia Ovalle,Mahesh Pasupuleti,Candace Ross,Vidya Sarma,Arjun Subramonian,Karen Ullrich,Will van der Vaart,Yijing Xin,Maximilian Nickel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了SCRuB框架用于评估LLM的社会概念推理能力，通过4711个评估提示和博士级专家标注数据，发现前沿模型在五维度批判性思维评估中全面超越人类专家，并首次证明社会概念推理的单回合考试式评估已达到饱和上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理能力研究多聚焦数学或技术任务，而较少关注社会概念推理能力——即对塑造社会规范、文化和制度的抽象概念的理解与推理。这一能力对于现代模型作为社会智能体运行至关重要，但目前缺乏系统性的评估方法。

Method: 研究团队开发了SCRuB框架，包含三个阶段：①从权威资源构建提示；②由专家和模型生成响应；③通过五维度批判性思维评分 rubric 进行比较评估。团队引入Panel of Disciplinary Perspectives集成方法，并经独立专家评审验证其泛化能力，最终形成SCRuBEval（4711个评估提示）、SCRuBAnnotations（300个专家撰写响应和150个专家比较判断，共45名博士级学者参与）。

Result: 前沿模型在全部五个rubric维度上持续优于人类专家。在1170次成对比较中，专家评审在80.8%的判断中将模型响应排在首位，74.4%的情况下整体偏好模型响应。

Conclusion: 该研究首次通过专家基准证明了社会概念推理的评估饱和现象，表明在单回合考试式评估中，模型与人类专家均已达到能力上限，为后续关于社会概念推理的研究提供了评估框架和数据资源。

Abstract: While many studies of Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning capabilities emphasize mathematical or technical tasks, few address reasoning about social concepts: the abstract ideas shaping social norms, culture, and institutions. This understudied capability is essential for modern models acting as social agents, yet no systematic evaluation methodology targets it. We introduce SCRuB (Social Concept Reasoning under Rubric-Based Evaluation), a framework designed for this setting of task indeterminacy. Our goal is to measure the degree to which a model reasons about social concepts with the depth and critical rigor of a human expert. SCRuB proceeds in three phases: prompt construction from established sources, response generation by experts and models, and comparative evaluation using a five-dimensional critical thinking rubric. To enable generalization of the pipeline, we introduce a Panel of Disciplinary Perspectives ensemble validated against independent expert judges. We release SCRuBEval (n=4,711 evaluation prompts) and SCRuBAnnotations (300 expert-authored responses and 150 expert comparative judgments from 45 PhD-level scholars). Our results show that frontier models consistently outperform human experts across all five rubric dimensions. Across 1,170 pairwise comparisons, expert judges ranked a model response first in 80.8% of judgments and preferred model responses overall 74.4% of the time. Ultimately, this study provides the first expert-grounded demonstration of evaluation saturation for social concept reasoning: the single-turn exam-style format has reached its ceiling for models and humans alike.

</details>


### [367] [PrefixGuard: From LLM-Agent Traces to Online Failure-Warning Monitors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06455)
*Xinmiao Huang,Jinwei Hu,Rajarshi Roy,Changshun Wu,Yi Dong,Xiaowei Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PrefixGuard是一个轨迹到监控器的框架，通过离线StepView归纳和监督式监控训练，在异构轨迹上实现轻量级前缀风险监控。在四个基准上AUPRC达0.533-0.900，比原始文本对照平均提升+0.137，优于LLM评判器。提供可观测性上限和DFA提取诊断，证明强排序能力不等于部署实用性，为前缀预警的实际干预转化提供诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体现在执行长时间、工具使用类任务，但最终结果检查来得太晚无法干预。需要在线预警机制，但面临挑战：(1) 手编事件模式脆弱且难以适应异构轨迹；(2) 部署时使用LLM评判成本高昂；(3) 异构轨迹上需要轻量级前缀监控。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：(1) 离线StepView归纳步骤，从原始轨迹样本中诱导确定性类型化步骤适配器；(2) 监督式监控训练，从终端结果中学习事件抽象和前缀风险评分器。监控器通过观察前缀学习事件抽象，通过终端反馈学习风险评分，实现轻量级在线警告而无需部署时LLM判断。

Result: 四个基准测试中，最强PrefixGuard监控器分别达到0.900/0.710/0.533/0.557 AUPRC。使用各表征最强后端时，平均比原始文本对照提升+0.137 AUPRC。LLM评判器在同一前缀预警协议下表现显著较弱。推导出基于分数的AUPRC可观测性上限，区分监控错误与缺乏证据的失败。有限状态审计中，DFA提取在WebArena（29状态）和τ²-Bench（20状态）紧凑，但在SkillsBench（151状态）和TerminalBench（187状态）膨胀。

Conclusion: PrefixGuard定位为实用的监控合成方案，提供明确诊断能力以判断前缀预警是否可转化为可操作干预。DFA提取在简单任务上紧凑（WebArena 29状态、τ²-Bench 20状态），但在复杂任务上膨胀（SkillsBench 151状态、TerminalBench 187状态）。关键发现：强排序能力不等于部署实用性——WebArena虽然排名表现好但无法支持低误报预警，而τ²-Bench和TerminalBench保留更多可操作的早期预警。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents now execute long, tool-using tasks where final outcome checks can arrive too late for intervention. Online warning requires lightweight prefix monitors over heterogeneous traces, but hand-authored event schemas are brittle and deployment-time LLM judging is costly. We introduce PrefixGuard, a trace-to-monitor framework with an offline StepView induction step followed by supervised monitor training. StepView induces deterministic typed-step adapters from raw trace samples, and the monitor learns an event abstraction and prefix-risk scorer from terminal outcomes. Across WebArena, $τ^2$-Bench, SkillsBench, and TerminalBench, the strongest PrefixGuard monitors reach 0.900/0.710/0.533/0.557 AUPRC. Using the strongest backend within each representation, they improve over raw-text controls by an average of +0.137 AUPRC. LLM judges remain substantially weaker under the same prefix-warning protocol. We also derive an observability ceiling on score-based area under the precision-recall curve (AUPRC) that separates monitor error from failures lacking evidence in the observed prefix. For finite-state audit, post-hoc deterministic finite automaton (DFA) extraction remains compact on WebArena and $τ^2$-Bench (29 and 20 states) but expands to 151 and 187 states on SkillsBench and TerminalBench. Finally, first-alert diagnostics show that strong ranking does not imply deployment utility: WebArena ranks well yet fails to support low-false-alarm alerts, whereas $τ^2$-Bench and TerminalBench retain more actionable early alerts. Together, these results position PrefixGuard as a practical monitor-synthesis recipe with explicit diagnostics for when prefix warnings translate into actionable interventions.

</details>


### [368] [Beyond Task Success: Measuring Workflow Fidelity in LLM-Based Agentic Payment Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06457)
*Donghao Huang,Joon Kiat Chua,Zhaoxia Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出ASR（Agentic Success Rate）指标，用于评估LLM多智能体系统的轨迹级执行保真度。该指标将性能分解为Transition Recall和Transition Precision，可发现TSR和HF1无法检测到的工作流偏差（如确认检查点的跳过）。在18个LLM和90,000个任务实例上的实验表明，该诊断驱动的优化可带来高达+93.8百分点的TSR提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指标（TSR和HF1）仅关注最终结果或无序路由决策，无法捕捉智能体执行序列中的关键偏差。在受监管的支付领域，这种缺陷可能导致合规问题，但现有评估体系无法有效识别。

Method: 提出ASR指标，对比观察到的与预期的智能体执行序列，在转换级别进行匹配。该指标进一步分解为Transition Recall（召回率）和Transition Precision（精确率）。实验覆盖18个LLM和90,000个支付任务实例。

Result: ASR揭示了10/18模型系统性跳过支付结账时的确认检查点（TSR和HF1均无法检测），8个模型完美执行检查点。GPT-4.1存在隐藏工作流捷径但TSR/HF1完美，GPT-5.2实现完美ASR。基于ASR诊断的提示优化和确定性路由守卫可显著提升性能。

Conclusion: 轨迹级评估在受监管领域至关重要。ASR能够发现传统指标遗漏的关键执行偏差，诊断驱动的优化策略可带来实质性性能提升（高达+93.8个百分点TSR）。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems are increasingly deployed for payment workflows, yet prevailing metrics, Task Success Rate (TSR) and Agent Handoff F1-Score (HF1), capture only final outcomes or unordered routing decisions. We introduce the Agentic Success Rate (ASR), a trajectory-fidelity metric that compares observed and expected agent execution sequences at the transition level, decomposing performance into Transition Recall and Transition Precision. Applied to the Hierarchical Multi-Agent System for Payments (HMASP) across 18 LLMs and 90,000 task instances, ASR reveals that 10 of 18 models systematically skip a confirmation checkpoint during payment checkout, a deviation invisible to both TSR and HF1, while 8 models enforce the checkpoint perfectly. Notably, GPT-4.1 exhibits hidden workflow shortcuts despite achieving perfect TSR and HF1, while GPT-5.2 achieves perfect ASR. Prompt refinements and deterministic routing guards guided by ASR diagnostics yield substantial TSR improvements, with gains up to +93.8 percentage points for previously struggling models, demonstrating that trajectory-level evaluation is essential in regulated domains.

</details>


### [369] [ReasonSTL: Bridging Natural Language and Signal Temporal Logic via Tool-Augmented Process-Rewarded Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06483)
*Bowen Ye,Zhijian Li,Junyue Huang,Junkai Ma,Xiang Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReasonSTL是一个工具增强框架，使用本地开源语言模型将自然语言翻译为信号时序逻辑(STL)，通过显式推理、确定性工具调用和结构化公式构建解决隐私和成本问题，并在4B参数模型上实现最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STL是用于指定时空需求的形式化语言，但用户通常使用自然语言表达需求。现有方案存在问题：手动规范需要时序逻辑专业知识且无法扩展；商业LLM API存在高Token成本和隐私泄露风险，不适合工业部署。

Method: ReasonSTL采用工具增强框架，将翻译过程分解为三个阶段：显式推理、确定性工具调用、结构化公式构建。引入过程奖励训练(process-rewarded training)监督工具使用轨迹和最终公式质量。同时提出STL-Bench基准测试，支持双语、具备计算感知能力，并基于真实世界信号。

Result: 4B参数模型经过ReasonSTL训练后，在自动指标和人类评估中均达到最优性能。

Conclusion: ReasonSTL为形式化规范编写提供了透明、低成本、隐私保护的替代方案，可有效替代商业LLM API进行自然语言到STL的翻译。

Abstract: Signal Temporal Logic (STL) is an expressive formal language for specifying spatio-temporal requirements over real-valued, real-time signals. It has been widely used for the verification and synthesis of autonomous systems and cyber-physical systems. In practice, however, users often express their requirements in natural language rather than in structured STL formulas, making natural-language-to-STL translation a critical yet challenging task. Manual specification requires temporal-logic expertise and cannot scale, while prompting commercial LLM APIs incurs substantial token costs and may expose sensitive system requirements to third-party services, raising privacy concerns for industrial deployment. To address these challenges, we present \textsc{ReasonSTL}, a tool-augmented framework that adapts local open-source language models for natural-language-to-STL generation. \textsc{ReasonSTL} decomposes the translation process into explicit reasoning, deterministic tool calls, and structured formula construction. We further introduce process-rewarded training to supervise both tool-use trajectories and final formulas, together with \textsc{STL-Bench}, a bilingual, computation-aware benchmark grounded in real-world signals. Experiments show that a 4B model trained with \textsc{ReasonSTL} achieves state-of-the-art performance in both automatic metrics and human evaluations, demonstrating that \textsc{ReasonSTL} provides a transparent, low-cost, and privacy-preserving alternative for formal specification drafting.

</details>


### [370] [From Token Lists to Graph Motifs: Weisfeiler-Lehman Analysis of Sparse Autoencoder Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06494)
*Ruben Fernandez-Boullon,Pablo Magariños-Docampo,Javier Perez-Robles*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Sparse autoencoders分析新方法：用token共现图表示每个SAE特征，通过WL-style图核计算相似性进行聚类，揭示了decoder权重和token频率方法无法捕捉的结构关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing SAE feature analysis relies exclusively on top-activating token lists or decoder weight vectors, leaving the higher-order co-occurrence structure shared across features largely unexamined.

Method: Model each SAE feature as a token co-occurrence graph (nodes=tokens near strong activations, edges=context co-occurrence), then use a custom WL-style frequency-binned graph kernel to compute similarity for clustering.

Result: Graph-based clustering recovers motif families (punctuation patterns, language/script clusters, code templates) not captured by decoder cosine similarity. While token-histogram baseline has higher purity, the graph view provides complementary structural insights. Results are stable across hyperparameters and random seeds.

Conclusion: Graph-structured representation provides complementary insights to existing SAE analysis methods, revealing structural relationships between features that token-frequency and decoder-weight views alone cannot capture. This graph-based approach surfaces higher-order co-occurrence patterns useful for mechanistic interpretability research.

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have become central to mechanistic interpretability, decomposing transformer activations into monosemantic features. Yet existing analyses characterise features almost exclusively through top-activating token lists or decoder weight vectors, leaving the higher-order co-occurrence structure shared across features largely unexamined. We introduce a graph-structured representation in which each SAE feature is modelled as a token co-occurrence graph: nodes are the tokens most frequent near strong activations, and edges connect pairs that co-occur within local context windows. A custom WL-style, frequency-binned graph kernel then provides a similarity measure over this structural space. Applied as a proof of concept to features from a large SAE trained on GPT-2 Small and probed with a synthetic mixed-domain corpus, our clustering recovers heuristic motif families (punctuation-heavy patterns, language and script clusters, and code-like templates) that are not recovered by clustering on decoder cosine similarity. A token-histogram baseline achieves higher overall purity, so the contribution of the graph view is complementary rather than dominant: it surfaces structural relationships that token-frequency and decoder-weight views alone do not capture. Cluster assignments are stable across graph-construction hyperparameters and random seeds.

</details>


### [371] [SpatialEpiBench: Benchmarking Spatial Information and Epidemic Priors in Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06530)
*Ruiqi Lyu,Alistair Turcan,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SpatialEpiBench是一个用于时空流行病预测的真实感基准测试，发现当前先进预测模型（包括使用流行病学先验的模型）普遍无法超越简单基准，揭示了模型在疫情预警、数据处理和空间信息利用三方面的核心失效原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏标准化的空间流行病预测基准，且现有评估方法使用简单的时序划分，无法真实反映实时预测场景，导致无法准确评估和比较不同预测方法在实际公共卫生决策中的效用。

Method: 研究者构建了SpatialEpiBench基准测试，包含11个流行病数据集，采用标准化滚动评估和疫情特定指标。他们评估了多种邻接信息感知预测模型与通用流行病学先验的结合效果。

Result: 大多数预测方法（包括结合流行病学先验的模型）在1天到1个月的预测范围内表现均不如简单的"最后值"基准模型。研究发现了三个主要失效模式：疫情预警能力不足、难以处理稀疏和噪声数据、常见地理邻接信息的流行病学效用有限。

Conclusion: SpatialEpiBench揭示了当前流行病预测方法存在的严重不足，即使结合流行病学先验知识，大多数模型仍无法超越简单的基准模型。这强调了开发真正具有实际应用价值的流行病预测模型的迫切需求，同时表明空间邻接信息在流行病学建模中的实用性可能有限。

Abstract: Accurate epidemic forecasting is crucial for public health response, resource allocation, and outbreak intervention, but remains difficult with sparse, noisy, and highly non-stationary data. Because epidemics unfold across interacting regions, spatiotemporal methods are natural candidates for improving forecasts. Despite growing interest in spatial information, no standardized benchmark exists, and current evaluations often use simple chronological train-test splits that do not reflect real-time forecasting practice. We address this gap with SpatialEpiBench, a challenging benchmark for spatiotemporal epidemic forecasting in realistic public-health settings. SpatialEpiBench includes 11 epidemic datasets with standardized rolling evaluations and outbreak-specific metrics. We evaluate adjacency-informed forecasting models with widely used epidemic priors that adapt general models to epidemiology, but find that most methods underperform a simple last-value baseline from 1 day to 1 month ahead, even during outbreaks and with these priors. We identify three major failure modes: (1) poor outbreak anticipation, (2) difficulty handling sparsity and noise, and (3) limited utility of common geographic adjacency for epidemiological spatial information. We release benchmark data, code, and instructions at https://github.com/Rachel-Lyu/SpatialEpiBench to support development of operationally useful epidemic forecasting models.

</details>


### [372] [Ex Ante Evaluation of AI-Induced Idea Diversity Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06540)
*Nafis Saami Azad,Raiyan Abdul Baten*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个无需人机交互数据即可评估AI对人类创意多样性影响的框架，通过将想法建模为可拥挤资源，定义过度拥挤系数Δ和多样性比率ρ作为核心指标。实验表明当前前沿LLM普遍存在过度拥挤问题，但通过优化生成协议可有效缓解，使多样性评估成为开发阶段可操作的评估维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有创意AI评估仅关注个体效用，忽视创意产出的群体消费特性——当大量主体产生相似想法时其价值会衰减。这导致评估盲点：AI可能提升个体输出质量，同时加剧人口层面的创意拥挤。

Method: 将创意构想建模为可拥挤资源(congessible resources)，通过分布内比较识别源级拥挤，提出过度拥挤系数Δ和人类相对多样性比率ρ作为评估指标，并将Δ与具有暴露依赖冗余成本的采用博弈相联系，实现无需人机交互数据的AI诱导多样性崩溃基准测试。

Result: 三款前沿LLM在短故事、营销口号和替代用途任务中均低于多样性平价阈值；样本量估计显示在可行的人工基准规模下可实现稳定估算；关键发现是拥挤效应可通过生成协议设计得到缓解。

Conclusion: 三款前沿LLM在短故事、营销口号和替代用途任务中均未达到多样性平价，但通过生成协议设计可减少拥挤效应，表明多样性崩溃可成为人口感知型创意AI的可操作开发时评估目标。

Abstract: Creative AI systems are typically evaluated at the level of individual utility, yet creative outputs are consumed in populations: an idea loses value when many others produce similar ones. This creates an evaluation blind spot, as AI can improve individual outputs while increasing population-level crowding. We introduce a human-relative framework for benchmarking AI-induced human diversity collapse without requiring human-AI interaction data, providing an ex ante protocol to estimate crowding risk from model-only generations and matched unaided human baselines. By modeling ideas as congestible resources, we show that source-level crowding is identifiable from within-distribution comparisons, yielding an excess-crowding coefficient $Δ$ and a human-relative diversity ratio $ρ$. We show that $ρ\ge1$ is the no-excess-crowding parity condition and connect $Δ$ to an adoption game with exposure-dependent redundancy costs. Across short stories, marketing slogans, and alternative-uses tasks, three frontier LLMs fall below parity across crowding kernels. Estimates stabilize with feasible model-only sample sizes. Importantly, generation-protocol variants show that crowding can be reduced through targeted design, making diversity collapse an actionable, development-time evaluation target for population-aware creative AI.

</details>


### [373] [Improved techniques for fine-tuning flow models via adjoint matching: a deterministic control pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06583)
*Zhengyi Guo,Jiayuan Sheng,David D. Yao,Wenpin Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于流式生成模型人类偏好对齐的确定性伴随匹配框架，将对齐问题形式化为速度场上的最优控制问题。通过截断伴随方案关注轨迹的终端部分以节省计算，并支持超越标准KL正则化的灵活权衡。在SiT-XL/2和FLUX.2-Klein-4B上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式生成模型（如 Diffusion Transformer、Flow Matching）的人类偏好对齐面临挑战，传统的 RLHF 方法计算开销大且训练不稳定，需要新的理论框架来指导对齐过程。

Method: 1) 将偏好对齐建模为速度场上的最优控制问题；2) 直接回归当前策略下由价值梯度引导的目标；3) 提出截断伴随（Truncated Adjoint）方案，仅在轨迹终端部分计算梯度以降低计算成本；4) 推广至超越 KL 正则化的灵活对齐强度控制。

Result: 在 SiT-XL/2 和 FLUX.2-Klein-4B 上实现了一致的对齐指标提升，同时显著改善了生成样本的多样性和模式覆盖。

Conclusion: 该框架为流式生成模型的偏好对齐提供了稳定、高效且可调节的理论基础，截断伴随方案在保持对齐质量的同时大幅降低计算开销。

Abstract: We propose a deterministic adjoint matching framework that formulates human preference alignment for flow-based generative models as an optimal control problem over velocity fields. One can directly regress the control toward a value-gradient-induced target under the current policy, leading to a simple and stable training objective. Building on this perspective, we introduce a truncated adjoint scheme that focuses computation on the terminal portion of the trajectory, where reward-relevant signals concentrate, which yields substantial computational savings while preserving alignment quality. We further generalize the framework beyond standard KL-based regularization, allowing more flexible trade-offs between alignment strength and distributional preservation. Experiments on SiT-XL/2 and FLUX.2-Klein-4B demonstrate consistent gains across multiple alignment metrics, along with substantially improved diversity and mode preservation.

</details>


### [374] [NeuroAgent: LLM Agents for Multimodal Neuroimaging Analysis and Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06584)
*Lujia Zhong,Yihao Xia,Jianwei Zhang,Shuo huang,Jiaxin Yue,Mingyang Xia,Yonggang Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NeuroAgent是一个LLM驱动的智能体框架，通过层级式多智能体架构和生成-执行-验证引擎，自动化异构神经影像数据(sMRI、fMRI、dMRI、PET)的预处理和分析流程，支持自然语言交互，在ADNI数据集上验证了系统有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经影像分析涉及复杂的模态特定预处理工作流，需要仔细配置、质量控制和异构工具链间的协调；下游统计分析和疾病分类通常需要任务特定代码、评估协议和数据格式约定，在原始采集与可重复科学分析之间形成额外障碍。

Method: 采用层级式多智能体架构，结合反馈驱动的生成-执行-验证引擎；智能体自主生成可执行预处理代码、检测并恢复运行时错误、验证输出完整性；支持自然语言查询进行交互式下游分析。在ADNI数据集上进行pipeline消融实验，测试多种LLM后端。

Result: 意图解析准确率最高达100%；最强后端(Qwen3.5-27B)端到端预处理步骤正确率达84.8%；四模态AD分类AUC达0.9518，优于所有单模态基线；自动化恢复机制将人工干预限制在需要人工审查的边缘情况。

Conclusion: NeuroAgent能够显著减少神经影像预处理所需的人工工作，并实现端到端的自动化神经影像分析流程。

Abstract: Multimodal neuroimaging analysis often involves complex, modality-specific preprocessing workflows that require careful configuration, quality control, and coordination across heterogeneous toolchains. Beyond preprocessing, downstream statistical analysis and disease classification commonly require task-specific code, evaluation protocols, and data-format conventions, creating additional barriers between raw acquisitions and reproducible scientific analysis. We present NeuroAgent, an LLM-driven agentic framework that automates key preprocessing and analysis steps for heterogeneous neuroimaging data, including sMRI, fMRI, dMRI, and PET, and supports interactive downstream analysis through natural-language queries. NeuroAgent employs a hierarchical multi-agent architecture with a feedback-driven Generate-Execute-Validate engine: agents autonomously generate executable preprocessing code, detect and recover from runtime errors, and validate output integrity. We evaluate the system on 1,470 subjects pooled across all ADNI phases (CN=1,000, AD=470), where all subjects have sMRI and tabular data, with subsets also having Tau-PET (n=469), fMRI (n=278), and DTI ($n=620$). Pipeline ablation studies across multiple LLM backends show that capable models reach up to 100% intent-parsing accuracy, with the strongest backend (Qwen3.5-27B) reaching 84.8% end-to-end preprocessing step correctness. Automated recovery limits manual intervention to edge cases where human review is required via the Human-In-The-Loop interface. For Alzheimer's Disease classification using automatically preprocessed multimodal data, our agent ensemble achieves an AUC of 0.9518 with four modalities, outperforming all single-modality baselines. These results show that NeuroAgent can reduce the manual effort required for neuroimaging preprocessing and enable end-to-end automated analysis pipelines for neuroimaging research.

</details>


### [375] [GlazyBench: A Benchmark for Ceramic Glaze Property Prediction and Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06641)
*Ziyu Zhai,Siyou Li,Juexi Shao,Juntao Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出首个AI辅助釉料设计数据集GlazyBench，包含23,148个真实配方，支持釉料烧制后表面属性预测和可视化生成两大任务，并建立了全面的评估基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 陶瓷釉料开发成本高、耗时长，传统试错法效率低；现有AI方法因缺乏大规模数据集而受限。

Method: 收集23,148个真实釉料配方构建数据集；基于传统机器学习和大语言模型建立属性预测基线；利用深度生成模型和大模型建立图像生成基准。

Result: 实验表明在属性预测和图像生成任务上均取得了有前景但仍具挑战性的结果。

Conclusion: GlazyBench建立了AI辅助陶瓷釉料设计的标准化基准，推动了该领域系统化研究的进程。

Abstract: Developing ceramic glazes is a costly, time-consuming process of trial and error due to complex chemistry, placing a significant burden on independent artists. While recent advances in multimodal AI offer a modern solution, the field lacks the large-scale datasets required to train these models. We propose GlazyBench, the first dataset for AI-assisted glaze design. Comprising 23,148 real glaze formulations, GlazyBench supports two primary tasks: predicting post-firing surface properties, such as color and transparency, from raw materials, and generating accurate visual representations of the glaze based on these properties. We establish comprehensive baselines for property prediction using traditional machine learning and large language models, alongside image generation benchmarks using deep generative and large multimodal models. Our experiments demonstrate promising yet challenging results. GlazyBench pioneers a new research direction in AI-assisted material design, providing a standardized benchmark for systematic evaluation.

</details>


### [376] [AI Co-Mathematician: Accelerating Mathematicians with Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06651)
*Daniel Zheng,Ingrid von Glehn,Yori Zwols,Iuliya Beloshapka,Lars Buesing,Daniel M. Roy,Martin Wattenberg,Bogdan Georgiev,Tatiana Schmidt,Andrew Cowie,Fernanda Viegas,Dimitri Kanevsky,Vineet Kahlon,Hartmut Maennel,Sophia Alj,George Holland,Alex Davies,Pushmeet Kohli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: The AI co-mathematician is an interactive workbench that leverages AI agents to support the full mathematical research workflow. Unlike narrow theorem provers, it provides holistic support including ideation, literature search, computation, and proof, while maintaining state and managing uncertainty. It achieved a new SOTA result (48% on FrontierMath Tier 4) and has already contributed to solving open problems.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mathematical research is inherently exploratory, iterative, and open-ended, requiring support across the entire workflow from ideation to theorem proving. Existing AI tools are typically fragmented and poorly suited to the collaborative and iterative nature of mathematical discovery.

Method: The system provides an asynchronous, stateful workspace optimized for mathematical workflows. Key features include managing uncertainty, refining user intent, tracking failed hypotheses, and outputting native mathematical artifacts. It supports multiple stages: ideation, literature search, computational exploration, theorem proving, and theory building.

Result: Early tests demonstrate the system's effectiveness: (1) helped researchers solve open problems, (2) identified new research directions, (3) uncovered overlooked literature references, and (4) achieved 48% on FrontierMath Tier 4—a new high score among all evaluated AI systems.

Conclusion: The AI co-mathematician represents a paradigm shift in AI-assisted mathematical discovery, providing holistic support throughout the entire mathematical workflow. Its success on FrontierMath Tier 4 (48%) demonstrates state-of-the-art performance among AI systems.

Abstract: We introduce the AI co-mathematician, a workbench for mathematicians to interactively leverage AI agents to pursue open-ended research. The AI co-mathematician is optimized to provide holistic support for the exploratory and iterative reality of mathematical workflows, including ideation, literature search, computational exploration, theorem proving and theory building. By providing an asynchronous, stateful workspace that manages uncertainty, refines user intent, tracks failed hypotheses, and outputs native mathematical artifacts, the system mirrors human collaborative workflows. In early tests, the AI co-mathematician helped researchers solve open problems, identify new research directions, and uncover overlooked literature references. Besides demonstrating a highly interactive paradigm for AI-assisted mathematical discovery, the AI co-mathematician also achieves state of the art results on hard problem-solving benchmarks, including scoring 48% on FrontierMath Tier 4, a new high score among all AI systems evaluated.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [377] [DADL: A Declarative Description Language for Enterprise Tool Libraries in LLM Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05247)
*Axel Dunkel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出DADL，一种声明式YAML格式，用于统一描述REST API的各项特性，由运行时执行层解释，避免为每个API单独部署服务器。通过集中式凭证管理和固定尺寸的Code Mode接口，解决了MCP在企业级部署中的上下文窗口线性增长问题，实现142倍的上下文成本压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决MCP（Model Context Protocol）在组织规模下的两个结构性问题：(1) 每个API集成都作为独立的服务器进程部署，带来各自独立的部署、依赖树和凭证处理负担；(2) 每个工具的注册模型导致上下文窗口随目录规模线性增长，迫使实际部署只能暴露组织实际使用的少量API。

Method: 提出DADL (Dunkel API Description Language)，一种YAML格式的声明式API描述语言，通过单一文件定义REST API的端点、认证、分页、响应塑造和访问分类。该文件由运行时执行层解释，无需为每个API部署独立服务器进程或生成集成代码，所有工具共享同一运行时，实现集中式凭证和授权管理。

Result: 在包含1,833个工具定义和20个服务的公共目录上，Code Mode将工具广告的上下文成本从约142,000个token降低到约1,000个token，实现了142倍的压缩。

Conclusion: DADL enables an "Enterprise Tool Library" — a versioned, auditable collection of API integrations that any team can extend, share, and consume through one authentication and authorization boundary. The specification is released under CC BY-SA 4.0.

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) is the standard interface between large language model (LLM) agents and external tools. At organizational scale, however, it exposes two structural problems. First, every API integration is shipped as a dedicated server process with its own deployment, dependency tree, and credential handling; recent empirical work shows the overwhelming majority of these servers are thin wrappers around REST APIs. Second, the per-tool registration model causes context window consumption to grow linearly with catalog size, forcing real deployments to expose only a small fraction of the APIs an organization actually uses.
  We present DADL (Dunkel API Description Language), a YAML format describing a REST API's endpoints, authentication, pagination, response shaping, and access classification in a single declarative file. A DADL file is interpreted by an execution layer at runtime; no per-API server process is deployed and no integration code is generated, though the runtime is itself a server. Because all tools share that runtime, credentials and authorization are managed centrally, and the catalog reaches the LLM through a fixed-size Code Mode interface independent of size. The result is an Enterprise Tool Library: a versioned, auditable collection of API integrations any team can extend, share, and consume through one authentication and authorization boundary.
  The DADL v0.1 specification is released under CC BY-SA 4.0, and a public registry contains 1,833 tool definitions across 20 services. On this catalog, Code Mode reduces the context cost of tool advertisement from approximately 142,000 tokens to approximately 1,000, a 142x reduction; the per-call cost of search and execute invocations is additional and depends on the task.

</details>


### [378] [Automated Population-Level Audit Assurance via AI-Based Document Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05252)
*Santosh Vasudevan,Velu Natarajan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于AI文档智能的大规模审计交易测试框架，使用Snowflake Document AI从PDF对账单中自动提取结构化数据（仅需约20份标注文档），与权威数据源进行对账以识别差异，实现人口级别的全量审计测试，提升审计覆盖率并支持持续性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的手工、基于抽样的审查方式审查非结构化PDF对账单效率低下，且无法扩展到百万量级交易数据的审计需求。

Method: 利用Snowflake Document AI从非结构化PDF对账单中提取结构化数据，仅需约20份标注文档的少量标注语料库即可完成模型训练；随后将提取的数据与权威的真实数据源进行对账校验，以识别差异；结果通过交互式仪表盘和自动化报告呈现。

Result: 实现大规模交易测试自动化，支持人口级别的全量测试而非抽样检查，改善审计覆盖率并支持持续性保证目标。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了规模化的人口级别审计测试，而非传统的抽样方法，显著提升了审计覆盖率，并支持持续性保证目标。文档智能与分析驱动审计框架的结合，实现了近实时的风险识别与持续性保证。

Abstract: Audit transaction testing validates accuracy and completeness of customer-facing statements against internal systems of record. Traditional manual, sample-based review of unstructured PDF statements is labor-intensive and does not scale to millions of transactions. This paper presents an automated framework for large-scale audit transaction testing using AI-based document intelligence. The solution leverages Snowflake Document AI to extract structured data from unstructured PDF statements using a small labeled corpus (approximately 20 documents). Extracted data are reconciled against authoritative source-of-truth datasets to identify discrepancies at scale. Results are surfaced through interactive dashboards and automated reports. The framework enables population-level testing rather than sampling-based approaches, improving audit coverage and supporting continuous assurance objectives. Recent advances in document intelligence and analytics-driven audit frameworks enable scalable, near real-time risk identification and continuous assurance.

</details>


### [379] [Bridging Generation and Training: A Systematic Review of Quality Issues in LLMs for Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05267)
*Kaifeng He,Xiaojun Zhang,Peiliang Cai,Mingwei Liu,Yanlin Wang,Chong Wang,Kaifeng Huang,Bihuan Chen,Xin Peng,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是关于训练数据质量问题如何传播并影响代码生成质量的系统文献综述，涵盖114项研究，建立了统一分类体系（9维代码质量+2类数据质量）、18种传播机制的因果框架，并综述了检测与缓解技术，指出质量保证正从事后过滤向主动数据中心治理转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在代码生成任务中频繁产生缺陷输出（从逻辑错误到安全漏洞），虽然这些失败常被视为模型层面的问题，但越来越多的经验证据表明其根本原因在于训练语料库的缺陷。然而，训练数据质量问题与生成代码质量问题之间的具体作用机制尚未被系统揭示。

Method: 本文采用系统文献综述方法，对114项原始研究进行系统性梳理。建立了统一分类体系：生成代码质量问题分为9个维度，训练数据质量问题分为代码属性和非代码属性两类。在此基础上，形式化了一个包含18种典型传播映射机制的因果框架。此外，综述了跨数据、模型和生成生命周期的最新检测与缓解技术。

Result: 研究揭示了从被动、启发式的事后过滤，向主动、数据中心化治理和闭环修复的方法论转变。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了质量保证方法论的关键转变：从被动的事后启发式过滤，向主动的数据中心治理和闭环修复演进。最后，识别了开放性挑战并提出通过集成数据策管和持续评估来开发可靠代码生成LLM的研究方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) frequently generate defective outputs in code generation tasks, ranging from logical bugs to security vulnerabilities. While these generation failures are often treated as model-level limitations, empirical evidence increasingly traces their root causes to imperfections within the training corpora. Yet, the specific mechanisms linking training data quality issues to generated code quality issues remain largely unmapped. This paper presents a systematic literature review of 114 primary studies to investigate how training data quality issues propagate into code generation. We establish a unified taxonomy that categorizes generated code quality issues across nine dimensions and training data quality issues into code and non-code attributes. Based on this taxonomy, we formalize a causal framework detailing 18 typical propagation mapping mechanisms. Furthermore, we synthesize state-of-the-art detection and mitigation techniques across the data, model, and generation lifecycles. The reviewed literature reveals a clear methodological shift: quality assurance is transitioning from reactive, heuristic-based post-generation filtering toward proactive, data-centric governance and closed-loop repair. Finally, we identify open challenges and outline research directions for developing reliable LLMs for code through integrated data curation and continuous evaluation. Our repository is available at https://github.com/SYSUSELab/From-Data-to-Code.

</details>


### [380] [Mise en Place for Agentic Coding: Deliberate Preparation as Context Engineering Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05400)
*Andrew Zigler*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对AI编码agent缺乏上下文导致的系统性对齐问题，借鉴烹饪概念"mise en place"提出三阶段准备方法论（MEP），包括情境扎根、协作规格化和任务分解，并在黑客松中验证了约2小时准备可实现并发AI agent的快速并行实现。文章还提出"情境流利度"作为AI辅助开发的新兴技能概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码agent的快速采用产生了被称为"vibe coding"的主导工作模式，优先考虑实现速度而非充分准备。这种方法造成了系统性对齐问题：缺乏足够上下文的agent生成的代码需要大量调试和重构，消耗大量开发时间。

Method: 提出三阶段准备方法论MEP（mise en place）：（1）情境扎根——将领域专业知识与隐性知识外化为结构化文档；（2）协作规格化——通过人-agent对话生成详细设计制品；（3）任务分解——将规格转换为结构化、依赖感知的任务记录。

Result: 在一场竞争性黑客松中，约两小时的MEP准备使并发AI agent能够快速并行实现完整的全栈教育平台，验证了该方法论的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出并验证了"情境流利度"（context fluency）作为AI辅助开发中的新兴技能，连接了逆向设计（backward design）框架与隐性知识外化理论。同时提出了一个研究议程，旨在实证验证AI辅助软件开发中准备阶段方法论的有效性。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of AI coding agents has produced a dominant workflow pattern -- often called "vibe coding" -- that prioritizes speed of implementation over deliberate preparation. We argue that this approach creates a systematic alignment problem: agents that lack sufficient context produce code requiring extensive debugging and refactoring, consuming substantial development time. Drawing on the culinary concept of mise en place (everything in its place; abbreviated MEP), we propose a three-phase preparation methodology for agentic coding: (1) contextual grounding, where domain expertise and tacit knowledge are externalized into structured documents; (2) collaborative specification, where human-agent dialogue produces detailed design artifacts; and (3) task decomposition, where specifications are converted into structured, dependency-aware task records. We report on the application of MEP during a competitive hackathon, where roughly two hours of preparation enabled a rapid parallel implementation of a full-stack educational platform by concurrent AI agents. We introduce the concept of context fluency as an emerging developer skill -- the ability to create rich, structured context that agents can act on -- and connect it to established frameworks in backward design and tacit knowledge externalization. We conclude with a research agenda for empirically validating preparation-phase methodologies in AI-assisted software development.

</details>


### [381] [Is this Build Failure Related to my Patch? An Empirical Study of Unrelated Build Failures in Continuous Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05564)
*Andie Huang,Daniel Alencar da Costa,Grant Dick,Mariam El Mezouar*

Main category: cs.SE
